### [1] [From amplituhedra to flagtopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37017)
*Grant T. Barkley,Steven N. Karp,Kaitao Xie*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文构造了“旗形振幅多面体（flag amplituhedra）”，将振幅多面体（amplituhedron）定义中的两个格拉斯曼流形（Grassmannian）替换为约化群（reductive group）$G$ 的任意一对部分旗簇（partial flag varieties），并详细研究了一个非格拉斯曼型的实例：$\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1$ 中的弯曲六边形（curved hexagon）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振幅多面体由 Arkani-Hamed 与 Trnka（2014）引入，用于几何化编码高能物理中的散射振幅（scattering amplitudes），可视为循环多面体（cyclic polytope）向格拉斯曼流形的推广。本文意图将这一构造置于更一般的李理论（Lie theory）框架下加以推广。

Method: 将定义中一对格拉斯曼流形替换为约化群 $G$ 的任意一对部分旗簇，并以一个格拉斯曼流形的全非负部分（totally nonnegative part）相对第二个格拉斯曼流形中全负元素（totally negative element）的投影作为推广模式，构造旗形振幅多面体。

Result: 给出旗形振幅多面体的李理论推广定义，并具体研究了一个不属于格拉斯曼振幅多面体的例子，即 $\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1$ 中的弯曲六边形。

Conclusion: 旗形振幅多面体构成振幅多面体的李理论推广，其构造涵盖不等同于格拉斯曼振幅多面体的新几何对象，如 $\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1$ 中的弯曲六边形。

Abstract: The amplituhedron was introduced by Arkani-Hamed and Trnka (2014) to geometrically encode scattering amplitudes in high-energy physics. It can be viewed as the generalization of a cyclic polytope into the Grassmannian, and is defined to be the projection of the totally nonnegative part of one Grassmannian with respect to a totally negative element of a second Grassmannian. We construct a Lie-theoretic generalization of amplituhedra called 'flag amplituhedra', by replacing the pair of Grassmannians in the definition with an arbitrary pair of partial flag varieties for a reductive group $G$. We study in detail an example of a flag amplituhedron which is not a Grassmannian amplituhedron; it is a curved hexagon in $\mathbb{P}^1\times \mathbb{P}^1$.

</details>


### [2] [Rank reduction in orthosymplectic types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37460)
*Kyungtak Hong,Alexander Tsymbaliuk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明了正交辛型量子仿射超群（quantum affine supergroups of orthosymplectic type）的 RLL-实现（RLL-realization）存在秩缩减（rank reduction），并由此给出正交辛型 superYangians 相应结果的更概念化证明，同时确定了通用 R-矩阵（universal R-matrix）求值中的精确标量前因子（scalar prefactor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（arXiv:2311.18818）在有理极限（rational limit）下对正交辛型 superYangians 得到秩缩减结果，但缺乏概念性解释；arXiv:2408.16720 的 R-矩阵求值中前因子（prefactor）尚未精确确定。本文旨在统一并精细化这些结果。

Method: 在 RLL-实现框架下对正交辛型量子仿射超群建立秩缩减；通过取有理极限（rational limit）将该结果退化到 superYangians 情形，并计算通用 R-矩阵求值中出现的标量前因子。

Result: 正交辛型量子仿射超群的 RLL-实现具有秩缩减性质；有理极限下可给出 arXiv:2311.18818 中正交辛型 superYangians 结果的更概念化证明；确定了通用 R-矩阵求值中的精确标量前因子，从而细化了 arXiv:2408.16720 的主要结论。

Conclusion: 该注记以秩缩减为主线统一了量子仿射超群与 superYangians 的相关结论，并提供 R-矩阵标量前因子的精确形式。

Abstract: This note establishes a rank reduction for RLL-realization of quantum affine supergroups of orthosymplectic type. In the rational limit, this provides a more conceptual proof of the analogous result for orthosymplectic superYangians from arXiv:2311.18818. We also determine the exact scalar prefactor appearing in the evaluation of the universal $R$-matrix, refining the main result of arXiv:2408.16720.

</details>

### [3] [Proximity-Induced Giant Majorana Spin Response in a UTe$_2$-Metal Film Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36053)
*Jushin Tei,Makoto Shimizu,Yukio Tanaka,Satoshi Fujimoto,Takeshi Mizushima*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 提出以UTe$_2$为平台、通过超导邻近效应将Majorana表面态关联延伸至邻近金属薄膜，从而用核磁共振（NMR）探测其内禀Ising自旋响应；无序非但不削弱反而锐化该信号，可用于判别配对对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana表面态局域于表面，其内禀Ising自旋响应难以直接测量，缺乏实验可及的识别途径；同时候选配对对称性之间难以区分。

Method: 以UTe$_2$为具体平台，借助超导邻近效应（superconducting proximity effect）将Majorana关联扩展至相邻金属薄膜，使原本表面局域的响应可被核磁共振（NMR）测量；并考察杂质散射无序对常规$p$波邻近关联与Majorana贡献的不同影响。

Result: 无序使Majorana特征更加显著：杂质散射抑制常规$p$波（$p$-wave）邻近关联，却保留Majorana贡献，产生NMR弛豫率（NMR relaxation rate）的巨大Ising各向异性增强。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了实验上可及的Majorana表面态识别途径，并可作为区分候选配对对称性（pairing symmetries）的有力手段。

Abstract: We establish an experimentally accessible route to identifying Majorana surface states through their intrinsic Ising spin response, using UTe$_2$ as a concrete platform. The key is to extend the Majorana correlations into an adjacent metallic film via the superconducting proximity effect, thereby making the otherwise surface-localized response accessible to NMR measurements. Remarkably, disorder further sharpens the Majorana signature: impurity scattering suppresses the conventional $p$-wave proximity correlations while preserving the Majorana contribution, yielding a giant Ising-anisotropic enhancement of the NMR relaxation rate. This approach also offers a powerful means to distinguish among candidate pairing symmetries.

</details>


### [4] [Determination of the Magnetic Penetration Depth from Finite-Element Susceptibility Calculations of Spheroidal and Cylindrical Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36133)
*Morgan Grant,Antony Carrington*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文用有限元方法（finite-element analysis）计算三维超导椭球体与有限长圆柱体在有限穿透深度（penetration depth, λ）下的磁响应，量化形状依赖的迈斯纳效应（Meissner effect）并给出有效退磁因子（demagnetization factor）及有效样品尺寸的改进解析表达式，为基于体积排斥的磁化率测量提供高精度测定 λ 的流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从迈斯纳态（Meissner state）磁化率（magnetic susceptibility, χ）测量中提取穿透深度 λ 需准确处理几何依赖的退磁效应，但解析结果仅在少数情形下可得，限制了对新型超导材料的表征。

Method: 对三维超导椭球体与有限长圆柱体开展有限元分析（finite-element analysis）：在较宽的纵横比（aspect ratio）范围内计算磁矩与磁化率 χ(λ)；在 λ→0 极限下提取有效退磁因子，并利用 χ(λ) 的初始变化确定有限小 λ 下的有效样品尺寸。

Result: 给出了形状依赖的迈斯纳响应随纵横比的定量刻画，提取了 λ→0 极限下的有效退磁因子，并针对小而非零的 λ 提出了有效样品尺寸的改进解析表达式。

Conclusion: 结果为从基于体积排斥（volume-exclusion）的磁化率测量中高精度确定 λ 提供了流程，预期可直接应用于新型超导材料的表征。

Abstract: The magnetic penetration depth, $λ$, is a fundamental parameter of the superconducting state and is commonly determined from precision measurements of the magnetic susceptibility, $χ$, in the Meissner state. However, extracting $λ$ from such measurements requires an accurate treatment of geometry-dependent demagnetization effects, for which analytic results are only available in a limited number of cases. Here we present a finite-element analysis of the magnetic response of three dimensional superconducting spheroids and cylinders with finite penetration depth. By calculating the magnetic moment and susceptibility, $χ(λ)$, over a wide range of aspect ratios, we quantify the shape-dependent Meissner response and extract effective demagnetization factors in the $λ\rightarrow 0$ limit. For small but finite $λ$, we further determine effective sample dimensions from the initial variation of $χ(λ)$ and propose an improved analytical expression for these. Our results provide a high-precision procedure for determining $λ$ from volume-exclusion based susceptibility measurements and should be directly applicable to the characterization of novel superconducting materials.

</details>


### [5] [Large superconducting diode effect indicates nonuniform triplet pairing in UTe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36332)
*Zheyu Wu,Daniel Shaffer,Dmitry V. Chichinadze,Mengmeng Long,Hanyi Chen,Andrej Cabala,Theodore I. Weinberger,Maurice Bal,Steffen Wiedmann,Dave Graf,Vladimir Sechovský,Michal Vališka,F. Malte Grosche,Alex Levchenko,Alexander G. Eaton*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在三维重费米子三重态超导体 UTe₂ 体样品中观测到高峰值效率（33%）且发生在 33.6 T 强磁场下的超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect, SDE），其严格局限于超导与场致自旋极化态共存的窄场区，被解释为自发反演对称破缺与有限动量库珀对（finite-momentum Cooper pairing）的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDE 源于空间反演与时间反演对称性的同时破缺；对单重态超导体，有限动量配对（FFLO 态）可规避高场下的泡利拆对（Pauli pair-breaking），但对等自旋配对（equal spin pairing）情形如何及为何出现该效应尚属开放问题。

Method: 对三维重费米子三重态超导体 UTe₂ 的体样品进行高磁场输运测量，考察非互易输运（nonreciprocal transport）随磁场与相界的依赖关系；并采用唯象金兹堡-朗道理论（Ginzburg-Landau theory）进行建模。

Result: 在 33.6 T 高场下获得峰值二极管效率 33%，为迄今最高场条件下的 SDE 观测；该非互易输运严格局限于超导与场致自旋极化态在一阶变磁相界（first-order metamagnetic phase boundary）附近共存的窄场区，而远离该相区时 SDE 消失。

Conclusion: SDE 在窄相区外的缺失有力排除了纯外在涡旋钉扎不对称（vortex pinning asymmetry）解释，支持自发反演对称破缺伴随有限动量库珀对配对；模型表明磁序与三重态超导序的交织可稳定奇宇称 FFLO 态（odd-parity FFLO state）。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect (SDE) is characterized by unequal critical current magnitudes for opposite-polarity current flow, owing to broken spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetries. For a superconducting pair wavefunction to spontaneously break inversion symmetry implies the formation of Cooper pairs with nonzero momentum, yielding a nonuniform superconducting state. For singlet superconductors, the formation of such a (FFLO) state has long been understood to provide an escape from high field Pauli pair-breaking -- how and why such an effect might occur for equal spin pairing is an open question. Here we report the observation of a large SDE in bulk specimens of the three-dimensional heavy-fermion triplet superconductor UTe$_2$, with a peak diode efficiency of 33% at the high magnetic field strength of 33.6 T, representing the highest-field observation of the SDE to date. Crucially, this nonreciprocal transport is strictly confined to a narrow field regime where superconductivity coexists with a field-induced spin-polarized state near a first-order metamagnetic phase boundary. The absence of the SDE away from this slender phase-intersection argues strongly against purely extrinsic vortex pinning asymmetries. Instead, our findings indicate spontaneous inversion symmetry breaking accompanied by finite-momentum Cooper pairing. We model this behavior using a phenomenological Ginzburg-Landau theory, demonstrating that the intertwining of magnetic and triplet superconducting orders can stabilize an odd-parity FFLO state.

</details>


### [6] [Vortex anisotropy in chiral and nematic superconductors with strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36358)
*David Shuliutsky,Moshe Goldstein*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 结合Ginzburg-Landau理论与数值涡旋求解，该工作解释了4Hb-TaS₂中"各向异性H_c2与各向同性涡旋"并存的现象：在应变下，系统在转变附近呈向列相主导、远离转变时呈手性相主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双分量序参量的非常规超导体可支持旋转对称性破缺的向列相（nematic phase）与时间反演对称性破缺的手性相（chiral phase）。近期4Hb-TaS₂实验同时观测到H_c2的二重各向异性（提示向列超导）与低温下各向同性涡旋（提示手性超导），二者表面矛盾，需要理论解释。

Method: 建立适用的Ginzburg-Landau方程，并发展数值高效的涡旋求解算法，从而在较宽参数范围内研究涡旋的形状与各向异性；同时用解析方法分析涡旋各向异性对晶格应变（strain）的依赖关系。

Result: 解析结果表明，在向列相中涡旋各向异性在主阶上（to leading order）与应变无关，而手性相中则随应变线性依赖，据此可区分两相；数值计算结果与实验结果一致，即在应变存在下，系统在转变附近呈向列行为、远离转变呈手性行为。

Conclusion: 该研究调和了实验中H_c2各向异性与涡旋各向同性并存的表观矛盾，并提出了通过应变依赖关系判别向列相与手性相的可操作判据。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductors with a two-component order parameter support the existence of a rotational symmetry breaking nematic phase and a time-reversal symmetry breaking chiral phase. A recent experiment on 4Hb-TaS$_2$ surprisingly shows both twofold anisotropy in $H_{c2}$, which suggests nematic superconductivity, as well as isotropic vortices at low temperatures, which suggest chiral superconductivity. To resolve this apparent conundrum we write down the appropriate Ginzburg-Landau equations and develop a numerically efficient algorithm for finding vortex solutions, allowing us to study their shape and anisotropy over a broad parameter range. We also demonstrate analytically that, to leading order, the vortex anisotropy is independent of strain in the nematic case, but is linearly dependent on it in the chiral phase, providing a possible way to distinguish the two phases. Using all these results we show that, in the presence of strain, in a large parameter regime the system behaves nematically near the transition, leading to an anisotropy of $H_{c2}$, but is dominantly chiral away from the transition, leading to parametrically smaller vortex anisotropy, in line with the experiment.

</details>


### [7] [Nano-Nb Superconducting 3D Josephson Junctions Arrays in Cu-Normal Matrix: Superconducting Phase Coherence and Quantum Fluctuations Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36482)
*R. L. de Almeida,J. Albino Aguiar,J. A. H. Coaquira*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过快速冷却制备不同Nb浓度的Cuxwt%Nb颗粒样品，获得尺寸与晶间距可控的铌析出物，并系统研究其超导耦合与双转变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对微观结构演化、相分离、超导相干性与量子涨落（quantum fluctuations）之间复杂相互作用的挑战，并建立可系统获得不同亚微米析出物分布样品的稳健方法。

Method: 采用快速冷却（rapidly cooling）制备Nb浓度x=3、5、15、20 wt%的Cuxwt%Nb颗粒样品；结合光学与扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、直流磁化Mdc[H,T]、交流感应测量及磁输运测量（Rdc[H,T]、Rdc[V,I,H,T]、I[V,H,T]、[H,T,I]），并用含10个横向电压桥的微型霍尔传感器（micro Hall sensor）标定样品腔内的真实外加磁场。

Result: 磁化与输运测量均观察到双转变（double transition）特征：输运中随电流强度变化、感应测量中随磁场变化而显现。该行为表明体系由浸没于有序非超导基体中的超导晶粒集合构成，超导性质通常通过两个转变温度Tc(H)与TcJ(H)体现。

Conclusion: 该快速冷却方法可稳定获得具有规则尺寸与晶间距的Nb析出物分布，Nb晶粒构型通过超导耦合序参量（coupling order parameter）调控颗粒超导体与正常Cu基体之间的耦合，从而决定双超导转变行为。

Abstract: To address the complex interplay of microstructural evolution, phase separation, superconductivity coherence and quantum fluctuations, we prepared Cuxwt%Nb series of granular samples by rapidly cooling, where the niobium concentration varied (x = 3, 5, 15, 20). By employing this method, we successfully obtained samples characterized by well-defined precipitates of niobium, featuring regular sizes and grain spacing. The goal was to establish a systematic and robust method approach for obtaining samples with different of sub-micrometric precipitates distributions but also to investigate their superconducting properties. Structural characterization by optical and Scanning Electron Microscopy, dc Mdc[H,T] ac inductive , as well the magneto-transport measures rhodc[H,T], Rdc[V,I.H,T], I[V,H,T] and [H,T,I] were done. Also, using a micro Hall sensor with 10 measuring transverse voltage bridges regularly distributed, we were able to obtain the real external applied magnetic field value inside of the chamber sample space. Thus e studied the dependence of configuration of the Nb grains in the superconducting coupling order parameter properties that connects the grains superconductors to the normal Cu matrix. We verify through magnetization and transport measures the signature of the double transition. This, when we apply current of different intensities, in the transport measures, and different field values, in the inductive measures. This behavior is an indication that our system is made up of by a collection of superconducting grains immersed in a well-organized in a non-superconducting matrix. The results indicate that the superconducting properties of these systems are manifested usually through two transition temperatures Tc(H) e TcJ(H).

</details>


### [8] [Structural supermodulation enables resonant Raman determination of crystalline orientation in Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36995)
*Flavia Lo Sardo,Tommaso Confalone,Shreya Kumbhakar,Genda Gu,Ahmad Omar,Christian G. F. Blum,Daniel Wolf,Davide Massarotti,Francesco Tafuri,Axel Lubk,Kornelius Nielsch,Nicola Poccia,Golam Haider*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文利用偏振共振拉曼光谱（polarized resonant Raman spectroscopy）无损地确定 Bi-2212 薄片的 a、b 晶轴取向，并揭示面内各向异性源于超调制诱导的电子共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bi-2212 是强各向异性的 d 波超导体，其非常规性质对量子电子学与光子学有吸引力，但晶体轴在薄片中难以事先确定，限制了器件工程（尤其扭转约瑟夫森结）。

Method: 在同一块体晶体上关联劳厄衍射（Laue diffraction）、HAADF-STEM 与偏振拉曼散射，结合 458、532、660 nm 多波长测量，将 27 cm⁻¹ 超调制模（supermodulation mode）强度极大值对应到 b 轴，并分析其与 118 cm⁻¹ Bi/Sr 模的反相行为。

Result: 面内对比度在 532 nm 达峰、在 660 nm 几乎消失，表明各向异性源自超调制诱导的电子共振（electronic resonance）；据此可确定剥离薄片的晶轴并获取其对称通道（symmetry channels）。

Conclusion: 偏振共振拉曼提供了一种快速、光学、非破坏性的铜氧化物范德华异质结构（van der Waals heterostructures）取向工具。

### [10] [Superconductivity and local stacking polymorphism in LaNiGa2 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37366)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: LaNiGa2 has attracted considerable interest as a candidate unconventional superconductor, particularly due to its reported time-reversal symmetry breaking and nonsymmorphic crystal structure hosting symmetry-protected electronic band degeneracies. Here, we report the growth and characterization of superconducting LaNiGa2 thin films on MgO substrates by magnetron sputtering. X-ray diffraction reveals highly crystalline, (010)-oriented films with well-defined in-plane alignment and rotational twinning. Electrical transport measurements establish superconductivity with a transition temperature of approximately 1 K. Atomic-resolution transmission electron microscopy reveals local variations in crystallographic orientation and interlayer stacking, with a predominantly Cmcm structure containing minority Cmmm regions. ### [11] [Continuum thermodynamics of chemically reacting multi-temperature fluid mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37637)
*Dieter Bothe,Tommaso Ruggeri*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文构建了化学反应性、多速度多温度流体混合物的连续介质热力学模型，以单一熵密度封闭各组分的质量、动量与总能量守恒方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对化学反应性多速度、多温度流体混合物，需要一个能够同时处理组分温度、化学势与非平衡输运过程且满足熵原理的热力学一致框架。

Method: 采用单一熵密度（依赖于组分质量密度与非动能内能密度）封闭偏守恒方程，其偏导数定义组分温度与化学势；通过强化熵原理（strengthened entropy principle）与单独处理的体积变形导出加权欧拉关系（weighted Euler relation），并构造粘性、热传导、Maxwell–Stefan动量交换、热松弛与非线性反应动力学的本构关系。

Result: 基线模型包含热扩散（thermo-diffusion）及其互易耦合，并在各向同性余因子类（isotropic co-factor class）内给出完全耦合的互易补全；另一表述通过指定基元过程耦合化学转化与能量再分配。给出封闭所需材料函数及组分能量零点容许平移。电离网络（分辨电子能级）在细致平衡（detailed-balance）假设下得到Saha型电离关系与Boltzmann激发比，并在共同温度下恢复Saha–Eggert关系。

Conclusion: 竞争性的电子与重粒子过程可在不同指定温度下维持稳态布居，同时在两组之间传递能量，表明模型可描述非平衡多温度反应混合物的稳态输运与能量交换。

Abstract: We develop a continuum-thermodynamic model for chemically reacting, multi-velocity and multi-temperature fluid mixtures. Partial balances of mass, momentum and total energy are closed using a single entropy density depending jointly on the constituent mass and nonkinetic internal-energy densities. Its partial derivatives determine the constituent temperatures and chemical potentials. For the chosen entropy flux and separately treated volume deformation, the strengthened entropy principle yields a weighted Euler relation constraining the partial thermodynamic pressures. We construct thermodynamically consistent constitutive relations for viscosity, heat conduction, Maxwell--Stefan momentum exchange, thermal relaxation and nonlinear reaction kinetics. The baseline model includes thermo-diffusion and its reciprocal coupling; a fully coupled reciprocal completion is given within the stated isotropic co-factor class. An alternative formulation couples chemical conversion and energy redistribution through specified elementary processes. We identify the material functions required for closure and admissible shifts of constituent energy zeros. An ionization network with resolved electronic levels yields Saha-type ionization relations and Boltzmann excitation ratios under the stated detailed-balance assumptions, recovering the Saha--Eggert relation at common temperature. Competing electron- and heavy-particle processes can maintain stationary populations while transferring energy between groups at different prescribed temperatures.

</details>

### [12] [Tunable turning point of the frequency--temperature response in suspended bilayer SiN_x/Al nanofilms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35977)
*Yanli Shi,Hui Wu,Guangyu Li,Zhuo Miao,Luyao Zhu,Chen Zheng,Weidong Zheng,Qun Cao,Zhe He,Zheng Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过悬空双层 SiNx/Al 纳米薄膜的温度依赖振动本征频率实现单调、连续、可重复的非侵入式低温测温，覆盖 90 K 至 300 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温实验需要非侵入式测温（non-invasive thermometry）以避免传统传感器的自热效应；此前基于单层 SiNx 纳米薄膜的频率-温度曲线存在转折点限制量程，且热响应慢只能进行离散点分步测量。

Method: 采用悬空双层 SiNx/Al 纳米薄膜，利用双层与衬底之间有效热膨胀系数（coefficient of thermal expansion, CTE）失配调控频率-温度关系，并通过两层厚度比调节转折点；制备三种不同 Al 层厚度样品，并结合芯片热锚定（thermal anchoring）改善。

Result: 72 nm Al 样品的转折点被推至 300 K 以上，在 90–300 K 全区间呈单调负温度系数；热响应缩短至 1 s 以内，可在 3 K/min 升温速率下连续跟踪频率；175 K 以上重复性优于 150 mK，1 Hz 频率分辨率对应约 5 mK 温度分辨率。

Conclusion: 双层纳米薄膜测温方案克服了单层方案在量程与响应速度上的限制，实现了宽温区、连续、高分辨的非侵入式温度读出，转折点可通过厚度比按需设计。

Abstract: Non-invasive thermometry is highly desirable in cryogenic experiments to avoid the self-heating inherent in conventional sensors. In previous work, temperature was probed via the vibrational eigenfrequency of a suspended single-layer SiNx nanofilm. However, the turning point of the frequency-temperature curve limited the usable measurement range. Moreover, the slow thermal response confined measurements to a stepwise protocol with only discrete temperature points. Here, we show that these limitations can be overcome with a suspended bilayer SiNx/Al nanofilm, whose temperature-dependent vibrational eigenfrequency provides a monotonic, continuous, and repeatable temperature readout. The frequency-temperature dependence is governed by the mismatch between the effective coefficients of thermal expansion (CTE) of the bilayer and the substrate, which makes the turning point tunable through the thickness ratio of the two layers. Over the 90 K to 300 K range, we characterize three bilayer nanofilms with different Al layer thicknesses and show how the turning point shifts with the thickness of the Al layer. For the sample with 72 nm Al, the turning point is pushed beyond 300 K, yielding a monotonic negative temperature coefficient over the entire 90 K to 300 K range. Combining a thin Al layer on the SiNx film with improved thermal anchoring of the chip shortens the thermal response to below 1 s, enabling continuous frequency tracking at a ramp rate of 3 K/min. We also demonstrate that the nanofilm with a 72 nm Al layer achieves a repeatability better than 150 mK above 175 K, and the frequency resolution of 1 Hz corresponds to a temperature resolution of ~5 mK.

</details>


### [13] [Transport statistics in asymmetric coupled chaotic dots in series: Brownian motion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37090)
*Ashutosh Dheer,Santosh Kumar,Jayendra N. Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在无时间反演对称情形下，用随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）与Dyson布朗运动（Dyson's Brownian motion）方法，解析并数值研究了两个串联量子混沌腔（quantum chaotic cavities）随结耦合增强从两个独立CUE到单一CUE的交叉，导出了任意引线不对称下电导与散粒噪声功率的时间依赖均值与方差闭合表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于串联量子混沌腔输运统计的工作多局限于对称引线或完全耦合、独立腔等极限情形，缺乏在任意引线不对称条件下、耦合连续可调过程中输运性质统计演化的统一描述，因此需要建立可覆盖整个交叉区域的解析框架。

Method: 在随机矩阵理论框架内，将结宽度作为类时间参数，采用Dyson布朗运动方法刻画散射矩阵（scattering matrix）从两个独立圆酉系综（circular unitary ensemble, CUE）到单一CUE的交叉，推导电导（conductance）与散粒噪声功率（shot noise power）的时间依赖均值与方差的闭合表达式；并以随机矩阵模拟及基于Kwant的紧束缚输运（tight-binding transport）计算进行验证，同时对每个可观测量单独校准布朗时间参数与物理结宽度的映射。

Result: 解析结果在对称引线与完全耦合极限下退化为已知表达式。布朗时间参数与物理结宽度的映射被发现依赖具体可观测量，反映电导与散粒噪声功率所探测的本征函数关联（eigenfunction correlations）不同，并已分别校准。与随机矩阵模拟及Kwant紧束缚计算比较，系综平均量在整个交叉区域定量吻合，而方差因对微观涨落更敏感而偏离布朗运动预言。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了任意引线不对称下串联双腔输运统计的闭式描述，并表明布朗时间与结宽度的映射需按可观测量分别标定；均值层面理论验证良好，方差层面的偏离提示布朗运动近似在刻画微观涨落敏感性方面存在局限。

Abstract: We study statistics of transport properties in a system of two quantum chaotic cavities coupled in series through a tunable junction and connected to reservoirs with asymmetric leads. Within the random matrix theory framework and in the absence of time-reversal symmetry, the scattering matrix S undergoes a crossover from two independent circular unitary ensembles to a single CUE as the inter-cavity coupling increases. Modeling this crossover using Dyson's Brownian motion approach, with the junction width acting as a time-like parameter, we derive closed-form expressions for the time-dependent averages and variances of conductance and shot noise power for arbitrary lead asymmetry. These analytical results reduce to known expressions in the symmetric and fully coupled limits. The mapping between the Brownian time parameter and the physical junction width is found to be observable-dependent, reflecting the different eigenfunction correlations probed by conductance and shot noise power, and this dependence is examined and separately calibrated for each observable. Validating against random-matrix simulations and tight-binding transport calculations using Kwant, we find quantitative agreement for ensemble-averaged observables throughout the crossover, while variances, being more sensitive to microscopic fluctuations, show deviations from the Brownian-motion predictions.

</details>


### [14] [Spin Relaxation and Electrical Control of Spin Transport in Few-Layer $T_{\rm d}$-MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36399)
*T. Wakamura,M. Hashisaka,M. Bard,S. Okazaki,T. Sasagawa,T. Taniguchi,K. Watanabe,K. Muraki,N. Kumada*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低对称性过渡金属二硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenides）中的自旋输运行为尚待阐明，尤其缺乏对其自旋弛豫可调控性及主导机制的系统研究。

Method: 采用弱反局域化（weak antilocalization）测量提取自旋轨道长度与自旋轨道散射时间，并施加垂直电场研究其对 Rashba 参数、自旋轨道长度与时间的影响。

Conclusion: 少层 T_d-MoTe₂ 可作为电学可调自旋输运平台，并为理解低对称性过渡金属二硫族化物中的自旋弛豫提供依据。

Abstract: We report electrical tuning of spin relaxation in atomically thin $T_{\rm d}$-MoTe$_2$, a representative low-symmetry transition metal dichalcogenide. Weak antilocalization measurements reveal strong spin--orbit interaction with spin--orbit lengths of tens of nanometers, consistent with recent theoretical predictions. Despite the semimetallic nature of $T_{\rm d}$-MoTe$_2$, in the ultrathin limit the spin--orbit scattering time can be tuned electrostatically by up to threefold. We further investigate vertical electric field effects on the spin--orbit length and time, and demonstrate the modulation of the Rashba parameters. Relation between spin--orbit scattering time and the diffusion coefficient indicates that spin relaxation is governed by the D'yakonov--Perel' mechanism in both monolayer and bilayer $T_{\rm d}$-MoTe$_2$. These results establish few-layer $T_{\rm d}$-MoTe$_2$ as a platform for electrically tunable spin transport, thereby providing insight into spin relaxation in low-symmetry transition metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [15] [Laser-Induced 90-Degree Magnetization Switching for Single-Element 2D Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36530)
*Floris J. F. van Riel,Niels F. L. van Kempen,Bert Koopmans,Diana C. Leitao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Co/Gd双层薄膜十字形结构的各向异性磁阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance）传感器，利用超短激光脉冲实现90°可切换的磁化方向，从而单器件实现二维矢量磁场探测与传感轴的光学重编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轴磁阻传感器（magnetoresistive sensors）在局部磁场矢量映射中有需求，但现有设计多采用分立传感元件离散化，或需复杂探测方案区分不同分量，亟需单器件、可重构的矢量传感方案。

Method: 器件为可光学切换的Co/Gd薄膜双层，图案化为十字形以稳定两个正交面内磁化方向；通过选择性激励十字的一条对角线，单次激光脉冲使中心磁化方向翻转90°，并以平面霍尔效应（planar Hall effect）电学检测灵敏度在x轴与y轴间的切换。

Result: 实现单元件二维矢量传感器，其传感轴可经超短激光脉冲按需重编程，磁化方向与场灵敏度在x、y轴之间以90°切换，并通过平面霍尔效应完成电学读取。

Conclusion: 该结果为光学可重编程集成磁矢量传感器（optically reprogrammable integrated magnetic vector sensors）迈出关键一步。

Abstract: Magnetoresistive sensors with multiaxial sensitivity are interesting for field mapping applications where local measurement of multiple magnetic field components is needed. Existing device designs are often discretized with separate sensing elements for each vector component, or they require complex detection schemes to distinguish the different components. In this work, we showcase a single-element two-dimensional vector sensor with on-demand reprogrammable sensing axis using light and optically switchable materials. Our device is an anisotropic magnetoresistive sensor with $90^\circ$ togglable sensitivity upon excitation with ultrashort laser pulses. The sensor consists of an optically switchable Co/Gd thin-film bilayer, patterned into a cross shape that stabilizes two orthogonal in-plane magnetization directions. By selectively addressing one diagonal of the cross, a single laser pulses toggle switches the magnetization direction in the center of the cross by $90^\circ$. Moreover, the field sensitivity of the device is toggled between the $x$- and $y$-axes, which we detected electrically with the planar Hall effect. Our results form a milestone towards optically reprogrammable integrated magnetic vector sensors.

</details>


### [16] [Ferron-driven motion of non-collinear ferroelectric domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36614)
*Huanhuan Yang,Gerrit E. W. Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文通过解析与数值计算研究铁电畴壁（domain wall, DW）对铁电振子（ferron，极化波量子）的散射，预测90°畴壁充当纵模铁电振子与横光学声子的分束器，并产生驱动畴壁运动的力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>

### [21] [Spin-Orbit-Torque-Driven Recurrent Motion of Synthetic Antiferromagnetic Skyrmions for Magnetic Clocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37014)
*Sayan Mathur,Payal Bhattacharjee,Saswati Barman,Anjan Barman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出基于合成反铁磁体（synthetic antiferromagnets, SAF）中斯格明子（skyrmion）周期性平移运动的可调频磁时钟概念，并通过微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）界定实现该概念所需的材料条件。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAF 斯格明子因拓扑补偿（topologically compensated）可抑制斯格明子霍尔效应（skyrmion Hall effect），利于电流驱动下的可靠输运，因而被视为自旋电子学（spintronics）技术中有吸引力的信息载体；本文旨在将其拓展为可调频的纳米尺度计时与信号生成元件。

Result: 在自旋轨道力矩激发下，SAF 斯格明子表现为稳健的纵向输运且横向偏转极小；振荡频率可由外加电流密度与材料特性调节；循环输运的阈值激活启动行为类似紧张性放电（tonic-spiking）特征。

Conclusion: 该工作将拓扑保护、可控输运与循环动力学相结合，确立 SAF 斯格明子作为未来自旋电子功能器件的通用构建单元，并指向无场（field-free）纳米尺度计时元件与神经形态信号生成（neuromorphic signal generation）的潜在应用。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions in synthetic antiferromagnets have emerged as attractive information carriers for spintronic technologies due to their compensated topology, which mitigates the skyrmion Hall effect and facilitates reliable current-driven transport. Here we present a frequency-tunable magnetic clocking concept based on the recurring translational motion of SAF skyrmions and define the material conditions needed to realize it in a Pt/Co/Ru/Pt/Co multilayer. Using micromagnetic simulations, we investigate the combined effects of interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and interlayer Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida exchange coupling, delineating the parameter space in which coupled Néel-type skyrmions with opposite topological charges form a stable, topologically compensated pair. Under spin-orbit torque excitation, the SAF skyrmions exhibit resilient longitudinal transport with minimal transverse deflection using periodic boundary conditions. Periodic boundary conditions serve as an idealized model for closed-loop racetrack geometries, facilitating the study of continuous transport cycles and long-range steady-state dynamics. The oscillation frequency is readily modified by the applied current density and material characteristics, offering a pathway to field-free nanoscale timing components. Moreover, the threshold-activated initiation of recurrent transport mimics tonic-spiking behavior, indicating possible use in neuromorphic signal generation. This work establishes SAF skyrmions as versatile building elements for future spintronic functional devices by integrating topological protection, controllable transport, and cyclic dynamics.

</details>


### [22] [Two Exceptional-Point Regimes in the Spinterface Model of Chirality-Induced Spin Selectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37072)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该摘要基于手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）的spinterface模型，提出两类代数上互斥的近奇异点（near-exceptional-point, near-EP）机制，并指出二者混合时判别式为负、不存在EP，从而证明金态级/链级分类的穷尽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性结（CISS junction）中非厄米谱物理（non-Hermitian spectral physics）的机制尚需明确刻画，尤其是近奇异点如何产生及其物理后果缺乏系统分类。

Result: 金态near-EP处自旋翻转耦合具有手性选择性（chirality-selective），可将非手性自旋阀输运（spin-valve transport）在明确定义的交叉点转变为纯手性输运；链级near-EP处Petermann因子（Petermann factor）随链长呈幂律增长，指数可由Rashba相干长度调控且对Rashba强度非单调。两类near-EP均为理想两态EP的“缀饰”版本，观测EP位置与本征EP位置之比度量合并态对的离域化程度，可作免拟合机制诊断。

Abstract: The spinterface model of chirality-induced spin selectivity supports two algebraically distinct near-exceptional-point (EP) regimes, which cannot be mixed. A \emph{gold-level} near-EP involves the two gold spin states under an anti-Hermitian, electrode-mediated spin flip with spin-symmetric dissipative broadening (Scheme~B). A \emph{chain-level} near-EP involves the collective coalescence of the two molecular eigenstates with largest weight on the first molecular site under a Hermitian on-site Rashba spin flip with spin-dependent broadening (Scheme~A). Mixing the diagonal cancellation of one scheme with the off-diagonal structure of the other yields a strictly negative discriminant and admits no EP on any parameter path, making the gold/chain classification exhaustive. At the gold near-EP the spin-flip coupling is chirality-selective, converting achiral spin-valve transport into purely chiral transport across well-defined crossover points. At the chain near-EP the Petermann factor grows as a power law in chain length, with an exponent tunable by the Rashba coherence length and non-monotonic in the Rashba strength, reflecting a balance between coherent enhancement and progressive breakdown of the Scheme-A condition. Both near-EPs are dressed versions of the ideal two-state EP, and the ratio between observed and bare EP locations measures the delocalisation of the coalescing pair, providing a fit-free diagnostic of the mechanism. These results identify CISS junctions as a solid-state platform for non-Hermitian spectral physics.

</details>

Abstract: Vertically-aligned MoS$_2$ films paired with a Cu top electrode have been used as electrochemical metallization cell memristor devices. Here we carry out a multi-scale computational study of Cu intercalation, clustering, and field-driven dynamics to eventually model the filament formation under operating conditions. By exhaustive enumeration of Cu intercalated structures, we find the most stable structure at MoS$_2$Cu$_{0.11}$ composition, with Cu atoms forming a dice lattice between the MoS$_2$ layers. These structures are shown to exhibit metallic character and can thus form conductive filaments under memristor operation. *Xin-Ran Ma,Kui Cao,Qian Du,Zheng Wei,Su-Peng Kou*

TL;DR: 该工作建立微观边界通道（boundary-channel）框架，统一解释Chern绝缘体中边界耗散如何重构拓扑边缘态并决定其在弱损耗与强损耗区域的衰减速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界耗散会改变Chern绝缘体中拓扑边缘模的空间分布与衰减性质，但其微观衰减机制及有效衰减率的定量确定尚缺乏统一描述。

Method: 保留有损耗的外层轨道与厄米内禀系统的归一化边缘态，构造直接耦合的亮边界通道（bright boundary channel），其余内部态通过Feshbach自能（Feshbach self-energy）纳入；并在Qi–Wu–Zhang模型与Haldane模型中求解有效衰减率。

Result: 弱损耗下衰减领头阶由原边缘态在损耗边界上的概率权重决定，耦合修正自三阶起始；强损耗下重构的内边缘态经虚耦合至阻尼亮通道衰减，速率正比于微观耦合平方、反比于裸损耗。Haldane模型显示同一领头机制成立，但矩阵值自能反馈会修正有限损耗下的边界谱并移动奇异点（exceptional point）阈值。

Conclusion: 该框架给出边界态重构及其衰减机制的统一下微观图像，并提供可用于光子晶体、声子晶体与拓扑电路实验检验的可观测预测。

Abstract: Boundary dissipation can reshape the spatial profiles and decay properties of topological edge modes in Chern insulators. We develop a microscopic boundary-channel framework to explain their decay mechanisms and determine the effective decay rates in the weak- and strong-loss regimes. By retaining the lossy outer orbitals and a normalized edge state of the Hermitian inner system, we identify a directly coupled bright boundary channel, while incorporating the remaining inner states through a Feshbach self-energy. In the Qi--Wu--Zhang model, weak-loss decay is governed to leading order by the original edge state's probability weight on the lossy boundary, with coupling-induced corrections starting at cubic order. At strong loss, the reconstructed inner-edge state instead decays through virtual coupling to the damped bright channel, giving a rate proportional to the squared microscopic coupling and inversely proportional to the bare loss. The Haldane model shows that the same leading decay mechanism holds for a different intercell hopping structure, while additional matrix-valued self-energy feedback modifies the boundary spectrum at finite loss and shifts the exceptional-point threshold. These results provide a unified microscopic description of boundary-state reconstruction and its decay mechanisms, offering testable predictions to guide experiments in photonic crystals, phononic crystals, and topolectrical circuits.

</details>

*Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了一维量子液体（one-dimensional quantum liquids）的流体力学涨落（hydrodynamic fluctuations）理论，将电子液体类比为超流氦的双流体（two-fluid）描述，并加入Langevin通量，计算了库仑耦合量子线中的动态结构因子、库仑拖曳电阻率与近场热输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温下单通道导线中相互作用电子的弛豫分为两类过程：改变左右移动电子数的平衡化（equilibration）以指数速率缓慢进行，而固定移动电子数下重分配能量与动量的热化（thermalization）按幂律速率进行。这种时间尺度分离赋予电子液体类似超流⁴He的双流体结构，而此前缺乏相应的涨落理论。

Method: 以Luttinger液体的零模（zero mode）作为超流分量、热激发气体作为正常分量；利用Onsager倒易关系（Onsager reciprocity）与涨落-耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem），在双流体流体力学方程中加入Langevin通量，其中包括由稀薄背散射事件产生的、在氦中无对应的互摩擦噪声（mutual-friction noise）。

Result: 应用于库仑耦合量子线（Coulomb-coupled quantum wires），得到动态结构因子，其中在等离激元双峰（plasmon doublet）旁出现第二声峰（second-sound peak）；库仑拖曳电阻率（Coulomb drag resistivity）中，线内平衡化成为瓶颈，使拖曳呈激活型温度依赖；并给出导线间近场热传递（near-field heat transfer）。模频率、谱权重、线宽与输运系数交叉尺度均由液体的热力学性质及少数耗散系数表达。

Conclusion: 该理论为在一维量子系统中通过谱学与输运手段探测双流体行为提供了框架，相关可观测量可由热力学性质与少量耗散系数统一描述。

Abstract: We develop the theory of hydrodynamic fluctuations for one-dimensional quantum liquids. At low temperatures the relaxation of interacting electrons in a single-channel wire separates into two classes: equilibration, which changes the numbers of right and left movers through backscattering and is exponentially slow, and thermalization, which redistributes energy and momentum at a fixed number of movers at a power-law rate. This separation of time scales gives the electron liquid a two-fluid description analogous to superfluid $^4$He, with the zero mode of the Luttinger liquid as the superfluid component and the gas of thermal excitations as the normal component. Using Onsager reciprocity and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, we add Langevin fluxes to the two-fluid hydrodynamic equations, including a mutual-friction noise generated by rare backscattering events that has no counterpart in helium. We apply the theory to Coulomb-coupled quantum wires and calculate the dynamic structure factors, which contain a second-sound peak next to the plasmon doublet; the Coulomb drag resistivity, where intrawire equilibration acts as a bottleneck and turns the hydrodynamic drag into an activated temperature dependence; and the near-field heat transfer between the wires. Mode frequencies, spectral weights, linewidths, and the crossover scales of the transport coefficients are expressed through the thermodynamic properties of the liquid and a few dissipative coefficients, providing spectroscopic and transport probes of two-fluid behavior in one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限电场与小幅扭转角的结合被认为可产生高度可调的电子结构并支持涌现现象（emergent phenomena），但其微观电子结构缺乏直接实验测量。

Method: 采用微区角分辨光电子能谱（micro-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, microARPES）对扭转角为3.1°和6.0°的扭转双层双层石墨烯（twisted double bilayer graphene）原位门控样品进行电子结构测量。

Result: 6.0°样品中价带与导带极值附近色散与独立双层石墨烯基本相同，但在更高结合能处出现电场可调的杂交带隙与带平坦化（band flattening）；3.1°样品中超晶格（superlattice）驱动带边色散改变，增大底层门控掺杂可在底层价带顶与顶层导带底之间形成平带（flat bands）。

Conclusion: 实验确定了可能支持强电子-空穴激发（electron-hole excitations）的扭转角与电场组合，并为中等扭转双层双层石墨烯的理论描述提供基准。

Abstract: Application of a finite electric field and small twist angle between two graphene bilayers leads to widely tunable electronic structures that support emergent phenomena. Here, we measure the electronic structure of twisted double bilayer graphene with twist angles of 3.1$^{\circ}$ and 6.0$^{\circ}$ using micro-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (microARPES) on \textit{in situ} gated samples. At a twist angle of 6.0$^{\circ}$, we find that the dispersion around the valence and conduction band extrema is unchanged compared to separate graphene bilayers, whereas electric field tunable hybridisation gaps and band flattening effects occur at higher binding energies. In the 3.1$^{\circ}$ structure, we find that the resulting superlattice drives dispersion changes around the valence and conduction band edges. Increasing the gate-induced doping in the bottom bilayer leads to the formation of flat bands between the valence band maximum in the bottom bilayer and conduction band minimum in the top bilayer. Our experiments establish combinations of twist angle and electric field that potentially support strong electron-hole excitations and that provide a benchmark for the theoretical description of moderately twisted double bilayer graphene.

</details>

Abstract: A technology for the in situ formation of a three-layer TiN$_x$/Ti/TiN$_x$ functional material within a single vacuum cycle is presented, together with the results of investigations of its structural and electrophysical properties. The maximum critical power density reached approximately 140 MW/cm$^2$. Under continuous operation at 90% of the critical power, the structures remained operational for 24 h. The characteristics of microbridges without and with a protective SiO$_x$ layer were compared. The presence of the coating modifies the electrothermal operating regime of the structures and enables estimated temperatures of approximately 500-700 degrees Celsius to be reached.

### [28] [Wavefunction-templated metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37248)
*Yutao Chen,Tao Xiao,Jing Li,Aodong Li,Biye Xie*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出“波函数模板化超材料（wavefunction-templated metamaterials）”，可物理实现具有多种预设、谱隔离本征态的超材料，其哈密顿量满足仅由单接触边界格点决定的边界律，并在离散晶格与声学超材料中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超材料的本征模空间分布通常由其预设结构（哈密顿量）决定，缺乏对本征态空间构型的自由定制能力，限制了功能设计的灵活性。

Method: 构造承载预设、谱隔离本征态的哈密顿量几何，使其满足边界律——所需非局域性（nonlocality）仅由单接触边界格点（single-contact boundary sites）决定，与态内部及晶格尺寸无关；随后对稀疏哈密顿量进行厄米化（Hermitianization），并通过谱重塑（spectral reshaping）在目标本征态周围打开保护性带隙。

Result: 在离散晶格与声学超材料（acoustic metamaterials）中验证了该方法的可行性，成功实现预设且谱隔离的本征态。

Conclusion: 该设计原理虽在声学中演示，但可推广至热学、电路（electrical-circuit）与光子超材料，为按需定制本征态的超材料设计提供通用框架。

Abstract: Metamaterials, which store, guide and emit waves through their eigenmodes, have enabled the development of devices with unprecedented functionalities. Yet the spatial profiles of conventional metamaterials' eigenmodes are typically determined by their pre-determined structures (Hamiltonians). In this work, we introduce `wavefunction-templated metamaterials', a class of physically realizable metamaterials hosting diverse prescribed, spectrally isolated eigenstates. The resulting Hamiltonian geometries obey a boundary law, with the required nonlocality determined solely by single-contact boundary sites, independent of the state interior or lattice size. The sparse Hamiltonians are then Hermitianized, followed by spectral reshaping to open a protective gap around the target eigenstate. We validate our approach in discrete lattices and acoustic metamaterials. Although demonstrated in acoustics, the principle can be applied to thermal, electrical-circuit and photonic metamaterials.

</details>

### [29] [The Keller problem for the lowest positive eigenvalue of Dirac operators with Lorentz scalar potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36240)
*David Gontier,Hanne Van Den Bosch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上 Dirac 算子 $\mathcal{D}_m - V\beta$ 的 Keller 问题，在给定 $L^p$ 范数下最小化最低正特征值，证明最优势存在且对应特征旋量解非线性 Soler 方程，并给出零模与集中形态的维数相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Keller 型谱优化问题（固定 $L^p$ 范数下优化特征值）推广到 Dirac 算子，并借此为非线性 Soler 方程孤立波（solitary waves）的存在性提供变分证明。

Method: 采用变分方法（variational method）：在所有具有给定 $L^p$ 范数的势 $V$ 上最小化最低正特征值，并分析对应特征旋量（eigenspinors）所满足的非线性方程；辅以一维显式计算与二维数值计算。

Result: 最优势始终存在，其特征旋量解非线性 Soler 方程；证明 $d=1,2$ 时此类算子不存在零模（zero modes），并猜想 $d=3$ 时可能存在零模。二维数值结果表明最优势集中于非必然径向的花形区域（flower-shape regions），暗示 Faber–Krahn 型结果对此类 Dirac 算子可能失效；一维情形所有量均可显式表达。

Conclusion: 该工作以变分框架统一处理 Dirac 算子的 Keller 问题与 Soler 方程孤立波存在性，揭示零模存在性及最优势几何形态均强烈依赖空间维数，并提示经典 Faber–Krahn 型结论在 Dirac 情形下未必成立。

Abstract: We study the Keller problem for the Dirac operator $\mathcal{D}_m - Vβ$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and minimize its lowest positive eigenvalue over all potentials $V$ with prescribed $L^p$ norm. We prove that optimal potentials always exist and that the corresponding eigenspinors solve the nonlinear Soler equation. This provides a variational proof of existence of solitary waves for this equation. We prove that there are no zero modes for such operators in dimensions $1$ and $2$, and conjecture that zero mode may exist in dimension $3$. In dimension $d = 2$, we provide numerical evidence that the optimal potentials concentrate on flower-shape regions, which are not necessarily radial, showing that Faber--Krahn type results may fail for such Dirac operators. In dimension $1$, all quantities are explicit.

</details>


### [30] [Almost Periodicity Properties Arising From the Sodin-Yuditskii Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36403)
*David Damanik,Long Li,Nicolae Strungaru*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在 Sodin-Yuditskii 框架可实现时，可导出 Weyl 几乎周期性（Weyl almost periodicity），从而为 Kotani-Last 问题与 Deift 猜想给出正面结果，并指出修改版 Deift 猜想仍是未解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kotani-Last 问题与 Deift 猜想的原始表述（基于 Bohr/Bochner 几乎周期性，Bohr/Bochner almost periodicity）均已被反证，作者希望与之对照并进一步阐明这两个问题的本质。

Method: 在 Sodin-Yuditskii 设定（Sodin-Yuditskii setup）可实现的情形下进行论证，该设定要求相关函数对应的 Schrödinger 算子（Schrödinger operator）具有纯绝对连续谱测度（purely absolutely continuous spectral measure），且谱满足有限总间隙长度（finite total gap length）与 Parreau-Widom 性质。

Result: 在该设定下可推出 Weyl 几乎周期性；此外，Deift 猜想语境中已知或被认为 Bohr/Bochner 几乎周期性失效的例子，实际上均为 Weyl 几乎周期。

Conclusion: 两个问题的正面前景在于以 Weyl 几乎周期性替代 Bohr/Bochner 几乎周期性；相应修改后的 Deift 猜想仍是引人关注的开放问题（open problem）。

Abstract: The Kotani-Last problem and the Deift conjecture are two well known statements, which both concern the almost periodicity properties of the functions under consideration, and which have both been disproved in their original formulation, which involves Bohr/Bochner almost periodicity. To contrast these disproofs, and to further elucidate the essence of these two challenging problems, we establish positive results regarding each of them by showing that Weyl almost periodicity can be deduced whenever the Sodin-Yuditskii setup can be implemented. The latter requires that the Schrödinger operator associated with the function in question has purely absolutely continuous spectral measure and a spectrum with finite total gap length and the Parreau-Widom property. We also show that the known examples for which Bohr/Bochner almost periodicity has been shown or suggested to fail in the context of the Deift conjecture are in fact Weyl almost periodic. As a result, an accordingly modified version of the Deift conjecture remains a tantalizing open problem.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画稳定分层旋转流体中小扰动的长时间稳定性（long-time stability），并厘清线性色散（linear dispersion）与三波共振（three-wave resonances）缺失对该时间尺度的不同贡献。

Method: 针对 $\varepsilon$ 小、局部化且位涡（potential vorticity）消失的初值，一方面利用振幅以 $\langle t\rangle^{-1/2}$ 衰减的线性色散估计；另一方面在 Brunt–Väisälä 频率与科里奥利参数（Coriolis parameter）相当的情形下，借由规范型变换（normal form transformation）利用三波共振缺失。

Result: 解在 $O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ 时间内保持 $O(\varepsilon)$ 量级；在频率可比区域该时间延长至 $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$。此外，结果可推广至小而非零的初始位涡，其量级通过内蕴坐标变换（intrinsic change of coordinates）得以传播。

Conclusion: 研究表明稳定分层旋转流体的小扰动长时间行为由线性色散与共振结构共同控制，规范型方法与坐标变换可将结论从零位涡情形延伸至小位涡情形。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of a vertical-slice model for incompressible inhomogeneous rotating fluids perturbed around a stably stratified rest state with linear buoyancy profile. The first result relies on linear dispersion with amplitude decay rate $\langle t\rangle^{-\frac12}$, while the second exploits the absence of three-wave resonances via a normal form transformation. Our results remain valid for small nonzero initial potential vorticity, whose size is propagated by means of an intrinsic change of coordinates.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注带 Sacks-Uhlenbeck 正则化的 Baby-Skyrme 能量 E^α 的度数 1 极小元在正则化参数 α→0 时的行为，旨在刻画其极限结构，并补充经典 Sacks-Uhlenbeck 能量框架下的紧性认识。

Result: 证明度数 1 极小元在 α→0 时收敛到一个简单气泡树（simple bubble tree）；同时得到经典 Sacks-Uhlenbeck 能量的一个新无颈部（no-neck）结果。

Conclusion: 带 Sacks-Uhlenbeck 正则化的 Baby-Skyrme 模型中，度数 1 极小元的极限结构可被气泡树完整描述，且气泡间不出现颈部能量损耗。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This paper focuses on the numerical and analytical determination of the mechanical parameters of aluminum-matrix nanocomposites using atomistic-scale simulations within a multiscale modeling framework. Molecular statics simulations were employed to evaluate the tensile and compressive behavior of Al based composites reinforced with nanosized alumina particles. Two classes of atomistic models were investigated. First, a unit-cell model containing a single Al2O3 monocrystal embedded in an aluminum matrix was used to examine the influence of the volume fraction of the ceramic inclusion on the effective mechanical properties. Second, a polycrystalline RVE model comprising an alumina particle was analyzed to assess the effect of the number of Al grains on effective properties. For each case, the mechanical response was characterized by stress-strain curves, enabling the identification of upper-scale material parameters associated with the elastic regime (bulk, shear and Young's modulus) and the damage stage (ultimate strength, ultimate strain, and failure strain). The isotropized elastic constants evaluated from molecular simulations were compared with classical Voigt-Reuss-Hill bounds to assess their applicability in the elastic regime. Finally, atomistic-scale simulation outputs provided insight into the dominant deformation/damage mechanisms (brittle versus ductile) and fracture modes (transgranular versus intergranular).

### [37] [Stability analysis of the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$ describing polytropic gas spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35932)
*S. C. Mancas,Ronald Adams*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究临界指数 n=5 的 Lane–Emden 方程，利用 Schuster 变换与对数变量将其化为自治 Hamilton 系统，给出轨道闭式解与 n=5 处的稳定性与谱分析结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n=5 是区分有限半径与无限半径自引力平衡态（self-gravitating equilibria）的临界多方指数（polytropic index）情形，但该临界情形下解的结构与稳定性缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 借助标度族 θ_λ(ξ)=λ^{1/2}θ(λξ) 构造中性模 φ_0=½θ+ξθ'，并通过 Schuster 变换与对数变量将方程化为自治 Hamilton 系统 ν̈=¼(ν−ν^5)；再用 Weierstrass ℘-函数求闭式解，并在同调不变变量（homology-invariant variables）下分析奇异性态，最后对 Schuster 剖面线性化得到 Schrödinger 算子 L=−Δ_r−5θ_S^4 并作 Friedrichs 扩张谱分析。

Result: 所有穿越 ν=0 的轨道均可用 ℘-函数闭式表达，且与数值积分吻合至约 10^{-12}；Schuster 解（至标度与符号）是唯一中心正则解，并分隔正解与无穷多零点解。同调不变变量下奇异解 θ=(2ξ)^{-1/2} 恰在 n=5 处为中心型平衡且稳定性发生改变；变距 Emden–Fowler 系统中 n=5 时唯一平衡态线性与非线性不稳定。L 的本质谱为 [0,∞)，在 0 处有由非归一化标度模承载的阈值共振（threshold resonance），且恰有一个负特征值 λ_0≈−1.21。

Conclusion: n=5 临界情形下 Schuster 剖面是对应能量泛函的鞍点（saddle point），奇异解在此处发生稳定性转变，结果为该临界多方情形的平衡态分类与稳定性提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We study the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$, the critical polytropic case separating finite-radius from infinite-radius self-gravitating equilibria. The scaling family $θ_λ(ξ)=λ^{1/2}θ(λξ)$ generates a neutral mode $φ_0=\tfrac12θ+ξθ'$ about the Schuster profile. Schuster's transformation and a logarithmic variable reduce the equation to the autonomous Hamiltonian system $\ddotν=\tfrac14(ν-ν^5)$ with a first integral. Besides the Schuster (separatrix) and Srivastava solutions, every orbit crossing $ν=0$ is given in closed form by a Weierstrass $\wp$-function, and the closed forms agree with direct numerical integration to about $10^{-12}$. Up to scaling and sign, the Schuster solution is the only solution regular at the center, and it separates positive solutions from solutions with infinitely many zeros. In homology-invariant variables the singular solution $θ=(2ξ)^{-1/2}$ is a center exactly at $n=5$, where its stability changes. For a distance-varying Emden--Fowler system with a shifted equilibrium we prove that at $n=5$ the unique equilibrium is linearly and nonlinearly unstable. Finally, the linearization about the Schuster profile is the Schrödinger operator $L=-Δ_r-5θ_S^4$ on $L^2((0,\infty),ξ^2dξ)$. Its Friedrichs extension has essential spectrum $[0,\infty)$, a threshold resonance at $0$ carried by the non-normalizable scaling mode, and exactly one negative eigenvalue, $λ_0\approx-1.21$, so the Schuster profile is a saddle point of the associated energy functional.

</details>

*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对满足 $n^2J_n\to B>0$ 的一切平移不变铁磁伊辛链（translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain）证明了临界关系式 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$，且不限制有限核或尾部收敛速率。证明的关键是估计宏观界面（macroscopic interface）能量，并在临界乘积大于 1 时排除界面存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长程铁磁伊辛链临界行为的刻画，作者希望在最一般的耦合条件下（仅要求 $n^2J_n\to B>0$）确立磁化强度跳跃（magnetization jump）与临界温度之间的精确关系，并厘清跳跃存在时磁化率（susceptibility）与临界行为的细节。

Method: 证明方法为界面能量估计：对宏观界面（macroscopic interface）的能量给出上界，并论证当临界乘积超过 1 时此类界面不可能存在。该论证对有限核（finite core）与尾部收敛速率（tail convergence rate）均不加限制，并进一步通过正 Stieltjes 类（positive-Stieltjes class）推广到连续时间下的 Ohmic 自旋—玻色子模型（Ohmic spin-boson models）。

Result: 得到 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$。尽管磁化强度存在非零跳跃，连通磁化率（connected susceptibility）在临界点及相邻的有序区间内均为无穷大；在临界耦合处，当外场（field）趋于零时，超出磁化强度（excess magnetization）的衰减比任何正幂次都慢。跳跃关系（jump relation）在连续时间情形下亦成立。

Conclusion: 该工作以无附加限制的普适形式确定了长程伊辛链的临界跳跃关系，并表明跳跃存在时连通磁化率仍发散、超出磁化强度呈极慢衰减；同一跳跃关系可自然推广至连续时间的 Ohmic 自旋—玻色子模型。

Abstract: For every translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, we prove $2β_cBm_c^2=1$, without restrictions on the finite core or the tail convergence rate. The proof bounds the energy of macroscopic interfaces and rules them out if the critical product exceeds one. Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. We also prove the jump relation in continuous time, for a positive-Stieltjes class of Ohmic spin-boson models.

</details>

Main category: math-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为高阶梯度弹性连续体（higher-gradient elastic continua）所服从的丰富非线性色散方程提供一个力学来源清晰、数学基础相对简单且可求解的入门模型。论文以受拉网微结构（pantographic microstructures）为背景，希望通过归一化应变测度与依赖变形的应变梯度模量（deformation-dependent strain-gradient moduli）来刻画此类材料的行波行为。

Method: 从有限应变运动学（finite-strain kinematics）出发，应用虚功率原理（Principle of Virtual Power）确定场方程与接触作用，推导平衡律并建立超弹性材料（hyperelastic materials）的机械能恒等式。针对归一化应变测度构造的能量，对运动方程取行波首次积分（traveling-wave first integral），得到孤立波存在的转折点判据；在适当弹簧能量下，通过应变变量变换将积分化为多项式方程，从而求出显式解；并分析Hencky能量的性质及其对$H^1$范数的强制性。

Result: 得到孤立波需满足的条件：正拉伸、指数衰减与有限机械能，以及在给定应变区间内每个固定波速下的唯一性。对Green–Lagrange与Hencky应变，在相应参数与运动学条件下给出了显式的拉伸与压缩波形（tensile and compressive profiles）。在四次势（quartic potential）正性条件下，Hencky能量非负、以自然状态为唯一零点与全局极小、线性化模量为正、惩罚拉伸梯度，并在材料坍塌与无限伸长时发散；该储能对对数应变的$H^1$范数具有强制性，结合机械能守恒可得到光滑解生存期内拉伸量的一致上界与正下界。

Abstract: We present a self-contained formulation of longitudinal motion in one-dimensional second-gradient continua. Starting from finite-strain kinematics, we then apply the Principle of Virtual Power to identify the governing field equation and contact actions, derive the balance laws, and establish the mechanical-energy identity for hyperelastic materials. Motivated by pantographic microstructures, we study a class of finite-strain energies formulated in terms of normalized strain measures and deformation-dependent strain-gradient moduli. For the associated equation of motion, a traveling-wave first integral yields a turning-point criterion for the existence of solitary waves with positive stretch, exponential decay, and finite mechanical energy, as well as uniqueness at each fixed speed within a prescribed strain interval. For suitable spring energies, a change of strain variable reduces this integral to a polynomial equation, yielding explicit tensile and compressive profiles for Green--Lagrange and Hencky strains under appropriate parameter and kinematic conditions. Under the positivity condition on the quartic potential, the constructed Hencky energy has several physically natural properties: it is nonnegative, admits the natural state as its unique zero and global minimizer, has a positive linearized modulus, penalizes stretch gradients, and diverges under both material collapse and unlimited extension. Furthermore, the stored energy is coercive with respect to the $H^1$ norm of logarithmic strain. Together with mechanical-energy conservation, this yields uniform upper and positive lower bounds on stretch throughout the lifespan of smooth solutions. The theory's accessible mechanical origin, relatively simple mathematical foundation, and diverse solitary waves offer a tractable entry point to studying the rich nonlinear dispersive equations governing higher-gradient elastic continua.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是建立多体玻色星方程在粒子数趋于无穷时的一致时间平均场（mean-field）收敛，并刻画多体及单体动力学中的速度传播性质。

Method: 在单体有效方程解的衰减假设下，比较多体态的单粒子约化密度矩阵（one-particle reduced density matrix）与单体解投影，证明一致于时间的迹范数（trace-norm）估计；并对多体动力学推导最大速度界（Lieb–Robinson 型界）与最小速度界。

Result: 获得一致于时间的迹范数估计，从而建立粒子数趋于无穷时的一致时间平均场收敛；同时得到多体动力学的最大速度界与最小速度界，并通过取平均场极限回收单体玻色星方程的最大与最小速度界。

Conclusion: 结果表明多体玻色星方程在平均场区域具有一致时间收敛性，其速度界可由多体层面传递至单体层面。

Abstract: We study the many-body boson star equation in the mean-field regime. Under a decay assumption on the solution to the effective one-body equation, we prove a uniform-in-time trace-norm estimate comparing the one-particle reduced density matrix of the many-body state with the projection onto the one-body solution. This establishes uniform-in-time mean-field convergence as the particle number goes to infinity. Furthermore, for the many-body dynamics, we establish both a maximal velocity bound, which can be seen as a Lieb--Robinson-type bound, and a minimal velocity bound. As an application, we recover maximal and minimal velocity bounds for the one-body boson star equation by passing to the mean-field limit.

</details>


### [41] [Existence of a third self-similar solution of the Yang--Mills equation in high odd dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36784)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在每一奇数空间维数 d≥11 中，Bizoń–Glogić–Wasserman（BGW）共振多项式的指定区间内除两个显式根外总存在第三个根，从而给出第三个光滑自相似 Yang–Mills 剖面（self-similar Yang–Mills profile）的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGW 将奇数维 d≥11 的光滑自相似 Yang–Mills 剖面分类化归为显式多项式在给定区间内的根计数问题，并猜想除对应两个显式剖面的根之外恰有一个额外根，但仅在部分奇数维中验证；该猜想缺乏一般性证明。

Method: 证明结合两项论证：对共振多项式（resonance polynomial）作端点符号计算（endpoint sign calculation），并以代数论证说明第三个根与两个显式根互不相同。

Result: 在每一奇数维 d≥11 中均证明了额外根的存在，因此第三个光滑自相似 Yang–Mills 剖面在所有这些维数中存在。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答"必须观测多少个大随机系统的样本，才能以可控不确定性推断全局平均"这一问题，并厘清样本量之外被忽视的误差来源。

Method: 以随机对换洗牌（random transposition shuffle）为例，采用共轭贝叶斯估计量（conjugate Bayesian estimator）处理平均位移，借助全方差定律（law of total variance）分解误差，并推广至二维Ising模型临界点与传感器网络相关场。

Result: 基于m张卡片的估计量相对不确定性为1/√(2m)且与牌堆规模n无关；地板项对洗牌为R_n=(2n²+7)/(5(n−1)²(n+1))，后验仍保持校准。二维Ising临界点能量地板为零、磁化地板约0.072（平均场极限为√2−1）；传感器网络中常规95%区间仅7%覆盖集合均值。

Abstract: We ask how much of a large random system must be observed before a global average can be inferred with controlled uncertainty. The law of total variance exposes a second error, the floor $R_n$, which arises because the observed configuration is a single draw from equilibrium: no relative tolerance below $κ\sqrt{R_n}$ can be certified, even by a census. For the shuffle we derive $R_n=(2n^2+7)/\bigl(5(n-1)^2(n+1)\bigr)$ and show that the posterior remains calibrated. At the critical point of the two-dimensional Ising model the floor of the energy vanishes, whereas that of the magnetisation stays near $0.072$; its mean-field limit is $\sqrt{2}-1$. For correlated fields read by sensor networks, the usual 95\% interval covers the ensemble mean in 7\% of cases. Bounding the floor from one realisation, as in single-chain Monte Carlo diagnostics, requires an assumption on the decay of correlations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Here we examine ratchet effects for the Adorjáni model coupled to asymmetric substrates. We find that swarmalator interactions can strongly enhance or suppress active matter ratcheting depending on the structural state of the system, and show that the ratchet efficiency depends on the substrate strength, substrate lattice spacing, and the motor force on the particles. The gel state exhibits the weakest ratcheting for small substrate spacing and can even produce a reversed ratchet motion, but shows the strongest ratcheting for large motor forces. The labyrinth state exhibits the strongest ratcheting over a wide range of parameters since the quasi-one-dimensional labyrinth morphology can match the substrate geometry well.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密多层 Ising 型玻尔兹曼机的自由能计算缺乏可控的解析框架，需要一种能刻画多层几何结构与空腔变量统计结构的构造性方法。

Conclusion: 平衡标量规范（balanced scalar gauge）可通过令 q_ℓ=q_ℓ1 回收；但非凸多层区域内物理值的选取仍是待解决的独立问题。

Abstract: We apply the Constructive Cavity Method (CCM) to the dense, Ising type, restricted and deep Boltzmann machine with Gaussian couplings between adjacent layers. After identifying the core and interface contributions, CCM identifies the blanket decomposition of the cavity variables with the Aizenman--Sims--Starr random overlap structure. We apply the Random Energy Model (REM) universality ansatz to represent the cavity free energies by those of a generalized random energy model (GREM) and evaluate them recursively through Poisson point processes (PPPs). In the layer-wise gauge each layer is cut with its own cumulative fraction $|U_\ell\cap L_p|=λ_p q_\ell^{(p)}N+o(N)$, with $\mathbf 0=\mathbf q_0\le\mathbf q_1\le\cdots\le\mathbf q_r=\mathbf 1$, where $\mathbf q_\ell=(q_\ell^{(1)},\ldots,q_\ell^{(K)})$. Here $U_\ell$ is the cumulative blanket union up to blanket $\ell$. The index $p$ labels architectural layers, whereas $\ell$ labels the blankets. The dependence on the multilayer geometry is carried by the two matrices $M=2M_0Λ$ and $M_1=ΛM_0Λ$. The constructive cavity field in layer $p$ has blanket variances $Q_\ell^{(p)}-Q_{\ell-1}^{(p)}=(M(\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}))_p$, while the correction field has blanket variances $Q_\ell-Q_{\ell-1}=\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell-\mathbf q_{\ell-1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell-1}$. The nested REM--PPP average gives the finite-level constructive cavity functional $\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{RBM/DBM}}^{\mathrm{CCM}}(ζ,\mathbf q)=\log2+\sum_{p=1}^Kλ_pX_0^{(p)}-\frac12\sum_{\ell=0}^{r-1}ζ_\ell(\mathbf q_{\ell+1}^\top M_1\mathbf q_{\ell+1}-\mathbf q_\ell^\top M_1\mathbf q_\ell)$. The balanced scalar gauge is recovered by imposing $\mathbf q_\ell=q_\ell\mathbf 1$. Selecting the physical value in the non-convex multilayer regime is a separate problem.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析 δ-Ising 模型（δ-Ising model）：仅在能量变化不超过可调阈值的条件下接受单自旋翻转，由此将参数空间划分为若干动力学扇区（kinetic sectors），并统一刻画对称性、迟滞（hysteresis）与类 Mpemba 反转（Mpemba-like reversal）等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望用一个简单直观的伊辛动力学规则（Ising dynamics rule），把局部动力学约束（local kinetic constraints）与场响应、记忆（memory）和弛豫（relaxation）联系起来，形成统一框架。

Method: 在动力学伊辛铁磁体（kinetic Ising ferromagnet）中引入能量变化阈值 δ，通过调节外场（field）与阈值开启或关闭局部翻转通道，将参数空间划分为由固定马尔可夫转移算子（Markov transition operator）控制的扇区；并在长度 L 的周期链（periodic chain）上分析无偏随机初态的吸收概率与平均吸收时间（mean absorption time）。

Conclusion: 该模型以单一可调阈值规则将局部动力学约束与场响应、记忆与弛豫现象统一起来，为伊辛型动力学的对称性破缺与反常弛豫提供可解析的框架。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze the $δ$-Ising model, a kinetic Ising ferromagnet in which single-spin flips are accepted when their energy change does not exceed a tunable threshold. Varying the field and threshold opens or closes local flip channels, dividing parameter space into kinetic sectors, each governed by a fixed Markov transition operator. We prove that the dynamics retains global spin-inversion symmetry exactly when up and down spins have equal numbers of open channels. In one such sector on a periodic chain of length $L$, unbiased random initial states therefore reach the field-aligned and antifield states with probability $1/2$ each. Closing a single channel leaves both states absorbing but makes the probability of ordering against the field decay as $(2/π)^L$. Parameter sweeps correspond to ordered products of sector operators, which separate reaching a state from holding it, so hysteresis appears when a state is reached and released at different sector boundaries. We also show how increasing the system size can induce a Mpemba-like reversal, allowing a state with more misaligned spins to reach the final ordered state sooner on average. On a periodic chain, this reversal arises because the mean absorption time grows logarithmically with system size for many short domains but linearly for a single macroscopic domain, and it disappears when the bulk flip channel is opened. These findings show how a simple, intuitive rule for Ising dynamics links local kinetic constraints to field response, memory, and relaxation within a unified framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解势能景观中粒子的涨落与响应是非平衡统计物理（nonequilibrium statistical physics）的基本问题，但负迁移率对这类随机行为的影响尚待厘清，尤其其对力学势稳定性与逃逸、响应机制的重塑作用。

Abstract: Understanding the fluctuations and response of particles in potential landscapes is a fundamental problem in nonequilibrium statistical physics. Here, we investigate how negative mobility reshapes the stochastic behavior of a particle moving in a mechanical potential landscape. We consider a one-dimensional overdamped particle subject to additive noise, with mobility that is negative at weak forces and positive at strong forces. In a potential well, negative mobility destabilizes the mechanical potential minimum and produces bimodal localization at two off-center positions. The same stability reversal transforms conventional Kramers escape from a potential well into a two-stage escape process involving an intermediate metastable state. When an additional spatially uniform force is applied, the mechanical perturbation translates rather than tilts the effective double well, yielding a positive response dominated at weak noise by intrawell relaxation, even though slow interwell switching dominates spontaneous positional fluctuations. These findings reveal how negative mobility reshapes the interplay among mechanical landscapes, stochastic stability, and response.

</details>


### [59] [Residual dynamics determine dissipation in strongly coupled linear Langevin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37030)

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文揭示强耦合会抑制直接耦合自由度对应的不可逆环流（irreversible circulation），在相同二维系统中熵产生率（entropy production rate, EPR）随耦合强度单调下降，而高维情形下残余自由度的动力学决定强耦合极限下的EPR，从而给出判断耦合变量选择是否降低耗散（dissipation）的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用广泛存在于物理与生物非平衡系统（nonequilibrium systems）中，但集体运作究竟提高还是降低熵产生仍难以预测，缺乏可判定的理论判据。

Method: 分析不同维度下耦合系统的概率流（probability current）与不可逆环流结构，考察耦合自由度与残余自由度对EPR的贡献，并推演强耦合极限（strong-coupling limit）下的残余动力学（residual dynamics）。

Result: 相同二维系统中，非零耦合模式下EPR随耦合单调递减，概率流在强耦合极限下消失；高维中抑制耦合自由度的环流通常不能消除全部不可逆环流，残余动力学的EPR决定强耦合极限值，并受有限耦合电流修正（current correction）与残余电流对齐方式影响；该收敛结论亦适用于非相同耦合系统。

Conclusion: 强耦合降低耗散具有明确条件：当耦合变量抑制的环流不被残余自由度补偿时EPR下降；该预测在脊椎动物毛细胞束（hair-cell bundles）与细菌趋化性（bacterial chemotaxis）的非线性模型中得到验证。

Abstract: Interactions shape the properties of many physical and biological nonequilibrium systems, but whether collective operation raises or lowers entropy production remains difficult to predict. Here, we show that strong coupling suppresses irreversible circulation associated with the directly coupled degrees of freedom. In identical two-dimensional systems, this suppression is monotonic for nonzero coupling modes and guarantees a monotonic decrease of the entropy production rate (EPR), with the corresponding probability currents vanishing in the strong-coupling limit. In higher dimensions, suppressing the circulation associated with the coupled degrees of freedom does not generally eliminate all irreversible circulation, since the remaining degrees of freedom can still sustain nonequilibrium currents. The EPR of these residual dynamics sets the strong-coupling limit, providing a criterion for whether a given choice of coupled variables leads to lower dissipation than the uncoupled system. The approach to this limit depends on how the finite-coupling current correction aligns with the residual current. Further, we show that convergence to residual dynamics in the strong-coupling limit also applies to coupled non-identical systems. Finally, we demonstrate the predicted EPR reduction in nonlinear models of vertebrate hair-cell bundles and bacterial chemotaxis.

</details>


### [60] [Langevin dynamics towards Generalized Gibbs Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37129)
*Rafael González-Albaladejo,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Gustavo S. Lozano,Daniel A. Stariolo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出将广义吉布斯系综（Generalized Gibbs Ensemble, GGE）解释为某一非标准力学模型的玻尔兹曼测度（Boltzmann measure），并通过定制朗之万方程（Langevin equations）中的确定性驱动力，使随机轨迹在长时极限下采样该测度；以可积的诺伊曼模型（Neumann Model）为基准，验证了在多种相区中均能重现相关可观测量的渐近值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计物理工具已被用于研究宏观量子与经典可积模型（integrable models）的动力学，并表明热力学极限下局域可观测量的长时间平均等于以GGE计算的统计平均；然而GGE涉及所有守恒量（constants of motion）与拉格朗日乘子（Lagrange multipliers），如何以随机方法直接采样该测度仍是待解决的问题。

Method: 将GGE视为另一非标准力学模型的玻尔兹曼测度，从而可用随机方法（如朗之万过程）采样；通过定制朗之万方程中的确定性驱动力，使随机轨迹在长时间后收敛至目标测度。以受各向异性谐振势（anisotropic harmonic potentials）约束于球面运动的经典粒子所对应的可积诺伊曼模型为基准，在拉格朗日乘子由淬火（quench）后孤立诺伊曼模型渐近动力学所确定GGE固定的情形下进行检验。

Result: 该问题依初始条件与淬火协议（quench protocol）呈现不同相（phases）；在所有情形下，经适当调参的朗之万过程的随机演化均能重现相关可观测量的渐近值。

Conclusion: GGE可被赋予玻尔兹曼测度的力学解释，并可借助调参朗之万动力学以随机采样方式有效实现；诺伊曼模型基准表明该方法在不同相区均具适用性，为可积系统长时间行为的数值研究提供了替代途径。

Abstract: In recent years, statistical physics tools have been used to study the dynamics of macroscopic quantum and classical integrable models. These studies have shown that, in the thermodynamic limit, long term time-averages of local observables equal statistical averages calculated with a Generalized Gibbs Ensemble (GGE). In the latter, the weight in phase space involves all constants of motion multiplied by Lagrange multipliers which are fixed by their initial values. We argue that the GGE can be interpreted as a proper Boltzmann measure of a different (non-standard) mechanical model. This measure can therefore be sampled with stochastic methods implemented, for example, as a Langevin process. We tailor the deterministic force in the Langevin equations so that at long times the stochastic trajectories sample the desired measure. We use the integrable Neumann Model -- the model of a classical particle constrained to move on a sphere under anisotropic harmonic potentials -- as a benchmark. We test the method in a setup where the Lagrange multipliers are fixed by the GGE governing the asymptotic dynamics of the isolated Neumann model following a quench. This problem has different phases depending on the initial conditions and quench protocol and, in all cases, with the stochastic evolution of an adequately tuned Langevin process we reproduce the asymptotic values of relevant observables.

</details>

Abstract: Within the framework of the classical Monte Carlo method with kinematic account of constant bosons concentration a model nickelate was studied, its magnetic structures for various values of exchange integrals considering up to third-nearest neighbours were determined.

</details>


### [62] [Quantum critical scaling in convective active matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37569)
*Jamir Marino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文构建了临界量子活性物质（critical quantum active matter）的最小场论，包含非守恒标量序参量、Landau-Ginzburg 有序化与自平流（self-advection）。在一层重整化群（renormalization-group）分析下，于 \(d=2-\varepsilon\) 得到纯耗散不动点与量子对流鞍点（quantum convective saddle），并指出对流域区别于常规 Burgers 流体力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是缺乏描述临界量子活性物质的场论框架，尤其是自平流与非高斯噪声（non-Gaussian noise）如何影响临界行为尚不清楚。

Method: 构建含非守恒标量序参量的最小场论，结合 Landau-Ginzburg 有序化与自平流项；在扩散噪声主导下纳入高阶量子顶点（quantum vertices）与非高斯噪声涨落，并在 \(d=2-\varepsilon\) 中做一层重整化群分析。

Result: 得到上临界维度 \(d_c=2\)；识别出两类不动点：非线性噪声抑制对流的纯耗散不动点，以及相互作用、对流与非高斯噪声共存的量子对流鞍点；并给出相应临界指数（critical exponents）。

Conclusion: 对流域与常规 Burgers 流体力学不同，可能主导量子活性物质微观模型在向半经典动力学（semi-classical dynamics）交叉前的瞬态普适标度行为。

Abstract: We construct a minimal field theory for critical quantum active matter, describing a nonconserved scalar order parameter with Landau-Ginzburg ordering and self-advection. When diffusive noise dominates, higher-order quantum vertices and non-Gaussian noise fluctuations remain relevant alongside the deterministic nonlinearities, with an upper critical dimension $d_c=2$. A one-loop renormalization-group analysis in $d=2-ε$ reveals a purely dissipative fixed point, where nonlinear noise suppresses convection, and a quantum convective saddle, where interactions, convection, and non-Gaussian noise coexist. We determine the critical exponents, show that the convective regime is distinct from conventional Burgers hydrodynamics, and discuss how it could govern transient universal scaling in microscopic models of quantum active matter before a crossover to semi-classical dynamics.

</details>

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 在反中微子（antineutrino）诱发超子（hyperon）产生中，本文计算了无奇异（non-strange）与奇异（strange）两体流（two-body current）对关联核子对（correlated nucleon pair）的贡献之比，得到两体流与准弹性（quasi-elastic）超子截面比 f = σ_2p2h/σ_QE 随能量由 0.6 GeV 的 0.14 升至 2-5 GeV 的 0.20–0.22，助推器中微子谱平均为 0.15–0.17，与 StrangeMC 原设的 15% 平值接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: StrangeMC 是唯一包含反中微子经两体流产生超子通道的生成器，但其两体流强度被设为未加计算的固定值（准弹性超子截面的 15%），缺乏物理依据。本文旨在确定该比值 f 的能量依赖及两体事件的探测器特征。

Method: 在相同核子对上计算奇异与非奇异两体流，借助 Valencia 非奇异 2p2h 与准弹性碳截面把两者比值换算为 f，假设核子对层面缺失的核效应为两者共有；并用碳的 f 在氩上模拟 SBND 反中微子模式曝光。

Result: f 在 0.6 GeV 为 0.14，2-5 GeV 为 0.20–0.22，助推器谱平均 0.15–0.17；最大不确定源于两体流与一体产生再散射的区分，1 GeV 处 f 在 0.12–0.22 之间，且未计算的一体干涉效应可与两体率同量级。模拟中两体事件约占产生奇异强子事件的 11%，贡献超过四分之一的无 K 介子 Σ+ 事件，在准弹性假设下能量重建偏低 12–30%。

Conclusion: 两体流是反中微子无 K 介子超子产生中不可忽略的贡献来源，StrangeMC 以 f(E) 取代固定 15% 具有合理性；但其强度受分离方法影响明显，且一体干涉效应尚未评估。

Abstract: Antineutrinos produce hyperons without kaons through Cabibbo-suppressed quasi-elastic scattering on a single nucleon. A two-body current acting on a correlated nucleon pair, nubar [NN] -> l+ Y N', gives the same hyperons plus a second nucleon. A companion paper constructs this current without fixing its size. StrangeMC, the only generator with the channel, set it to a flat 15% of the quasi-elastic hyperon cross section. We determine the ratio f = sigma_2p2h/sigma_QE of the two-body to the quasi-elastic hyperon cross section on carbon versus antineutrino energy, and the detector signatures of two-body events. The strange two-body current and its non-strange counterpart are computed on the same nucleon pair. Their ratio is converted into f with the Valencia non-strange 2p2h and quasi-elastic cross sections for carbon, assuming that nuclear effects missing from the pair are common to both. Under this assumption f rises from 0.14 at 0.6 GeV to 0.20-0.22 at 2-5 GeV. Averaged over the Booster antineutrino spectra it is 0.15-0.17, close to the 15% placeholder, which StrangeMC now replaces with f(E). The largest evaluated uncertainty comes from separating the two-body current from one-body production followed by rescattering: across the separation prescriptions explored, f at 1 GeV ranges from 0.12 to 0.22. Interference with one-body production is not calculated and could be as large as the two-body rate. In a simulated SBND antineutrino-mode exposure on argon with the carbon f, two-body events are about 11% of events that produce a strange hadron. They supply more than a quarter of the kaon-less Sigma+ events used to tag hyperon charge exchange, partly as Sigma+ made at the weak vertex. They reconstruct 12-30% low in energy under the quasi-elastic hypothesis. After final-state interactions half of them have a proton above 40 MeV at the vertex, against a fifth of quasi-elastic events.

</details>


### [64] [On-shell reduction of spin-zero Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau covariants and implications for the relativistic impulse approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37263)
*Luis B. Castro*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了五维自旋为零的Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau代数在弹性自旋0–自旋1/2散射中的在壳（on-shell）结构，表明标量、矢量与张量协变量最终仅归约为宇称守恒物理过程所允许的两个独立振幅。结论为区分真正独立的元振幅与光学嵌入（optical embedding）导致的表示依赖效应提供了系统依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介子-核光学势（meson-nucleus optical-potential）计算中使用了四种不变形式的耦合选择，但哪些差异属于物理的、哪些仅为表示依赖，缺乏系统判据。作者希望厘清Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau（DKP）代数各定域洛伦兹协变量（local Lorentz covariants）在壳上的真实独立自由度。

Method: 将五维自旋为零DKP代数中的标量、矢量、张量结构显式约化到物理宇称守恒过程所允许的两个独立振幅，并逐项考察其在壳行为：两个标量结构成比例；非最小矢量（nonminimal vector）$i[P,\beta^\mu]$为纵向且在守恒费米子矢量流收缩后消失；投影矢量$P\beta^\mu$与$\beta^\mu P$各约化至最小矢量贡献的一半；张量协变量不引入新的在壳方向。

Result: 所有协变量在壳上均未超出两个独立振幅所张成的空间；不同核方程只可能在壳等价代表被提升为光学算符（optical operators），并迭代超出基本冲量近似（impulse approximation）所固定的阶数之后才出现。

Conclusion: 在壳分析表明真正的独立元振幅数目受限，表示层面的多样性本身不构成物理差异；该结果为区分本质独立的元振幅与光学嵌入生成的表示依赖效应提供了系统基础。

Abstract: We analyze the on-shell content of the local Lorentz covariants of the five-dimensional spin-zero Duffin-Kemmer-Petiau algebra in elastic spin-zero-spin-$1/2$ scattering. The scalar, vector, and tensor structures are reduced explicitly to the two independent amplitudes allowed by the physical parity-conserving process. The two scalar structures are proportional on shell, the genuine nonminimal vector $i[P,β^μ]$ is longitudinal and vanishes after contraction with the conserved fermion vector current, and the projected vectors $Pβ^μ$ and $β^μP$ each reduce to one half of the minimal-vector contribution. The tensor covariants introduce no additional on-shell directions. We discuss the consequences for the four invariant choices used in meson-nucleus optical-potential calculations and show that different nuclear equations can arise only after on-shell-equivalent representatives are promoted to optical operators and iterated beyond the order at which the elementary impulse approximation is fixed. These results provide a systematic basis for distinguishing genuinely independent elementary amplitudes from representation-dependent effects generated by the optical embedding.

</details>


### [65] [Side jump and anomalous Lorentz transformation of a coherent Dirac wave packet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36416)
*Jin-Rui Wu,Shuai Y. F. Liu*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该工作提出“迹方法（trace method）”，导出任意有限洛伦兹推动（Lorentz boost）下三维狄拉克波包（Dirac wave packet）概率中心移动的精确且紧凑的变换核，揭示由自旋-轨道耦合引发的侧跃（side jump）及其纹理与质量依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）使自旋粒子的平均位置在洛伦兹推动下出现反常移动，即侧跃（side jump），而既有描述多依赖半经典展开（semi-classical expansion），难以完整保留波包信息。

Method: 发展一种迹方法（trace method），用于推导一般三维狄拉克波包在任意有限洛伦兹推动下概率中心的精确变换核（transformation kernel），无需半经典展开即可纳入有限动量宽度、自旋-动量关联（spin-momentum correlations）与相干相位结构。

Result: 所得表达式包含有限动量宽度等物理效应；发现在初始平均动量、自旋与轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）固定时仍存在依赖纹理的侧跃，且固定动量宽度的高斯波包（Gaussian packet）表现出非单调的质量依赖。显式核进一步区分了有质量与无质量费米子的自旋及轨道角动量贡献。

Conclusion: 精确变换核为保留波包信息的协变粒子自旋输运（covariant spin transport）提供了运动学基础。

Abstract: Spin-orbit coupling makes the mean position of a spinning particle transform anomalously under a Lorentz boost, with an additional shift known as a side jump. In this work, we develop a trace method to derive an exact but compact transformation kernel for the probability center of a general three-dimensional Dirac wave packet under an arbitrary finite Lorentz boost. The expression includes the physics of finite momentum width, spin-momentum correlations, and coherent phase structure without a semi-classical expansion. We identify texture-dependent side jumps even at fixed initial mean momentum, spin, and orbital angular momentum, as well as a non-monotonic mass dependence for Gaussian packets at fixed momentum width. The explicit kernels further resolve the spin and orbital angular-momentum contributions for massive and massless fermions, providing a kinematic basis for covariant particle-based spin transport that retains wave-packet information.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the covariant Wigner function for the spin-$3/2$ Rarita-Schwinger field and its relation to the spin density matrix and the mean spin polarization vector. The resulting formula can be used to predict the spin polarization of spin-$3/2$ hadrons, such as the $Ω^-$, in heavy-ion collisions.
