### [67] [Agentic Swarms Discover Enhanchment of Fractional Chern Insulator Gaps Beyond Ideal Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35934)
*Yitong Zhang,Changwen Xu,Andrew Boshi Li,Siddhartha Sarkar,Kai Sun,Venkatasubramanian Viswanathan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: MOSAIC（多智能体协作群体，基于 Claude Opus 5.5）自主探索分数量子拓扑序（fractional topological order）能隙鲁棒性问题，发现偏离理想量子几何（quantum geometry）可显著增强能隙，并识别出实空间量子度规（real-space quantum metric）为关键调控量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨 AI 能否超越求解良定义问题，参与基础物理中的开放式发现：自主提出问题、识别意外现象并揭示机制；具体切入点是长期存在的瓶颈——限制分数量子拓扑序鲁棒性及高温任意子（anyon）研究的小能隙问题。

Method: 不预设模型，先由前沿模型（GPT-6 Astra）提出理论框架，再由 MOSAIC 这一 AI 智能体协作群体（swarm of AI agents）以多样化策略与快速思想交流进行自主探索、跨智能体结果整合；并结合 AI 辅助理论分析构建简化模型与解析能隙增强因子。

Result: 发现与“理想量子几何有利”这一常规认识相反：偏离理想极限可使多体能隙（many-body gap）提升一个数量级以上。无集中协调的智能体自主探索并整合发现，表现优于使用相同工具的孤立智能体。理论分析给出单带近似（single-band approximation）下无界的解析能隙增强因子，并识别实空间（而非动量空间）量子度规为控制多体能隙的关键量，给出可由单粒子波函数计算、无需多体精确对角化（ED）的指标。

Conclusion: 研究表明协作式 AI 智能体可将探索、意外物理发现与理论理解整合于一体，为基础物理中的 AI 驱动研究开辟新可能。

Abstract: Can artificial intelligence (AI) move beyond solving well-defined problems to contribute to open-ended discovery in fundamental physics? Could AI propose what to explore, identify unexpected phenomena, and uncover their mechanisms? We address this using MOSAIC (Multi-agent Open-ended Scientific Analysis and Investigation through Collaboration), a collaborative swarm of AI agents (Claude Opus 5.5), to tackle a longstanding bottleneck: the small energy gaps limiting the robustness of fractional topological order and the study of anyons at higher temperatures. Rather than prescribing a model, we ask frontier models (GPT-6 Astra) to propose a theoretical framework, which MOSAIC explores through diverse strategies and rapid exchange of ideas. This reveals a striking finding: although ideal quantum geometry is considered favorable for fractional states, departing from this limit can dramatically enhance their robustness, with many-body gaps more than an order of magnitude larger than in the ideal case. Without centralized coordination, the agents explored autonomously, combined findings across agents, and formed insights from individual runs, outperforming isolated agents with the same tools. AI-assisted theoretical analysis further develops simplified models with an analytical gap enhancement factor that is unbounded within the single-band approximation. Remarkably, AI identifies the real-space quantum metric, rather than the momentum-space one, as the key quantity governing the many-body gap, revealing how flat-band wavefunctions shape two-electron interactions and yielding an index computable from single-particle wavefunctions without many-body ED to guide gap optimization. Our study demonstrates how collaborative AI agents can integrate exploration, discovery of unexpected physics, and theoretical understanding, opening new possibilities for AI-driven research in fundamental physics.

</details>


### [68] [Planckian dissipation per unit critical entropy as a coupling-free probe of the strange-metal bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35939)
*Dino Škrgić,Dejan Stojkovic*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 若电子被局域临界浴（local critical bath）散射，则电阻率线性项与比热对数项的系数之比 Q 与耦合无关，成为可测量的判据；该比值可将临界浴分为共形浴（conformal bath，Q≤4.91）、Yukawa–SYK 场（9–15）与 Kondo 晶格（远小）等类别，LSCO 的 Q≈9 指向 Yukawa–SYK 场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异金属（strange metal）同时表现出电阻率随温度线性变化与 C/T 随降温对数上升这两个特征，但二者的系数关系此前缺乏统一、可测量的判据。

Method: 假设电子被局域临界浴散射，解析地考察电阻率线性系数与比热对数系数之比 Q，指出耦合在比值中抵消；并以 SYK 模型、Yukawa–SYK 场与 Kondo 晶格作为不同类型临界浴进行对照。

Result: Q 与耦合无关，仅由两个可测量量决定：浴的弛豫速率的缓慢程度（可由光电子能谱或光学测量观测），以及临界熵中参与弛豫电流的份额。不同浴类别给出分层取值：共形浴 Q≤4.91，Yukawa–SYK 场为 9–15，Kondo 晶格则远小于此。

Conclusion: LSCO 的 Q≈9 超出共形界（conformal bound），表明其临界浴更符合 Yukawa–SYK 场而非共形浴；Q 可作为区分奇异金属中临界浴类型的实验判据。

Abstract: Strange metals pair a resistivity linear in temperature with a specific heat $C/T$ rising logarithmically on cooling. We find that, if electrons scatter off a local critical bath, the coupling cancels in the ratio $Q$ of their coefficients, leaving two measurable numbers: how slowly the bath relaxes, seen in photoemission or optics, and the share of critical entropy that relaxes current. Baths form classes: $Q\le4.91$ for a conformal bath, as SYK models confirm; 9--15 for the Yukawa--SYK field; and far less for Kondo lattices. LSCO, at $Q\approx9$, exceeds the conformal bound, pointing to the Yukawa--SYK field.

</details>


### [69] [AlgebrasTQD: Computing Algebras and Interfaces in Twisted Quantum Doubles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36029)
*Alison Warman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发布开源代码 AlgebrasTQD，可基于 GAP 计算扭量量子双重（twisted quantum double）$D^\omega(G)$ 的可凝聚代数（condensable algebra）及其拓扑边界与界面，用于分类、界面映射及基于非阿贝尔表面码的容错逻辑门构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可凝聚代数可用于分类经规范化（gauging）与带反常（anomaly）$\omega$ 的有限群 $G$ 关联的 1+1 维有隙/无能隙相、研究 2+1 维任意子增殖（anyon proliferation），并在非阿贝尔拓扑量子码中构造逻辑门；此前缺少系统化计算工具。

Method: 以 GAP 实现的 AlgebrasTQD 枚举所有可凝聚代数 $A(H,F,\gamma,\varepsilon)$ 及其代数结构与任意子，按同构类排序并识别"孪生代数（twin algebras）"；计算子代数关系与哈斯图（Hasse diagram）、约化拓扑序及 $3$-余循环、跨接口任意子映射，并构造非 Clifford 逻辑门。

Result: 代码给出了所实现公式的总结，并以 $\mathbb{Z}_2^3$（含 $\omega_\text{III}$）、$D_8$、有无反常的 $A_4$、孪生代数与孪生相、二面体群量子双重中的 Clifford 层级门等算例演示其功能；所有运算均为精确算术。

Conclusion: AlgebrasTQD 是该任务的首个开源实现，适用于任意有限群 $G$ 与 3-余循环 $\omega$，仅受计算时间与规模限制，代码已公开于 GitHub。

Abstract: We present AlgebrasTQD, an open-source code written in GAP (Groups, Algorithms, and Programming), for computing condensable algebras and the corresponding topological boundaries and interfaces of twisted quantum doubles $D^ω(G)$. These algebras can be used to classify 1+1d gapped and gapless phases whose symmetry is related by gauging to a finite group $G$ with anomaly $ω\in H^3(G,U(1))$, to study 2+1d anyon proliferation and to construct logical gates in non-Abelian topological quantum codes. The functions in AlgebrasTQD compute all condensable algebras $A(H,F,γ,ε)$ with their algebra structure and anyons, sort them into isomorphism classes and identify the twin algebras that are inequivalent but share the same anyon decomposition. AlgebrasTQD also determines all the subalgebra relations, the corresponding Hasse diagram and the types of 1+1d phases for any chosen, possibly non-invertible, group-theoretical symmetry. For non-maximal algebras, the code computes the reduced topological order with its $3$-cocycle and the map of anyons across the topological interface. Finally, AlgebrasTQD includes functions that construct certain non-Clifford logical gates via non-Abelian surface codes. We summarize the formulas that are implemented in the AlgebrasTQD code, and we illustrate its use with Abelian and non-Abelian groups including $\mathbb{Z}_2^3$ with $ω_\text{III}$, $D_8$, $A_4$ with and without anomaly, twin algebras and twin phases, and the Clifford-hierarchy gates in quantum doubles of dihedral groups. All arithmetic is exact and AlgebrasTQD can be used with any finite group $G$ and 3-cocycle $ω$, limited only by the time and size of the calculation. The code is available at https://github.com/AlisonWarman/AlgebrasTQD

</details>


### [70] [Mesons, Confinement, and Complexity: A Krylov Space Perspective on the Long-Range Ising Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36054)
*Xuhao Jiang,Jad C. Halimeh,N. S. Srivatsa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文以长程横场Ising链为平台，研究禁闭（confinement）与Krylov复杂度（Krylov complexity）的关系，发现复杂度可充当探测相互作用诱导禁闭的谱学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作已在禁闭场论中识别出Krylov复杂度的若干特征，但缺乏微观格点模型中的对应验证。本文旨在无显式对称破缺场条件下，借助幂律相互作用产生的畴壁间有效禁闭势，考察禁闭与复杂度的关联。

Method: 采用长程横场Ising链（相互作用∼1/r^α），以淬火（quench）后时间平均的Krylov态复杂度为诊断量；禁闭区内将复杂度振荡频率与有效双扭结（two-kink）模型的介子质量比对；并用Wightman内积研究有限温度下的算符Krylov复杂度。

Result: 在小α与小横场h_x（禁闭最强）时复杂度被强烈压低且对系统尺寸依赖微弱，大α时转为快速增长的尺寸依赖行为。禁闭区内振荡频率与有效双扭结模型介子质量一致：主导频率随最轻介子质量线性增长，时间平均复杂度约按其平方反比下降。低温算符复杂度呈有界振荡并直接编码介子谱，升温后该结构逐渐被抹平。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度可作为相互作用诱导禁闭的光谱探针，并在微观格点模型中重现了禁闭场论中已识别的Krylov复杂度标志性特征。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between confinement and Krylov complexity in the long-range transverse-field Ising chain, where power-law interactions $\sim 1/r^α$ generate an effective confining potential between domain walls without an explicit symmetry-breaking field. Using the time-averaged Krylov state complexity after a quench as a diagnostic, we find that the complexity is strongly suppressed and only weakly system size-dependent at small $α$ and small transverse field $h_x$, where confinement is strongest, and crosses over to rapid growth at larger $α$, where it increases with system size. Within the confined regime, the oscillation frequencies of the complexity agree with the meson masses of an effective two-kink model, with the dominant frequency growing linearly with the lightest meson mass and the time-averaged complexity decreasing approximately as its inverse square. We further study operator Krylov complexity at finite temperature using the Wightman inner product. At low temperature, it exhibits bounded oscillations that directly encode the meson spectrum, whereas this structure is progressively washed out as the temperature increases. These results identify Krylov complexity as a spectroscopic probe of interaction-induced confinement and reproduce, in a microscopic lattice model, the hallmarks of Krylov complexity identified in confining field theories.

</details>


### [71] [Coulomb- and exchange-driven redistribution of spectral correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36115)
*Zanya Gonzalez Tellez,Miguel Angel Ocaña Bribiesca*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用第一性原理密度泛函理论结合随机矩阵谱统计，研究γ-Ce中库仑相互作用U与交换相互作用J对Ce-4f能谱集体结构的独立影响，发现二者响应性质不同且无法用单一的U-J有效参数完全描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子关联不仅改变单个电子态能量，还重组能谱的集体结构；以往常以单一有效参数U-J概括相互作用效应，缺乏对库仑项与交换项各自贡献的区分。

Method: 在静态自旋极化DFT+U+SOC框架下计算γ-Ce，并借助随机矩阵谱统计（random-matrix spectral statistics）分析最近邻间距、约化间距比（reduced spacing ratios）、能带速度与能带曲率，并分辨j=5/2与j=7/2分量。

Result: 增大U并不产生Poisson型与Wigner-Dyson型统计之间的单调转变，最强能级排斥区域随相互作用增强而在4f流形中迁移；固定U时，交换作用导致强排斥区与强聚集区共存，且总自旋矩与4f占据几乎不变，该聚集在分辨j分量后仍存在，并在相同U-J数值下差异显著。

Conclusion: 在本文DFT+U+SOC描述下，谱响应分别依赖库仑与交换相互作用，不能由单一有效U-J参数完全刻画；能量与轨道特征分辨的谱统计可揭示常规积分观测量所掩盖的关联驱动电子重组。

Abstract: Electronic correlations reorganize not only the energies of individual electronic states but also the collective structure of the spectrum. Here, first-principles density functional theory calculations combined with random-matrix spectral statistics are used to examine this reorganization in gamma-Ce within a static spin-polarized DFT+U+SOC description. We distinguish the effects of the Coulomb interaction U from those of the exchange interaction J by analyzing nearest-neighbor spacings, reduced spacing ratios, band velocities, and band curvatures across the Ce-4f manifold. Increasing U does not produce a monotonic transition between Poisson- and Wigner-Dyson-like statistics; instead, the region of strongest level repulsion migrates across the 4f manifold as the interaction increases. Exchange produces a qualitatively different response. At fixed U, strongly repulsive and strongly clustered spectral regions coexist within the same 4f manifold even while the total spin moment and 4f occupation remain nearly unchanged. The clustering persists after resolving the spectrum into its dominant j = 5/2 and j = 7/2 components and differs strongly between calculations having the same numerical value of U-J. Within the present DFT+U+SOC description, the spectral response therefore depends on the separate Coulomb and exchange interactions and cannot be represented completely by a single effective U-J parameter. These results show that energy- and character-resolved spectral statistics expose correlation-driven electronic reorganization that is hidden by conventional integrated observables.

</details>


### [72] [Three-dimensional quantum Hall insulators in magnetic pyrochlore systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36199)
*Ze-Xun Lin,Robert-Jan Slager,Claudio Castelnovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出，磁性烧绿石材料可作为实现三维量子霍尔绝缘体（3D Quantum Hall insulator）的新平台，其机制源于磁织构（magnetic texture）与平带干涉（flat-band interference）的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子霍尔物理（3D quantum Hall physics）的实现平台相对有限，本文旨在探索在磁性烧绿石（pyrochlore）体系中通过磁织构调控实现该效应的可能性。

Method: 研究烧绿石晶格上的巡游电子（itinerant electrons）与另一套互穿烧绿石晶格上的局域磁矩通过近藤耦合（Kondo coupling）相互作用，磁矩形成场选的三进一出（three-in/one-out）非共面织构；该织构产生复的自旋相关跃迁振幅与不等的局域交换能，将紧致局域母平带重构为被全局三维带隙分隔的窄带。

Result: 低能态主要局域于三维晶格内的 kagome 面，反映量子干涉与织构诱导子晶格势的共同作用；最低带全占据时，响应由陈数三元组（Chern triplet）C=(1,1,1) 与倒易霍尔矢量 G_H=(2π/a)(1,1,1) 刻画，给出 σ_yz=σ_zx=σ_xy=e²/(ha)，并伴随手性表面谱流（chiral surface spectral flow），表现为表面非收缩费米环（noncontractible Fermi loops）。

Conclusion: 研究为在磁性烧绿石体系中寻找三维量子霍尔物理开辟新方向，并提出基于磁织构与平带干涉相互作用的普适设计原则。

Abstract: We show that a class of pyrochlore materials provides a route to realize 3D Quantum Hall insulators, based on magnetic texture and flat band interference. We study itinerant electrons on a pyrochlore lattice Kondo-coupled to localized moments living on another interpenetrating pyrochlore lattice, with the moments forming a field-selected three-in/one-out texture. The noncoplanar texture generates complex spin-dependent hopping amplitudes and unequal local exchange energies, reconstructing the compact-localized parent flat bands into narrow bands separated by a global three-dimensional gap. The low-energy states are concentrated predominantly on kagome planes within the three-dimensional lattice, reflecting the combined effects of quantum interference and the texture-induced sublattice potential. When the lowest band is fully occupied, the response is characterized by a Chern triplet $\mathbf{C}=(1,1,1)$ and reciprocal Hall vector $\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{H}}=(2π/a)(1,1,1)$, giving $σ_{yz}=σ_{zx}=σ_{xy}=e^2/(ha)$. The quantized bulk Hall response enforces chiral surface spectral flow, visible as noncontractible Fermi loops at the surface. Our work opens a new direction in the search for three-dimensional quantum Hall physics in magnetic pyrochlore systems, and suggests a broad design principle based on the interplay of magnetic texture and flat-band interference.

</details>


### [73] [A cascade of orbital-field-driven phases with a Lifshitz-type transition to unidirectional spiral order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36214)
*Hiroki Nakai,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究由键依赖轨道场（bond-dependent orbital field）引入的新型阻挫（frustration），在蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）反铁磁海森堡模型（antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model）中产生级联相变与Lifshitz型转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁系统中的阻挫（frustration）可导致非传统序与奇异自旋液体，本文旨在探索源于内禀键依赖轨道场、将轨道自由度与自旋关联耦合的一类新阻挫。

Method: 在蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）反铁磁海森堡模型（antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model）中引入轨道场相互作用（orbital-field interaction），并分析局部自旋-轨道约束（local spin-orbital constraints）及序中无序（order-by-disorder）机制。

Result: 局部自旋-轨道约束产生亚扩展简并（subextensive degeneracy），经序中无序（order-by-disorder）选择出不同的共线磁体（collinear magnets），随轨道场增大形成级联相；更大场下出现自发破缺手征对称性（chiral symmetry）的不可公度单向螺旋（incommensurate unidirectional spiral）。

Conclusion: 该转变由Goldstone模与有隙动力学自旋-轨道涨落（gapped dynamical spin-orbital fluctuations）的耦合驱动，自旋刚度（spin stiffness）沿特定方向变为负，螺旋波矢（spiral wavevector）随距转变点距离的平方根增长，呈Lifshitz型转变（Lifshitz-type transition）；文中还提出轨道场的微观实现路径并讨论候选材料。

Abstract: Frustration in magnetic systems gives rise to unconventional orders and exotic spin liquids. Here, we explore a new type of frustration arising from an intrinsic bond-dependent orbital-field that couples orbital degrees of freedom to spin correlations. Introducing this orbital-field interaction into the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the honeycomb lattice, we show that local spin-orbital constraints generate a subextensive degeneracy, lifted by order-by-disorder to select distinct collinear magnets in a cascade of phases as the orbital field increases from a para-orbital liquid. At larger fields, the selected phase gives way to an incommensurate unidirectional spiral that spontaneously breaks chiral symmetry. This transition is driven by the coupling of a Goldstone mode to gapped dynamical spin-orbital fluctuations. The spin stiffness becomes negative along a particular direction, while the spiral wavevector grows as the square root of the distance from the transition, signaling a Lifshitz-type transition. We present a microscopic route to realizing this orbital field and discuss candidate materials.

</details>


### [74] [Towards Quantum Computation of Bulk FeO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36268)
*Daniel Staros,Federico Petrovich,Ammar Kirmani,Daniel N. Blaschke,M. Cerezo,Jian-Xin Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作以分子 FeO 与 Fe₂O₂ 的模型哈密顿量为对象，比较经典精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、变分量子本征求解器（Variational Quantum Eigensolver, VQE）与基于采样的量子对角化（Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization, SQD）三种方法求解基态性质，发现 SQD 配合双连接壳层可在极少量子资源下恢复精确基态能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基材料（iron-based materials）对地质学与天体物理研究具有重要意义，但其电子结构（electronic structure）用经典计算机极难计算；而高原子序数（high-Z）原子或分子在近中期量子硬件上的完整电子结构处理仍具挑战。

Method: 采用经典精确对角化、VQE 与 SQD 三种手段，求解 FeO 与 Fe₂O₂ 分子模型哈密顿量（model Hamiltonians）的基态，并比较各方法在能量精度与量子资源消耗上的表现。

Result: 经典方法与量子算法均重现了 FeO 实验观测的五重态（quintet）基态，Fe₂O₂ 则得到九重态（nonet）基态；原始 VQE 在两种分子上均未达到化学精度（chemical accuracy），而采用双连接壳层（two connectivity shells）的 SQD 以极少的量子计算资源恢复了二者的精确基态能量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，由第一性原理（ab initio）信息构建的关联材料低能模型（low-energy models）与 SQD 相结合，为在现有量子硬件上扩展强关联固体（strongly correlated solids）基态性质计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum computation offers a promising path towards efficient calculations of the electronic structure of correlated materials. The properties of iron-based materials, which are key for advancing geological and astrophysical understanding, are notoriously difficult to calculate with classical computers. However, despite the many recent advances in quantum computing on near intermediate scale quantum hardware, a full treatment of the electronic structure of high-$Z$ atoms or molecules remains challenging. Here, we investigate the ground-state properties of molecular FeO and Fe$_2$O$_2$ model Hamiltonians using classical exact diagonalization, Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE), and Sample-based Quantum Diagonalization (SQD). The experimental quintet ground state of FeO is recovered classically and with quantum algorithms, while a nonet ground state is recovered for Fe$_2$O$_2$. Although raw VQE does not achieve chemical accuracy for the ground state of FeO or Fe$_2$O$_2$, SQD with two connectivity shells recovers the exact ground energy for both molecules utilizing remarkably few quantum computational resources. Overall, our results indicate that $ab \; initio$-informed low-energy models of correlated materials, combined with SQD, offer a new alternative for scaling up calculations of ground-state properties of strongly correlated solids on existing quantum hardware.

</details>


### [75] [Measurement-induced entanglement phases in hyperbolic gauge-Higgs models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36528)
*Cole Kelson-Packer,Hiroki Sukeno,Akimasa Miyake*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究双曲晶格（hyperbolic lattice）上规范-希格斯模型（gauge-Higgs model）在纯测量动力学（measurement-only dynamics）下的纠缠相（entanglement phases），发现欧氏空间中不存在的“群岛”相（archipelago phase），并借助三维资源态（3D resource state）将时空纠缠映射为经典渗流理论（classical percolation theory）以定位临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平直时空的规范-希格斯理论是研究多相物质及其不同纠缠结构的典型模型，而近期对双曲空间（hyperbolic space）及其广延边界（extensive boundaries）如何影响此类纠缠结构的兴趣日益增长；非平衡相之间的纠缠转变可由非对易测量（non-commutating measurements）的竞争实现。

Method: 在双曲晶格上构建纯测量动力学的规范-希格斯模型，利用拓扑纠缠熵（topological entanglement entropy）并引入针对双曲希格斯相（hyperbolic Higgs phases）的广义测量诱导纠缠（generalized measurement-induced entanglement）来绘制二维相图；核心工具是受基于测量的量子计算（measurement-based quantum computation）启发的三维资源态，用以模拟二维量子态的时空演化。

Result: 借助对偶性（duality）发现欧氏空间中不存在的相：其体能晶格承载彼此分离的对称保护拓扑序（symmetry-protected topological order）团簇；该“群岛”相与相邻拓扑相、平庸相之间的转变具有属于平均场普适类（mean-field universality class）的临界指数 δ。时空纠缠“海绵”（sponges）可映射到经典渗流理论，从而推断相图边界上的临界点数值。

Conclusion: 双曲几何与非对易测量的结合可产生平直（欧氏）空间中缺失的新奇纠缠相，三维资源态方法为分析时空纠缠结构及确定相边界临界点提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Gauge-Higgs theory in flat spacetime provides a prime theoretical model of multiple phases of matter with distinct entanglement structures. More recently, there has been interest in hyperbolic space and the influence its extensive boundaries have on these structures. Entanglement transitions between non-equilibrium phases, such as topological phases, can be realized by the competition of non-commutating measurements. Here we study the entanglement phases of gauge-Higgs models on hyperbolic lattices described by measurement-only dynamics. We chart 2D phase diagrams by employing topological entanglement entropy and introducing a generalized measurement-induced entanglement tailored for hyperbolic Higgs phases. Utilizing duality, we find a phase absent in Euclidean space, wherein the bulk lattice hosts separated clusters of symmetry-protected topological order. Transitions between this "archipelago" phase and neighboring topological and trivial phases feature a critical exponent $δ$ belonging to a mean-field universality class. Our essential tool is a 3D resource state inspired by measurement-based quantum computation that simulates the spacetime evolution of the 2D quantum state with a third direction of entanglement. This allows us to analyze spacetime "sponges" of entanglement, which map to classical percolation theory and allow us to infer values of critical points on our phase diagrams' boundaries.

</details>


### [76] [Continuum neural wavefunctions for anyons on a torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36567)
*Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: AnyFormer 是一个基于变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）的神经网络变分框架，用于研究环面上连续空间中的任意子（anyons），并以无外场、无相互作用的半离子（semions）验证了该框架的准确性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑有序相（topologically ordered phases）中的可动任意子在有限密度下的集体行为，特别是分数量子交换统计（fractional exchange statistics）如何塑造关联，尚缺乏微观研究工具；环面上的连续任意子还需处理矩阵值边界条件（matrix-valued torus boundary conditions）与整体缠绕约束（global winding constraints）。

Method: 构建光滑的轨道参考系（orbital frame）以满足环面的矩阵值边界条件，并引入解析配对因子（analytic pair factor）提供规则的碰撞行为；轨道几何与多体振幅通过变分蒙特卡洛并行优化。将该框架应用于无外场与无相互作用势的半离子体系，以分离仅由统计产生的关联。

Result: 框架与精确两半离子能量高度吻合；在 N=4 与 N=6 时给出低于有限截断精确对角化（exact diagonalization）的变分能量；扩展至 N=16 时观察到显著的关联空穴（correlation holes）、长波密度涨落受抑，以及成对环流（pair circulation）在约等于平均粒子间距处发生反转；N=12 与 16 时环流出现阻尼空间振荡。

Conclusion: AnyFormer 为体相任意子物质（bulk anyonic matter）的关联、手性运动（chiral motion）与集体相提供可扩展的神经网络变分研究框架。

Abstract: Mobile anyons in topologically ordered phases provide a setting to investigate how fractional exchange statistics shapes collective behavior at finite density. We introduce AnyFormer: a neural variational framework for continuum anyons on a torus that explicitly incorporates statistical interactions and global winding constraints. We construct a smooth orbital frame that enforces the matrix-valued torus boundary conditions, and supplement it with an analytic pair factor that supplies the regular collision behavior. The orbital geometry and many-body amplitudes are optimized in parallel by variational Monte Carlo. We apply AnyFormer to semions without an external field or interaction potential, isolating unique correlations generated solely by statistics. Our framework closely matches exact two-semion energies and yields lower variational energies than finite-cutoff exact diagonalization for $N = 4$ and $6$ semions. Scaling up to $N = 16$ semions, well beyond the limits of continuum diagonalization, our calculations reveal states with pronounced correlation holes, suppressed long-wavelength density fluctuations, and a reversal of pair circulation at a distance set by the mean particle spacing. For $N = 12$ and $16$ semions, the circulation develops damped spatial oscillations. AnyFormer thus provides a scalable variational neural-net framework for microscopic studies of correlations, chiral motion, and collective phases of bulk anyonic matter.

</details>


### [77] [Ground-State Properties of a Lattice-Coupled Strongly Correlated Model for Solid Oxygen under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36697)
*Naomichi Sato,Makoto Naka,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文从含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的双轨道 Peierls–Hubbard 模型导出有效自旋哈密顿量（effective spin Hamiltonian），指出其中存在易面单离子各向异性（easy-plane single-ion anisotropy）与易轴交换各向异性（easy-axis exchange anisotropy）的竞争；量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）计算表明四聚体畸变（tetramer distortion）在所考察参数范围内最为有利，且两种各向异性竞争会增强晶格畸变倾向与四聚化（tetramerization）偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态氧（solid oxygen）是自旋 S=1 的分子磁体（molecular magnet），自旋与晶格自由度（spin and lattice degrees of freedom）耦合强烈，近期研究提示压力下磁矩与四聚体畸变共存，但其微观机制与畸变模式选择尚需理论澄清。

Method: 由含自旋轨道耦合的双轨道 Peierls–Hubbard 模型（two-orbital Peierls–Hubbard model）推导有效自旋哈密顿量；在方晶格（square lattice）上采用量子蒙特卡洛模拟（quantum Monte Carlo simulations），计算多种晶格畸变模式（含四聚体模式）下的磁对晶格刚度（lattice stiffness）的贡献。

Result: 在所研究的整个参数范围内，四聚体模式（tetramer pattern）在所有考察的畸变模式中最为有利；两种各向异性的竞争增强了晶格畸变倾向，并强化了四聚化相对于其他模式的偏好。

Conclusion: 磁各向异性竞争可作为驱动固态氧中四聚体畸变的关键机制，为压力下磁性与晶格畸变共存的实验现象提供微观解释框架。

Abstract: Solid oxygen is an $S=1$ molecular magnet with strong
  coupling between spin and lattice degrees of freedom.
  Recent studies suggest that magnetic moments
  coexist with tetramer distortions under pressure.
  We derive an effective spin Hamiltonian from a two-orbital
  Peierls--Hubbard model with spin-orbit coupling.
  The resulting model contains competing easy-plane
  single-ion and easy-axis exchange anisotropies.
  We analyze this model on a square lattice using quantum Monte Carlo simulations
  and evaluate the magnetic contribution to the lattice stiffness
  for several lattice distortion patterns including the tetramer pattern.
  We find that the tetramer pattern is the most favorable
  among the considered distortion patterns throughout the parameter range studied.
  Moreover, competition between the two anisotropies
  enhances the tendency toward lattice distortion
  and strengthens the preference for tetramerization over the other patterns.

</details>


### [78] [Magnon Super-Bloch Oscillations and Switchable Transport in Driven Magnonic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36721)
*Xiang Liu,Xi-guang Wang,Zhi-xiong Li,Xiufeng Han,Guang-hua Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文预测在由静态与交流磁各向异性场梯度驱动的周期磁振子晶体（magnonic crystals）中可实现磁振子波包的超布洛赫振荡（super-Bloch oscillations, SBOs），并可将同一结构在常规布洛赫振荡、SBO与定向输运三种模式间切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBOs 是控制驱动晶格中波包传播的有力途径，但其在磁振子系统（magnonic systems）中的实现此前仍属空白，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 采用解析推导结合微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations），研究静态与近共振 MHz 量级交流磁各向异性场梯度驱动下的磁振子波包动力学，并推广至反铁磁磁振子晶体（antiferromagnet magnonic crystals）。

Result: 单一静态梯度产生恒定载波频率的磁振子布洛赫振荡；叠加近共振交流驱动后出现慢速大振幅包络振荡，即 SBO 特征。在精确共振时，交流驱动实现相位可控的定向输运，载波频率偏移可达 GHz 量级。振幅、超周期（superperiod）、漂移速度与频率演化的解析表达式经微磁学模拟验证；在反铁磁体系中，两类相反极化磁振子模式在 THz 频段呈现相反运动。

Conclusion: 磁振子 SBOs 可作为一个多功能平台（versatile platform），为可重构磁振子器件（reconfigurable magnonic devices）开辟新途径。

Abstract: Super-Bloch oscillations (SBOs) offer a powerful route to control wave packet propagation in driven lattices, yet their realization in magnonic systems has remained elusive. Here, we predict SBOs of magnon wave packets in periodic magnonic crystals driven by static and ac gradients of magnetic anisotropy field. A static gradient alone induces magnon Bloch oscillation (BO) with a constant carrier frequency. Remarkably, adding a near-resonant MHz-scale ac driving gives rise to a slow large-amplitude envelope oscillation, the hallmark of SBO. At exact resonance, the ac driving enables phase-controlled directional transport with a carrier-frequency shift up to GHz scale. Consequently, the same structure can be switched among three distinct regimes: conventional BO, SBO, and directed transport. Analytical expressions for the oscillation amplitudes, superperiod, drift velocity, and frequency evolution are validated by micromagnetic simulations. Extending to antiferromagnet magnonic crystals, we further demonstrate that these effects drive opposite motions of two oppositely polarized magnon modes in THz range. Our work establishes magnon SBOs as a versatile platform, opening a new avenue for reconfigurable magnonic devices.

</details>


### [79] [Stripe antiferromagnetic order and spin dynamics in the Shastry-Sutherland Ising compound Er2Be2GeO7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36799)
*Donger Cheng,Otkur Omar,Wenbin Wei,Jan Peter Embs,Xiao Wang,Cheng Su,Hui Cheng,Zhengdong Fu,Xinzhi Liu,Wentao Jin,Ze Hu,Wei Luo,Liming Wang,Jiazheng Hao,Feiran Shen,Lunhua He,Shang Gao,Erxi Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合宏观测量与中子散射，在亚开尔文温区揭示稀土Shastry-Sutherland化合物Er2Be2GeO7中倾斜Er3+矩形成的k=(0,0,0)条纹状反铁磁序，并给出其Ising临界行为与分数磁化平台的经典解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土化合物中几何阻挫（geometric frustration）与晶体电场（crystal electric field）诱导的奇异单离子各向异性（single-ion anisotropy）之间的相互作用常导致新奇磁相，故需系统研究这类化合物的磁性质。

Method: 采用宏观物性测量与飞行时间中子粉末衍射、热中子及冷中子非弹性散射，温度低至亚开尔文；并用非等价最近邻耦合的平均场随机相近似（mean-field random-phase approximation）与经典蒙特卡洛模拟（classical Monte Carlo）分析激发与磁化行为。

Result: 观察到k=(0,0,0)的条纹状反铁磁序（伴随Er3+矩局域倾斜），T_N=0.84 K处有λ形比热异常；有序矩对应的磁峰强度呈二维Ising普适类（two-dimensional Ising universality class）行为；强Ising各向异性源自局域晶体电场，有序态中存在弱色散的有能隙磁激发；经典蒙特卡洛给出1/4与1/2分数磁化平台。

Conclusion: 该工作全面刻画了Er2Be2GeO7中的自旋关联（spin correlations），并特别强调其分数磁化平台（fractional magnetization plateaus）的经典起源。

Abstract: In rare-earth compounds, the interplay between geometric frustration and unusual single-ion anisotropy caused by the crystal electric field often leads to exotic magnetic phases. Here, we investigate the magnetic properties of the rare-earth Shastry-Sutherland compound Er$_2$Be$_2$GeO$_7$ using bulk measurements and neutron scattering down to sub-Kelvin temperatures. A $k=(0,0,0)$ stripe-like antiferromagnetic order with locally tilted Er$^{3+}$ moments is revealed by time-of-flight neutron powder diffraction, accompanied with a $λ$-shape specific-heat anomaly at $T_N=0.84$ K. The temperature dependence of the magnetic peak intensity corresponding to the ordered moment of Er$^{3+}$ approximates to the two-dimensional Ising universality class. Thermal- and cold-neutron inelastic scattering experiments demonstrate that the strong Ising anisotropy originates from the local crystal electric field, and reveal gapped magnetic excitations with weak dispersion in the ordered state. Mean-field random-phase approximation with inequivalent nearest-neighbor couplings accounts well for the collective excitations, while classical Monte Carlo simulations yield the fractional $1/4$ and $1/2$ magnetization plateaus. Our work provides a comprehensive description of the spin correlations in Er$_2$Be$_2$GeO$_7$ with a particular focus on the classical origin of its fractional magnetization plateaus.

</details>


### [80] [Spin-polarized tunneling from a Kondo insulator: equilibrium surface magnetism versus magneto-electric response in SmB$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36886)
*Maxim Dzero,Brian M. Andersen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了 SmB₆ 针尖与双共线反铁磁体 Fe₁₊ₓTe 之间的自旋极化隧穿，指出隧道磁对比需针尖表面时间反演对称性破缺，并给出平衡磁矩与场诱导磁矩之比的解析关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在 SmB₆ 针尖与 Fe₁₊ₓTe 之间观测到磁对比，但其来源（平衡长程有序磁矩与表面磁电效应诱导磁矩）难以区分，需从理论上厘清两者对隧穿电导的贡献及可测量关系。

Method: 基于自旋极化隧穿理论建模，将磁对比电导分解为平衡表面长程有序磁矩项与结电场经表面磁电效应（magneto-electric effect）诱导的磁矩项，并解析推导二者的比值关系及其对偏压与针尖-样品间距的依赖。

Result: 平衡磁矩与零偏压附近响应之比在符号上等于磁对比变号偏压的两倍，且与两种机制相对强度无关；在 |eV|≲10 meV 实验窗口内，场诱导贡献至多为平衡贡献的百分之几，仅在约 100 meV 及以上偏压时才可比拟；场诱导与平衡贡献之比与针尖-衬底距离成反比。

Conclusion: 该比值本身不能用于度量磁电效应强度；在常规低偏压实验条件下，隧穿磁对比主要由平衡长程有序磁矩主导，磁电效应贡献可忽略，为实验数据解读提供了定量判据。

Abstract: We theoretically analyze the spin-polarized tunneling between SmB$_6$ tip and the bicollinear antiferromagnet Fe$_{1+x}$Te. The emergence of magnetic contrast in tunneling requires time-reversal symmetry breaking on the surface of the tip. The magnetic contrast part of the tunneling conductance then has two contributions: the first one originates from the magnetic moment due to the equilibrium long-range order on the surface, while the other is governed by the magnetic moment induced by the electric field of the junction through the surface magneto-electric effect. We show that the ratio of the equilibrium moment to the response extracted near zero bias equals, up to the sign, twice the bias at which the contrast changes sign, independently of the relative strength of the two mechanisms, so that this ratio itself does not measure the strength of the magneto-electric effect. We find that within the experimental energy window $|eV|\lesssim10$~meV the {bias-dependent} field-induced contribution is at most a few percent of the equilibrium one, and the two become comparable only at biases of order $100$~meV or higher. The ratio of the field-induced to the equilibrium contribution is inversely proportional to the distance between the tip and the substrate.

</details>


### [81] [Coulomb Crystallization in Free Electron Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37352)
*Valerio Di Giulio,Rudolf Haindl,Armin Feist,Claus Ropers*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 无外部束缚的自由传播电子脉冲中可出现瞬态库仑结晶（transient crystallization），其由前后端电子自致纵向势（self-induced longitudinal potential）对内部电子的暂态约束所驱动；该现象可用几何与库仑能/纵向热能之比等宏观束流参量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典库仑晶体（classical Coulomb crystals）与量子维格纳晶体（quantum Wigner crystals）通常依赖空间受限环境中的排斥相互作用实现同号粒子有序化，本文旨在探究无外部束缚的自由传播电子脉冲是否也能产生类似的瞬态有序化。

Method: 利用透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscope）测量最多含九个电子的光发射脉冲中粒子数分辨的能量关联（number-resolved energy correlations），并结合多体模拟（many-body simulations）与一维统计力学模型（one-dimensional statistical-mechanical model）进行解释。

Result: 小粒子数下观测到强关联，且随电子数增加在脉冲内部逐渐消失；模型表明脉冲前端与后端电子形成自致纵向势，瞬态约束内部电子并促成一维有序链，据此构建的结晶相图（phase diagram）以束流几何与平均库仑相互作用能对纵向热能之比为控制参量。

Conclusion: 理论上进一步表明，通过静电力或光有质动力（optical ponderomotive forces）施加弱横向导引可在更大粒子数下维持结晶，并预测连续电子束中出现自发纵向周期聚束（periodic bunching）；结果为在脉冲与连续两种体制下利用随机库仑相互作用构建自结构化、强关联电子束指明了条件。

Abstract: Classical Coulomb crystals and quantum Wigner crystals typically arise when repulsive interactions order like-charged particles in spatially confined environments. Here, we show that transient crystallization can also emerge in freely propagating electron pulses without external trapping. Experimentally, we use a transmission electron microscope to measure number-resolved energy correlations in photoemitted pulses containing up to nine electrons, revealing strong correlations at small particle numbers that progressively disappear in the pulse interior as the number increases. To explain these observations, we combine many-body simulations with a one-dimensional statistical-mechanical model, showing that the leading and trailing electrons generate a self-induced longitudinal potential that transiently confines the inner particles, enabling the formation of an ordered chain during propagation. The resulting crystallization phase diagram reveals how many-body ordering dynamics can be understood in terms of evolving macroscopic beam properties, namely its geometry and the ratio of the mean Coulomb interaction energy to the longitudinal thermal energy. Extending this framework, we theoretically demonstrate that weak transverse guiding through electrostatic or optical ponderomotive forces can sustain crystallization at larger particle numbers and predict spontaneous longitudinal periodic bunching in continuous electron beams. Our results identify conditions under which stochastic Coulomb interactions can be harnessed to generate self-structured, strongly correlated electron beams in both pulsed and continuous regimes.

</details>


### [82] [New Developments in Diagrammatic Monte Carlo for Interacting Lattice Fermions: Efficient Algorithms and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37505)
*Boyuan Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文针对相互作用格点费米子（interacting lattice fermions）发展图形化（diagrammatic）计算方法，用于热力学量与子系统量（如第二Renyi熵）的参考计算，并给出面向量子模拟器（quantum simulators）的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟器与多体近似方法需要高精度参考值来校验，而相互作用费米子体系的热力学与子系统量计算困难，现有图形展开方法在组合求和效率与规范展开方面存在局限。

Method: 围绕自洽Hartree鞍点构造逆味（inverse-flavour）展开，枚举非高斯图形，并用离散Lehmann表示（discrete Lehmann representation）解析积分部分内部顶点；将组合求和推广至味依赖传播子与展开平移，提出CoS++（部分收缩获得连通和）与行列式多项式筛（determinant-polynomial sieve）；对第二Renyi熵推导费米子复制路径积分（replica path integral）及其双时Green函数的稳定求值。

Result: 给出70×70格点、六味Hubbard模型在T/t=0.15下31个状态点的密度结果；在九阶上行列式多项式筛比指定CoS基线快24–25倍（三味稠密随机矩阵基准）；第二Renyi熵在3×3团簇达十阶、更大格点达九阶，50×50格点(U/t,μ/t,βt)=(3,1,8)、α=1.3时九阶延拓给出S_2/N_s=0.06695，Monte Carlo不确定度3.0×10^-5，两种平移的端点差为2.6×10^-4。

Conclusion: 图形化展开结合高效组合求和与稳定复制路径积分，可对相互作用费米子的热力学与子系统量提供可控精度的参考计算；不同展开平移的端点一致性表明延拓误差受控。

Abstract: This thesis develops diagrammatic methods for thermodynamic and subsystem quantities of interacting lattice fermions, providing reference calculations for quantum simulators and many-body approximations. I formulate an inverse-flavour expansion about a self-consistent Hartree saddle, enumerate non-Gaussian diagrams, and use a discrete Lehmann representation to integrate selected internal vertices analytically. Density results cover 31 state points of the six-flavour Hubbard model on a $70\times70$ lattice at $T/t=0.15$.
  I generalize combinatorial summation to flavour-dependent propagators and expansion shifts. CoS++ combines partial contractions to obtain connected sums for interaction subsets. A determinant-polynomial sieve evaluates the same sums through local coefficient filters; at ninth order it is 24--25 times faster than the specified CoS baselines in three-flavour dense-random-matrix benchmarks. Thermodynamic applications include a 2048-site honeycomb lattice and a three-flavour square lattice with 1024 sites.
  For the second Renyi entropy, I derive a fermionic replica path integral and a stable evaluation of its two-time Green functions. The connected-determinant calculation reaches tenth order on a $3\times3$ cluster and ninth order on larger lattices. On $50\times50$ sites at $(U/t,μ/t,βt)=(3,1,8)$, the ninth-order continuation at shift $α=1.3$ gives $S_2/N_s=0.06695$ with propagated Monte Carlo uncertainty $3.0\times10^{-5}$. Comparing two expansion shifts at their respective retained orders gives an endpoint difference of $2.6\times10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [83] [Topological Criticality with Charge-\(3e\) Fluctuations in the \(\mathrm{SU}(3)\) Triangular-Lattice Hofstadter--Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37575)
*Yang Liu,Ya-Hui Zhang,Xue-Yang Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在SU(3)三角晶格Hofstadter–Hubbard模型中，整数量子霍尔绝缘体与手性自旋液体之间的转变可在单电子能隙不闭合的情况下变为临界，中性激发与电荷3e激发的关联长度同时显著增长，支持Chern–Simons–Higgs临界理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数量子霍尔绝缘体（integer quantum Hall insulator）与手性自旋液体（chiral spin liquid）之间的转变若能不闭合单电子能隙而发生，则拓扑序（topological order）的改变须由某种复合激发（composite excitation）主导，其具体性质尚不明确。论文旨在识别支配该拓扑相变的复合激发。

Method: 采用无限密度矩阵重整化群（infinite density matrix renormalization group）研究SU(3)三角晶格Hofstadter–Hubbard模型，参数为每格点一个粒子、每个三角磁通量π/3。

Result: 计算识别出弱耦合的整数量子霍尔相与相互作用驱动的SU(3)₁手性自旋液体。在转变附近，中性（neutral）与电荷3e关联长度（correlation length）均显著增长，而单电子关联长度保持很短，为Chern–Simons–Higgs临界理论提供证据；该理论中规范不变的单极子（monopole）携带费米型SU(3)单态三子（trion）的量子数。

Conclusion: 结果支持以带三子量子数的单极子为核心的Chern–Simons–Higgs临界描述，并讨论了该图像对系统掺杂（doping）以及近期tMoTe₂中掺杂ν=−2/3分数量子反常霍尔（fractional quantum anomalous Hall）实验的启示。

Abstract: A transition between an integer quantum Hall insulator and a chiral spin liquid can become critical without closing the single-electron gap, raising the question of which composite excitations govern the change in topological order. We investigate this question in the \(\mathrm{SU}(3)\) triangular-lattice Hofstadter--Hubbard model at one particle per site and magnetic flux \(π/3\) per triangle. Using infinite density matrix renormalization group calculations, we identify a weak-coupling integer quantum Hall phase and an interaction-driven \(\mathrm{SU}(3)_1\) chiral spin liquid. Near the transition, both the neutral and charge-\(3e\) correlation lengths increase substantially, whereas the single-electron correlation length remains short, providing evidence for a Chern--Simons--Higgs critical theory, where a gauge-invariant monopole carries the quantum numbers of a fermionic \(\mathrm{SU}(3)\)-singlet trion. We discuss the implications for doping the system and recent experiments on doping \(ν=-2/3\) fractional quantum anomalous Hall in tMoTe\(_2\).

</details>

### [84] [Parametrized Jacobian Problem over $\mathbb{C}^n$ and its Complete Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35918)
*Yisong Yang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 通过极分解在多项式映射的线性部分引入独立尺度参数，构造参数化雅可比问题；证明参数空间上雅可比行列式一致为常数可将雅可比方程解耦为扰动矩阵所有主子式为零的方程组，从而强制（至变量置换意义下）下三角化，给出多项式可逆的充要条件与逆映射的显式构造方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是在 $\mathbb{C}^{n}$ 上处理多项式映射的可逆性问题（即雅可比猜想相关框架），希望通过引入参数化手段获得更易处理的判据。摘要仅表明参数化处理可使雅可比方程解耦，未展开更多背景动因。

Method: 方法核心为：利用极分解（polar decomposition）将独立尺度参数（scale parameters）嵌入多项式映射的线性部分，构建参数化雅可比问题（parametrized Jacobian Problem）；再要求雅可比行列式（Jacobian determinant）在整个参数空间上一致保持常数，由此将雅可比方程（Jacobian equation）解耦为扰动矩阵（perturbation matrix）全部主子式（principal minors）为零的方程组。

Result: 主要结果为：该主子式方程组严格迫使映射在变量置换（variable permutations）意义下下三角化（lower triangularization），从而建立多项式可逆性（polynomial invertibility）的一个充要条件；同时给出在任意维数下构造逆映射（inverse map）的显式、典则方案（canonical scheme）。

Conclusion: 结论是参数化雅可比框架可为多项式可逆性提供等价刻画，并附带构造性算法意义：逆映射可由该典则方案显式给出，适用于任意维数 $n$。

Abstract: We formulate a parametrized Jacobian Problem over $\mathbb{C}^{n}$ by incorporating independent scale parameters into the linear part of a polynomial map via polar decomposition. We prove that requiring the Jacobian determinant to remain constant uniformly across the parameter space decouples the Jacobian equation into a system enforcing the vanishing of all principal minors of the perturbation matrix. This system strictly forces lower triangularization up to variable permutations, establishing a necessary and sufficient condition for polynomial invertibility and providing an explicit, canonical scheme for constructing the inverse map in every dimension.

</details>


### [85] [Bethe Cycles, Frobenius Identities, and Quantum/Classical Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36975)
*Peter Koroteev*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究完全旗流形余切丛所关联的三角 Ruijsenaars–Schneider 系统（trigonometric Ruijsenaars–Schneider system，tRS）上的 Frobenius 作用，说明 Tarasov–Varchenko 单值本征向量与 Bethe 方程—tRS 能级集之间的量子/经典对偶相契合，并证明在单位根处联合本征值问题与 Frobenius 作用相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作延续 Etingof–Varchenko 所启动、并由作者与 Smirnov 在量子 K-理论（quantum K-theory）中发展的研究纲领，旨在厘清 Frobenius 作用与 tRS 系统本征结构之间的关系。

Method: 在完全旗流形（complete flag varieties）余切丛对应的 tRS 系统上考察 Frobenius 作用；将 Tarasov–Varchenko 近期构造的单值本征向量（monodromy eigenvectors）纳入 Bethe 方程与 tRS 能级集（level sets）之间的量子/经典对偶框架；并在单位根（root of unity）处分析 tRS 哈密顿量的 Newton–Adams 组合。

Result: 在单位根处，tRS 哈密顿量的 Newton–Adams 组合中所有部分平移（partial shifts）相互抵消，恰余下由 Frobenius 作用所得的算子；由此证明联合本征值问题与 Frobenius 作用在任意秩（every rank）与任意根阶（every root order）下均相容。

Conclusion: Frobenius 作用与 tRS 系统联合本征值问题的相容性在一般秩与一般根阶下成立，为量子/经典对偶及量子 K-理论框架提供了支持。

Abstract: We study Frobenius action on the trigonometric Ruijsenaars--Schneider system associated with cotangent bundles to complete flag varieties. The construction continues the program initiated by Etingof and Varchenko and developed in quantum $K$-theory by the author and Smirnov. We explain how the monodromy eigenvectors recently constructed by Tarasov and Varchenko fit the quantum/classical duality between Bethe equations and tRS level sets. At a root of unity, all partial shifts cancel in the Newton--Adams combinations of the tRS Hamiltonians, leaving precisely the operators obtained by Frobenius action. This proves compatibility of the joint eigenvalue problem with Frobenius action in every rank and at every root order.

</details>

### [86] [Emergent Fermi Surfaces in Periodically Driven Bose Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36038)
*Li-kun Shi,Oles Matsyshyn,Inti Sodemann Villadiego*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文指出周期驱动且耦合热浴的玻色气体在稳态下可涌现费米面（Fermi surface），无论高温态还是玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）态；其源于非平衡碰撞缺乏细致平衡（detailed balance）导致的占据函数非解析性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在玻色系统中实现费米面（Fermi surface）长期是追求的难题；本文提出以非平衡条件作为实现该目标的新途径。

Method: 研究周期驱动（periodically driven）玻色气体与热浴（thermal bath）耦合的稳态，分析动量依赖占据函数的非解析性，并讨论其物理后果与超冷原子（ultra-cold atomic）实验探测策略。

Result: 涌现费米面在 BEC 态强于正常态，且对有限温度抹平（finite temperature smearing）比常规平衡费米气费米面更稳健；可产生类似弗里德尔振荡（Friedel oscillations）与磁场下的量子振荡（quantum oscillations），但存在显著定量差异。

Conclusion: 非平衡条件可作为在玻色系统中涌现费米面的可行途径，并给出在超冷原子云中实验发现这些奇异态（exotic states）的策略。

Abstract: We show that the steady state of a periodically driven Bose gas coupled to a thermal bath features emergent Fermi surfaces both in its high temperature and Bose-Einstein-condensate (BEC) states. These emergent Fermi surfaces are non-analyticities in the occupation function as a function of momentum arising from the lack of detailed balance of collisions away from equilibrium. They are stronger in the BEC than in the normal gas, and can be more robust against finite temperature smearing than the usual equilibrium Fermi surfaces of Fermi gases. We also show that they give rise to analogous rich phenomena, such as Friedel oscillations in the density correlations and quantum oscillations in response to applied magnetic fields controlled by their areas, albeit with marked quantitative differences. Our results establish non-equilibrium conditions as a promising alternative pathway to achieve the long-sought-after emergence of Fermi surfaces in bosonic system. We outline strategies to experimentally discover these exotic states in ultra-cold atomic clouds.

</details>


### [87] [Ensemble-dependent condensate statistics and correlations in an ideal Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36118)
*Szymon Augustynowicz,Jan Okoński,Maciej B. Kruk,Ilja Zebergs,Jan Joachim Arlt,Kazimierz Rzążewski,Krzysztof Pawłowski*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文提出分段围道方法（segmented-contour approach），在正则与微正则系综中计入非高斯修正，揭示理想玻色气体中系综选择对凝聚体涨落与关联函数的可测量影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计系综（statistical ensemble）的选择对玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensates）的统计描述有可测量的影响，且这种影响超越已知的巨正则反常（grand canonical anomaly），需要超越高斯近似的精细方法来定量刻画系综差异。

Method: 针对理想玻色气体（ideal Bose gas）发展分段围道方法（segmented-contour approach），通过纳入非高斯修正来改进鞍点近似（saddle-point approximation），适用于广泛的一类无相互作用能谱。

Result: 该方法可高效获取凝聚体涨落、高阶矩与关联函数；对固定各向异性的三维谐振子势阱，微正则与正则凝聚体数方差极大值之比随粒子数增加仅极缓慢地趋近理论渐近值 1−3ζ(3)²/[4ζ(2)ζ(4)]≈0.391；系综效应在凝聚交叉附近的二阶关联函数 g⁽²⁾(x) 中可见，并在弹道释放（ballistic release）后的时间演化中得以保留。

Conclusion: 系综选择在凝聚体统计中留下可观测印记，分段围道方法为无相互作用体系的系综分辨统计提供有效工具；渐近比的缓慢收敛与 g⁽²⁾(x) 的系综依赖可为实验区分系综效应提供依据。

Abstract: The choice of statistical ensemble has a measurable impact on the statistical description of Bose-Einstein condensates beyond the well-known grand canonical anomaly. We develop a segmented-contour approach for the ideal Bose gas, which refines the saddle-point approximation by including non-Gaussian corrections in the canonical and microcanonical ensembles. The method applies to a broad class of noninteracting spectra, and provides efficient access to condensate fluctuations, higher moments, and correlation functions.
  For a three-dimensional harmonic trap with fixed anisotropy, we find that the ratio of the maxima of the microcanonical and canonical condensate-number variances approaches the theoretically predicted asymptotic value $1-3ζ(3)^2/[4ζ(2)ζ(4)]\approx 0.391$ only extremely slowly with increasing particle number. We also show that ensemble effects are visible in the second-order correlation function $g^{(2)}(x)$ near the condensation crossover. After ballistic release from a three-dimensional harmonic trap, the time evolution of $g^{(2)}(x)$ preserves the differences between statistical ensembles.

</details>


### [88] [Kinetic Slush in Dissipative Rydberg Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36661)
*Hufeng Wen,Ya-Xin Xiang,Qun-Li Lei,Thomas Pohl,Zhengyang Bai,Yu-Qiang Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文利用动力学蒙特卡洛（kinetic Monte Carlo）模拟研究了二维耗散里德堡原子阵列（dissipative Rydberg atom array）中由受助激发产生的“动力学雪泥态（kinetic slush state）”的非平衡演化，揭示无序与受挫竞争诱导的动力学交叉及分形关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索受挫与受助动力学（facilitated dynamics）竞争如何催生复杂非平衡软物质现象，并检验驱动-耗散里德堡系统可否作为可控平台。

Method: 在强退相位（strong dephasing）区采用动力学蒙特卡洛模拟；将激光失谐（laser detuning）调到补偿最近邻相互作用以实现受助动力学，而残余的次近邻相互作用导致动力学受挫（kinetic frustration）。

Result: 该竞争产生具有显著动力学异质性（dynamical heterogeneity）的雪泥相；实时演化显示从各向同性扩展到一维受限生长的动力学交叉，且可由受挫强度连续调控，并伴随分形团簇几何与广义Ornstein-Zernike空间关联；有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling）支持系统接近临界性。

Conclusion: 研究确立驱动-耗散里德堡系统为工程化复杂非平衡软物质现象的高度可控平台。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium dynamics of a kinetic slush state realized via facilitated excitations in a two-dimensional dissipative Rydberg atom array. Utilizing kinetic Monte Carlo simulations in the strong dephasing regime, we show that tuning the laser detuning to compensate for nearest-neighbor interactions enables facilitated dynamics, while residual next-nearest-neighbor interactions induce kinetic frustration. This competition gives rise to an emergent slush phase characterized by pronounced dynamical heterogeneity and the coexistence of liquid- and solid-like configurations. By probing its nonequilibrium real-time evolution, we uncover a dynamical crossover from isotropic spreading to one-dimensional constrained growth, which can be continuously tuned by the frustration strength. Within this crossover regime, the slush dynamics generate fractal cluster geometries and generalized Ornstein-Zernike spatial correlations. Finite-size scaling analysis supports the system's proximity to criticality. Our findings establish driven-dissipative Rydberg systems as a highly controllable platform for engineering complex nonequilibrium soft-matter phenomena.

</details>


### [89] [Intercomponent vortex interaction and dynamics in a uniform Bose quantum liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36710)
*Rabeea Kiran,Hiroki Saito,Hsiang-Hua Jen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究二元均匀量子液体中不同分量涡旋的相互作用与动力学，基于含对数超越平均场修正的耦合扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程导出远距涡旋渐近相互作用势，发现其系数依赖背景密度并可导致吸引–排斥转变及涡旋对轨道旋转方向反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元均匀量子液体中不同分量涡旋之间的相互作用形式及其随体系参数（如背景密度）的变化尚待澄清，尤其是量子涨落修正对涡旋间作用性质的影响。

Method: 采用含对数超越平均场修正（logarithmic beyond-mean-field corrections）的耦合扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程（coupled extended Gross-Pitaevskii equations），推导充分分离涡旋间的渐近相互作用势（asymptotic interaction potential），并进行实时模拟（real-time simulations）。

Result: 相互作用势仍保持随涡旋间距s的s^{-2}渐近形式，但其系数通过量子涨落依赖背景密度；由此涡旋间相互作用在临界密度处由吸引转为排斥，实时模拟显示涡旋对轨道运动由顺时针转为逆时针，与其Magnus响应一致。

Conclusion: 量子涨落修正使二元量子液体中异分量涡旋的相互作用系数成为密度的函数，从而提供通过背景密度调控涡旋间吸引/排斥性质与涡旋对轨道运动方向的机制。

Abstract: We investigate the interaction and dynamics of vortices belonging to different components of a binary uniform quantum liquid. Using the coupled extended Gross-Pitaevskii equations with logarithmic beyond-mean-field corrections, we derive the asymptotic interaction potential between well-separated vortices. The interaction potential retains the asymptotic form $s^{-2}$ as a function of the intervortex distance s, but its coefficient depends on the background density through the quantum fluctuation. As a result, the interaction between vortices changes from attractive to repulsive at a critical density. Real-time simulations demonstrate a corresponding reversal of orbital motion of a vortex pair from clockwise to counterclockwise, consistent with their Magnus response.

</details>


### [90] [Impurity-induced trimer formation across the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36974)
*M. C. Gordillo,J. Boronat*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 利用扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）计算研究了单个杂质嵌入两个平衡费米浴（Fermi baths）中、跨越BCS-BEC渡越（BCS-BEC crossover）的行为；当吸引足够强时形成由杂质与每个浴中各一个原子构成的三聚体（trimer），该团簇在BCS侧为Borromean态并在BEC侧连续演化为tango型三聚体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单个杂质在双费米浴环境中的多体团簇形成机制，特别是弱到中等吸引下（不支撑两体束缚态）是否能形成三体团簇，以及其在不同渡越区域的性质差异。

Method: 采用扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo）数值方法；杂质通过不同强度的Pöschl-Teller势与两个浴发生吸引相互作用，且该相互作用不支撑杂质-浴两体束缚态（two-body bound state）；通过分析总能量扣除一个三聚体结合能与剩余浴-浴二聚体（dimer）能量，并考察粒子间距离（interparticle distances）。

Result: 在吸引足够强时观测到三聚体的形成：BCS侧为Borromean三聚体（无任何两体子系统束缚），并连续演化为BEC侧的tango型三聚体；扣除三聚体与二聚体能量后恢复无杂质体系的状态方程（equation of state），支持稀薄团簇解释；粒子间距分析表明嵌入的三聚体是真正的三体团簇，而非被杂质修饰的二聚体。

Conclusion: 杂质在双费米浴中可形成真实的三体团簇，其性质随BCS-BEC渡越连续变化；稀薄团簇图像可自洽解释能量结构，且该团簇并非简单二聚体加杂质的复合体。

Abstract: By means of diffusion Monte Carlo calculations, we study the behavior of a single impurity embedded in two balanced Fermi baths across the BCS-BEC crossover. The impurity interacts attractively with both baths through Pöschl-Teller potentials of different strengths. That interaction is such that does not support an impurity--bath two-body bound state. When that attraction is sufficiently strong, we observe the formation of trimers composed of the impurity and one atom from each bath. These clusters are Borromean on the BCS side, where no two-body subsystem is bound, and evolve continuously into tango-like trimers on the BEC side. Subtracting from the total energy the binding energy of one trimer and that of the remaining bath-bath dimers restores the equation of state of the impurity-free system, supporting a dilute-cluster interpretation. The analysis of interparticle distances shows that the embedded trimer is not simply a dimer dressed by the impurity, but a genuine three-body cluster formed inside the two Fermi baths.

</details>


### [91] [Mediated Interactions between Fermi Polarons in the Strongly Interacting Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37654)
*Renato Pessoa,S. A. Vitiello,Luis A. Peña Ardila*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 零温量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）计算表明，费米极化子（Fermi polaron）之间的朗道相互作用（Landau interaction）在整个极化子分支上始终为排斥，与实验观察到的近共振下降趋势相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准粒子间的媒介相互作用（mediated interactions）是朗道费米液体理论（Landau Fermi-liquid theory）的核心，但其在强关联区域的行为尚不清楚；本文受近期实验（Nat. Phys. 20, 68–73 (2024)）启发而展开研究。

Method: 采用零温量子蒙特卡洛方法（zero-temperature quantum Monte Carlo methods），研究由费米海（Fermi sea）中涌现的费米极化子之间的媒介相互作用，并计算静态杂质（static impurities）之间的实空间相互作用。

Result: 极化子间的朗道相互作用在任意耦合强度下均为排斥；在短程普适区域（short-range universal regime）结果对相互作用势的微观细节不敏感；实空间杂质相互作用在弱耦合下重现类 RKKY 行为，在强耦合下重现非微扰的 Born-Oppenheimer 结果。

Conclusion: 结果为基态诱导相互作用（ground-state induced interaction）的符号提供了稳健证据，并给出微观测度基准（microscopic benchmark），说明朗道准粒子相互作用如何超越强关联量子物质的微扰区域（perturbative regime）而存续。

Abstract: Mediated interactions between quasiparticles lie at the heart of Landau Fermi-liquid theory, yet their behavior in the strongly correlated regime is not fully understood. Motivated by recent experiments [Nat. Phys. 20, 68--73 (2024)], we investigate mediated interactions between quasiparticles emerging from a Fermi sea---Fermi polarons---using zero-temperature quantum Monte Carlo methods. Our main result is that the Landau interaction between polarons remains repulsive in the strongly interacting regime for any coupling strength in the polaronic branch, in contrast to the downward trend observed experimentally near resonance. Deep in the short-range universal regime, our results are insensitive to the microscopic details of the interaction potential, providing robust evidence for the sign of the ground-state induced interaction. In addition, we determine the real-space interaction between static impurities, recovering a Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY)-like behavior at weak coupling and the nonperturbative Born-Oppenheimer result at strong coupling. More broadly, our results establish a microscopic benchmark for how Landau quasiparticle interactions survive beyond the perturbative regime of strongly correlated quantum matter.

</details>

### [92] [Growing-time trace fluctuations and self-averaging form factors of Grover walks on random lifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36320)
*Nino Nagata,Ryoichi Suzuki*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了固定有限连通的 d-正则图（d≥3）的随机 N-重覆盖（random N-lift）上 Grover 游走（Grover walk）迹统计量的泛函中心极限定理（functional central limit theorem），所有观测量取自同一随机重覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注随机重覆盖上量子游走（quantum walk）观测量随时间涨落的普适性刻画，旨在给出迹统计量平方中心化后平均值收敛与部分和收敛到布朗运动（Brownian motion）的定量条件，并厘清二分与非二分基图（bipartite base）的差异。

Method: 证明综合了四项工具：增长维数的泊松逼近（growing-dimensional Poisson approximation）、一致的双线性 Chebyshev 能量估计（uniform bilinear Chebyshev-energy estimate）、时间衰减的泊松—高斯耦合（time-decaying Poisson–Gaussian coupling），以及高斯二次阵列定理（Gaussian quadratic-array theorem）。另有独立矩估计给出期望中心化版本。

Result: 中心化迹平方除以观测时间后的平均值依概率收敛到与度数相关的常数；其部分和经该常数中心化并除以窗口长度平方根后收敛到布朗运动。窗口长 M、起点 t≥1、R=t+M 时，只需 M→∞ 且 R^4(d-1)^R/N→0；收敛与低次多项式迹统计量联合成立且极限向量与布朗运动独立。非二分基图在固定时间偏移下还得到高斯迹极限；共同初始时间归一化并减去确定性增长剖面后，当 M/t 有有限极限时给出显式布朗时间变换。

Conclusion: 二分基图保持相同的平均常数但布朗方差率翻倍。结果刻画了随机重覆盖上 Grover 游走迹统计量的时空涨落结构，并说明初始时间可保持固定而无须对 M/t 施加额外限制。

Abstract: We prove a functional central limit theorem for trace statistics of Grover walks on random $N$-lifts of a fixed finite connected simple $d$-regular graph, $d\geq3$. All observations are computed from the same random lift. The squared centred traces, divided by their observation times, have averages converging in probability to a degree-dependent constant. Their partial sums, centred at this constant and divided by the square root of the window length, converge to Brownian motion. For a window of length $M$ beginning at $t\geq1$, it suffices that $M\to\infty$ and $R^4(d-1)^R/N\to0$, where $R=t+M$. The initial time may remain fixed, with no further restriction on $M/t$. Convergence holds jointly with fixed low-degree polynomial trace statistics; their limiting vector is independent of the Brownian motion. For non-bipartite bases, we also identify Gaussian trace limits at fixed time offsets. Under common initial-time normalisation, subtracting a deterministic growth profile gives an explicit Brownian time change when $M/t$ has a finite limit. Bipartite bases retain the same averaged constant and have twice the Brownian variance rate. The proof combines a growing-dimensional Poisson approximation, a uniform bilinear Chebyshev-energy estimate, a time-decaying Poisson--Gaussian coupling, and a Gaussian quadratic-array theorem. Separate moment estimates give expectation-centred versions under an additional sufficient condition.

</details>


### [93] [Dirichlet-Constrained Random Operators and Quenched Reflection Quantum Walks: Tree Spectra and Poisson Criticality at High Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36326)
*Nino Nagata,Ryoichi Suzuki*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究边权经局部归一化耦合的随机算子（random operators）及其反射量子游走（reflection quantum walks）的谱极限，在局部树状图序列上给出经验谱测度的几乎必然收敛，并刻画高维尺度下由 $d\alpha_d^2$ 分类的多族极限律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是理解度数趋于无穷时，Dirichlet 分布定位的随机边权所诱导的自伴算子谱律如何依赖度数 $d$ 与 Dirichlet 参数 $\alpha_d$，并把这些极限与量子游走的特征相位联系起来。

Method: 在 $d$-正则图每个顶点放置独立 Dirichlet 概率向量分配单位质量；对极限树律的每个矩给出闭路公式（closed-walk formula）；将确定性期望根谱律按 $\sqrt d$ 重标度后求弱极限，并在方差尺度上加权谱质量再次重标度。

Result: 重标度后期望根谱律存在弱极限当且仅当 $d\alpha_d^2$ 在 $[0,\infty]$ 中收敛；极限从零处的点质量、非半圆型临界律（non-semicircular critical laws，具 Bessel 强度的 Poisson 树表示）一直到半圆律（semicircle law），并给出对正 Dirichlet 参数一致的第一 Wasserstein 近似。当 $d\alpha_d^2\to0$ 时重标度律弱收敛到点质量而二阶矩仍为一，进一步按 $d\alpha_d$ 分类得到具显式 Beta 乘积描述的极限族。

Conclusion: 定量比较将方差尺度的分类转移到量子游走中心化并重标度的继承特征相位（inherited eigenphases），从而把谱极限结果与反射量子游走的相位行为统一起来。

Abstract: We study spectral limits of random operators whose incident edge weights are coupled by local normalization, and the reflection quantum walks associated with them. At each vertex of a $d$-regular graph, an independent Dirichlet probability vector distributes unit mass among outgoing directions. At fixed degree, the empirical spectral measures of the associated self-adjoint operators converge almost surely on locally tree-like graph sequences. We give a closed-walk formula for every moment of the limiting tree law.
  Our main result identifies two distinct high-degree scales. After rescaling the deterministic expected root spectral law by $\sqrt d$, a weak limit exists if and only if $dα_d^2$ converges in $[0,\infty]$, where $α_d$ is the Dirichlet parameter. The limits range from a point mass at zero through non-semicircular critical laws to the semicircle law. The critical laws admit a Poisson-tree representation with an explicit Bessel intensity. The extended family, including both endpoint laws, provides a first-Wasserstein approximation uniform over positive Dirichlet parameters.
  In the regime $dα_d^2\to0$, the rescaled law converges weakly to a point mass although its second moment remains one. Weighting spectral mass by its contribution to this moment and rescaling again reveals a different family of limits, classified by $dα_d$. These limits have explicit Beta-product descriptions. A quantitative comparison transfers the variance-scale classification to the centered and rescaled inherited eigenphases of the quantum walk.

</details>


### [94] [Spectral delocalization and central limit theorems for Fourier functionals of stationary Gaussian fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37113)
*Leonardo Maini*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文以谱去局域化（spectral delocalization）为工具，刻画局部紧阿贝尔群（locally compact Abelian groups）上平稳高斯场（stationary Gaussian fields）的 Fourier-Hermite 泛函的高斯涨落，并将高斯收敛性归约为谱测度卷积幂的小球估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有高斯收敛（如四阶混沌）结果多依赖模型特定的收缩估计，且对奇频、非绝对连续谱及算术随机波（arithmetic random waves）的高阶混沌情形缺乏统一刻画，因此需要一种与模型无关的判据。

Method: 引入谱去局域化条件：在总谱和接近所考察频率的条件下无部分和集中；由此把高斯收敛性约化为谱测度卷积幂（convolution powers of the spectral measure）的小球估计（small-ball estimates），无需模型特定的收缩估计。

Result: 在 T^d 上得到带限场与随机三角多项式的逐频 CLT，并将平面算术随机波高斯收敛的失效从四阶混沌推广至所有偶数阶 q≥4；在 R^d 与 Z^d 上建立逐频的 Breuer-Major 定理；对奇异连续谱测度（含线性 PDE 平稳高斯解）给出 CLT 与方差渐近，零频处弱 Ahlfors 正则性加 L² 密度即可保证二阶混沌 CLT，并覆盖分形集、Airy 曲面与光滑紧流形。

Conclusion: 对球形谱密度有界的随机波，逐频高斯收敛性被完全分类：仅当平面二阶混沌且 0<‖z‖<2 时失效；其余情形均可获得 CLT 与已知方差速率。

Abstract: We characterize Gaussian fluctuations of Fourier-Hermite functionals of stationary Gaussian fields on locally compact Abelian groups via spectral delocalization: conditional on the total spectral sum being near the probed frequency, no partial sum concentrates. Under suitable assumptions, this reduces Gaussian convergence to small-ball estimates for convolution powers of the spectral measure, without model-specific contraction estimates.
  On $\mathbb T^d$, the criterion yields CLTs at every fixed frequency for band-limited fields and random trigonometric polynomials, and extends the known failure of Gaussian convergence for planar arithmetic random waves from the fourth chaos to all even orders $q\ge4$. On $\mathbb R^d$ and $\mathbb Z^d$, we establish a Breuer-Major theorem for Fourier functionals at every frequency with nonzero limiting variance. Cyclical long-memory models exhibit frequency-dependent Gaussian convergence.
  We also establish CLTs and variance asymptotics for singular continuous spectral measures, including those of stationary Gaussian solutions of linear PDEs. At zero frequency, weak Ahlfors regularity of the spectral support and an $L^2$ density suffice for second-chaos CLTs, covering fractal sets, the Airy surface of Gaussian sea waves, and smooth compact manifolds. For a class of smooth hypersurfaces, we obtain the same variance rates and CLTs known for classical nonlinear functionals of Berry's random wave model. Finally, for random waves with spherical spectral densities bounded above and below, we classify Gaussian convergence at every frequency $\|z\|\le q$: it fails only for the planar second chaos when $0<\|z\|<2$.

</details>


### [95] [Local Law and Eigenvector Delocalization for Random Lifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37463)
*Jiaoyang Huang,Ruizhe Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文为固定有限连通简单图（最小度不小于三）的随机 N-重覆盖（random N-lifts）建立了定量局部定律（quantitative local law）。在避开万有覆盖谱（universal-cover spectrum）奇异能量处，得到高概率的 Green 函数（Green's function）逐点估计，并由此推出完整的特征向量离域化（eigenvector delocalization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机覆盖图（random lifts）是连接稀疏随机图与确定性基图谱性质的重要模型，而非正则图上局部定律与特征向量离域化的定量刻画仍待完善，尤其需处理万有覆盖谱奇异能量附近的行为。

Method: 研究固定有限连通简单图（最小度至少三）的随机 N-重覆盖，在远离万有覆盖谱奇异能量的区域，证明 Green 函数逐点估计（entrywise Green's function estimates），并能下探至谱尺度（spectral scale）(\log N)^C/N，且在能隙内部（bulk）及正则边（regular edges）与尖点（cusps）附近一致成立。

Result: 得到高概率的 Green 函数逐点估计；作为推论，建立完整的特征向量离域化：凡特征值与奇异能量保持固定小正距离的归一化特征向量 u，均以高概率满足 \|u\|_\infty \leq (\log N)^C/\sqrt{N}。

Conclusion: 该工作给出随机覆盖图上达到 (\log N)^C/N 谱尺度的定量局部定律，并由此获得完整的特征向量离域化界，覆盖能隙内部及奇异能量邻域之外的全谱区域。

Abstract: We establish a quantitative local law for random $N$-lifts of a fixed finite connected simple graph with minimum degree at least three. Away from the singular energies of the universal-cover spectrum, we obtain high-probability entrywise Green's function estimates down to spectral scales $(\log N)^C/N$, uniformly in the bulk and near regular edges and cusps. As a consequence, we establish complete eigenvector delocalization: every normalized eigenvector $u$ whose eigenvalue stays a small but fixed positive distance away from the singular energies satisfies $\|u\|_\infty\leq(\log N)^C/\sqrt{N}$ with high probability.

</details>


### [96] [Sharp approximations for the stationary measures of the open ASEP III: The fan region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37620)
*Dominik Schmid,Zongrui Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究扇形区域（fan region）中一般边界速率下开放非对称简单排斥过程（open asymmetric simple exclusion process, ASEP）的平稳测度，建立了其与懒惰简单随机游走（lazy simple random walk）游走与蜿蜒路径对应的离散极限刻画，并给出中度偏差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果已知扇形区域内最大流相（maximal current phase）及其边界上的平稳高度涨落具有涉及布朗游走（Brownian excursion）与布朗蜿蜒（Brownian meander）的极限，但缺乏对应的离散层面刻画，且一般非对称性下的测度变换与偏差估计尚待处理。

Method: 方法结合三类工具：Motzkin 路径的精细组合估计、完全非对称情形下的矩阵乘积拟设（matrix product ansatz），以及基于 Askey–Wilson 多项式的一般非对称性下测度变换的傅里叶型分解（Fourier-type decomposition）；并借助辅助路径测度的协方差恒等式与矩阵的二阶全非负性（total nonnegativity）得到负相关。

Result: 在远离边界的区间上，占据变量（occupation variables）的联合分布以全变差距离（total variation distance）被由懒惰简单随机游走的游走与蜿蜒路径构造的粒子构型逼近；在扇形区域的高密度相与低密度相中，得到误差随距相关边界距离指数衰减的伯努利乘积逼近（Bernoulli product approximation）；同时在最大流相及其边界上证明了任意站点集合粒子数的中度偏差（moderate deviation）估计。

Conclusion: 结果为扇形区域 ASEP 平稳测度提供了离散化的涨落刻画与偏差估计，表明边界涨落可由随机游走路径组合结构刻画，且负相关性与矩阵全非负性之间存在联系。

Abstract: We study the stationary measure of the open asymmetric simple exclusion process (ASEP) in the fan region with general boundary rates. The stationary height fluctuations in the maximal current phase and on its boundaries within the fan region are known to have limits involving Brownian excursions or meanders. We establish discrete counterparts of these limits: on intervals away from the boundaries, the joint law of the occupation variables is approximated in total variation distance by particle configurations constructed from lazy simple random walk excursions and meanders. In the high- and low-density phases of the fan region, we establish Bernoulli product approximations with errors that decay exponentially in the distance from the relevant boundary. Our methods combine refined combinatorial estimates for Motzkin paths and the matrix product ansatz in the totally asymmetric case with a Fourier-type decomposition (based on Askey--Wilson polynomials) of the change of measure for general asymmetry. In addition, in the maximal current phase and on its boundaries within the fan region, we prove moderate deviation estimates for particle counts on arbitrary sets of sites. These estimates rely on a covariance identity for an auxiliary path measure, through which total nonnegativity (of order two) of the matrices yields negative correlations.

</details>


### [97] [Sharp approximations for the stationary measures of the open ASEP II: The coexistence line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37636)
*Dominik Schmid,Zongrui Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究共存线（coexistence line）上开边界非对称简单排他过程（open asymmetric simple exclusion process, ASEP）的平稳测度（stationary measure），给出了激波位置（shock location）的显式可观测刻画，并证明其分布与格点上的均匀测度在总变差距离（total variation distance）意义下的最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画开边界 ASEP 在共存线上的平稳测度，并利用 Bryc 双层 Gibbs 表示（two-layer Gibbs representation）与本系列第一篇论文中的概率解释来获得对激波结构的定量理解。

Method: 以 Bryc 的双层 Gibbs 表示（Bryc's two-layer Gibbs representation）为起点，结合 Bryc 多项式在非齐次生灭链（inhomogeneous birth–death chain）上的概率解释，对平稳测度进行逼近分析。

Result: 将激波位置识别为粒子构型的一个显式可观测（observable），并获得其分布与格点上均匀测度之间总变差距离的最优界；在给定激波位置的条件下，以不同密度的伯努利乘积测度（Bernoulli product measures）逼近远离激波处的占据变量联合分布。

Conclusion: 上述逼近带来若干应用，包括涨落极限（fluctuation limits）、粒子密度的中等偏差界（moderate deviation bounds）以及平稳测度下关联函数（correlation functions）的极限。

Abstract: We study the stationary measure of the open asymmetric simple exclusion process (ASEP) on the coexistence line. Our starting point is Bryc's two-layer Gibbs representation, together with the probabilistic interpretation, established in the first paper in this series, of the associated Bryc polynomial in terms of an inhomogeneous birth--death chain. Our main result gives a sharp approximation of the stationary measure. Specifically, we identify the shock location as an explicit observable of the particle configuration and obtain optimal bounds on the total variation distance between its law and the uniform measure on the lattice. Conditional on the shock location, we approximate, in total variation distance, the joint law of the occupation variables away from the shock by Bernoulli product measures with different densities to its left and right. These approximations lead to further applications, including fluctuation limits, moderate deviation bounds for the particle density, and limits for correlation functions under the stationary measure.

</details>


### [98] [Sharp approximations for the stationary measures of the open ASEP I: The shock region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37639)
*Dominik Schmid,Zongrui Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究可积概率模型（integrable probabilistic models）中非对称性对带边界相互作用平稳测度（stationary measures）的作用，聚焦开放非对称简单排它过程（open asymmetric simple exclusion process, ASEP）。作者对平稳测度两层Gibbs表示（two-layer Gibbs representation）中的修正项——Bryc多项式（Bryc polynomial）——给出概率解释，并据此得到激波区（shock region）高、低密度相下用Bernoulli乘积测度逼近平稳测度的尖锐全变差（total variation）估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放ASEP的平稳测度虽具有两层Gibbs表示，但其中编码非对称参数依赖性的修正项（Bryc多项式）缺乏概率层面的理解，限制了对平稳测度结构的定量刻画。本文旨在为该修正项提供概率解释，并由此获得平稳测度的精细逼近结果。本文为三篇系列论文中的第一篇。

Method: 将Bryc多项式解释为某类生灭型马尔可夫链（birth-death-type Markov chain）的概率量；利用该解释，在激波区的高密度相与低密度相中构造Bernoulli乘积测度（Bernoulli product measures）对平稳测度进行全变差逼近，并推导中偏差（moderate deviation）估计及带轻粒子（light particles）的开放ASEP模型的渐近结果。

Result: 获得了激波区高、低密度相下平稳测度被Bernoulli乘积测度逼近的尖锐全变差估计；作为进一步应用，得到开放ASEP平稳测度的中偏差估计，以及相关带轻粒子开放ASEP模型的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 非对称性通过Bryc多项式在平稳测度中发挥可刻画的作用，其生灭链概率解释为平稳测度的定量分析提供了有效工具。该框架可推广至中偏差估计与轻粒子模型等应用，并为系列后续工作奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the role of asymmetry in the stationary measures of integrable probabilistic models with boundary interactions. This paper is the first in a series of three focusing on the open asymmetric simple exclusion process (ASEP). The stationary measure of open ASEP admits a two-layer Gibbs representation in which a correction term, which we call the Bryc polynomial, encodes the dependence on the asymmetry parameter. We give a probabilistic interpretation of the Bryc polynomial in terms of a birth-death-type Markov chain. As an application, we obtain sharp total variation approximations of the stationary measure by Bernoulli product measures in the high- and low-density phases of the shock region. These approximations yield further applications, including moderate deviation estimates for the stationary measure of open ASEP, as well as asymptotic results for the related model of open ASEP with light particles.

</details>


### [99] [Approximate Counting for Mixed $p$-Spin Glasses in Weak External Fields via Cluster Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37630)
*Qiang Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文给出近似 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick（SK）模型及有限混合 Ising p-spin 模型配分函数 $Z_N$ 的算法，在二阶矩阈值 $	heta_{\mathrm f}$ 以下的固定边距、均匀弱外场条件下，以关于 $N$、$\varepsilon^{-1}$、$\delta^{-1}$ 联合多项式级的精确操作数实现 $\log Z_N$ 的加性 $\varepsilon$ 误差近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为自旋玻璃（spin glass）类模型的配分函数（partition function）提供具有可证明复杂度保证的近似算法，并将适用范围扩展至 SK 模型与更高阶有限混合 p-spin 模型，同时放宽对无序分布（disorder）的假设（弱化到有限四阶矩）。

Method: 方法基于 Aizenman–Lebowitz–Ruelle 与 Dey–Wu 的簇展开（cluster expansion）。对高阶混合模型保留顶点数较少的多超图（hypergraph），通过局域自旋平均（local spin averages）与容斥（inclusion–exclusion）确定性计算总权重；对 SK 模型施加对度至少为三的顶点数及总边数的截断（cutoff），并用随机颜色编码（randomized color coding）估计保留项之和。

Result: 对任意 $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ 与失败参数 $\delta\in(0,1)$，算法以加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 近似 $\log Z_N$，精确操作数为 $N$、$\varepsilon^{-1}$、$\delta^{-1}$ 的联合多项式；失败概率不超过 $\delta$ 加一个显式无序尾部界（disorder tail bound），该尾部界在有限四阶矩条件下趋于零。

Conclusion: 借助基于反向超压缩性（reverse hypercontractivity）的下尾控制（lower tail control），可将归一化配分函数的均方近似（mean-square approximation）转化为对数精度，从而在所述条件下得到多项式时间可证明近似算法。

Abstract: We give algorithms for approximating the partition function $Z_N$ of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) model and finite mixed Ising $p$-spin models with $p\ge 3$. The algorithms apply at a fixed margin below the second-moment threshold $β_{\mathrm f}$ with homogeneous weak external fields $|h|\leqρ_0N^{-1/4}$, for fixed $ρ_0>0$. For any accuracy $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ and failure parameter $δ\in(0,1)$, they approximate $\log Z_N$ to additive error $\varepsilon$. The number of exact operations is polynomial jointly in $N$, $\varepsilon^{-1}$, and $δ^{-1}$. The disorder variables have i.i.d symmetric distribution with unit variance. The failure probability is at most $δ$ plus an explicit disorder tail bound. This tail bound tends to zero under a finite fourth moment condition. Our approach builds on the cluster expansions of Aizenman--Lebowitz--Ruelle~\cite{ALR87} and Dey--Wu~\cites{DW23,DW26}. For higher-order mixtures, we retain hypergraphs using few vertices and evaluate their total weight deterministically through local spin averages and inclusion--exclusion. For SK, we impose cutoffs on the number of vertices of degree at least three and on the total number of edges. We estimate the retained sum using randomized color coding~\cite{AYZ95}. A lower tail control based on reverse hypercontractivity~\cite{MOS13} converts the mean-square approximation of the normalized partition function into logarithmic accuracy.

</details>

### [100] [Reflection equations and Littlewood identities for spin Hall-Littlewood and $q$-Whittaker functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36644)
*Svetlana Gavrilova,Sergei Korotkikh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出非齐次稳定自旋 Hall-Littlewood 及非齐次自旋 q-Whittaker 对称函数的双参数 Littlewood 型恒等式，并以可积格点模型方法完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广 Warnaar（2006）关于普通 Hall-Littlewood 多项式的 Littlewood 恒等式，将系数由 Rogers-Szegö 多项式替换为 Al-Salam-Chihara 多项式的乘积。

Method: 证明基于可积格点模型（integrable lattice models），关键步骤是给出完全融合六顶点模型（fully fused six-vertex model）反射方程与边界方程的三个新解，借助求值 U_q(L sl_2)-模张量积之间的同构及在 q-Onsager 余理想子代数（q-Onsager coideal subalgebra）下稳定的余向量获得。

Result: 得到非齐次稳定自旋 Hall-Littlewood 函数与非齐次自旋 q-Whittaker 函数的双参数 Littlewood 恒等式，其系数表示为 Al-Salam-Chihara 多项式的乘积。

Conclusion: 所获恒等式构成对 Warnaar 结果的推广，并表明完全融合六顶点模型的反射与边界方程新解可用于推导对称函数的双参数恒等式。

Abstract: We describe two-parametric Littlewood identities for inhomogeneous stable spin Hall-Littlewood and inhomogeneous spin $q$-Whittaker symmetric functions. These identities generalize the Littlewood identity found by Warnaar (2006) for the ordinary Hall-Littlewood polynomials. However, instead of Rogers-Szegö coefficients used by Warnaar, coefficients in our identities are given by products of Al-Salam-Chihara polynomials.
  Our proof relies on integrable lattice models. A key step includes three new solutions to the reflection and boundary equations for the fully fused six-vertex model. To find these solutions we use isomorphisms between tensor products of evaluation $U_q(L\mathfrak{sl}_2)$-modules, as well as covectors stable under a $q$-Onsager coideal subalgebra.

</details>


### [101] [Mutation-acyclicity in rank four is decidable but not semialgebraic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36179)
*Kymani T. K. Armstrong-Williams,Edward Hirst,Blake Jackson,Kyu-Hwan Lee*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明四顶点箭图的变异无环性（mutation-acyclicity）是可判定的，并给出基于高度界（height bound）的有限搜索算法，复杂度为 O(h^24)，并论证在 n≥4 时不存在半代数判据（semialgebraic criterion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异无环性与变异等价（mutation equivalence）的判定问题在秩三已有结论，但四顶点及更高阶情形缺乏可判定性结果；同时需要厘清是否存在仅依赖箭权重多项式的统一判据。

Method: 证明若四顶点箭图 Q 是变异无环的，则存在变异序列将其化为无环箭图，且箭重不超过 max{ht(Q), m(Q)}，其中 ht(Q) 为最大箭重、m(Q) 为 Fomin–Neville 的 Markov 不变量（Markov invariant）在 totally proper wiggle class 上的取值；在该界的可计算形式内进行状态搜索，并构造仿射族（affine family）反例。

Result: 得到 O(h^24) 状态的判定算法，结合 Caldero–Keller 定理可判定一个箭图变异无环时的四顶点箭图变异等价（至重标号）；猜想仅 ht(Q) 即可作界（对应 O(h^6) 状态），并在高度不超过三的情形验证；对每个 n≥4 构造连通仿射族，其非负偶数成员变异无环、正奇数成员变异循环，从而否定半代数判据的存在性。

Conclusion: 四顶点箭图的变异无环性可判定，且判定可推广至部分变异等价问题；但 n≥4 时不存在固定的有限布尔组合多项式等式与严格不等式判据。文中还修正了此前关于高度不超过二的四顶点箭图判据，并以简短证书恢复其分类计数，高度界证明中的计算由计算机验证。

Abstract: We prove that mutation-acyclicity of quivers on four vertices is decidable. If a four-vertex quiver $Q$ is mutation-acyclic, then some sequence of mutations takes it to an acyclic quiver without any arrow multiplicity exceeding $\operatorname{max}\{\operatorname{ht}(Q),m(Q)\}$, where $\operatorname{ht}(Q)$ is the largest arrow multiplicity of $Q$ and $m(Q)$ is the Markov invariant of Fomin and Neville evaluated on the totally proper wiggle class of $Q$. A search within a computable form of this bound decides mutation-acyclicity of four-vertex quivers with $O(h^{24})$ states for input height $h\ge1$. Together with a theorem of Caldero and Keller, it also decides mutation equivalence of two four-vertex quivers up to relabeling whenever one of them is mutation-acyclic. We conjecture that $\operatorname{ht}(Q)$ alone suffices as the bound, which would reduce the search to $O(h^{6})$ states, and we verify this for all four-vertex quivers of height at most three. In the other direction, for every $n\ge4$ we construct an affine family of connected $n$-vertex quivers whose nonnegative even members are mutation-acyclic and whose positive odd members are mutation-cyclic. Consequently, unlike in rank three, no fixed finite Boolean combination of polynomial equations and strict polynomial inequalities in the arrow weights (i.e., a semialgebraic criterion) decides mutation-acyclicity of $n$-vertex quivers, even allowing polynomials that are not mutation-invariant. Finally, we correct the criterion for four-vertex quivers of height at most two stated in our earlier paper, and recover its classification counts with short certificates. The calculations in the proof of the height bound are verified by computer for all values of their parameters.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Large number of hyperbolic and periodic solutions of sine-Gordon equation with nontrivial time dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36891)
*Thokala Soloman Raju,T. Shreecharan,Avinash Khare,Avadh Saxena*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 通过引入合适拟设（ansatz），将含非平凡时间依赖的 sine-Gordon 方程精确解问题约化为两个一阶对称 φ⁴ 型耦合方程的求解，从而构造出大量双曲型与周期型精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是求解具有非平凡时间依赖的 sine-Gordon 方程（sine-Gordon equation）的精确解，并将求解过程简化到可处理的耦合一阶方程框架中。

Method: 采用合适的拟设（ansatz），把原方程约化为关于空间变量 x 与时间 t 的两个一阶对称 φ⁴ 型耦合方程（first order symmetric φ⁴-like coupled equations），再据此求解。

Result: 获得大量双曲型与周期型解，既包括已知的双孤子（two-soliton）、孤子-反孤子（soliton-antisoliton）与呼吸子（breather）解，也获得周期型双孤子、周期型孤子-反孤子与周期型呼吸子解；此外还得到与上述三类解相关的实值与复值（宇称-时间反演，parity-time reversal，PT）不变叠加解。

Conclusion: 该拟设方法能有效统一生成 sine-Gordon 方程的多类精确解，并将已知孤子/呼吸子结果推广至周期型与 PT 对称叠加形式。

Abstract: By using suitable ansatz which reduces the problem of obtaining exact solutions of the sine-Gordon equation with nontrivial time dependence to obtaining exact solutions of two first order symmetric $φ^4$-like coupled equations in $x$ and $t$, we obtain a large number of hyperbolic and periodic solutions of the sine-Gordon equation.
