### [139] [Development of a real-time FPGA-based CNN trigger operating on full-granularity calorimeter images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36720)
*Jia-Jen Wu,Yun-Tsung Lai,Yu-Chen Tung,Sin-Ting Chen,Yu-Sheng Liu*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向触发电子学的实时 FPGA（field-programmable gate array）卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network, CNN）实现，直接处理保留晶体级信息的高粒度量能器图像，并以 KOTO 实验为基准验证其在数十微秒内完成事例分类的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触发电子学存在资源与延迟约束，此前难以在触发层面使用全粒度（full-granularity）量能器图像进行 CNN 事例选择；本文旨在验证此类高精度在线分类在数十微秒延迟预算内的可行性。

Method: 将网络参数与隐藏激活量化至 8 位（quantization-aware training），并用无损的 space-to-depth 重排把输入图像映射到更少空间位置；固件在 AMD Versal Premium VPK120 评估板上实现，以 J-PARC KOTO 探测器记录数据与数据采集（data-acquisition, DAQ）约束为基准，目标是在线区分稀有衰变与主导本底。

Result: 在 100,000 个测试事例上，于信号效率 95% 时拒绝 $(99.34\pm0.04)\%$ 的富含本底的记录样本；测得 CNN 推理延迟为 17.9 μs，结合图像组装、链路传输与分数回传后端到端延迟预计为 25.9 μs，处于 32 μs 的 DAQ 延迟预算之内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，使用全粒度量能器图像进行基于 CNN 的事例选择具备可行性，可在保持触发延迟于数十微秒量级的同时实现高本底拒绝。

Abstract: We present a real-time field-programmable gate array (FPGA) implementation of a convolutional neural network (CNN) for event classification on full calorimeter images that retain crystal-level shower information. To meet the resource and latency constraints of trigger electronics, the network parameters and hidden activations were quantized to 8 bits with quantization-aware training, and a lossless space-to-depth reorganization rearranged the input image into fewer spatial positions, reducing the estimated CNN inference latency by more than an order of magnitude. The approach was evaluated with the J-PARC KOTO detector, recorded data, and data-acquisition (DAQ) constraints as a benchmark, targeting the online separation of the rare $K^{0}_{L}\rightarrowπ^{0}e^{+}e^{-}$ decay from its dominant $K^{0}_{L}\rightarrowπ^{+}π^{-}π^{0}$ background. As a proof of principle, the firmware was implemented and validated on an AMD Versal Premium VPK120 evaluation board. On 100,000 test events, inference on the board rejected $(99.34\pm0.04)\%$ of the recorded background sample enriched in $K^{0}_{L}\rightarrowπ^{+}π^{-}π^{0}$ decays at a nominal $K^{0}_{L}\rightarrowπ^{0}e^{+}e^{-}$ efficiency of 95%, with a measured CNN inference latency of 17.9 $μ$s. Combining the measured image-assembly and CNN inference latencies with the estimated link-transfer and score-return times gives a projected end-to-end latency of 25.9 $μ$s, within the adopted 32 $μ$s DAQ latency budget. These results demonstrate the feasibility of using full-granularity calorimeter images for CNN-based event selection that achieves high background rejection while keeping the trigger latency within tens of microseconds.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Rotational Perturbation Theory for Magnetic Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36424)
*Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文发展了共线与非共线磁体系中磁相互作用的旋转微扰理论（rotational perturbation theory），区分交换场的指定旋转与磁矩的受约束旋转，并据此导出能量曲率、自旋波刚度和有效 Heisenberg 交换的投影方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁相互作用中旋转能量曲率（rotational curvature）与集体磁行为、可迁移交换系数之间的关系尚需在统一微扰框架下厘清，尤其需区分指定场旋转与受约束磁矩旋转两种情形。

Method: 建立旋转微扰理论：在全交换劈裂（full exchange splitting）下由微扰论直接导出指定场能量曲率的格林函数（Green-function）表达式，不依赖局域力定理（local force theorem）；受约束磁矩曲率则需静态磁响应并关联逆静态磁化率（inverse static susceptibility）。分析非共线对相互作用及平面与锥形自旋螺旋（spin spiral），并处理轨道非局域磁势与关联电子响应。

Result: 导出了长波极限下的铁磁自旋波刚度（spin-wave stiffness）及在绝热子晶格进动假设下的反铁磁自旋波速度（spin-wave velocity）；将完整横向对 Hessian 投影到有效标量 Heisenberg 交换并量化未重构曲率（unreconstructed curvature）；双二次（biquadratic）与三自旋算例表明局域提取的交换系数不一定代表可迁移的双线性相互作用。

Conclusion: 旋转曲率到集体磁行为的映射需考虑轨道非局域性与关联电子响应；局域提取的交换系数不具普遍可迁移性，投影残余曲率与非双线性项需显式处理。

Abstract: We develop rotational perturbation theory for magnetic interactions in collinear and noncollinear systems, distinguishing prescribed rotations of exchange fields from constrained rotations of magnetic moments. At the full exchange splitting, we derive Green-function expressions for prescribedfield energy curvatures directly from perturbation theory, without invoking the local force theorem; constrained-moment curvatures additionally require the stationary magnetic response and are related to the inverse static susceptibility. We examine noncollinear pair interactions and planar and conical spin spirals, and derive the long-wavelength ferromagnetic spin-wave stiffness and, assuming adiabatic sublattice precession, the antiferromagnetic spin-wave velocity. Orbital-nonlocal magnetic potentials and correlated electronic response reveal further requirements for connecting rotational curvatures to collective magnetic behavior. As a practical extension, we project the complete transverse pair Hessian onto an effective scalar Heisenberg exchange and quantify the unreconstructed curvature. Biquadratic and three-spin examples illustrate why locally extracted exchange coefficients need not represent transferable bilinear interactions.

</details>

### [141] [All Dark Matter as Primordial Black Holes: a Gravitational-Wave Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36177)
*A. Ianniccari,A. J. Iovino,G. Perna,D. Perrone,A. Riotto*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种检验"宇宙暗物质（dark matter）完全由小行星质量原初黑洞（asteroidal-mass primordial black holes）构成"这一假说的新方法，依据球形尖峰（spherically symmetric spikes）中累积的轻原初黑洞并合到快速旋转的超大质量天体物理黑洞（supermassive astrophysical black holes）上所产生的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background），并讨论未来空间引力波天文台 LISA 与 DECIGO 可观测该信号的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对"暗物质全部为小行星质量原初黑洞"假说的检验手段有限，需要一种独立且可观测的新判据。

Method: 利用球形尖峰内累积的轻原初黑洞向快速旋转超大质量天体物理黑洞并合的过程，计算其产生的随机引力波背景，并将其作为该暗物质假说的检验信号。

Result: 摘要指出该信号在特定场景下可被未来空间引力波天文台 LISA 和 DECIGO 探测到，但未给出具体谱形或信噪比数值。

Conclusion: 随机引力波背景可作为一种检验小行星质量原初黑洞暗物质假说的观测途径，其可行性依赖于未来空间引力波观测能力。

Abstract: We propose a novel test for the hypothesis that the dark matter of the Universe is composed entirely by asteroidal-mass primordial black holes. It relies on detecting the stochastic gravitational-wave background generated by the coalescence onto rapidly spinning supermassive astrophysical black holes of the light primordial black holes accumulated in spherically symmetric spikes. We discuss the scenarios in which the signal can be observed by the future space-based gravitational-wave observatories LISA and DECIGO.

</details>


### [142] [Non-minimal $α$-attractor magnetogenesis with potential dependent couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36228)
*Antonino G. Marino,Tommaso Mengoni*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在暴胀磁成因（inflationary magnetogenesis）框架下，研究大场（large-field）α-attractor 启发模型中非共形耦合（non-conformal coupling）驱动的电磁场放大；引入依赖势的电磁耦合函数 f(φ) 并分析非最小耦合 φ²R 对磁场振幅的影响，在满足 CMB 约束的构型下给出现今磁场约 10⁻²³ G 的条件性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴胀期间的电磁场产生需要破坏共形不变性，而大场 α-attractor 模型中耦合函数与非最小引力耦合对电磁放大的作用尚需系统刻画，因此本文针对该问题开展分析。

Method: 引入势依赖的电磁耦合函数 f(φ)，使其在慢滚（slow roll）期间复现 f∝a^p 的标度；以 Starobinsky 暴胀为起点解析导出 E-model 与 T-model 势的显式耦合；在 φ²R 非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）情形下，借助梯度展开形式（gradient expansion formalism）计入电磁反作用（backreaction）与 Schwinger 对产生（Schwinger pair production），比较不同耦合强度 ξ 的取值。

Result: 数值结果表明，非最小耦合影响电磁部分的演化及由此得到的磁场振幅；在所分析的构型中，最小耦合与 ξ>0 情形与所采用的 CMB 约束相容，并给出现今磁场估计量级为 10⁻²³ G。

Conclusion: α-attractor 启发的大场模型配合势依赖耦合函数可产生与 CMB 约束一致的暴胀磁场；所得 10⁻²³ G 量级的估计依赖于所选参数构型，属条件性结论。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary magnetogenesis driven by a non conformal coupling in the case of large-field $α$-attractor inspired models. We introduce potential-dependent electromagnetic coupling functions $f(φ)$ that reproduce the scaling $f\propto a^p$ during slow roll. Starting from Starobinsky inflation, we analytically derive explicit couplings for $(E)$-model and $(T)$-model potentials. We then study how these functions affect electromagnetic amplification in the regime of non-minimal coupling between the inflaton field and the Ricci scalar $φ^2 R$. In particular, we compare different values for the coupling strength $ξ$, accounting for electromagnetic backreaction and Schwinger pair production through the gradient expansion formalism. Our numerical results show that the non-minimal coupling affects the evolution of the electromagnetic sector and the resulting magnetic-field amplitude. We find that, within the analyzed configurations, minimally coupled and $ξ>0$ scenarios are compatible with the adopted CMB constraints, yielding conditional present-day magnetic-field estimates of order $10^{-23}$ G.

</details>


### [143] [Relativistic N-body simulations with mass-varying neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36752)
*Øyvind Christiansen,Julian Adamek,Mainak Baidya,Vitor da Fonseca,Eric V. Linder,David F. Mota*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文利用完全动力学、相对论性的 N 体与格点模拟研究中微子与 quintessence 场耦合的质量可变中微子模型，发现中微子团块高度动态且自旋，并可通过相对论效应（含各向异性应力导致的引力势滑移）区分于 LCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期宇宙学数据对中微子质量的暗示弱于中微子振荡实验所需值，这促使研究者考察中微子质量随宇宙时间变化的可能性；现有研究对非线性场演化与相对论效应的处理尚不充分。

Method: 构建中微子与充当暗能量的 quintessence 场耦合的模型，采用完全动力学且相对论性的 N 体（N-body）与格点（lattice）模拟计算结构形成，包含非线性场演化与弱场度规展开。

Result: 与前人研究类似，中微子团块（neutrino lumps）普遍出现，但被观测为高度动态并带自旋；在若干模型中，计入标量场对宇宙膨胀的非线性效应后，有效暗能量可呈现幻影穿越（phantom crossing）。

Conclusion: 模拟框架中的相对论物理可借助中微子与暗能量性质观测、以及各向异性应力（anisotropic stress）引起的引力势滑移（slip），将此类模型与 LCDM 区分；质量可变中微子的丰富现象学值得进一步探索与模拟技术发展。

Abstract: Recent cosmological data provide weak hints for smaller masses than what is required by neutrino-oscillation experiments, motivating investigations into variation of the neutrino masses with cosmic time. Here we study a model where the neutrinos couple to a quintessence field that acts as dark energy. We compute structure formation using fully dynamical and relativistic N-body and lattice simulations, with nonlinear field evolution and a weak-field metric expansion. Similar to previous studies, we find the generic appearance of neutrino lumps, but observe them to be highly dynamical and spinning. We study several models where the effective dark energy, including nonlinear effects of the scalar field on the cosmic expansion, can show phantom crossing. The relativistic physics available in our simulation framework can help distinguish such models from LCDM, through observations of neutrino and dark energy properties, and slip between the gravitational potentials due to anisotropic stress. The rich phenomenology offered by mass-varying neutrinos motivates further exploration and development of the simulation techniques.

</details>


### [144] [Cosmological constraints on single-field inflation models from ACT/SPT data and observations at sub-Mpc scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37383)
*Qing-Yang Wang,Jing-Zhi Zhou,Fei-Yu Chen,Zhi-Chao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究单场暴胀模型（single-field inflation）能否在亚兆秒差距（sub-Mpc）尺度产生大曲率扰动（large curvature perturbations），同时与多尺度宇宙学观测一致。作者指出这一目标与ACT/SPT测得的标量谱指数（scalar spectral index）n_s存在张力，并提出三类势能形式以缓解该矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产生小尺度增强的原初扰动（primordial perturbations）与满足ACT和SPT对标量谱指数n_s的测量之间存在冲突，该冲突使若干常规单场暴胀模型不再受青睐，因此需要寻找可同时满足两类要求的模型构建方式。

Method: 提出三类暴胀势（inflationary potentials）：bump/dip修正型、阶梯型（step-like）与双拐点型（double-inflection-point）；计算其原初功率谱（primordial power spectra），并利用CMB、BAO、PBH、PTA与CMB畸变（CMB-distortion）等多尺度观测约束参数空间。

Result: 三类势均可在足够放大原初扰动的同时，给出落在当前观测界限2σ范围内的n_s。多尺度约束表明，部分模型可同时满足ACT/SPT限制并解释现有PTA观测，但同时满足PBH丰度（PBH abundance）限制仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 经 bump/dip、阶梯与双拐点修正的单场暴胀模型是缓解小尺度扰动增强与n_s观测之间张力的可行方案；然而在同时兼顾PTA解释与PBH丰度上限方面仍存在困难，构成后续模型构建的主要约束。

Abstract: We investigate single-field inflation models that can produce large curvature perturbations at sub-Mpc scales while remaining consistent with cosmological observations across multiple regimes. We highlight the tension between generating enhanced small-scale perturbations and maintaining agreement with the scalar spectral index $n_s$ measured by ACT and SPT, which disfavors several conventional single-field inflation models. To address this issue, we propose three viable classes of inflationary potentials: bump/dip-modified, step-like, and double-inflection-point. Each can sufficiently amplify primordial perturbations while predicting $n_s$ that lie within the $2σ$ range of current observational bounds. In addition, we examine the primordial power spectra predicted by these models and constrain their parameter spaces using cosmological observations at different scales, including CMB, BAO, PBH, PTA, and CMB-distortion. Our results show that some of the models considered can simultaneously satisfy the ACT/SPT constraints and account for the current PTA observations, although doing so while respecting the constraints on the PBH abundance remains challenging.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Elasticized magnetohydrostatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36241)
*J. W. Burby,I. Cho,M. Ruth*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出首个能严格保证任意指定磁拓扑下平衡存在的仿星器（stellarator）平衡模型，通过将等离子体势能泛函替换为快尺度动理学涨落的系综平均（ensemble average），使磁场拓扑的确定与平衡存在问题彻底分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿星器平衡建模存在两大盲点：理论与计算缺乏基础性一致，且难以可靠探索具经济吸引力的高β概念。其技术根源在于缺乏描述平衡的偏微分方程（partial differential equations）的可用存在性理论（existence theory），以及约束磁场拓扑模型粗糙、缺乏灵活性。

Method: 以等离子体势能泛函（plasma potential energy functional）对快速动理学尺度涨落的系综平均替代标准泛函；平均使势能"弹性化（elasticize）"，在能量上惩罚磁力线分离，并将拓扑确定从平衡存在性问题中分离，从而可引入真实的撕裂物理（tearing physics）。

Result: 弹性化势能对等离子体应变呈超线性（super-linear）依赖，构成剖面刚度（profile stiffness）的唯象模型，并在存在性理论中起关键作用；模型严格保证任意选定磁拓扑下平衡的存在。

Conclusion: 该模型为仿星器平衡提供了具有严格存在性保证的新框架，弥合理论与计算间的差距，并为可靠探索高β位形及引入撕裂物理奠定基础。

Abstract: The modern approach to modeling equilibria in stellarators features two blind spots: lack of foundational agreement between theory and computation, and inability to reliably explore economically-attractive high-$β$ concepts. These blind spots originate at a technical level from lack of a useful existence theory for the partial differential equations believed to describe equilibria, and from the encumbrance of crude, inflexible models for the topology of the confining magnetic field. This Article proposes the first stellarator equilibrium model that rigorously guarantees existence of an equilibrium with any chosen magnetic topology. It completely separates determination of the magnetic topology from the equilibrium existence question, and thereby enables importing realistic tearing physics into equilibrium computations. The new model replaces the standard plasma potential energy functional with its ensemble average over rapid kinetic-scale fluctuations. Averaging ``elasticizes" the potential by energetically penalizing separation of field lines. The elasticized potential's super-linear dependence on plasma strain embodies a phenomenological model for profile stiffness, and plays an essential role in the existence theory.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Optimization of the second Robin eigenvalue on a planar exterior domain under fixed area constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34320)
*David Krejcirik,Vladimir Lotoreichik*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究平面外部区域上带负边界参数的 Robin 拉普拉斯算子（Robin Laplacian），证明在固定面积与凸性假设下，第二个 Robin 特征值由圆盘外部区域取得最大值，且对非圆区域不等式严格成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当负边界参数绝对值足够大时，该算子在本性谱（essential spectrum）底部之下至少有两个负特征值；作者希望确定在给定面积约束下第二个 Robin 特征值的最优上界，并解决其 2024 年猜想中的第一项。

Method: 证明改进了平行坐标法（parallel-coordinate method），允许试探函数（trial function）的角相位（angular phase）随到边界的距离变化；分析在固定面积约束与外部区域有界补集的凸性假设下进行。

Result: 在固定面积与凸性假设下，第二个 Robin 特征值被圆盘的外部区域最大化；当对照圆盘的第二特征值为负时，对任意非圆外部区域不等式严格成立。

Conclusion: 该结果在附加凸性假设下解决了作者 2024 年猜想的第一项；放松凸性假设与周长约束（perimeter constraint）仍是未解决的问题。

Abstract: We consider the Robin Laplacian on a planar exterior domain with a negative boundary parameter. For a negative boundary parameter of a sufficiently large absolute value this operator has at least two negative eigenvalues below the bottom of the essential spectrum. We prove that under a fixed area constraint and convexity assumption on the bounded complement of the exterior domain the second Robin eigenvalue is maximized by the exterior of the disk. The inequality is strict for every exterior of a non-circular domain whenever the comparison disk has a negative second eigenvalue. The proof modifies the parallel-coordinate method by allowing the angular phase of a trial function to vary with the distance from the boundary. This result resolves the first item in a 2024 conjecture by the authors under an additional convexity assumption. Relaxing the convexity and the perimeter constraint remain open problems.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Closure of Traceful Radiation-Gauge Reconstruction with Matter: General Kerr Conditions, a Thin-Shell Benchmark, and a Conserved Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35938)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在有物质（matter）的克尔背景（Kerr background）上，构建一阶含迹辐射规范度规重构（traceful radiation-gauge metric reconstruction）的闭合问题，并给出该三角传输系统（triangular transport system）的局部存在唯一性及闭合全局表述所需的附加条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度规重构（metric reconstruction）通常面临规范与物质源耦合导致的闭合性（closure problem）难题，本文旨在明确在有物质克尔背景下重构方程何时可局部闭合、何时需要额外条件。

Method: 将重构分解为外尔曲率（Weyl-curvature）、里奇迹（Ricci-trace）与质量-角动量完备化（mass–angular-momentum completion）三个扇区（sectors）；对相容的初值、规范与完备化数据，在正则克尔区域（regular Kerr domain）上证明所得三角传输系统的局部存在唯一性。并以薄壳（thin shell）与常角动量流体环面（fluid torus）两种守恒物质源（conserved matter sources）进行检验。

Result: 史瓦西黑洞薄壳情形下，Israel 匹配（Israel matching）、正则性、渐近平直性、正压状态方程与固定静质量定出静态、轴向与主导旋转扇区：ADM 质量偏移为 $\sqrt{1-2M/R}$ 乘以壳静质量，轴向扇区给出角动量、转动惯量与内部参考系拖曳（frame dragging）。克尔上的定常常角动量环面计算显示非球对称源一般激发耦合的里奇扇区。

Conclusion: 薄壳提供了闭合的基准（benchmark），环面则说明一般克尔源所需额外结构；本文结论为局部结果而非全局存在性定理（not a global existence theorem）。

Abstract: We formulate the closure problem for first-order, traceful radiation-gauge metric reconstruction on a Kerr background with matter. The reconstruction is decomposed into Weyl-curvature, Ricci-trace, and mass--angular-momentum completion sectors. For compatible initial, gauge, and completion data, we establish local existence and uniqueness of the resulting triangular transport system on a regular Kerr domain, and identify the additional conditions needed for a closed global formulation. This is not a global existence theorem. We test the framework using two conserved matter sources. For a thin shell around a Schwarzschild black hole, Israel matching, regularity, asymptotic flatness, a barotropic equation of state, and fixed rest mass determine the static, axial, and leading rotational sectors. The ADM-mass shift is $\sqrt{1-2M/R}$ times the shell rest mass, while the axial sector determines the angular momentum, moment of inertia, and interior frame dragging. We also construct a stationary constant-angular-momentum fluid torus on Kerr and compute its Newman--Penrose Ricci projections, showing that nonspherical sources generally excite coupled Ricci sectors. The shell provides a closed benchmark, while the torus illustrates the additional structure required for general Kerr sources.

</details>


### [148] [Can a Wormhole Induce a Monopole?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35969)
*Sergei Slobodov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出，关于绕虫洞（wormhole）一端运行的天体能否使另一端观测质量周期变化的设想不成立：把不同质量的静态解当作同一轨道系统的连续状态是错误做法，一阶微扰下源的运动不能改变观测到的史瓦西质量（Schwarzschild mass）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验针对银河系中心 Sgr A* 观测所提出的“周期性质量变化”信号假说——即绕虫洞一端的恒星轨道运动是否会在另一端引起可探测的质量起伏，并澄清其理论基础是否成立。

Method: 采用类比与微扰分析：以麦克斯韦类比（Maxwell analogue）说明只有当电流穿过测量面时测得电荷才变化；在引力情形下于一阶微扰层面分析源运动对观测史瓦西质量的影响，并进一步考察真空观测端（observer end）的邦迪质量（Bondi mass）。

Result: 预测信号源于将不同质量的静态解误作同一轨道系统的相继状态。引力中源的运动在一阶上不能改变观测到的史瓦西质量；真空观测端的邦迪质量即便超过一阶也不能增加，尽管引力辐射（gravitational radiation）可使其减小。

Conclusion: 所提出的 Sgr A* 周围周期性质量变化信号不会出现。此外，当两端间距足够大时，同一观测端动力学可在两端处于同一外部区域（exterior）的构型中重现，说明该效应并非虫洞所特有。

Abstract: Could a star orbiting one mouth of a wormhole make the mass seen near the other rise and fall? We show that the periodic mass change proposed for searches around Sgr A* does not occur. The predicted signal comes from treating static solutions with different masses as successive states of the same orbiting system. In the Maxwell analogue, charge measured around a mouth changes only when current crosses the measuring surface. Similarly, in gravity, source motion cannot change the observed Schwarzschild mass at first order. Furthermore, the Bondi mass of a vacuum observer end cannot increase even beyond first order, though gravitational radiation can reduce it. The same observer-side dynamics can be reproduced with both mouths in one exterior at sufficiently large separation.

</details>


### [149] [Attractors and initial-condition sensitivity in Horndeski cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36036)
*Yulia A. Ageeva,Andrey K. Sharofeev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究邻近均匀宇宙演化史是趋向还是远离创世解（Genesis solutions）与收缩解（contracting solutions），并分析晚期标量速度不确定性对推断初始速度的影响；结果显示背景解的吸引性不足以判定物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验创世模型与收缩模型中均匀背景解（homogeneous background solutions）的稳定性与吸引性质，并厘清晚期标量速度（scalar velocity）不确定度如何反向影响初始速度的推断。

Method: 通过对邻近均匀宇宙轨迹（nearby homogeneous trajectories）与创世解、收缩解的收敛/发散关系进行解析考察，并将引力动力学（gravitational dynamics）纳入创世模型的速度关系分析。

Result: 在创世模型中，引入引力动力学可显著改变晚期速度与初始速度的关系；均匀解的收敛（convergence）甚至可出现在扰动不稳定的背景附近。收缩模型（contracting model）中，一个幂律解（power-law solution）吸引邻近轨迹，另一个则排斥；效应强度依赖于宇宙学态（cosmological state）的参量化方式。

Conclusion: 背景解的吸引性（attraction）不足以证明宇宙学解在物理上可行，须同时考虑扰动稳定性；后续反弹（bounce）阶段的演化未在本文讨论。

Abstract: We study whether nearby homogeneous cosmological histories approach or move away from the Genesis and contracting solutions. We also determine how an uncertainty in the scalar velocity at a later stage affects the inferred initial velocity. In the Genesis models, including the gravitational dynamics can substantially change this relation. We find that convergence of homogeneous solutions can occur even around backgrounds with unstable perturbations, and therefore attraction of the background is not sufficient to establish that a cosmological solution is physically viable. In the contracting model, one power-law solution attracts nearby trajectories, whereas another repels them. The strength of these effects depends on how the cosmological state is parametrized. Their evolution through the subsequent bounce is not considered here.

</details>


### [150] [Semi-agnostic inspiral modeling: the massless scalar case at leading post-Newtonian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36037)
*Dario Rossi,Leonardo Gualtieri,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出半不可知（semi-agnostic）建模框架，在广义相对论（General Relativity）之外、不指定具体理论的情况下固定场内容，用后牛顿展开与 l/L 展开描述黑洞双星旋进（inspiral），并表明该过程可由少量普适参数刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常需选定某一具体替代理论；本文旨在建立不依赖特定理论的建模方式，以便对多源进行联合限制并搜寻特定场。

Method: 采用后牛顿展开（post-Newtonian expansion）与 l/L 幂次展开，其中 l 为控制偏离广义相对论的特征长度尺度，L 为系统特征长度尺度；聚焦无质量标量场（massless scalar field）与度规耦合、且在领头后牛顿阶的情形，并同时考虑相互作用项与源物理量随 l/L 的标度。

Result: 在该设定下，旋进过程可用少量普适参数（universal parameters）描述，形成适用于多源联合限制与特定场搜寻的参数化方案。

Conclusion: 该半不可知参数化在无需选定具体理论的前提下提供了统一描述，便于结合多个源获得联合约束并开展针对性搜寻。

Abstract: We introduce a semi-agnostic modeling framework for black hole binary inspirals beyond general relativity that fixes the field content without selecting a particular theory. We expand in the post-Newtonian order and in powers of $l/L$, where $l$ is the characteristic length-scale that controls deviations from General Relativity and $L$ is the characteristic length-scale of the system. We focus on the case of a massless scalar field coupled with the metric, at leading post-Newtonian order. Taking into account the scaling with $l/L$ of both the interaction terms and the quantities that characterise the sources, we find that the inspiral can be described in terms of a small number of universal parameters. Such a parametrisation is suitable for obtaining combined bounds from multiple sources and performing searches for specific fields.

</details>


### [151] [Fresh Look at Shift Symmetric Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36063)
*Rodrigo Calderon,Eric V. Linder*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明移位对称 Horndeski 引力（shift symmetric Horndeski gravity）可穿越幽灵分界（phantom divide，w=-1），并通过动力学系统分析（dynamical systems analysis）揭示两种穿越机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移位对称 Horndeski 理论结构简单、提法良定且可抵御量子修正（quantum corrections），但传统观点认为此类理论无法穿越 w=-1，本文旨在检验并推翻这一成见。

Method: 同时给出解析与数值证明，并对模型族开展详细的动力学系统分析，刻画无幽灵（ghost free）与稳定区域的演化，识别不动点（fixed point）与动力学吸引子（dynamical attractor）结构，并推导 w=-1 曲线与这些相空间结构的相互作用。

Result: 分析表明移位对称 Horndeski 宇宙学能够穿越幽灵分界，且该穿越由相空间结构给出两种不同机制，同时保持移位对称性（shift symmetry）。

Conclusion: 移位对称 Horndeski 引力可提供穿越 w=-1 的可行宇宙学方案，挑战了既有认知，并为无幽灵、稳定的穿越情形提供了相空间判据。

Abstract: Shift symmetric Horndeski gravity can be a simple, well posed theory with protection against quantum corrections. Overturning conventional thought that such a theory cannot cross the phantom divide ($w=-1$) we present both analytic and numerical proof that it can. We carry out a detailed dynamical systems analysis showing the evolution of ghost free and stable regions within the model family, identify the fixed point and dynamical attractor structure, and derive the interaction of the $w=-1$ curve with these phase space structures. This reveals two mechanisms for cosmologies crossing the phantom divide while preserving shift symmetry.

</details>


### [152] [Scalarized Einstein-Maxwell black holes in external scalar fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36085)
*Lang Cheng,Guangzhou Guo,Yuhan Li,Peng Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论（Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory）中构造了置于给定外部标量场（external scalar field）下的静态黑洞（static black holes），刻画其存在域（existence domain）及标量荷（scalar charge）对外场的依赖，并进一步推广至双星系统（binaries）中的相互标量诱导（mutual scalar induction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注无外源时黑洞的标量化（scalarization）阈值，而外部标量场对黑洞解的存在域与标量荷分布的影响尚不清晰，双星环境下伴星生成标量场的相互效应亦缺乏系统分析。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中求解给定外部标量场下的静态黑洞解，聚焦正标量荷分支，映射其存在域并计算标量荷随外场的变化；随后通过领头阶伴星生成标量场（leading companion-generated scalar fields）将单体响应耦合，考察双星中的相互诱导。

Result: 正向外场可在零源标量化阈值（zero-source scalarization threshold）以下为 Reissner-Nordström（RN）黑洞诱导出标量荷，并扩展允许的荷质比（charge-to-mass）范围；负向外场则引入有限的存在下边界，并允许相同外部条件下存在不同黑洞解。双星中，同号标量荷的双星其标量荷不必随间距减小而增大；异号情形下部分双星存在多重平衡（multiple equilibria），无法沿原平衡分支延续至更小间距。

Conclusion: 外部标量场可显著改变黑洞的存在域与标量荷结构，并使孤立 RN 黑洞在双星中产生相互标量诱导；伴星诱导效应可导致非单调的标量荷行为与平衡分支的不连续性。

Abstract: We construct static black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory under prescribed external scalar fields, mapping their existence domain and determining how their scalar charges vary with the external field. Focusing on solutions with positive scalar charge, we find that a positive external field induces scalar charge in Reissner-Nordström (RN) black holes below the zero-source scalarization threshold and extends the allowed charge-to-mass range. A negative external field instead introduces a finite lower existence boundary and permits distinct black holes under identical external conditions. Coupling these isolated responses through the leading companion-generated scalar fields, we find mutual scalar induction in binaries whose components are RN black holes in isolation. For binaries with scalar charges of the same sign, their scalar charges need not increase as the separation decreases, while some binaries with opposite scalar charges admit multiple equilibria and cannot continue along their original equilibrium branches to smaller separations.

</details>


### [153] [Improved Gauge Conditions for Large Expansion Cosmological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36123)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种新的规范条件（gauge condition），用于在具有非平凡空间拓扑（non-trivial spatial topologies）的宇宙时空上对爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equation）进行长时间数值演化，使空间体积可膨胀超过一百万倍仍保持可靠演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有非平凡空间拓扑的宇宙学时空上进行爱因斯坦方程的长时间数值演化存在困难，亟需一种能使此类流形（manifolds）上演化稳定、可靠的新规范条件。

Method: 提出新的规范条件，并在均匀且各向同性（homogeneous and isotropic）的初始数据上进行测试，选取三种空间拓扑示例流形：Sixth-Turn space、透镜空间 L(5,2)（lens space）以及 Seifert 纤维空间 SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)]（Seifert fiber space）。

Result: 新规范条件使得在这些流形上进行可靠数值演化成为可能，演化过程中空间体积增大了超过一百万倍。

Conclusion: 新规范条件适用于非平凡空间拓扑宇宙时空的长时间数值演化，并在三种示例拓扑上验证有效。

Abstract: This study develops a new gauge condition for performing long duration numerical evolutions of Einstein's equation on cosmological spacetimes with non-trivial spatial topologies. This new gauge makes it possible to perform reliable numerical evolutions on these manifolds in which the spatial volumes become more than one million times larger. This new condition was tested for this study by evolving homogeneous and isotropic initial data on exemplar manifolds with three spatial topologies: Sixth-Turn space, the lens space L(5,2), and the Seifert fiber space SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)].

</details>


### [154] [Unified Dynamics of Black-Hole Interiors: From Geometric to Scalar-Kinetic Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36129)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一动力学图景，将事件视界处的小振幅标量输入与标量–Kasner内部结构相连接：面积收缩（area contraction）是驱动从几何主导转向标量动能主导的共同物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立事件视界处微扰标量输入与黑洞内部标量–Kasner（scalar–Kasner）几何之间的普适联系，解释内视界（inner horizon）丧失的动力学起源。

Method: 在边界支撑的定常族（boundary-supported stationary families）中分析几何项与标量动能项的相对权重；并对Schwarzschild型分支及四维定常轴对称Kerr–Newman内部（含Reissner–Nordström与Kerr极限）进行全耦合数值求解。

Result: Schwarzschild型分支中，面积收缩抑制相对曲率贡献，使标量动能项主导而梯度保持较小；Kerr–Newman型分支中，面积收缩使解趋近拟Cauchy视界零点，标量梯度被强烈放大，随后经狭窄过渡进入动能主导。动能项与几何项的相消使某度规诊断量出现正极小值，从而动态解释内视界丧失。

Conclusion: 面积收缩是两分支共有的物理机制，而拟Cauchy视界零点决定梯度放大的强度与相变局域位置；数值解与解析图景一致。

Abstract: We identify a unified dynamical picture connecting small-amplitude scalar input at the event horizon to scalar--Kasner interiors. Within boundary-supported stationary families, area contraction drives the transition from geometric to scalar kinetic dominance. For the Schwarzschild-like branch, contraction suppresses the relative curvature contribution, so the scalar kinetic term becomes dominant while the gradient remains small. For the Kerr--Newman-like branch, the scalar gradient is strongly amplified as area contraction carries the solution toward the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero, followed by a narrow transition to scalar kinetic dominance. Cancellation between the kinetic and geometric terms produces a positive minimum of a metric diagnostic, explaining inner-horizon loss dynamically. Fully coupled numerical solutions for the Schwarzschild-like branch and four-dimensional stationary axisymmetric Kerr--Newman interiors, including the Reissner--Nordström and Kerr limits, support this picture. Area contraction provides the common physical mechanism, while the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero determines the strength of gradient amplification and the localization of the transition.

</details>


### [155] [Non-Perturbative Approach to Gravitational Particle Production in the Inflation-Kination Transition: Surveying a Wide Range of the Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36137)
*Amirsalar Nikandish,Shiva Rostam Zadeh,Nematollah Riazi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在暴胀—动能主导（inflation-kination）背景转变中，引力粒子产生（gravitational particle production, GPP）作为重加热（reheating）通道的作用，通过修正非微扰绝热真空方法同时追踪场质量与非共形引力耦合常数的影响，并在三维参数空间中扫描得到最高重加热温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在暴胀模型中保持普适性，需考察不依赖具体暴胀模型的引力重加热通道；已有工作虽讨论了超重场质量对重加热温度的影响，但未考虑引力耦合常数非共形取值对GPP速率的效应。

Method: 采用文献中提出的连续标度因子函数（scale-factor function）描述暴胀到动能主导的转变；以具有Ricci—物质引力耦合的超重旁观标量场（spectator scalar field）的引力激发作为GPP来源；在微扰方法之外，修正基于绝热真空（adiabatic vacuum）的非微扰方法，使场质量与引力耦合常数两种效应均可追踪，并配合一个可解模型的解析方法作平行对照。

Result: 重加热温度受三个参数控制：场的裸质量（bare mass）、引力耦合常数与转变时间（transition time）；通过对三维参数空间的广泛数值扫描，分别及联合考察各参数影响后，得到所允许的最高重加热温度，该温度足以使宇宙进入热大爆炸（Hot Big-Bang）时代。

Conclusion: 引力粒子产生可作为暴胀—动能主导转变中有效的重加热通道，修正后的非微扰方法能同时纳入质量与非共形耦合效应；解析部分仅作为数学工具提供独立验证，不构成主要结论依据。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the gravitational particle production (GPP) as a natural reheating channel in time-dependent backgrounds. We focus on reheating in an inflation-kination background. To maintain generality across inflationary models, we use a continuous scale-factor function that produces this transition and has been proposed in the literature. In this scenario, the gravitational reheating channel arises from the gravitational excitation of a superheavy spectator scalar field with Ricci-matter gravitational coupling. Besides all the perturbative approaches, a recently proposed non-perturbative one that uses the notion of the adiabatic vacuum has also been applied to the case of superheavy particles. Although the effect of the mass of the field on the reheating temperature has been investigated, the impact of the non-conformal values of the gravitational coupling constant on the GPP rate is not considered. Hence, we modify the non-perturbative approach in such a way that both effects can be traced. Three parameters affect the reheating temperature in this case: the bare mass of the field, the gravitational coupling constant, and the transition time. In this paper, the effect of each of these parameters alone on the resultant reheating temperature, as well as the combined effects of these parameters, is studied. Taking into account all involved parameters and surveying a wide range of the 3D parameter space, the highest reheating temperature possible is obtained. All of the aforementioned investigations are done based on numerical calculations, and we obtain that the highest possible reheating temperature is quite sufficient for the universe to enter the Hot Big-Bang era. For the sake of completeness, we study the GPP in such a transition using an analytical method in an independent, parallel, solvable model. This analysis is presented only as a mathematical tool.

</details>


### [156] [On the Interpretation of Tests of the Horizon Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36162)
*Selma Vangstein,Christopher J. Moore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出引力波对视界面积律（horizon area law）的检验依赖于假设广义相对论（general relativity, GR）成立的模型，因而具有模型依赖性；通过模拟并合玻色星（boson stars），证明无视界的奇异致密天体（exotic compact objects）可模仿并合黑洞，并以约92%的置信度通过面积律检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波检验虽为广义相对论提供了有力的一致性检验（consistency check），但其解释依赖假定GR成立的模型，因此结果本质上具有模型依赖性，需要考察非黑洞天体能否同样通过此类检验。

Method: 采用并合玻色星的数值模拟作为具体反例（counterexample），考察奇异致密天体是否能通过视界面积律的引力波检验。

Result: 模拟显示，奇异致密天体能够模仿并合黑洞，并在完全不存在事件视界（event horizons）的情况下，以高达约92%的置信度通过面积律检验。

Conclusion: 基于引力波、并以GR模型为前提的面积律检验不足以确证事件视界的存在，其结论须谨慎解读，可能将非黑洞致密天体误判为黑洞。

Abstract: This Letter investigates gravitational-wave tests of the horizon area law. Although they provide a striking consistency check of general relativity (GR), these tests rely on models that assume GR, making their interpretation inherently model dependent. We show with a specific example---simulations of merging boson stars---that exotic compact objects can mimic merging black holes and pass an area-law test at a confidence as high as $\sim 92\%$ despite not even having event horizons.

</details>


### [157] [The Symbiosis of Vainshtein and Chameleon Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36166)
*Sergi Sirera,Tessa Baker,Clare Burrage,James Hallam,Krishna Naidoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次研究宇宙结构中 Vainshtein 屏蔽（Vainshtein screening）与 Chameleon 屏蔽（Chameleon screening）共存时的相互影响，发现两者相互作用可修正各自单独的屏蔽行为，并改变宇宙学引力检验的可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏蔽机制（screening）在局部隐藏第五力（fifth forces），使修改引力（modified gravity）理论保持可行性；而现实暗能量（dark-energy）模型可同时激活多种屏蔽机制，但这种相互作用此前被忽视。

Method: 基于宇宙结构（cosmic structures）中的理论分析，考察 Vainshtein 与 Chameleon 两种屏蔽机制共存情形下的行为。

Result: 发现三点：(i) Chameleon 机制解决了长期存在的 Galileon 空洞病理（Galileon void pathology）；(ii) 它抑制或消除大质量源周围的 Vainshtein 半径（Vainshtein radius）；(iii) Vainshtein 项则缩小 Chameleon 的屏蔽核心（screened core）。

Conclusion: 两种屏蔽机制的共存重塑了宇宙学引力检验所依赖的核心可观测特征（observable signatures），表明多机制相互作用对修改引力的宇宙学检验具有实质影响。

Abstract: We present the first study of coexisting Vainshtein and Chameleon screening in cosmic structures. Screening hides fifth forces locally, keeping modified gravity viable. Realistic dark-energy models can activate several mechanisms at once, yet this interplay has been overlooked. We find that (i) the Chameleon resolves the long-standing Galileon void pathology; (ii) it suppresses or eliminates the Vainshtein radius around massive sources; and (iii) the Vainshtein term shrinks the Chameleon's screened core. These effects reshape the observable signatures central to cosmological tests of gravity.

</details>


### [158] [Testing Conformal Weyl Gravity with Plasma Modified Black Hole Shadows and EHT Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36562)
*Zhichen Guan,Connor McMillin,Owen Gartlan,William Horner,Leo Rodriguez,Shanshan Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在四阶共形外尔引力（conformal Weyl gravity, CWG）中研究浸没于等离子体（plasma）的带电旋转 de Sitter 黑洞阴影，旨在区分等离子体色散环境效应与引力理论本身效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是分离两类观测效应：等离子体传播环境造成的效应与底层引力理论（CWG 与广义相对论 GR 差异）造成的效应，避免将环境效应误判为引力理论信号。

Method: 在可分离 Hamilton-Jacobi 等离子体模型下，推导径向与角向光子势（photon potential）、赤道面有限距离偏折角（finite-distance deflection angle），并因 CWG 时空渐近非平坦而采用有限观测者阴影边界（finite-observer shadow boundary）。

Result: 等离子体强度增大通常减小阴影半径、有符号偏折角与能量发射峰值（CWG 与 GR 均如此）；电荷 q 则表现出更强理论依赖性：增大 q 使 CWG 阴影变大而 GR 阴影变小。有限观测者无量纲阴影半径与 Sgr A*、M87* 的 EHT 推断尺寸区间进行了比较。

Conclusion: 等离子体效应可模仿部分阴影尺寸偏移，但 CWG 对电荷的独特响应为区分共形引力特征与环境传播效应提供了潜在观测途径。

Abstract: We study the shadows of a charged rotating de Sitter solution in fourth order conformal Weyl gravity (CWG) immersed in plasma and analyze the joint charge-plasma constraints. Our main goal is to distinguish observational effects caused by the dispersive plasma environment from those caused by the underlying gravitational theory. Within a separable Hamilton-Jacobi plasma model, we derive the radial and angular photon potentials, the equatorial finite-distance deflection angle, and the finite-observer shadow boundary due to the asymptotically non-flat nature of the CWG spacetime. Our results separate two effects. Increasing the plasma strength generally reduces the shadow radius, the signed deflection angle, and the energy emission peak in both CWG and GR. Charge ($q$) produces a stronger theory dependence: for the representative parameters studied, increasing $q$ enlarges the CWG shadow but reduces the GR shadow. The lensing behavior independently supports the geometry-dependent response found from the photon region and shadow observables. We further compare the finite observer dimensionless shadow radius with EHT-inferred size intervals for Sgr~A$^*$ and M87$^*$. Our results indicate that plasma effects can mimic part of the shadow size shift, but the distinct charge response of CWG offers a potential observational route for separating conformal gravity signatures from environmental propagation effects.

</details>


### [159] [Reconstruction of the Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36695)
*Nathan Madsen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在因果集（causal set）框架下，于给定维数与采样密度条件下构造近似正规坐标（normal coordinates）、估计平行移动（parallel transport），并仅由因果序与元素计数重建完整黎曼张量（Riemann tensor），同时证明曲率误差上界随采样密度增大而一致趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果集理论以局部有限偏序集（locally finite partially ordered set）作为时空物理的离散基础，但需与连续物理衔接，即恢复广义相对论（general relativity）所用几何量并控制离散性引入的误差。

Method: 在指定维数与采样密度的前提下，利用因果序关系与元素计数构造近似正规坐标、估计平行移动，并据此重建完整黎曼张量；针对嵌入固定光滑时空且满足所述均匀计数假设（uniform counting assumptions）的样本进行论证。

Result: 对满足条件的嵌入样本，证明了曲率误差的一致上界（uniform curvature error bound），且该上界随采样密度提高而趋于零。

Conclusion: 结果表明，因果集的离散序与计数信息在稠密采样极限下可一致地恢复连续曲率，为因果集与连续时空几何之间的对应提供了定量误差控制。

Abstract: A causal set is a locally finite partially ordered set. Causal set theory proposes such discrete structures as a basis for spacetime physics. Connecting this description with continuum physics requires recovering the geometric quantities used in general relativity and controlling the errors introduced by discreteness. Here, with dimension and sampling density specified, we construct approximate normal coordinates, estimate parallel transport, and recover the full Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts. For samples embedded in a fixed smooth spacetime and satisfying stated uniform counting assumptions, we prove a uniform curvature error bound that tends to zero as the sampling density increases.

</details>


### [160] [Improved constraints on higher-derivative effective field theory extensions of general relativity with GW230627_015337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36728)
*Tao Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用 SEOBNRv5HM_ROM 波形并纳入高阶模态与完整旋近-并合-铃宕信号，对三个宇称偶（parity-even）高曲率算符耦合进行 Fisher 矩阵筛选与全贝叶斯分析，结果均与广义相对论（general relativity, GR）一致，给出约 10–20 km 量级的特征长度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）观测是强场区检验广义相对论的有力手段；在有效场论（effective field theory, EFT）扩展中，高曲率算符会修改双星动力学并对旋近（inspiral）相位产生修正，需要借助现有观测对其耦合给出限制。

Method: 考虑两个宇称偶三次曲率耦合与一个四次曲率耦合，其领头相位修正分别进入 5PN、6PN 与 8PN 阶；通过理论无关的灵活框架将这些修正写入 SEOBNRv5HM_ROM 波形的各个多极（multipole）中，从而可利用高阶模态与完整旋近-并合-铃宕（inspiral-merger-ringdown）信号。先从公开 LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 目录的 273 个事件母样本中选取 92 个做 Fisher 矩阵分析，再对预报约束最紧的 GW230627_015337 进行全贝叶斯（Bayesian）分析。

Result: 后验分布与 GR 一致，三个耦合在 90% 置信度下均与零相容；特征长度尺度（characteristic length scale）的单侧 90% 上限分别为 17.1、11.1 与 18.3 km。三个 EFT 模型相对 GR 的对数贝叶斯因子（log Bayes factor）介于 −5.49 至 −2.51，在所用先验下支持 GR。

Conclusion: 所得限制探测到 10–20 km 量级的高曲率长度尺度，数值上比以往代表性的旋近与铃宕（ringdown）限制更紧；但模型与分析假设的差异应在比较时予以考虑。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations of binary black holes provide a powerful probe of general relativity (GR) in the strong-field regime. In effective field theory (EFT) extensions of GR, higher-curvature operators modify the binary dynamics and introduce corrections to the inspiral phase. In this work, we consider two parity-even cubic-curvature couplings and one quartic-curvature coupling, whose leading phase corrections enter at 5PN, 6PN, and 8PN order, respectively. We incorporate these corrections into the individual multipoles of the \texttt{SEOBNRv5HM\_ROM} waveform using the flexible theory-independent framework, allowing higher-order modes and the full inspiral-merger-ringdown signal to be used in the analysis. We first perform a Fisher-matrix analysis of 92 binary-black-hole events selected from a parent sample of 273 events in the public LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA catalogs. GW230627\_015337 provides the tightest forecast constraints on all three EFT couplings and is therefore selected for a full Bayesian analysis. The posterior distributions are consistent with GR, with all three couplings consistent with zero at 90\% credibility. The corresponding one-sided 90\% upper limits on the characteristic length scales are \(17.1\), \(11.1\), and \(18.3\,\mathrm{km}\). The log Bayes factors of the three EFT models relative to GR range from \(-5.49\) to \(-2.51\), favoring GR under the adopted priors. These bounds probe higher-curvature length scales at the level of \(10\)--\(20\,\mathrm{km}\) and are numerically tighter than representative previous inspiral and ringdown constraints, although differences in the models and analysis assumptions should be taken into account.

</details>


### [161] [Impact of deformation parameters on the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansions of Schwarzschild-AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36730)
*Faisal Javed,A. Eid,Arfa Waseem,Jiafeng Guo,Abdelmalek Bouzenada,Sulaman Shaukat,Farruh Atamurotov,N. Mustapha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了变形史瓦西-反德西特黑洞（deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole）的灰体因子（greybody factor）与焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀（Joule-Thomson expansion），通过构造类薛定谔波动方程与等焓曲线，揭示了变形参数对有效势及热力学行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察变形参数、质量与角动量等物理参数如何影响无质量标量场的吸收与散射，以及变形黑洞在焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀下的热力学特征。

Method: 借助乌龟坐标（tortoise coordinates）将克莱因-戈尔登方程（Klein-Gordon equation）转化为类薛定谔波动方程（Schrödinger-like wave equation），推导精确有效势（effective potential）并分析其参数依赖行为；同时利用焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀分析黑洞热力学并绘制等焓曲线（isenthalpic curves）。

Result: 增大变形参数（deformation parameter）会增强有效势，显著影响无质量标量场的吸收与散射，且其存在使史瓦西黑洞有效势大幅提升；近视界处高频波更易穿透势垒、低频波被反射。等焓曲线表明存在由压强与温度变化控制的加热区与冷却区，增大变形参数与黑洞质量会提升等焓曲线。

Conclusion: 变形参数在灰体因子与焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀两方面均产生显著效应，为变形史瓦西-AdS黑洞的散射与热力学性质提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: This paper investigates the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansion of deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole models. We constructed the Klein-Gordon equation into a Schrödinger-like wave equation using tortoise coordinates and derived the exact effective potential. Also, we analyzed its behavior under varying physical parameters, including deformation parameters, mass and angular momentum. In this case, our results illustrate that increasing the deformation parameter enhances the effective potential, significantly impacting the absorption and scattering of massless scalar fields. It is noted that the Schwarzschild black hole effective potential is greatly increased when the deformation parameter is present. Near the event horizon, we found that waves behave differently, with higher-frequency waves more easily penetrating potential obstacles and lower-frequency waves bouncing off them. Furthermore, we investigated the thermodynamic characteristics of black holes using the Joule-Thomson expansion, detecting isenthalpic curves that indicate heating and cooling zones controlled by pressure and temperature changes. In this context, we tested the influence of deformation black hole parameters; the isenthalpic curves are enhanced by increasing the deformation parameters and the black hole mass.

</details>


### [162] [Novel topological subclasses of a magnetic black hole in causal nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36796)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-jiang Yin,Yu-Die Wan,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将黑洞态视为热力学参数空间中的拓扑缺陷（topological defects），利用广义离壳自由能与Duan的φ-映射拓扑流理论研究爱因斯坦引力耦合因果非线性电动力学（causal nonlinear electrodynamics）下的静态渐近平直磁黑洞，识别出包括新子类W̄^{0+↔1-}在内的四类缺陷曲线，并揭示出由物理视界边界诱发的热力学拓扑相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过拓扑缺陷视角刻画黑洞态在热力学参数空间中的结构，检验并拓展已有的热力学拓扑分类方案（如W^{0+↔1-}类与W̄^{1-}子类），并厘清物理视界边界在拓扑分类中的作用。

Method: 在正则系综（canonical ensemble）中考察单视界物理扇区，采用广义离壳自由能（generalized off-shell free energy）与Duan的φ-映射拓扑流理论（Duan's φ-mapping topological current theory）对缺陷曲线进行分类，并追踪辅助逆温度变化下的缺陷演化。

Result: 识别出四类代表性缺陷曲线轮廓，实现了 conjectured 的W^{0+↔1-}类与W̄^{1-}子类，并发现新的子类W̄^{0+↔1-}，其特征为成对的产生与湮灭点且最内与最外分支不稳定。随辅助逆温度升高，系统由两个平衡态演化为三个再回到一个，不稳定的小黑洞缺陷经物理下边界进入，使全局拓扑数由W=0变为W=-1。

Conclusion: 该边界诱发的拓扑数变化标志着一种热力学拓扑相变（thermodynamic topological phase transition）；相同的逆温度端点行为与小到大黑洞的绕数序列（winding-number sequence）可伴随不同的缺陷演化，凸显物理视界边界在热力学拓扑分类中的关键作用。该因果非线性电动力学黑洞族可作为发现更多缺陷结构与热力学拓扑相变的平台。

Abstract: By treating black-hole states as topological defects in thermodynamic parameter space, we investigate a static, asymptotically flat magnetic black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to causal nonlinear electrodynamics. Using the generalized off-shell free energy and Duan's $φ$-mapping topological current theory, we analyze the physical single-horizon sector in the canonical ensemble. We identify four representative defect-curve profiles, including realizations of the conjectured $W^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$ class and $\overline{W}^{1-}$ subclass, as well as a new thermodynamic topological subclass, $\overline{W}^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$. This new subclass features paired generation and annihilation points, with unstable innermost and outermost branches. As the auxiliary inverse temperature increases, the system evolves from two equilibrium states to three and then back to one, with an unstable small-black-hole defect entering through the physical lower boundary and changing the global topological number from $W=0$ to $W=-1$. This boundary-induced change signals a thermodynamic topological phase transition. These results show that the same inverse-temperature endpoint behavior and winding-number sequence from small to large black holes can accompany different defect evolution, highlighting the role of the physical horizon boundary in thermodynamic topological classification. More broadly, this causal nonlinear-electrodynamics black-hole family provides a setting for discovering further defect structures and thermodynamic topological phase transitions.

</details>


### [163] [Realistic neutron stars in the constant-Q branch of f(Q) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36814)
*Debadri Bhattacharjee,Pradip Kumar Chattopadhyay,P. H. R. S. Moraes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）的常$Q$分支下，构建三种非线性$f(Q)$模型并结合真实状态方程，系统计算中子星（neutron star）的静水平衡与整体结构，揭示最大质量与半径约束之间的现象学互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非度规标量$Q$主导的对称远平行引力为修改引力提供了新途径，但其所预言的中子星结构与稳定性，尤其与多信使观测（multimessenger observations）的相容性，尚缺乏系统检验。

Method: 在度规-仿射（metric-affine）表述下采用保持星体中心正则性的常$Q$分支（$Q=Q_*$），提出Born-Infeld型、Hu-Sawicki类比型与Tsujikawa启发型三种非线性$f(Q)$模型；结合GPPVA(DD2)与SLy4统一状态方程（equation of state）数值求解修改的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程，并用Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov判据评估稳定性。

Result: Born-Infeld型与Hu-Sawicki类比型在控制参数增大时平滑回归广义相对论（general relativity）极限，低质量天体（$M<2.0\,M_\odot$）半径与X射线双星及GW170817约束相符，但最大质量分别被限制在$2.43\,M_\odot$(DD2)与$2.06\,M_\odot$(SLy4)以下；Tsujikawa启发型显著增强有效引力耦合，可支持约$3.36\,M_\odot$的大质量致密天体，为GW190814的$2.59_{-0.09}^{+0.08}\,M_\odot$次级天体提供可能解释，但会高估典型低质量中子星半径。

Conclusion: 常$Q$框架内不同非线性$f(Q)$模型在最大质量与半径约束上呈现互补行为，表明多信使观测对限制非线性非度规引力具有重要价值。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium and global structure of neutron stars within symmetric teleparallel gravity, where gravitational interactions are mediated by the non-metricity scalar $Q$. Working within the metric-affine formulation, we adopt the regular, constant-$Q$ branch ($Q = Q_*$) of $f(Q)$ gravity, which preserves regularity at the stellar centre while maintaining explicit dependence on the non-linear functional form through $f(Q_*)$ and its derivatives. We propose three novel non-linear $f(Q)$ extensions: (i) a Born-Infeld-inspired model, (ii) a Hu--Sawicki analogue, and (iii) a Tsujikawa-inspired model. Incorporating state-of-the-art realistic unified equations of state, namely GPPVA(DD2) and SLy4, we numerically solve the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations and evaluate stellar stability via the Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov criterion. The Born-Infeld and Hu-Sawicki analogues smoothly recover the general relativistic limit as their control parameters increase, predicting stellar radii for low-mass objects ($M < 2.0\,M_\odot$) that match observational constraints from X-ray binaries and GW170817; however, their maximum masses remain capped below $2.43\,M_\odot$ (DD2) and $2.06\,M_\odot$ (SLy4). Conversely, the Tsujikawa-inspired model significantly stiffens the effective gravitational coupling, accommodating massive compact objects up to $\sim 3.36\,M_\odot$ and offering a viable physical origin for the $2.59_{-0.09}^{+0.08}\,M_\odot$ secondary object in GW190814, albeit at the expense of overpredicting radii for typical low-mass neutron stars. These contrasting behaviours demonstrate a key phenomenological complementarity between maximum mass and radius bounds within the constant-$Q$ framework, highlighting the utility of multimessenger observations in constraining non-linear non-metricity gravity.

</details>


### [164] [Primordial black holes from a type-IIB/F theory inflaton potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36863)
*Chitrak Sarkar,Amitava Choudhuri,Buddhadeb Ghosh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在IIB型/F型弦理论的大体积场景中构建带高斯凸起的暴胀子势，产生超慢滚阶段，从而在k~1.12×10^9 Mpc^-1处增强曲率功率谱，得到可满足现有限制的原初黑洞丰度，并给出其霍金温度与蒸发时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索在弦论大体积场景（large volume scenario）所构造的暴胀势下能否形成原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs），并检验其丰度是否与现有观测限制相容。

Method: 方法为在IIB型/F型弦理论中同时采用微扰与非微扰修正稳定全部Kähler模（Kähler moduli），并在其中一个Kähler模中引入局部高斯凸起（Gaussian bump）以修改模空间势；随后用Mukhanov-Sasaki方程并配以Bunch-Davies初始条件数值求解曲率功率谱演化，并以Press-Schechter形式计算PBH丰度。

Result: 结果表明，高斯凸起在k~1.12×10^9 Mpc^-1附近产生可持续的曲率功率谱增强；对质量10^-6 M_⊙、阈值δ_c=0.439的情形，丰度与微引力透镜（microlensing）限制相容；霍金温度（Hawking temperature）约为2.78×10^-15 GeV，蒸发时间约8.5×10^38 Gyr。

Conclusion: 结论是弦论大体积场景中经高斯凸起修饰的暴胀势可支持PBH形成，且曲率功率谱增强落在SKA观测的敏感区域内，提示标量诱导引力波（scalar-induced gravitational waves）有望在未来观测中被探测。

Abstract: We study the possibility of the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs) using an inflaton potential constructed by stabilizing all Kähler moduli employing both the perturbative and non-perturbative corrections in the large volume scenario in type-IIB/F string theory. We further introduce a small perturbation in one of the Kähler moduli that modifies the existing potential in the field space. The effective correction in the moduli space is made by introducing a localised Gaussian bump, which induces an ultra-slow-roll feature in the inflaton potential during the early cosmological inflation. The evolution of the amplitude of the curvature power spectrum is solved numerically by using the Mukhanov-Sasaki equation, with Bunch-Davies initial conditions. The inclusion of the Gaussian bump produces a sustainable enhancement in the amplitude of curvature power spectra around $k\sim 1.12 \times 10^{9} Mpc^{-1}$ for the formation of PBHs. The abundance of PBHs ($f_{PBH}$) is calculated using the Press-Schechter formalism for different values of critical density contrast and presented in the background of contemporary $f_{PBH}$ bounds and checked for viability with existing microlensing bounds, corresponding to a mass value $10^{-6}M_\odot$ for $δ$ threshold value $δ_c=0.439$. The Hawking temperature and evaporation time are also determined for these black holes and found to be of the order of $2.78\times 10^{-15}$ GeV and $8.5\times10^{38}$ Gyr, respectively. The enhancement of curvature power spectra lies in the sensitivity region of the SKA observations, suggesting an indication that scalar-induced gravity waves may be probed in future observations.

</details>


### [165] [Self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson Solitons in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36923)
*Kei-ichi Maeda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文在弯曲时空中以局域惯性系描述自引力薛定谔–泊松（Schrödinger–Poisson, SP）孤子，引入由时空曲率决定的等效潮汐势，并计算其沿Kerr黑洞圆轨道运动时的四极潮汐响应与引力波特征应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自引力玻色子孤子在黑洞时空中的潮汐响应及其引力波信号缺乏系统研究，需要把背景时空曲率效应纳入SP方程的框架。

Method: 在弱场、非相对论极限下，于孤子中心的局域惯性系（local inertial frame）中将背景时空效应写成等效潮汐势（effective tidal potential）并加入SP方程；应用于沿Kerr黑洞赤道圆测地线运动的孤子，将潮汐场分解为定常与含时四极分量，用潮汐形变率（tidal deformability）与无量纲Love数刻画四极响应，并估算形变旋转孤子的引力辐射。

Result: 潮汐场导致孤子形变并产生质量四极矩；据此估算了代表性孤子尺寸与轨道半径下的特征应变（characteristic strain），与规划中的空间引力波探测器灵敏度曲线比较，识别出估计应变可能落入探测灵敏区间的参数范围。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究黑洞时空中自引力玻色子孤子的潮汐响应及其潜在引力波特征提供了系统基础。

Abstract: We formulate a description of self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson (SP) solitons in curved spacetime using a local inertial frame centered on the soliton. In the weak-field and non-relativistic regime, the effect of the background spacetime is incorporated into the SP equations through an effective tidal potential determined by the spacetime curvature. We apply this framework to a soliton moving along a circular equatorial geodesic around a Kerr black hole. The tidal field contains stationary and time-dependent quadrupolar components, which induce a deformation of the soliton and an associated mass quadrupole moment. We formulate the quadrupolar response in terms of the tidal deformability and the dimensionless Love number. We then estimate the gravitational radiation associated with the rotating deformed soliton and evaluate the corresponding characteristic strain for representative soliton sizes and orbital radii. A comparison with representative sensitivity curves of planned space-based gravitational-wave detectors identifies parameter regimes in which the estimated characteristic strain may lie within their sensitivity ranges. Our framework provides a systematic basis for investigating the tidal response of self-gravitating bosonic solitons in black-hole spacetimes and their potential gravitational-wave signatures.

</details>


### [166] [Can One Hear the Absence of a Singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37028)
*Matteo Della Rocca,Paolo Pani,Andrea P. Sanna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 对一大类正则黑洞（regular black holes），其高泛音极限（large-overtone limit）的准正规模谱（quasinormal-mode spectrum）与史瓦西黑洞不连续地不同：频率实部渐近趋于零而非非零常数，即使正则化参数任意小。该结果经单值性方法（monodromy method）解析建立并数值验证，被解释为黑洞谱不稳定性（spectral instability）的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力（quantum gravity）的核心预期之一是解决广义相对论（General Relativity）预言的曲率奇点（curvature singularities），可能产生视界内部正则的黑洞几何。论文旨在考察这类奇点消除是否在黑洞可观测谱中留下可辨识印记。

Method: 针对包含若干已知解在内的广泛正则黑洞类，通过系统化的单值性方法（monodromy method）解析推导高泛音极限下的准正规模（quasinormal modes），并进行数值验证。

Result: 高泛音极限下，准正规模频率的实部渐近消失，而广义相对论及其他预言曲率奇点的理论中该实部通常趋于非零常数；这一差异是间断性的，在正则化参数（regularization parameter）任意小时仍存在。

Conclusion: 渐近黑洞谱保留了奇点消除的独特印记；该现象被解释为黑洞谱不稳定性（black-hole spectral instability）的表现，即对真空广义相对论的任意小偏离会在高泛音谱中产生非微扰（nonperturbative）改变。

Abstract: One of the central expectations of quantum gravity is the resolution of the curvature singularities predicted by General Relativity, potentially giving rise to effective black-hole geometries that are regular inside the horizon. We show that, for a very broad class of regular black holes including several known solutions, the quasinormal-mode spectrum in the large-overtone limit differs discontinuously from that of a Schwarzschild black hole, even when the regularization parameter is arbitrarily small. The real part of the quasinormal frequencies vanishes asymptotically, rather than approaching a nonzero constant, as generically occurs in General Relativity and other theories predicting black holes with curvature singularities. The asymptotic black-hole spectrum thus retains a distinctive imprint of singularity resolution. We establish these results analytically through a systematic formulation of the monodromy method and confirm them numerically. We interpret this phenomenon as a manifestation of black-hole spectral instability, whereby arbitrarily small deviations from vacuum General Relativity produce nonperturbative changes in the large-overtone spectrum.

</details>


### [167] [Genesis in Minimally Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37063)
*Alexander Ganz,Paul Martens,Shinji Mukohyama,Pavel Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在II型最小修改引力（type-II minimally modified gravity）框架内构建了创世（Genesis）情景：背景解在过去测地完备（past geodesically complete）并渐近趋于闵可夫斯基时空，标量微扰无鬼（ghost-free）、无紫外梯度不稳定性，数值演化给出近标度不变、略红倾的标量谱与蓝倾且被强烈压低的张量谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是在避免初始大爆炸奇点的替代方案中，于修改引力框架下实现创世情景，同时满足物质扇区的零能条件（null-energy condition）并保证微扰稳定性。

Method: 采用II型最小修改引力构造背景解并使哈勃膨胀率增长；分析标量微扰的鬼（ghost）与紫外梯度不稳定性，并进行含超视界增长（superhorizon growth）的数值演化；借助渐近归一化正则的物质涨落 δχ 考察背景相关立方耦合与残余相互作用。

Result: 标量微扰无鬼且无紫外梯度不稳定性，尽管声速平方 c_R² 在有限红外的傅里叶区间、有限时间间隔内为负；在较宽参数空间内得到近标度不变、略红倾的标量谱；张量谱为蓝倾且张标比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）被强烈压低；固定非零动量下背景相关立方耦合在远过去消失，存留的物质—引力子及引力子自相互作用受普朗克压低（Planck suppressed）。

Conclusion: II型最小修改引力可支持一个过去测地完备、微扰稳定且谱形与观测大致相容的创世情景，其残余相互作用在远过去消失并受普朗克压低，表明该框架是可行且可预测的早期宇宙模型候选。

Abstract: We construct a Genesis scenario within the framework of a type-II minimally modified gravity. The background solution is past geodesically complete and approaches Minkowski spacetime in the asymptotic past. While the Hubble expansion rate grows, the matter sector satisfies the null-energy condition. Scalar perturbations are ghost-free and have no ultraviolet gradient instability, although the squared sound speed $c_\mathcal{R}^2$ becomes negative within a finite infrared region in the Fourier space for a finite time interval. Numerical evolution, including superhorizon growth, yields a nearly scale-invariant, slightly red-tilted scalar spectrum over a broad region of parameter space. The tensor spectrum is blue, with a strongly suppressed tensor-to-scalar ratio. Finally, using the matter fluctuation $δχ$, whose asymptotic normalization is regular, we find that the background-dependent cubic couplings vanish in the far past at fixed nonzero momenta. The surviving matter--graviton and graviton self-interactions are Planck suppressed.

</details>


### [168] [Thermodynamics and phase structure of rotating Hayward-AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37194)
*Qi-Quan Li,Yu Zhang,Hoernisa Iminniyaz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用修改的 Newman–Janis 算法（modified Newman–Janis algorithm）构造旋转 Hayward-AdS 黑洞的"母"解，并在正则化条件下导出旋转 Hayward-AdS 正则黑洞（regular black hole）的热力学性质，揭示其 8 字形/C 字形相结构在旋转情况下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究表明正则 AdS 黑洞具有区别于 Reissner–Nordström-AdS 与 Kerr–Newman-AdS 黑洞的相结构，但其是否仅源于球对称尚不清楚；本文旨在检验在引入旋转后该相结构是否保持。

Method: 采用修改的 Newman–Janis 算法由非旋转解生成旋转"母"黑洞解，在完整无约束相空间（full unconstrained phase space）$(S,J,q,\alpha,P)$ 中计算基本热力学量，再施加正则化条件（regularity condition）$M_1=q^3/\alpha$ 得到正则黑洞的热力学量。

Result: 该运算顺序使 Hawking 温度（Hawking temperature）与表面引力温度（surface gravity temperature）一致，并给出正确的响应函数（response functions）。当 $P<P_c$ 时，$T_2$ 存在局部极大与极小，将黑洞划分为小、中、大三支，$P$–$V$ 等温线呈液/气相变（liquid/gas phase transition）特征的振荡行为；小、大支局部稳定，中间支热容为负而局部不稳定。增大旋转参数 $a$、磁荷 $q$ 或压强 $P$ 会缩小中间支区域。$G_2$–$T_2$ 图呈 8 字形或 C 字形，二者由特征压强 $P_z$ 分界：$P<P_z$ 为 8 字形，对应一阶小/大黑洞转变；$P_z<P<P_c$ 为 C 字形，对应零阶转变。

Conclusion: 正则 AdS 黑洞特有的 8 字形/C 字形相结构在旋转情形下依然保持，表明该结构源自正则化条件对热力学相空间的重构，而非源自球对称性。

Abstract: We use the modified Newman--Janis algorithm to obtain the rotating ``mother'' black hole solution of the rotating Hayward-AdS black hole. We further derive the fundamental thermodynamic quantities of the ``mother'' black hole in the full unconstrained phase space $(S,J,q,α,P)$, and impose the regularity condition $M_1=q^3/α$ to obtain the thermodynamic quantities of the rotating Hayward-AdS regular black hole. This order of operations gives a Hawking temperature identical to the surface gravity temperature and produces the correct response functions of the regular black hole. For $P<P_c$, the Hawking temperature $T_2$ possesses a local maximum and a local minimum, which divides the black hole into small, intermediate, and large black hole branches, and the $P$--$V$ isotherms display oscillatory behavior characteristic of a liquid/gas phase transition. The small and large black hole branches are locally stable, whereas the intermediate black hole branch has negative heat capacity and is locally unstable. Increasing the rotation parameter $a$, magnetic charge $q$, or pressure $P$ reduces the region of the intermediate branch. The $G_2$--$T_2$ diagram shows that the phase structure, in contrast to the standard swallowtail of the Reissner--Nordström-AdS and Kerr--Newman-AdS black holes, is 8-shaped or C-shaped. These two shapes are divided by the characteristic pressure $P_z$. For $P<P_z$, the curve is 8-shaped, corresponding to a first-order small/large black hole transition. For $P_z<P<P_c$, the curve is C-shaped, corresponding to a zeroth-order transition. These results show that the 8-shaped/C-shaped phase structure characteristic of regular AdS black holes persists under rotation, indicating that it stems from the restructuring of the thermodynamic phase space by the regularity condition rather than from spherical symmetry.

</details>


### [169] [Noether Symmetries in Scalar--Vector--Two-Form Cosmology with a Topological BF Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37278)
*Amin Rezaei Akbarieh,Yusuf Kucukakca*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究标量场与各向同性三重阿贝尔一形式、二形式通过标量依赖动能函数及恒定拓扑BF项耦合的模型，在均匀约化下识别全部12个物理模式具有正动能项与类光高频传播的充分区域，并借Noether分析给出三类分支及加速解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是考察多重形式场（one-forms、two-forms）与标量场耦合时，动能项正定性与因果传播性质是否受约束，并厘清形式场贡献与标量驱动加速之间的区别，以及BF项在其中的作用。

Method: 在均匀约化中保留lapse（lapse）以恢复Friedmann约束（Friedmann constraint），检验12个物理模式的动能符号与高频传播速度；另设指数型模型求加速混合解；并在平坦mini-superspace坐标下取二次型有效势，求解零边界determining方程进行Noether分析（Noether analysis），辅以无耦合与约化场极限的对比。

Result: 得到存在全部模式动能项为正且高频传播类光的充分区域；指数模型存在a∝t²的加速混合解，渐近形式分数非零且归一化线性微扰有界；Noether分析给出双曲平方型或指数型标量势（而非幂律），并按势坐标的因果性质分为三支：Branch I具de Sitter极限，Branch II在正则未来完备膨胀轨道上无法维持加速，Branch III给出两个形式扇区均活跃、晚期膨胀a∝t^{4/3}的精确解。

Conclusion: BF项仅改变形式扇区间的有限时间交换，不提供直接的负压源；通过与无耦合及约化场极限比较，可将形式场贡献与标量驱动的加速分离开来。

Abstract: We study a canonical scalar field coupled to isotropic triplets of Abelian one-forms and two-forms through scalar-dependent kinetic functions and a constant topological $BF$ interaction. By retaining the lapse in the homogeneous reduction, we recover the Friedmann constraint and identify a sufficient domain in which all twelve physical modes have positive kinetic terms and luminal high-frequency propagation. A separate exponential model admits an accelerating mixed solution with $a\propto t^2$, nonzero asymptotic form fractions, and bounded normalized linear perturbations. For the Noether analysis, we choose a quadratic effective potential in flat minisuperspace coordinates and solve the zero-boundary determining equations. The canonical scalar potential is hyperbolic-squared or exponential, rather than a power law in the scalar field. The causal character of the potential coordinate separates three branches: Branch I admits a de Sitter limit, Branch II cannot sustain acceleration on regular future-complete expanding trajectories, and Branch III gives an exact solution with both form sectors active and late expansion $a\propto t^{4/3}$. Comparisons with consistent uncoupled and reduced-field limits separate the form contributions from the scalar-driven acceleration. The $BF$ term changes the finite-time exchange between the form sectors but supplies no direct negative-pressure source.

</details>


### [170] [Geodesic dynamics and tidal forces in a quantum-regularized black hole from string T-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37331)
*G. Alencar,Micael S. Oliveira,Diego Sáez-Chillón Gómez,Marcos V. de S. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究T-对偶启发的正则时空中中性及带电情形下的测地线动力学与潮汐力效应，揭示T-对偶尺度与电荷如何决定黑洞/无视界几何转变并改变强场轨道的潮汐响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力启发的正则化（T-对偶尺度）与电荷共同作用下，时空几何中心正则结构对粒子轨道及潮汐力的影响，弥补强场区域相关分析的不足。

Method: 先分析解的几何性质（中心正则结构、视界构型），再通过有效势研究有质量与无质量粒子运动、圆轨道存在性与稳定性，并考察径向自由下落观测者的径向与角向潮汐力及测地线偏离矢量演化。

Result: T-对偶正则化使中心区域潮汐力有限，产生不同拉伸/压缩区间（含径向与角向同时压缩区域）；电荷改变潮汐特征的强度与位置；T-对偶尺度与电荷均抑制累积径向拉伸，而角向形变对电荷不敏感。

Conclusion: T-对偶诱导的量子正则化与电荷共同修正强场区域的轨道动力学与潮汐响应，且相应经典极限可被恰当恢复。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the geodesic dynamics and tidal force effects in neutral and electrically charged regular spacetimes inspired by T-duality. We first analyze the geometric properties of these solutions, including their regular central structure and horizon configurations, and show how the interplay between the T-duality scale $\ell_0$ and the electric charge $Q$ determines the transition between black hole and horizonless geometries. We then study the motion of massive and massless particles through the corresponding effective potentials, examining the existence and stability of circular orbits and the behavior of their characteristic radii. We further investigate the tidal forces experienced by observers in radial free fall, focusing on the behavior of the radial and angular components, their extrema and sign changes, and the location of these characteristic points relative to the horizon structure of the geometry. We show that the T-duality regularization renders the tidal forces finite in the central region and gives rise to different stretching and compression regimes, including a region of simultaneous compression in both the radial and angular directions, while the electric charge modifies the strength and location of these tidal features. Finally, we analyze the evolution of the geodesic deviation vector for two sets of initial conditions. We find that both the T-duality scale and the electric charge tend to suppress the accumulated radial stretching, whereas the angular deformation is considerably less sensitive to the charge. These results show how the quantum regularization induced by T-duality and the presence of electric charge modify the orbital dynamics and tidal response of these geometries in the strong-field regime. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the corresponding classical limits are recovered appropriately.

</details>


### [171] [Quasinormal Ringing of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by a King Dark Matter Halo: Higher-Order WKB Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37501)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Hassan Hassanabadi,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用六阶WKB及其Padé改进方法，计算King密度剖面暗物质晕包围的Schwarzschild黑洞的标量与电磁扰动准正规模，并与时域演化结果交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质晕通过其包裹质量函数改变黑洞时空几何，需定量刻画其对黑洞扰动谱（准正规模）的影响，并厘清该效应在频率与阻尼率上的物理来源。

Method: 采用时滞函数（lapse function）$1-2[M+M_{\rm DM}(r)]/r$ 描述晕的包裹质量效应；用六阶WKB方法及Padé改进版计算准正规模（quasinormal frequencies），并以反双曲正弦（inverse hyperbolic sine）改写时滞函数以规避高阶导数的严重数值抵消；同时以爱因斯坦星团（Einstein cluster）将晕置于视界之外，并与时域演化结果对照。

Result: 基频与时域演化结果吻合在 $0.2\%$ 以内；晕降低振荡频率与阻尼率，实部变化几乎完全由光子球（photon sphere）内包裹的晕质量决定，阻尼率的额外降低遵循圆轨道零测地线的Lyapunov指数与角频率之比。爱因斯坦星团情形下频率降幅相近，但实部遵循乘以晕引力红移因子（gravitational redshift factor）的Schwarzschild值；晕内边缘密度跃变可散射波动并使阻尼率改变约 $9\%$，连续剖面下红移关系偏差在 $r_{\rm in}=3M$ 时低于约 $1.5\%$、$r_{\rm in}\geq4M$ 时低于约 $0.5\%$。

Conclusion: 在所考察的扰动扇区与参数配置内未发现不稳定模式（unstable modes）；暗物质晕对黑洞准正规模的影响可借包裹质量、Lyapunov指数比与引力红移等几何量加以系统解释。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole (BH) surrounded by a dark matter halo described by the King density profile. The lapse function can be expressed as $1-2[M+M_{\rm DM}(r)]/r$, where $M_{\rm DM}(r)$ is the halo mass enclosed within radius $r$; thus, the halo affects the geometry through its enclosed-mass function. We compute the quasinormal frequencies using the sixth-order WKB method and its Padé-improved version. Rewriting the lapse function in terms of the inverse hyperbolic sine avoids the severe numerical cancellations that arise when evaluating its high-order derivatives. For the configurations examined, the fundamental frequencies agree with those extracted from time-domain evolution to within $0.2\%$.The halo lowers both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate. The change of the real part is almost entirely accounted for by the halo mass enclosed by the photon sphere, and the additional decrease of the damping rate follows the ratio of the Lyapunov exponent to the angular frequency of the circular null orbit. Since part of the halo lies inside the horizon in this metric, we also place the halo outside the BH by means of an Einstein cluster. The frequencies then decrease by a similar amount, but the real part follows the Schwarzschild value multiplied by the gravitational redshift factor of the halo. A jump in the density at the inner edge of the halo scatters the waves and changes the damping rate by up to about $9\%$. For a continuous density profile, deviations from the redshift relation remain below about $1.5\%$ for $r_{\rm in}=3M$ and below about $0.5\%$ for the cases with $r_{\rm in}\geq4M$. No unstable modes are found within the perturbation sectors and parameter configurations examined.

</details>


### [172] [One Method to Find Them All: Analytic Black Holes beyond Einstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37515)
*Dongjun Li,Jierui Hu,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一框架，可由潮汐曲率（tidal curvature）与物理源构造克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）的稳态、轴对称形变，并给出六种高阶曲率相互作用（higher-curvature interactions）在耦合领头阶的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑洞是检验引力的强有力探针，但在真空广义相对论（general relativity）之外构造其几何仍具挑战，且既有方法常依赖慢旋转展开（slow-rotation expansion）、数值拟合或模式分解（mode decomposition）。

Method: 建立从潮汐曲率与物理源出发构造克尔稳态轴对称形变的统一框架；以六种高阶曲率相互作用为例，在耦合领头阶、对一般亚极值自旋（subextremal spin）求得闭式解，无需慢旋转展开、数值拟合或模式分解。

Result: 所得解通过场方程、既往逐模式（mode-by-mode）构造以及视界相关量的已知解析预言等严格检验；并发现相互作用的螺旋度手征性（chirality）组织了这些解的解析结构，该规律可能推广至一大类纯度规高阶曲率理论。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越爱因斯坦理论及微扰天体物理环境中的黑洞几何提供系统途径，为引力波（gravitational waves）强场检验、黑洞成像与X射线光谱（X-ray spectroscopy）等观测通道以及近极值黑洞（near-extremal black holes）的理论研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Rotating black holes offer powerful probes of gravity, but constructing their geometries beyond vacuum general relativity remains challenging. Here we develop a unified framework for constructing stationary, axisymmetric deformations of Kerr from their tidal curvature and physical sources. As a demonstration, we obtain closed-form solutions for six higher-curvature interactions at leading order in the couplings and for generic subextremal spin, without slow-rotation expansions, numerical fitting, or mode decomposition. The solutions pass stringent checks against the field equations, previous mode-by-mode constructions, and known analytic predictions for horizon quantities. We find that the chirality of the interactions organizes the analytic structure of these solutions, a pattern that likely extends to a large class of purely metric higher-curvature theories. Our framework opens a systematic route to black-hole geometries in beyond-Einstein theories and perturbative astrophysical environments. These geometries provide a foundation for strong-field tests with gravitational waves and other observational channels, such as black-hole images and X-ray spectroscopy, as well as theoretical studies of near-extremal black holes.

</details>


### [173] [Gravitational wave signatures of periodic orbits around a Schwarzschild-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37525)
*Mirzabek Alloqulov,Sanjar Shaymatov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究改进型史瓦西黑洞（new improved Schwarzschild black hole）中偏离史瓦西几何对有质量试验粒子运动及其周期性轨道引力辐射的影响，发现参数ξ与γ对轨道构型与波形产生适度改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究偏离史瓦西几何（Schwarzschild geometry）的修正如何影响有质量试验粒子的运动，以及其周期性轨道所伴随的引力辐射，从而考察该黑洞模型的动力学特征。

Method: 通过考察径向与角向轨道频率的可通约性（commensurability）确定周期性轨道构型，对代表性轨迹数值重构粒子运动，并采用对称无迹质量四极矩方法（symmetric trace-free mass-quadrupole approach）计算引力波偏振。

Result: 将所得信号与史瓦西情形对照表明，参数ξ与γ对轨道构型及相关波形产生适度变化，说明修正时空几何的信息被编码于辐射之中。

Conclusion: ξ与γ引起的适度改动为研究该黑洞模型的动力学特征（dynamical signatures）提供了一种途径。

Abstract: We investigate how deviations from the Schwarzschild geometry affect the motion of massive test particles and the gravitational radiation associated with their periodic trajectories around a new improved Schwarzschild black hole. Periodic configurations are determined by examining the commensurability of the radial and azimuthal orbital frequencies. For representative trajectories, we numerically reconstruct the particle motion and calculate corresponding gravitational wave polarizations using the symmetric trace-free mass-quadrupole approach. By comparing these signals with their Schwarzschild counterparts, we assess how the modified spacetime geometry is encoded in the radiation. Our analysis shows that the parameters $ξ$ and $γ$ produce modest changes in the orbital configurations and their associated waveforms, providing a means of investigating the dynamical signatures of this BH model.

</details>


### [174] [Weighing dark matter particles with gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37638)
*Rodrigo Vicente,Francisco Duque,Theophanes K. Karydas,Gianfranco Bertone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过LISA可观测的极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspiral, EMRI）信号，可在致密暗物质尖峰中测量超轻暗物质粒子质量；对低质量端大质量黑洞（\(M\sim 10^5 M_\odot\)），\(10^{-14}-10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}\)范围的粒子质量可被测量到一个数量级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为，当暗物质表现为无碰撞物质时，无法通过其引力效应确定暗物质粒子质量；本文旨在突破这一常规认知，寻找测量超轻暗物质粒子质量的新途径。

Method: 利用双黑洞间的尺度分离，使超轻暗物质在大质量主黑洞周围形成无碰撞密度尖峰，同时在与恒星级伴星的相互作用中保留波动效应；将波依赖的吸积（accretion）与动力学摩擦（dynamical friction）纳入相对论性旋近波形，并从模拟LISA信号中联合推断暗物质粒子质量、尖峰归一化及双星参数。

Result: 对处于LISA族群低质量端的大质量黑洞（\(M\sim 10^5 M_\odot\)），预测显示跨度为\(10^{-14}-10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}\)的粒子质量可被测量到一个数量级以内的精度。

Conclusion: 极端质量比旋近为测量超轻暗物质粒子质量提供了可行手段，表明无碰撞暗物质的引力效应并非完全无法约束粒子质量，毫赫兹引力波观测台（如LISA）在该测量中具有潜力。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that the mass of dark matter particles cannot be determined from their gravitational effects when dark matter behaves as collisionless matter. Here we show that extreme-mass-ratio inspirals observable by millihertz gravitational-wave observatories such as the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) could measure the mass of ultralight dark matter particles in sufficiently dense dark matter spikes. The separation of scales between the two black holes allows ultralight dark matter to form a collisionless density spike around the massive primary while retaining wave effects in its interaction with the stellar-mass companion. We incorporate wave-dependent accretion and dynamical friction into relativistic inspiral waveforms and jointly infer the dark matter particle mass, spike normalisation and binary parameters from simulated LISA signals. For massive black holes at the low-mass end of the LISA population ($M\sim 10^5 M_\odot$), our forecasts show that particle masses spanning $10^{-14}-10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$ could be measured to within an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [175] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义狄拉克方程（generalized Dirac equation）在对称远平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）弱静态球对称背景下的非相对论极限，通过Foldy–Wouthuysen变换导出含新型自旋-引力耦合的有效哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统狄拉克理论在引力背景下的非相对论展开仅包含动能、自旋-轨道与达尔文项的引力对应物；而广义旋量联络（generalized spinor connection）包含完整Clifford代数基底，会引入超出常规理论的非度规性（non-metricity）耦合，其低能表现尚待澄清。

Method: 在重合规范（coincident gauge）与各向同性坐标下的弱场Schwarzschild几何中，构造广义狄拉克哈密顿量，并实施逐次Foldy–Wouthuysen变换至1/m²阶，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项。

Result: 所得块对角哈密顿量除包含动能、自旋-轨道与达尔文相互作用的预期引力对应项外，还自然出现直接自旋-引力、各向异性自旋-动量-引力及潮汐自旋-动量耦合等新算符结构；并对地球弱场中的电子做了量级分析以论证截断的合理性。

Conclusion: 对称远平行背景下的广义狄拉克方程产生涉及费米子自旋、动量与引力场空间导数的新低能相互作用通道，所得有效哈密顿量为检验广义旋量联络附加耦合的现象学约束提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Machine learning enabled design of sustainable alloys considering real world constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35946)
*Quan Li,Zhilong Wang,Lei Shi,Baojing Feng,Changhui Liu,Jie Wang,Jingya Wang,Weisen Zheng,Matic JovicevicKlug,Ziyuan Rao,Jinjin Li,Dierk Raabe,Xiaoqin Zeng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出物理信息驱动集成（PIE）学习框架，将结构-性能关系、加工路径与可持续性准则耦合，用于镁合金的机理型可持续设计，并从数百万候选合金数字库中识别出两种突破性能-成本-排放权衡的合金方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续合金设计需兼顾原料稀缺性与波动、加工与能耗要求、法规阈值及经济限制，远超传统以性能为中心的框架；需同时处理性能-加工-使用权衡与可持续生产路径。
