Method: 构建物理信息集成（physics-informed ensemble, PIE）学习框架，融合基本结构-性能关系、加工路线与可持续性判据；由成分-微观组织关系预测性能，并在可持续性约束下推断加工路线；以镁合金为验证对象，基于基准数据集训练，系统探索成分与微观组织以构建含数百万候选合金的数字库。

Result: 识别出两种可持续镁合金方案：含稀土合金在成本与排放相当条件下，屈服强度与抗拉强度分别提升72.8%与61.1%；无稀土合金在不牺牲力学性能前提下，成本与碳排放分别较原生原料对应合金降低40.4%与88.9%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通用、物理信息驱动且可量化的框架，用于在现实约束下设计可持续合金，并表明可持续性与高性能可同时实现。

Abstract: Designing sustainable alloys must reconcile high performance with real-world constraints, including feedstock scarcity and variability, processing and energy requirements, regulatory thresholds, and economic limitations. Addressing these constraints reaches far beyond today's conventional performance-focused frameworks. It needs to consider not only complex property-processing-performance trade-offs but also sustainable production pathways. Here, we propose a physics-informed ensemble (PIE) learning framework that integrates fundamental structure-property relationships with processing routes and sustainability criteria for mechanism-based alloy design. It predicts properties from composition-microstructure relationships and infers processing routes under sustainability constraints, establishing an interdisciplinary approach to identifying sustainable alloy solutions. We validate the approach for Mg alloys, a lightweight yet energy-intensive metallic material class, considering performance, cost, and carbon emissions. Trained on a benchmark dataset, the PIE model systematically explores plausible alloy compositions and microstructures to construct a digital library of millions of candidate alloys. From this space, we identify two sustainable Mg alloy solutions that overcome the conventional performance-cost-emission trade-off: a rare-earth-containing alloy increases yield and tensile strength by 72.8 percent and 61.1 percent, respectively, over alloys from the same family at comparable cost and emissions; and a rare-earth-free alloy reduces cost and carbon emissions by 40.4 percent and 88.9 percent, respectively, relative to its primary-feedstock counterpart, without compromising mechanical performance. This work introduces a general, physics-informed, and quantifiable framework for designing sustainable alloys under real-world constraints.

</details>


### [177] [Amorphization-Depth-Dependent Bismuth Redistribution and Activation in Silicon for Quantum Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35993)
*Youcef A. Bioud,Simon-Jean Michel,Sebastien Francoeur,Sjoerd Roorda,Eva Dupont-Ferrier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 对比直接注入与预非晶化两条路径下20 keV Bi在Si中的再分布与电激活，发现非晶层深度（约47 nm与177 nm）与再生长几何决定Bi的再分布及电激活效率，而不仅取决于替位并入比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近表面铋（Bi）施主因丰富的电子-核自旋结构与长自旋相干时间而适用于硅量子器件，但离子注入引起的非晶化（amorphization）强烈影响其电激活，需明确非晶化深度对Bi再分布与激活的作用。

Method: 对20 keV Bi分别直接注入Si与经Si预非晶化（preamorphization）后注入，650 °C退火300 s；采用卢瑟福背散射谱与沟道分析（Rutherford backscattering spectrometry and channeling）、霍尔测量（Hall measurements）、深度分辨离子散射、X射线光电子能谱（XPS）及低温光致发光（PL）进行表征。

Result: 两条路径非晶层深度约47 nm与177 ± 15 nm；替位Bi分数相近（91 ± 2%与89 ± 4%），但霍尔电激活产额分别约60%与90%。深度分辨分析显示在初始非晶/晶体界面处或其外的末端损伤区（end-of-range）存在埋藏Bi相关特征，且更高退火温度下表面偏析增强；仅注Bi样品未见可分辨的Bi氧化物贡献，并出现施主相关光学信号。

Conclusion: 非晶化深度与再生长几何（regrowth geometry）主导Bi的再分布，并在替位并入之外进一步影响电激活产额；埋藏特征与残余末端损伤缺陷处的捕获（trapping）一致。

Abstract: Near-surface bismuth donors are promising for silicon quantum devices because of their rich electron-nuclear spin structure and long spin coherence, but their electrical activation is strongly influenced by implantation-induced amorphization. We compare redistribution and electrical activation of 20 keV Bi implanted directly into Si and after Si preamorphization. The two routes produce amorphous-layer depths of approximately 47 nm and 177 +/- 15 nm, respectively. After annealing at 650 C for 300 s, Rutherford backscattering spectrometry and channeling reveal comparable substitutional Bi fractions of 91 +/- 2% and 89 +/- 4%, whereas Hall measurements indicate Hall-derived electrical activation yields of approximately 60% and 90%, respectively. Depth-resolved ion-scattering analysis reveals a buried Bi-related feature at or beyond the initial amorphous/crystalline interface, within the end-of-range region, together with increasing surface segregation at higher annealing temperatures. The buried feature is consistent with trapping near residual end-of-range defects. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy and low-temperature photoluminescence on the Bi-only sample reveal no resolvable Bi-oxide contribution and a donor-related optical signature, respectively. These results show that amorphization depth and regrowth geometry govern Bi redistribution and influence electrical activation beyond substitutional incorporation alone.

</details>


### [178] [STEM-Bank-26: An Open Dataset of Analytical Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy Images and Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36086)
*George Hollyer,Eric Stach*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: STEM-Bank-26 是一个 929 GB 的公开扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）数据集，含 37,921 张图像，覆盖 1,095 个样品与 1,499 次实验会话，其中 65.5% 的会话配有能谱（EDS）或电子能量损失谱（EELS）数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STEM 作为多模态纳米尺度表征手段虽用途广泛，但跨越多年度、覆盖多样样品的公开数据集极为稀缺，限制了数据驱动方法的发展。

Method: 构建并发布大规模开放数据集，整合 STEM 成像与 EDS/EELS 谱学信号；通过比较序列图像间的元数据（metadata）提取显微镜控制轨迹，并提供从档案数据构建类似数据集的工具。

Result: 数据集规模为 929 GB、37,921 张 STEM 图像，涵盖 1,095 个材料样品、1,499 次会话，65.5% 的会话包含 EDS 或 EELS 数据。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，该数据集是当前公开可用的、在样品与会话分布广度上最大的、整合实验 STEM、EDS 与 EELS 数据的数据集。

Abstract: Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) is a versatile, multi-modal method for studying materials at the nanoscale. It integrates multiple signal sources to provide atomic resolution imaging alongside spectroscopy for elemental composition and electronic structure. Despite its utility, few datasets spanning multiple years of data have been published. We introduce STEM-Bank-26, a 929 GB open dataset of 37,921 STEM images that spans 1,095 material samples across 1,499 sessions and includes energy-dispersive spectroscopy (EDS) or electron-energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) data for 65.5% of sessions. We extract microscope control trajectories by comparing metadata between sequential images and provide tools for building similar datasets from archival data. To our knowledge, this is the largest openly available dataset that integrates experimental STEM, EDS and EELS data across a broad distribution of samples and sessions.

</details>


### [179] [A Hybrid Brownian Dynamics-kinetic Monte Carlo Framework for Coupled Magnetic and Rotational Dynamics of Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36102)
*Siraj Ul Haq,Iago López-Vázquez,Kazuya Okada,Sergiu Ruta,Amira Páez-Rodríguez,Lucía Gutiérrez,Òscar Iglesias,David Serantes,Roy W. Chantrell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合的布朗动力学—动力学蒙特卡罗（BD–kMC）框架，用于在实验相关时间尺度上模拟悬浮磁性纳米颗粒（MNPs）的磁—转动耦合动力学，并在非相互作用单畴颗粒上与随机LLG模拟结果取得一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低频交变磁场下，悬浮磁性纳米颗粒的磁动力学与转动动力学耦合模拟在实验相关时间尺度上计算代价高昂，现有方法难以高效覆盖长时程。

Method: 构建BD–kMC混合框架：以Metropolis蒙特卡罗（MC）采样引入磁矩的局部涨落，使其偏离能量极小值，从而捕捉主导颗粒转动的热平均各向异性力矩；同时设置势垒捕获判据（barrier-trapping criterion）将MC试探步限制在当前能谷内，把热激活磁翻转交由kMC过程处理，以区分局部涨落与磁反转。

Result: 对非相互作用单畴MNPs，与随机Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert（LLG）模拟相比，平衡力矩与稳态易轴取向一致，包括随外场由以垂直为主向平行排列的转变。

Conclusion: 该BD–kMC框架为磁动力学与布朗动力学的耦合提供了一种高效途径，降低了探索低频磁应用相关长时程的计算成本。

Abstract: Simulating the coupled magnetic and rotational dynamics of suspended magnetic nanoparticles (MNPs) over experimentally relevant timescales remains computationally demanding, particularly under low-frequency alternating magnetic fields. Here, we develop a hybrid Brownian Dynamics--kinetic Monte Carlo (BD--kMC) framework that combines thermally activated magnetic switching with particle rotation while retaining local fluctuations of the magnetic moment. These fluctuations are incorporated through Metropolis Monte Carlo (MC) sampling, allowing the moment to depart from the energy minima and thereby capturing the thermally averaged anisotropy torque that governs particle rotation. To preserve the distinction between local fluctuations and magnetic reversals, a barrier-trapping criterion confines MC trial moves to the current energy well, leaving thermally activated switching to the kMC process. For non-interacting single-domain MNPs, comparison with stochastic Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert (LLG) simulations demonstrates agreement in the equilibrium torque and stationary easy-axis orientation, including the field-driven transition from predominantly perpendicular to parallel alignment. The resulting BD--kMC framework provides an efficient approach to coupled magnetic and Brownian Dynamics, reducing the computational cost of exploring the long timescales relevant to low-frequency magnetic applications.

</details>


### [180] [Physical structure as an organizing principle for universal Hamiltonian learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36135)
*Mingkang Liu,Zhanghao Zhouyin,Guanghui Wang,Daye Zheng,Lei Shen,Qiangqiang Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: UniTB 是一个通用 E(3) 等变哈密顿量表示框架，将密度泛函紧束缚理论（DFT-TB）的物理先验与深度学习相结合，实现跨化学组分与无序固体的泛化，并在 29,242 个晶体上达到 0.48 meV 的矩阵元误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论（DFT）可给出材料电子结构，但计算成本限制了大超胞计算与材料筛选；机器学习哈密顿量虽规避自洽循环，却通常局限于特定体系，亟需能覆盖新组分与无序固体的通用模型，并保持下游性质的物理性与大体系计算的经济性。

Method: UniTB 以密度泛函紧束缚理论（density-functional tight-binding）的物理先验为基础，用等变神经网络对先验哈密顿量进行修正；通过由该先验路由的专家混合（mixture of experts）与类似自洽电荷紧束缚的电荷均衡头（charge-equilibration head）实现跨化学泛化，并以投影约束能带偏移；同时提供快速的 SO(2) 张量积 GPU 内核，UniTB-Flash 变体以 O(N) 成本从双中心表组装先验。

Result: 在 29,242 个晶体上训练后，UniTB 在覆盖 68 种元素的非磁性测试晶体上取得 0.48 meV 的矩阵元误差；在无定形训练数据条件下，两个模型对无定形 Ge-Sb-Te 的光学与电导性质均与 DFT 一致；UniTB-Flash 的候选排序与 UniTB 无显著差异，二者共同筛选了 16,195 个晶体并覆盖五个工业相关应用领域。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，融合物理先验的通用等变哈密顿量框架可在保持下游性质物理性的同时实现跨化学与无序体系的泛化，并为大规模材料筛选提供高效可行的工具。

Abstract: Density functional theory (DFT) gives the electronic structure of materials, but its cost limits large-supercell calculations and materials screening. Machine-learned Hamiltonians bypass the self-consistent cycle but are usually system-specific; universal ones could cover new compositions and disordered solids. Such models must generalize across chemistry and keep downstream properties physical, while staying inexpensive for large cells. Here we introduce UniTB, a universal E(3) equivariant Hamiltonian representation framework that integrates physical priors rooted in density-functional tight-binding theory with deep learning. UniTB's network, which corrects a prior Hamiltonian, generalizes across chemistry through a mixture of experts routed by that prior and a charge-equilibration head as in self-consistent-charge tight binding. A projection bounds band shifts from the prior, suppressing spurious states and keeping downstream properties physical. We provide the fastest SO(2) tensor-product GPU kernels for the network. The variant UniTB-Flash assembles its prior from two-center tables at $O(N)$ cost for $N$ atoms, against $O(N\log N)$ on a grid. Trained on 29,242 crystals, UniTB reaches a 0.48 meV matrix-element error on nonmagnetic test crystals spanning 68 elements. Without amorphous training data, both models agree with DFT optics and conductivity of amorphous Ge-Sb-Te. UniTB-Flash ranks candidates with no significant difference from UniTB, whose property values are more accurate; together they screen 16,195 crystals for five application domains of industrial relevance.

</details>


### [181] [Anisotropic Structural and Magnetic Response in Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$: Al-Sublattice Reconstruction upon Sm Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36140)
*Mingyu Xu,Weiwei Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文报道了 Sm2Ir8Al31 的合成、晶体结构与物性：以较大的4f Sm 替代较小的5f U，化学压力诱导强各向异性结构响应，Al亚晶格重构，并出现取向依赖的各向异性磁交叉而非体相热力学相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学压力（chemical pressure）对金属间化合物（intermetallic compounds）的结构与物性具有关键作用，本文以 UIr4Al15 为参照，通过 f 位元素替代探究化学压力如何调控 Ir-Al 骨架与磁性响应。

Method: 以较大的 Sm 替代 UIr4Al15 中较小的 U，合成 Sm2Ir8Al31；采用单晶 X 射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）解析结构，并开展磁化率、热容、电阻率及晶格参数测量。

Result: 结构表现为空间群 P-421m 的四方 √2×√2×1 超结构（superstructure），源自 P42/nmc 的平均结构；U→Sm 替代引起强各向异性响应，一种 Ir 配位环境变化有限、另一种显著局域重排，Al 亚晶格（Al sublattice）通过位置劈裂、各向异性原子位移椭球与部分占据容纳畸变，成分富 Al；磁化率呈方向依赖，部分取向在 50 K 附近有宽异常。

Conclusion: 热容、电阻率与晶格参数无对应异常，表明 50 K 特征更符合各向异性磁交叉（anisotropic magnetic crossover）而非体相热力学相变；f 位化学替代可诱导 Al 亚晶格重构并改变相近 Ir-Al 结构框架内的磁响应。

Abstract: Chemical pressure plays a critical role in determining the crystal structures and physical properties of intermetallic compounds. Here, we report the synthesis, crystal structure, and physical properties of Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$, a structural analog of UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$, in which the smaller 5f uranium atoms are replaced by larger 4f samarium atoms. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction reveals a tetragonal $\sqrt{2}\times\sqrt{2}\times1$ superstructure with space group $P\bar{4}2_1m$, derived from a UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$-like average structure with space group $P4_2/nmc$. Comparison with UIr$_4$Al$_{15}$ shows that chemical pressure from U-to-Sm substitution produces a strongly anisotropic structural response rather than a uniform expansion of the Ir-Al framework. While one Ir coordination environment undergoes only modest changes, the other exhibits substantial local rearrangement. The Al sublattice primarily accommodates these structural distortions, with selected Al sites showing positional splitting, anisotropic atomic displacement ellipsoids, and partial occupancies, resulting in an Al-rich composition of approximately Sm$_2$Ir$_8$Al$_{31}$. Magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal a direction dependence, including a broad anomaly near 50 K for selected magnetic-field orientations. However, the absence of corresponding anomalies in heat capacity, electrical resistivity, and lattice parameters suggests that this feature is more consistent with an anisotropic magnetic crossover than a bulk thermodynamic phase transition. These results highlight how chemical substitution at the f-element site can induce Al-sublattice reconstruction and modify the magnetic response within a closely related Ir-Al structural framework.

</details>


### [182] [Learning the Chronology of Materials Discovery for Synthesizability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36193)
*Junkil Park,Aron Walsh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出时序感知模型ChronoSyn，将材料发现的历史时间顺序作为额外监督信号，与可合成性预测联合学习，用于筛选可实验实现的理论组分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量计算与生成方法提出的候选材料数量远超合成速度，亟需判断哪些假设材料可被实验实现；现有正例-无标记（positive-unlabeled, PU）学习式可合成性预测把已报道材料视为静态集合，忽略了发现历程与驱动进展的实际因素。

Method: ChronoSyn通过共享的组分表示（composition representation），联合学习材料的可合成性与已知材料间的相对报道顺序，从而在传统可合成性标签之外引入发现时序（discovery chronology）作为补充监督。

Result: 引入发现时序带来额外监督，提升了预测性能，较基线模型（baseline models）更有效地识别可实验实现组分；在约5000万个假设组分上，ChronoSyn识别出化学上自洽的高分家族，其中包含训练中未出现的已实验报道组分。

Conclusion: 材料发现的时序是可提供信息的学习信号，已知材料的发现路径可指示未来可能被发现的新材料。

Abstract: High-throughput computational and generative approaches are proposing candidate materials far faster than they can be synthesized, making it increasingly important to identify which hypothetical materials are likely to be experimentally realizable. Positive-unlabeled (PU) learning has emerged as a representative framework for synthesizability prediction, yet existing approaches treat experimentally reported materials as a static collection, overlooking the historical trajectory of materials discovery and the diverse practical factors that have driven its progress. Here we introduce ChronoSyn, a chronology-aware machine learning model that jointly learns synthesizability and the relative reporting order of known materials through a shared composition representation. Incorporating discovery chronology provides additional supervision beyond conventional synthesizability labels, improving prediction performance and enabling ChronoSyn to identify experimentally realizable compositions more effectively than baseline models. Applied to approximately 50 million hypothetical compositions, ChronoSyn identifies chemically coherent high-scoring families that include experimentally reported compositions unseen during training. These results highlight the chronology of materials discovery as an informative learning signal and suggest that the path along which known materials were discovered can point the way to those that will be discovered in the future.

</details>


### [183] [Confinement, registry and spin in chromium migration between graphene layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36197)
*Mingguang Chen,Senfu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以统一的第一性原理协议（PBE+D3(BJ)）比较单个Cr原子在石墨烯表面、AB堆垛层间与AA堆垛层间的迁移行为，发现AB环境中Cr保持磁性且近乎自由扩散，而AA环境中形成非磁性η⁶-η⁶夹心结构导致迁移势垒显著升高、扩散慢3–4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cr原子在石墨烯层间与表面的迁移机制及磁性状态对其输运与结构稳定性的影响尚缺乏在同一计算协议下的系统比较；AB与AA堆垛层间环境的差异尤其需要澄清。

Method: 采用第一性原理协议PBE+D3(BJ)，在统一晶胞族、配准锚点（registry anchors）与自旋态搜索框架下，计算Cr在石墨烯表面、AB层间（kagome反演中心）与AA层间的吸附位点、磁矩与跃迁路径；AA路径按不同自旋分支分别弛豫，并考察3x3与5x5晶胞、远场间距固定/自由及不同哈伯德U（Hubbard U）的影响。

Result: 表面与AB层间Cr保持磁性并近乎自由迁移：表面C-C桥位为极小点且最高采样点仅高10 meV（5x5），AB层间Cr位于kagome反演中心，跃迁势垒约15 meV、磁矩约4.32 μ_B。AA层间极小点为非磁性η⁶-η⁶夹心并使层间距收缩，离开需打开层间并切换至4–5 μ_B磁态；势垒估计在3x3晶胞为636 meV，5x5晶胞（远场固定/自由）分别为260与185 meV且尚未收敛。300 K下若自旋变化不限速且前因子相同，AA层间跃迁比AB慢3–4个数量级。AA结果依赖泛函：Cr d轨道加U=1.0 eV即使磁性态成为AA基态。此外Cr在AA配准中的结合比AB强246–666 meV。

Conclusion: 快速AB层间跃迁要求Cr周围层片保持Bernal配准（Bernal registry）；AA与AB环境的迁移差异源于磁性态与夹心构型，且AA势垒数值对晶胞尺寸与U值敏感、尚未收敛。

Abstract: We compare the migration of a single Cr atom on monolayer graphene, between AB-stacked layers and between AA-stacked layers, with one first-principles protocol (PBE+D3(BJ), one cell family, registry anchors and spin-state search). We find three main results. First, on the surface and in the AB gallery Cr stays magnetic and moves almost freely. On the surface the C-C bridge is the minimum in 4x4 and 5x5 cells, and the highest point we sample on a hop lies 10 meV above it (5x5). In the AB gallery Cr sits on the inversion centres of the bilayer, which form a kagome net, and hops between them over 15 meV with a moment of about 4.32 $μ_B$. Second, in the AA gallery the minimum is a nonmagnetic $η^6$-$η^6$ sandwich that pulls the layers together. Leaving it requires opening the gallery and switching Cr to a moment of 4-5 $μ_B$, so we map the path with separately relaxed spin branches. The barrier estimate is 636 meV in a 3x3 cell but 260 and 185 meV in 5x5 cells with the far-field spacing pinned and free, and it is not yet converged. At 300 K, with equal prefactors and if the spin change is not rate-limiting, Cr hops between AA layers 3-4 orders of magnitude more slowly than between AB layers, using the 5x5 AA estimates and the 3x3 AB band. These AA numbers are specific to PBE+D3: an on-site U of 1.0 eV on Cr d already makes the magnetic states the AA ground state. Third, Cr binds 246-666 meV more strongly in AA than in AB registry, so the fast AB hop requires that the layers keep their Bernal registry around Cr.

</details>


### [184] [Spin Rectification in Series and Parallel Networks of Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36213)
*Md. Majibul Haque Babu,Maxim Tsoi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究实验考察了铁磁导体并联与串联网络中的自旋整流效应，发现其可等效为铁磁元件电阻的增加，且总光电压遵循电路中串联求和、并联取倒数和相加的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究多元素自旋电子器件中铁磁导体网络的自旋整流（spin rectification）行为，寻找一种简单可控地调节自旋整流光电压（spin-rectification photovoltage）大小的方式。

Method: 构建由 Ni36Fe64 元件（导线）组成的串联与并联网络，在铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）条件下测量其产生的微小自旋整流光电压，并将该效应建模为铁磁元件电阻的增加。

Result: 串联网络中总自旋整流光电压等于各单根导线贡献的代数和；并联网络中则需将各单根贡献的倒数相加（即取倒数和），与常规串并联电路总电阻的依赖关系一致。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种简单途径，可在多元素自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）中控制与调控自旋整流光电压的大小。

Abstract: We present the experimental study of spin rectification in parallel and series networks of ferromagnetic conductors. The networks were composed of Ni36Fe64 elements (wires) which, when subject to ferromagnetic resonance (FMR), develop a small spin-rectification photovoltage. We found that the effect of spin rectification can be modeled as an increase in resistance of the ferromagnetic elements and closely follows the usual dependence of total network resistance in series and parallel electric circuits: the total spin-rectification photovoltage in ferromagnetic wires connected in series is equal to the algebraic sum of contributions from individual wires, while in the parallel connection one needs to add the reciprocals of the individual contributions. Our results support a simple way to control and manipulate the magnitude of spin-rectification photovoltage in multi-element spintronic devices.

</details>


### [185] [Exciton-driven coherent dressing in transient absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36263)
*Jinyuan Wu,Chang Shu,Yang-hao Chan,Diana Y. Qiu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在含时绝热GW（time-dependent adiabatic GW）框架下建立激子驱动修饰（exciton-driven dressing）对瞬态吸收光谱（transient absorption spectroscopy）的理论与计算方法，导出两条受对称性与能带拓扑（band topology）保护的激子修饰瞬态吸收选择定则（selection rules），并在单层MoS2中数值验证及其在对称性破缺后的解除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干激子（coherent excitons）修饰电子态已在时间与角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）中被观测，但光学光谱中激子驱动的修饰效应仍基本未被探索，缺乏相应的理论描述与选择定则。

Method: 在含时绝热GW（time-dependent adiabatic GW）形式体系中发展处理瞬态吸收光谱中激子驱动修饰效应的理论与计算方法，并解析推导受对称性与能带拓扑保护的选择定则；随后对单层MoS2进行数值计算。

Result: 导出两条对激子修饰瞬态吸收成立的选择定则，其受对称性与能带拓扑保护，且区别于tr-ARPES中的相应规则；单层MoS2的计算复现了这些选择定则，并显示其在对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）后被解除（lifting）。

Conclusion: 激子驱动的修饰在光学光谱中具有可分辨且受对称性与拓扑约束的特征，为瞬态吸收光谱中激子效应的识别提供了理论与判据；对称性破缺可作为调控此类选择定则的手段。

Abstract: While coherent excitons have recently been observed to dress electronic states in time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (tr-ARPES), exciton-driven dressing in optical spectroscopy remains largely unexplored. This work develops a theoretical and computational approach to exciton-driven dressing effects in transient absorption spectroscopy in the time-dependent adiabatic GW formalism and derives two symmetry- and band topology-protected selections rules for exciton-dressed transient absorption, unique from tr-ARPES. We then numerically calculate exciton-dressed transient absorption spectra of monolayer MoS2 and demonstrate the selection rules and their lifting after symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [186] [Multiwavelength Photoluminescence as a Non-destructive Probe of Depth-Dependent UV Damage in Perovskite Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36305)
*Zixu Huang,Julisa Juarez,Akash Dasgupta,David S. Ginger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多波长光致发光（multiwavelength photoluminescence）的无损探测策略，利用钙钛矿（perovskite）波长依赖吸收特性，快速表征器件在工作条件下的深度分辨降解行为，并据此识别出埋底界面（buried interface）为紫外诱导降解的优先发生位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 埋底界面（buried interface）对钙钛矿光电器件（perovskite optoelectronic devices）的工作稳定性至关重要，但难以进行无损探测，现有方法多依赖破坏性测试，缺乏快速、非破坏性的深度分辨表征手段。

Method: 以紫外光浸泡（UV light-soaking）配合不同氧化铟锡（ITO）修饰层为测试案例，采用宽场光致发光成像（wide-field PL imaging）、时间分辨光致发光（time-resolved photoluminescence, trPL）以及不同波长激发下的理想因子（ideality factor）测量，对钙钛矿薄膜与光伏器件进行表征。

Result: 仅借助无损表征即发现，p-i-n 结构钙钛矿太阳能电池（perovskite solar cells）中的紫外诱导降解优先发生于空穴传输层（HTL）界面，对器件稳定性产生不利影响，该结论与离位破坏性测试（ex-situ destructive tests）结果一致；同时，采用 3-巯丙基三甲氧基硅烷（3-mercaptopropyltrimethoxysilane）修饰埋底界面可减少该界面的紫外诱导损伤，提升薄膜与器件的紫外稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种快速、无损的深度依赖降解筛选策略，为钙钛矿器件的界面设计与稳定性优化提供依据。

Abstract: Buried interfaces play a critical role in the operational stability of perovskite optoelectronic devices, yet remain challenging to probe non-destructively. Here, we demonstrate a multiwavelength photoluminescence-based approach exploiting the wavelength-dependent absorption of perovskites, enabling fast, and non-destructive probing of depth-sensitive degradation behavior of perovskite devices under operational conditions. Using ultraviolet (UV) light-soaking with different indium tin oxide (ITO) modifiers as a test case, we employ wide-field photoluminescence (PL) imaging, time-resolved photoluminescence (trPL) and ideality factor measurements with different wavelength excitation to characterize degradation behavior of both perovskite thin films and photovoltaic devices under UV soaking. Using only nondestructive characterization, we show that UV-induced degradation in p-i-n perovskite solar cells occurs preferentially at the HTL interface, adversely affecting device stability, consistent with ex-situ destructive tests. Our results also show that modifying the buried interface with 3-mercaptopropyltrimethoxysilane reduces UV-induced damage at the buried interface, thus improving the UV stability of both perovskite films and devices. This work establishes a fast, non-destructive strategy to screen depth-dependent degradation in perovskite devices, offering insights for interface design and stability optimization.

</details>


### [187] [Oxygen partial pressure induced dielectric phase evolution in PLD deposited Barium Strontium Titanate thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36313)
*Shashi Pandey,Suk Hyun Sung,Jacob Fernandez,John T. Heron*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition, PLD）在系统改变氧分压条件下生长 SrRuO3/BST/SrRuO3 外延异质结，建立氧生长压力、薄膜成分与晶体结构同介电相（铁电、弛豫型、顺电）之间的映射关系，并揭示压力驱动的四方相向近立方相的结构转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钛酸锶钡（(BaxSr1-x)TiO3, BST）中 BaTiO3 铁电相与 SrTiO3 顺电相的竞争可通过 A 位合金浓度调控，为研究介电相竞争提供平台；但薄膜沉积参数如何影响并控制介电相，文献中尚缺乏充分报道。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积（PLD），以 (Ba0.9Sr0.1)TiO3 靶材在系统变化的氧分压下制备 SrRuO3/BST/SrRuO3 外延薄膜异质结，并将介电相与氧生长压力、薄膜成分及晶体结构进行对应分析，辅以扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）表征。

Result: 氧生长压力发生相对温和的变化即可获得铁电相（ferroelectric）、弛豫型相（relaxor-like）或顺电相（paraelectric）；STEM 分析表明该压力依赖的相演化伴随 BST 晶格由四方相（tetragonal）向近立方相（nearly cubic）的结构转变，后者对应弛豫型状态。

Conclusion: 介电相可由氧生长压力有效调控，该调控途径为研究相演化及发展未来计算技术提供了可能。

Abstract: Barium Strontium Titanate ((BaxSr1-x)TiO3, BST) thin films are amongst the most extensively studied dielectric alloys over the last decade. This is due to the tunability of the dielectric constant and dissipation factor in the underlying ferroelectric and paraelectric states. One important feature of BST is that the competition between the ferroelectric BaTiO3 and the paraelectric SrTiO3 parent phases can be tuned by the A-site alloy concentration, opening a platform for exploring dielectric phase competition. However, the understanding of the interplay between and establishing the control of the dielectric phase from thin film deposition parameters has not been sufficiently reported in the literature. Here, we synthesized a series of SrRuO3/BST/SrRuO3 epitaxial thin film heterostructures by Pulsed Laser Deposition (PLD) from a (Ba0.9Sr0.1)TiO3 target under systematic variation of the oxygen partial pressure during the BST deposition. We mapped the dielectric phase of the film to the oxygen growth pressure, film composition, and film crystal structure. A ferroelectric, relaxor-like, or paraelectric phase can be obtained with relatively moderate change in oxygen growth pressure. Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) analysis further indicates that this pressure-dependent phase evolution is accompanied by a structural transition of the BST lattice from tetragonal toward a nearly cubic phase, with the latter corresponding to the relaxor-like state. The control of the dielectric phase opens a pathway to study phase evolution and enable future computing technologies.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Geometry: Hole-Edge Atomic Configuration as a Design Variable in Graphyne Kirigami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36468)
*Guilherme B. Kanegae,Alexandre F. Fonseca*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟首次对γ-石墨炔（γ-graphyne）纳米剪纸（kirigami）进行原子尺度研究，发现其屈服应变（yield strain）最高可达原始γ-石墨炔的9.6倍，且孔边缘原子构型构成独立于切口几何的额外设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪纸（kirigami）切口是实现二维材料可拉伸性的成熟途径，但纳米尺度下切口边缘由离散原子构型构成，会影响力学响应；石墨烯中切口几何唯一决定边缘结构，而多孔碳同素异形体石墨炔（graphyne）可在相同切口几何下呈现不同孔边缘构型，从而提供了新的研究契机。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，开展首个γ-石墨炔剪纸（GYK）的原子尺度研究，并与石墨烯剪纸进行对比分析。

Result: GYK的屈服应变最高达原始γ-石墨炔的9.6倍，其力学响应同时受切口几何与局部孔边缘结构调控；在选定边缘位点仅移除两个碳原子，可使屈服应变进一步提升最多35%，而屈服应力（yield stress）损失很小。

Conclusion: 孔边缘原子构型（hole-edge atomic configuration）被确认为一项额外的设计参数，拓展了纳米尺度剪纸工程（nanoscale kirigami engineering）的设计空间。

Abstract: Kirigami-inspired cuts are an established route to stretchability in two-dimensional materials. At the nanoscale, however, cut edges are not purely geometric: they comprise discrete atomic configurations that can alter the mechanical response. In graphene, a given cut geometry fixes the edge structure; graphyne, a porous carbon allotrope with an intrinsic band gap, lifts this constraint, as identical cut geometries can yield distinct hole-edge configurations. Using molecular dynamics simulations, we report the first atomistic study of $γ$-graphyne kirigami (GYK), and compared with graphene kirigami. GYK reaches yield strains up to 9.6 times that of pristine $γ$-graphyne, with a response governed by both cut geometry and local hole-edge structure. Removing just two carbon atoms at selected edge sites further raises the yield strain by up to 35%, with little loss of yield stress. These findings identify hole-edge atomic configuration as an additional design parameter, expanding the design space for nanoscale kirigami engineering.

</details>


### [189] [Learning Multiscale Dynamics of Nanoparticle Self-Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36498)
*Shuaifeng Li,Puquan Pan,Jonas Hallstrom,Nicholas Kotov,Paul Bogdan,Qian Chen,Xiaoming Mao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理知情（physics-informed）、随时间变化的自回归框架，可直接从液相透射电子显微镜（LPTEM）视频中学习多尺度自组装动力学，并能提取隐含的偏微分方程与基本系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米颗粒自组装（nanoparticle self-assembly）动力学源自多尺度、多体及环境依赖的相互作用，理解困难；LPTEM虽可实时观测，但从高度随机轨迹中提取物理规律仍是挑战。

Method: 将单帧视频粗粒化为含局域密度与取向序（orientational order）的连续欧拉场（Eulerian fields），并用在受约束矩阵流形（constrained matrix manifold）上优化的稀疏最近邻算符演化；通过追踪算符波动性刻画时间尺度行为。

Result: 该方法可捕捉长时间大尺度相变（phase transitions）及短时局域演化与输运规则，并成功提取出期望对称性自然涌现的偏微分方程（partial differential equations），实现基本系数的直接推断。

Conclusion: 该框架为从观测数据中发现隐藏物理机制提供了稳健且计算高效的工具，并为自组装纳米结构的预测性设计开辟路径。

Abstract: Nanoparticle self-assembly offers a powerful route to programmable functional materials, but its dynamics remain difficult to understand because they arise from multiscale, many-body, and environment-dependent interactions. Liquid-phase transmission electron microscopy (LPTEM) enables real-time observation of these dynamics, yet extracting the underlying physical laws from highly stochastic trajectories remains a significant challenge. Here we develop a physics-informed, time-varying autoregressive framework that learns multiscale self-assembly dynamics directly from LPTEM videos. Continuous Eulerian fields with local density and orientational order are coarse-grained from individual video frames and evolved by a sparse, nearest-neighbor operator optimized over a constrained matrix manifold. This interpretable approach captures long-time large scale phase transitions by tracking operator volatility over time, and short-timescale local evolution and transport rules. Crucially, we extract the underlying partial differential equations, where expected symmetries naturally emerge, enabling the direct inference of fundamental coefficients. Our framework provides a robust, computationally efficient tool for discovering hidden physical mechanisms from observational data, opening a path toward the predictive design of self-assembled nanostructures.

</details>


### [190] [Spin-Charge Conversion in an Epitaxial Kagome Flat-Band Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36501)
*Wenyi Zhou,Yuqing Zhu,Katherine Johnson,Hari Paudyal,Duy Nguyen,Dimitri King,Jaden Ma,Jay A. Gupta,Michael E. Flatte,Roland K. Kawakami*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作研究平带笼目金属（flat-band kagome metal）CoSn 的自旋-电荷转换，测得 CoSn 层内有效自旋霍尔角（effective spin Hall angle）达 0.484，远超 Pt 的 0.0436，并结合 DFT 揭示其内禀自旋霍尔效应（intrinsic spin Hall effect）的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笼目结构金属在费米能级附近形成拓扑平带（topological flat bands），已引发广泛关注，并导致电荷输运的强各向异性；然而这类平带笼目金属的自旋输运性质（spin transport properties）仍基本未被探索。

Method: 以代表性平带笼目金属 CoSn 为对象，用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）实现原子分辨表面成像并以 dI/dV 谱确认平带特征；采用磁光技术（magneto-optical techniques）定量测量铁磁镍（Ni）与 c 面 CoSn 薄膜双层体系中的自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque, SOT）；并用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算给出内禀自旋霍尔效应的微观解释。

Result: CoSn 层内获得较大的有效自旋霍尔角 θ_SH = 0.484 ± 0.005，显著高于 Ni/Pt 双层中 Pt 的 θ_SH = 0.0436 ± 0.0003；DFT 计算阐明了动量空间中贝里曲率的来源。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立笼目金属作为高效自旋-电荷转换（spin-charge conversion）与低功耗自旋电子学（low-power spintronics）候选材料的潜力。

Abstract: The formation of topological flat bands near the Fermi level in kagome-structured metals has generated substantial research interest. One key consequence of this electronic structure is the strong anisotropy in charge transport, such as the significantly higher in-plane resistivity relative to the out-of-plane direction. However, the spin transport properties of these flat-band kagome metals are still largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate spin-charge conversion in CoSn, a representative flat-band kagome metal. Scanning tunneling microscopy is employed to image the CoSn surface with atomic resolution, and $dI/dV$ spectroscopy shows clear signatures of the flat bands. Using magneto-optical techniques, we quantify the spin-orbit torque (SOT) in bilayers of ferromagnetic nickel (Ni) and c-plane CoSn thin films. We find a large effective spin Hall angle of $θ_{SH} = 0.484 \pm 0.005$ within the CoSn layers, significantly exceeding the value measured for Pt of $θ_{SH} = 0.0436 \pm 0.0003$ in Ni/Pt bilayers. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations provide a microscopic understanding of the intrinsic spin Hall effect in CoSn, elucidating the sources of Berry curvature in momentum space. These findings establish kagome metals as promising materials for efficient spin-charge conversion and low-power spintronics.

</details>


### [191] [First-principles-based search for emergent topological spin textures in transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36542)
*Yuhyun Cha,Do Hoon Kiem,Myung Joon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多尺度第一性原理发现框架，将相对论密度泛函理论、磁力理论与原子自旋动力学模拟相结合，系统筛选312种单层过渡金属硫族化合物，识别出可稳定拓扑自旋织构（skyrmion、bimeron等）的二维材料类别，并阐明其稳定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑自旋织构（topological spin textures）如斯格明子（skyrmion）和双半子（bimeron）具有丰富的物理功能与自旋技术应用潜力，但微观磁相互作用与大尺度自旋织构之间的复杂耦合使宿主材料的识别十分困难，亟需高效的筛选与发现手段。

Method: 构建多尺度第一性原理框架（multiscale first-principles discovery framework），整合相对论密度泛函理论（relativistic density functional theory）、磁力理论（magnetic force theory）与原子自旋动力学模拟（atomistic spin-dynamics simulations），实现电子相互作用与涌现磁织构的直接关联，并对312种单层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides，覆盖1T与2H相及3d、4d、5d过渡金属）进行系统筛选。

Result: 发现包括斯格明子、双半子和复杂畴态（complex domain states）在内的多种涌现自旋织构，并厘清由交换相互作用（exchange interactions）、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用及磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）驱动的不同稳定机制。

Conclusion: 研究给出了拓扑自旋织构的有前景候选材料，并建立一个可迁移的计算框架，用于加速二维复杂磁态的材料发现。

Abstract: Topological spin textures such as skyrmions and bimerons offer rich physical functionalities and strong potential for future spin-based technologies. Identifying materials that host such emergent magnetic states, however, remains challenging due to the complex interplay between microscopic magnetic interactions and large-scale spin textures. Here, using a multiscale first-principles discovery framework, we identify a broad class of two-dimensional materials that stabilize nontrivial spin textures. The framework integrates relativistic density functional theory, magnetic force theory, and atomistic spin-dynamics simulations, enabling a direct connection between electronic interactions and emergent magnetic textures and allowing efficient screening of systems with large magnetic unit cells and competing exchange interactions. Applying this approach, we systematically screen 312 monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides across 1T and 2H phases of 3d, 4d, and 5d transition metals. We uncover a diverse set of emergent spin textures, including skyrmions, bimerons, and complex domain states, and clarify their distinct stabilization mechanisms driven by exchange interactions, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, and magnetic frustration. These results identify promising candidate materials for topological spin textures and establish a transferable computational framework for accelerating materials discovery of complex magnetic states in two dimensions.

</details>


### [192] [Comprehensive QM/ML Study of Dislocation Loop Line Segments in α-Zr Part I: <a> Loop Core Geometries, Solute Binding, and Transition Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36619)
*Junting Zhang,Andrew P. Horsfield,Mark R. Wenman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用混合量子力学/机器学习（QM/ML）框架系统表征hcp α-Zr中棱柱<a>位错环的九类位错线段，揭示其核结构稳定化、溶质偏聚与层错-完美转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zr包壳合金在高中子通量下服役，辐照缺陷与溶质偏聚是理解其服役行为的关键；而位错核同时涉及短程化学环境与长程弹性场，传统原子尺度方法难以兼顾。

Method: 基于混合量子力学/机器学习（QM/ML）框架，并新实现QM/ML-NEB计算，考察α-Zr中棱柱<a>位错环的全部三种变体（层错型与完美型、间隙型与空位型、一阶与二阶构型），从核几何、溶质结合、滑移势垒及层错向完美的转变路径等方面表征九种可能的位错线段。

Result: Shockley与半Shockley分位错以及基面、棱柱层错带共同主导核稳定化；Fe与H优先结合于层错线段，Sn与Nb偏好完美线段；溶质还可驱动分位错运动、复合及局部去层错化（unfaulting）。

Conclusion: 研究构建了α-Zr中<a>位错环微观结构、溶质捕获与转变机制的完整图像，为辐照条件下Zr合金行为理解提供基础。

Abstract: Zr cladding alloys in nuclear reactors are exposed to high neutron fluxes, making irradiation-induced defects and solute segregation central to understanding their in-service behaviour. Dislocation cores involve short-range chemistry and long-range elastic fields, which are difficult to describe simultaneously using conventional atomistic methods. Using a hybrid quantum mechanics/machine learning (QM/ML) framework, this part of work examines prismatic <a> loops in hcp α-Zr. All three loop variants are considered, including faulted and perfect, interstitial- and vacancy-type, and first- and second-order prismatic configurations. Nine possible dislocation line segments are characterised in terms of core geometry, solute binding, glide barriers and faulted-to-perfect transition pathways using QM/ML and newly implemented QM/ML-NEB calculations. Shockley and half-Shockley partials, together with basal and prismatic fault ribbons, are found to govern core stabilisation. Fe and H preferentially bind to faulted segments, whereas Sn and Nb favour perfect segments. Solutes can also drive partial motion, recombination and local unfaulting. These results establish a comprehensive picture of <a> loop microstructure, solute trapping and transition mechanisms in α-Zr.

</details>


### [193] [Designing Geared Molecular Rotors in Flexible Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36647)
*Konstantin Stracke,Jack D. Evans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作在柔性金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）NU-2002 中刻画二维互锁连接体（linker）的协同旋转，并据此将框架“呼吸”定义为切换转速与耦合模式的分子齿轮箱（molecular gearbox）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物机器高效性的启发，将齿轮等宏观机械概念推广到分子尺度，有望实现对纳米尺度运动的精确控制；本文旨在探究柔性框架中连接体旋转的集体耦合机制及其可调控性。

Method: 采用偏置删除元动力学（bias-deletion metadynamics）映射随体积变化的自由能景观（free energy landscape），识别出六个特征相；随后在各相进行无偏分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，统计共旋转（co-rotation）事件、计算自由能垒并测量相邻连接体间的成对径向力传递（radial force transfer）。

Result: 自由能垒起到“转速过滤器”（rotational-speed filter）的作用——稀有旋转跃迁平均对应更高转速。跨体积变化可划分出四种齿轮区域：小体积下的空间受限旋转（Jammed Gear）、耦合延伸至第三近邻连接体的旋转（First Gear）、面内配对连接体耦合（Second Gear）、以及大体积下无耦合的独立旋转（Neutral Gear）。

Conclusion: 框架“呼吸”可视为一个分子齿轮箱，能同时切换连接体旋转的速度与集体耦合模式；框架柔性因而为在固态分子机器（solid-state molecular machines）中编程集体运动提供了调控手段。

Abstract: Translating macroscopic mechanical concepts such as gears to the molecular scale offers a route to precise control over nanoscale motion, inspired by the efficiency of biological machines. In this work, we explore two-dimensional interlocked linker rotations in the flexible metal-organic framework NU-2002. The volume-dependent free energy landscape is mapped using bias-deletion metadynamics to identify six characteristic phases, ranging from very closed to very open pore. We then perform unbiased molecular dynamics simulations at each phase to track rotation events and evaluate co-rotation statistics, calculate free energy barriers, and measure pairwise radial force transfer between neighbouring linkers. We show that the free energy barrier acts as a rotational-speed filter, where rare rotational transitions lead to higher speeds on average. Across the volume profile, we classify four distinct geared regimes: sterically restricted rotations at small volumes (Jammed Gear), rotational coupling extending to third neighbour linkers (First Gear), in-plane partnered linker coupling (Second Gear), and uncoupled, independent rotation at large volumes (Neutral Gear). Together, these results establish framework breathing as a molecular gearbox that switches both the speed and collective coupling of linker rotation. Framework flexibility therefore offers a handle for programming collective motion in solid-state molecular machines.

</details>


### [194] [Where Does the Anion-Redox Hole Go? Reliability of Open-Shell Projection-Based Embedding for Li-Rich Cathodes, from DFT Environments to Quantum Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36659)
*Pritam Das,Satadeep Bhattacharjee,Pil-Ryung Cha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作发展了自旋非限制的基于投影的嵌入方法（spin-unrestricted projection-based embedding），并在Li(2-x)MnO3点电荷团簇上以DMRG-in-DFT研究富锂层状氧化物中的氧空穴分布。结果表明空穴始终位于一对氧上且无O-O键，其分布主要由泛函、几何、自旋投影与态搜索等设置决定，而嵌入平均场与关联求解器影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富锂层状氧化物（Li-rich layered oxides）中的阴离子氧化还原（anion redox）将空穴置于反铁磁Mn(IV) d3晶格旁的氧上，构成强关联开壳层问题；基于投影的波函数嵌入DFT（wavefunction-in-DFT embedding）可衔接关联与量子求解器，但既有误差评估多限于闭壳层分子，开壳层情形缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 发展自旋非限制的投影嵌入方案，在含点电荷的闭壳层、破缺对称性与高自旋参考下达到DFT-in-DFT层面微哈特里（microhartree）精度；随后对去锂化Li(2-x)MnO3点电荷团簇施加DMRG-in-DFT，系统改变环境泛函（0-100%精确交换）、几何、活性区、活性空间自旋、虚空间、团簇尺寸与磁参考，并辅以扩展态搜索。

Result: 所有设置下最低SCF态均把空穴置于一对氧上，且无O-O成键；空穴共享方式取决于泛函与几何，杂化泛函在DFT+U几何下给出完整无耦合空穴，在自身几何下则为部分弱共价空穴，单个氧上变化可达0.5 e。跨活性/环境边界的自旋分布使整数自旋投影带来至多0.26 e的选择（纳入两个Mn近邻后降至至多0.09 e）；扩展态搜索在两个泛函中发现被遗漏的更低态，使对氧自旋改变至多0.45 e。全部82次运行中嵌入平均场给出相同空穴（DMRG中位变化0.02-0.04 e），关联主要增加O-O共价性。

Conclusion: 不确定性的主导来源是态控制与几何一致性而非求解器，且总能量在30-40轨道下尚未收敛；面向量子求解器时嵌入哈密顿量必须为自旋非限制形式（共享轨道形式会反转空穴），基于采样的量子对角化（sample-based quantum diagonalization）可复现精确对角化，精度与经典选择CI相当但并不更优。

Abstract: Anion redox in Li-rich layered oxides places holes on oxygen next to an antiferromagnetic Mn(IV) d3 lattice, a strongly correlated open-shell problem for which projection-based wavefunction-in-DFT embedding offers a route to correlated and quantum solvers, but whose errors are known mainly for closed-shell molecules. We develop a spin-unrestricted projection-based embedding, exact at the DFT-in-DFT level to the microhartree scale for closed-shell, broken-symmetry and high-spin references with point charges, and apply DMRG-in-DFT to point-charge clusters of delithiated Li(2-x)MnO3, varying the environment functional (0-100% exact exchange), geometry, active region, active-space spin, virtual space, cluster size and magnetic reference. In every setting the lowest SCF state places the hole on an oxygen pair, with no O-O bond. How the hole is shared depends on the functional together with the geometry: hybrids give a full, uncoupled hole at the DFT+U geometry but a partial, weakly covalent hole at their own geometry, a change of up to 0.5 e on one oxygen. Where spin spreads across the active/environment boundary, the integer spin projection of the active space becomes a choice worth up to 0.26 e, and at most 0.09 e once the two Mn neighbors are included. An extended state search found missed lower states for two functionals, changing a pair-oxygen spin by up to 0.45 e. Across all 82 runs the embedded mean field gives the same hole (median DMRG change 0.02-0.04 e); correlation mainly adds O-O covalency. State control and geometry consistency, not the solver, dominate the uncertainty, and total energies are not converged at 30-40 orbitals. For quantum solvers, the embedded Hamiltonian must be spin-unrestricted: a shared-orbital form reverses the hole, while sample-based quantum diagonalization reproduces exact diagonalization, as accurately as, but not better than, classical selected CI.

</details>


### [195] [Fracture Mechanics and Self-Healing in Molecular Crystal Resonators for Lasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36692)
*Melchi Chosenyah,Vuppu Vinay Pradeep Kapil Yadav,Ankur Khapre,Biswajit Kumar Barman,Raghavaiah Pallepogu,Rajadurai Chandrasekar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用机械化光学（mechanophotonic）方法在有机光学晶体HDMAC中引入微尺度裂纹与完全断裂，证实其具备自愈合（self-healing）能力，并在愈合后保持光波导与谐振器功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微尺度损伤威胁微型化光子器件（photonic devices）的可靠性与寿命，而有机光学晶体中微裂纹与断裂的机制及修复问题尚缺乏研究。

Method: 用原子力显微镜（AFM）悬臂探针在HDMAC单晶中诱导微裂纹与完全断裂，考察沿(010)与(001)晶面的裂纹自愈合行为，并结合光学损耗测量、谐振器特性与纳米力学分析评估愈合效率。

Result: HDMAC微晶对沿(010)和(001)晶面引入的裂纹均表现自愈合，显示该现象跨不同晶体取向的普适性；研究识别了不同断裂机制，并建立了综合愈合效率评估方法。

Conclusion: 多维度评估方法可公平评价自愈合后结构完整性与光子功能性的恢复，为有机光学晶体的损伤修复与激光应用可靠性提供依据。

Abstract: Microscale damage poses a significant challenge to the reliability and longevity of miniaturized photonic devices; however, the mechanisms and repair of microscopic cracks and fractures in organic optical crystals remain largely unexplored. Here, we employ a mechanophotonic approach to induce microscale cracks and complete fractures in single crystals of 3-[4-(dimethylamino)phenyl]-1-(2-hydroxyphenyl)prop-2-en-1-one (HDMAC) using an atomic force microscopy (AFM) cantilever tip. We demonstrate the self-healing capability of these crystals while retaining their optical waveguiding and resonator properties, which are essential for lasing applications. HDMAC microcrystals exhibit self-healing of cracks introduced along the (010) and (001) crystallographic planes, demonstrating the versatility of this phenomenon across distinct crystal orientations. Furthermore, we identify different fracture mechanisms and establish a comprehensive assessment of healing efficiency by integrating optical loss measurements, resonator characteristics, and nanomechanical analyses. This multifaceted approach enables fair evaluation of the recovery of structural integrity and photonic functionality following self-healing.

</details>


### [196] [Spatial localization of magnetic-branch force contrasts in substitutional Mn-doped Ge: A first-principles study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36740)
*ChienHsu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要指出磁能隙（magnetic energy gap）无法反映磁态依赖原子力的空间分布，并通过对64位替代式Mn掺杂Ge的共线PBE计算，比较铁磁与反铁磁分支在相同核位置上的力，识别出配位数关联的力对比局域化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁能隙不能揭示磁态依赖的原子力（magnetic-state-dependent atomic forces）在空间上的分布，因此需要直接在固定核位置上比较不同磁序分支的受力差异。

Method: 采用共线PBE（collinear PBE）计算，在64位替代式Mn掺杂Ge（substitutional Mn-doped Ge）模型中，对铁磁（ferromagnetic）与反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）分支在完全相同的核位置上比较原子力；并使用四组数值设置检验桥位位移-能隙斜率与直接力导数的一致性。

Result: 最近邻Mn对（nearest-neighbour Mn pairs）的径向力投影在两磁分支间变号；第二近邻构型中，共用Ge近邻的力对比最大，约为仅配位一个Mn的同等距离Ge位的2.2倍。四组数值设置下桥位位移-能隙斜率与直接力导数一致；电子阈值收紧100倍后磁有序与主导位置不变，力对比矢量仅变化1.08%，而能隙几乎不变。

Conclusion: 结果在冻结模型（frozen model）内确认了磁分支力对比与配位相关的局域化，但未确立弛豫后的畸变、完整数值收敛性或唯一交换机制（exchange mechanism）。

Abstract: Magnetic energy gaps do not reveal the spatial distribution of magnetic-state-dependent atomic forces. We compare ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic forces at identical nuclear positions in 64-site substitutional Mn-doped Ge using collinear PBE calculations. For nearest-neighbour Mn pairs, the radial force projection reverses sign between magnetic branches. In the second-neighbour geometry, the shared Ge neighbour has the largest force contrast, about 2.2 times that of equally near Ge sites coordinated to one Mn. Bridge-displacement gap slopes agree with direct force derivatives across four numerical settings. Tightening the electronic threshold by 100-fold preserves magnetic ordering and the leading site, while the force-contrast vector changes by 1.08% despite a nearly unchanged energy gap. The results identify coordination-associated localization of magnetic-branch force contrasts within the frozen model, without establishing relaxed distortions, full numerical convergence or a unique exchange mechanism.

</details>


### [197] [Antisymmetric thermopower without magnetism in three-phase composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36742)
*Joohan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要提出无需磁性、仅凭三组各向同性相复合材料即可在三分或更高旋转轴下实现轴向热电势（axial thermopower），并证明在此对称约束下三相为最小数目。理论揭示其对三阶偏离项与三点手性关联的依赖，并给出可打印三维结构的定量预测与对称性开关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将温度梯度转为垂直于梯度的面内电场的轴向热电势此前依赖磁性；互易性（reciprocity）虽允许无磁性实现，但在三分或更高轴下，互易弹性带输运与两各向同性相的理想界面复合材料中该响应均消失，因而需要新的材料构型。

Method: 采用输运矩阵与对称性分析：论证三各向同性相复合可承载该响应且三相为最小，指出第三个线性独立输运矩阵（transport matrix）逃避两相精确归约；在弱对比下给出首个允许项为三次项，正比于三矩阵行列式与材料加权手性三点关联；并以颜色标记相、结合网状化学（reticular chemistry）晶体网络筛选可打印结构，辅以双侧界定（two-sided bounds）计算。

Result: 双侧界定给出平面单元轴向 Seebeck 系数 49.5–50.2 μV K⁻¹，可打印木堆（woodpile）结构为 76–84 μV K⁻¹；含轴平面的镜像反射反转响应，将 24 个网络重着色以恢复母对称性则关闭响应；线热源周围轴向驱动场呈直导线磁场式 1/r 方位角形式并驱动环流。

Conclusion: 无磁性轴向热电势可由三相各向同性复合材料实现，三为该对称约束下的最小相数，且其强度与符号由手性三点关联与对称性严格决定；该结果提供了可设计、可打印且可对称性开关的轴向热电响应平台。

Abstract: A thermopower that turns a temperature gradient into a perpendicular electric field in every in-plane direction has relied on magnetism. Reciprocity permits it without magnetism, yet under a three-fold or higher axis it vanishes both in reciprocal elastic band transport and in composites of two isotropic phases with ideal interfaces. Here we show that composites of three isotropic phases carry it, and that under such an axis three is the minimum. A third, linearly independent transport matrix escapes the exact two-phase reduction; at weak contrast the first allowed axial term is cubic, proportional to a determinant of the three matrices times material-weighted, handed three-point correlations. Treating the phases as colours identifies the arrangements that allow the response, and crystal nets from reticular chemistry supply printable three-dimensional ones. Certified two-sided bounds give axial Seebeck coefficients of 49.5-50.2 $μ$V K$^{-1}$ in a planar cell and 76-84 $μ$V K$^{-1}$ in a printable woodpile; reflections in planes containing the axis reverse the response, and recolouring 24 nets to restore their parent symmetry switches it off. Around a line heat source the axial driving field has the $1/r$ azimuthal form of the magnetic field of a straight wire and drives circulating currents.

</details>


### [198] [Charge density-supervised real-space representations for atomistic energy prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36779)
*Heting Liao,Masato Wakeda,Takahito Ohmura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出电荷密度引导图神经网络（charge-density-informed graph neural network, CDGNN），在等变原子图（equivariant atomic graph）上联合学习能量与价电子密度，并验证了密度监督对能量预测的收益具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态电子密度（ground-state electron density）足以确定基态能量并提供空间电子信息，但常规能量预测模型未充分利用该信息，故探索将其纳入能量回归的可行性。

Method: 构建CDGNN，将密度监督的实空间探针表示（real-space probe representation）融入全局能量回归器（global energy regressor），从等变原子图中同时学习能量与价电子密度（valence electron density）。

Result: 在QM9数据集上，CDGNN相对等变GNN基线（equivariant GNN baseline）降低了内插及向大分子外推的能量预测误差；在含H的Fe与Fe-C小数据集上与基线相当；并能准确预测分子及铁基体系的电荷密度分布，注意力分析显示Fe基结构中H原子附近探针权重更高。

Conclusion: 整合电荷密度信息用于能量预测具有潜力，但其收益依赖于具体任务与数据集，密度监督的价值呈数据集依赖特征。

Abstract: Ground-state electron density contains sufficient information to determine ground-state energy, while providing spatial electronic information. This study developed a charge-density-informed graph neural network (hereafter CDGNN) that jointly learns energy and valence electron density from equivariant atomic graphs. This approach incorporates density-supervised real-space probe representations into the global energy regressor, allowing density-related spatial information to contribute to energy prediction. On the QM9 dataset, CDGNN reduced the energy prediction errors relative to an equivariant GNN baseline for interpolation and extrapolation to larger molecules. For small H-containing Fe and Fe-C datasets, CDGNN performed similarly to the baseline, indicating the dataset-dependent benefits of density supervision. CDGNN also accurately predicted the charge density distributions for molecular and Fe-based systems. The attention analysis showed higher weights for probe representations near the H atoms in Fe-based structures. These findings highlight the potential of integrating charge-density information for energy prediction and its task-dependent benefits.

</details>


### [199] [Real-Space Selection Rule for Moiré Exciton-Phonon Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36782)
*Yixuan Liu,Shiyu Peng,Yifan Wang,Peiyao Qin,Wang Yao,Chen Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立了基于多体Bethe-Salpeter方程与声子微扰理论统一框架的莫尔激子-声子耦合（ExPC）实空间表征，揭示了电子-空穴空间匹配与相干干涉共同决定耦合强度的实空间选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格中激子-声子耦合（exciton-phonon coupling, ExPC）主导光激发量子材料中的能量弛豫与激子动力学，但莫尔激子与莫尔声子耦合的微观机制尚不清楚。

Method: 构建莫尔ExPC的实空间表示，其理论基础为结合多体Bethe-Salpeter方程（Bethe-Salpeter equation）与声子微扰理论（phonon perturbation theory）的统一理论框架。

Result: 提出并验证一条实空间选择规则（real-space selection rule）：莫尔激子电子与空穴密度同莫尔声子诱导势扰动的空间匹配决定各自耦合贡献，电子与空穴贡献间的相干干涉（coherent interference）控制总ExPC。在WSe2/WS2莫尔异质双层中展示出显著的激子与声子模式依赖耦合，包括由电子-空穴相消干涉导致的ExPC近完全抑制。

Conclusion: 莫尔激子的内部电子-空穴空间构型是决定其与莫尔声子耦合强度及模式选择性的关键自由度，为通过空间剪裁莫尔势工程化ExPC提供了途径。

Abstract: Exciton-phonon coupling (ExPC) governs energy relaxation and exciton dynamics in photoexcited quantum materials. Recent advances have revealed rich and tunable excitonic phenomena in moiré superlattices, yet the microscopic mechanisms underlying the coupling between moiré excitons and moiré phonons remain elusive. Here, we develop a real-space representation for moiré ExPC based on a unified theoretical framework combining the many-body Bethe-Salpeter equation with phonon perturbation theory. Furthermore, we uncover a general real-space selection rule: the spatial matching of a moiré exciton's electron and hole densities with the moiré-phonon-induced potential perturbations determines their individual coupling contributions, while coherent interference between the electron and hole contributions controls the total ExPC. We demonstrate this rule in prototypical WSe2/WS2 moiré heterobilayers, revealing pronounced exciton- and phonon-mode-dependent coupling, including nearly complete suppression of ExPC through destructive electron-hole interference. These findings establish the internal electron-hole spatial configuration of moiré excitons as a key degree of freedom governing the strength and mode selectivity of their coupling to moiré phonons, opening opportunities to engineer ExPC through spatially tailored moiré potentials.

</details>


### [200] [HorizON: A Multi-Horizon Deep Operator Network for Property Prediction of Organic Battery Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36860)
*Subhash V. S. Ganti,Lukas Woelfel,Arla Diengdoh,Tzvetan Mihaylov,Thomas Nevolianis,Jan Meyer,Christopher Kuenneth*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将原有的数据融合多任务框架扩展为多步长（multi-horizon）预测，提出多保真度多步长深度算子网络（Multi-horizon Deep Operator Network），在单一模型中同时学习DFT氧化还原电位（redox potential）与随循环变化的比容量轨迹，并以完整电池堆栈（battery-stack）表征与使用条件为输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属基电池依赖锂、钴、镍等关键元素，其开采带来环境与供应链（supply-chain）压力；有机电极材料（organic electrode materials, OEMs）可降低依赖并具备分子可调性，但长期循环稳定性（cycling stability）有限，且电压、比容量、容量保持率、能量密度等耦合指标需联合优化并依赖整个电池堆栈而非仅活性材料。

Method: 在先前面向平均放电电压与首圈比容量（均为单值目标）的数据融合多任务框架（data-fusion multi-task framework）基础上，扩展为逐循环预测放电轨迹属性的多步长预测；采用多保真度、多步长的深度算子网络（Deep Operator Network），在完整电池堆栈表征与使用条件下训练。

Result: 模型在同一框架内学习DFT氧化还原电位与循环依赖的比容量（specific capacity）轨迹；因训练输入包含完整电池堆栈表征与使用条件，模型学到的是电池堆栈内部相互作用（interactions）而非仅正极（cathode）效应。

Conclusion: 该框架支持有机电池体系的长期性能预测、逆向设计（inverse design）与工艺优化（process optimization）。

Abstract: Transition-metal-based batteries rely on critical elements such as lithium, cobalt, and nickel. Their extraction creates environmental and supply-chain challenges. Organic electrode materials (OEMs) reduce this reliance and provide broad molecular tunability. However, OEM-based batteries still show limited long-term cycling stability. Their design also requires the joint optimization of coupled metrics such as voltage, specific capacity, capacity retention, and energy density. These metrics depend on the full battery stack and on the interactions among its components rather than on the active material alone. In our previous work, we developed a data-fusion multi-task framework for average discharge voltage and first-cycle specific capacity. Both targets are single-valued. Here, we extend that framework to multi-horizon prediction by predicting a property at every cycle of a discharge trajectory rather than at a single cycle. Our multi-fidelity and Multi-horizon Deep Operator Network learns DFT redox potentials and cycle-dependent specific-capacity trajectories within one model. We train it on complete battery-stack representations and usage conditions, so it learns battery-stack interactions rather than cathode-only effects. This framework supports long-term property prediction, inverse design, and process optimization for organic battery systems.

</details>


### [201] [ZnO/Ni interface-activated enhancement of spintronic terahertz emission from Ni/Pt bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36889)
*Jia Xu,Shaohua Zhang,Xianguo Jiang,Lei Hao,Yaxuan Jin,Ning Yang,Yao Wang,Tong Fu,Hao Meng,Chao Lu,Wendeng Huang,Chao Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在Ni/Pt下方插入ZnO形成ZnO/Ni/Pt异质结，可在保持Ni/Pt直接接触的前提下将自旋太赫兹发射幅度提升至约2.8倍，归因于短程ZnO/Ni界面效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于铁磁/重金属双层（ferromagnet/heavy-metal bilayer）的自旋电子太赫兹发射器可高效产生宽带太赫兹辐射，但Ni/Pt双层的发射强度较弱，限制了其作为高效发射器的应用。

Method: 制备ZnO/Ni/Pt异质结构，将ZnO置于Ni下方并保留Ni/Pt直接接触；开展ZnO、Ni、Pt厚度依赖测量，并以Al2O3、Cu、Fe对照样品排除单纯底层、光学或生长质量因素。

Result: 太赫兹幅度相较Ni/Pt参考样品最高提升约2.8倍；发射随磁场反转极性，并符合超快自旋-电荷转换（spin-to-charge conversion）预期的角度依赖；发射仍由Ni/Pt转换过程主导，但受ZnO/Ni底界面强烈影响。

Conclusion: 增强源于短程ZnO/Ni界面效应，可能涉及氧相关缺陷（oxygen-related defects）、界面电场及Ni界面态的改变；该工作提出一种半导体/铁磁界面工程（interface-engineering）策略以激活弱Ni/Pt自旋太赫兹发射器。

Abstract: Spintronic terahertz (THz) emitters based on ferromagnet/heavy-metal bilayers provide an efficient route for generating broadband THz radiation through ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion. However, Ni/Pt bilayers usually exhibit relatively weak THz emission, limiting their use as efficient spintronic emitters. Here, we report an interface-assisted enhancement of THz emission in ZnO/Ni/Pt heterostructures. By placing ZnO underneath Ni while preserving the direct Ni/Pt contact, the THz amplitude is increased by up to approximately 2.8 times compared with the Ni/Pt reference. The enhanced emission reverses polarity with the magnetic field and follows the expected angular dependence of ultrafast spin-to-charge conversion. ZnO-, Ni-, and Pt-thickness-dependent measurements show that the emission remains governed by the Ni/Pt conversion process but is strongly affected by the ZnO/Ni bottom interface. Control samples with Al2O3, Cu, and Fe further exclude a simple underlayer, optical, or growth-quality origin. The enhancement is attributed to a short-range ZnO/Ni interface effect, possibly involving oxygen-related defects, interfacial electric fields, and modification of the Ni interfacial state. This work provides a semiconductor/ferromagnet interface-engineering strategy for activating weak Ni/Pt spintronic THz emitters.

</details>


### [202] [The role of A in ARPES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36909)
*Sergey Borisenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，当前对角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）数据的主流处理方式——将二维强度分布（坐标轴为结合能与动量）直接与布里渊区高对称方向能带计算比较——属于过度简化的处理，会导致对实验结果的错误解读，并提出在数据采集与分析中避免该问题的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES自20世纪70—90年代发展为动量分辨的固体电子结构探测手段，但其基本结果如今几乎被固化为“结合能—动量”二维强度分布，并与高对称方向能带计算直接比对；作者认为这一惯例掩盖了处理上的过度简化问题，需要加以纠正。

Method: 摘要未给出具体技术细节，仅说明通过论证说明现有处理方式过度简化，并针对数据采集（data collection）与数据分析（data analysis）两个环节提出实用性的规避方案。

Result: 摘要未报告定量结果或实验数据，核心论断是常规ARPES数据处理方式会产生对实验结果的错误解释（incorrect interpretations）。

Conclusion: ARPES数据的简化处理惯例需被重新审视；作者建议在采集与分析阶段采用具体可行的做法以避免误读，但摘要未展开这些做法的具体内容。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) was developed in the 1970s-1990s as a momentum-resolved probe of the electronic structure of solids, with its basic principles summarized in early seminal works and classic reviews. The basic ARPES result is currently almost exclusively associated with the two-dimensional intensity distribution with coordinate axes labeled as Binding Energy and Momentum. Such datasets are usually directly compared to the band structure calculations along high symmetry directions of the Brillouin zone. Here we show that this is the result of an oversimplified treatment of the ARPES data leading to incorrect interpretations of the experimental results. We suggest practical ways of how to avoid it in both data collection and analysis.

</details>


### [203] [Spontaneous topological diode effect induced by non-coplanar antiferromagnetic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37003)
*Nguyen Duy Khanh,Hirotaka Takagi,Rina Takagi,Susumu Minami,Tatsuto Hatanaka,Hikaru Watanabe,Michi-To Suzuki,Yuki Yanagi,Aki Kitaori,Takuya Nomoto,Ryotaro Arita,Shinichiro Seki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在手性晶格非共面反铁磁体 CoTa3S6 中，零磁场下观测到沿 [001] 方向的非互易电子输运（non-reciprocal electron transport），即“自发拓扑二极管效应（spontaneous topological diode effect）”，其幅值由反铁磁序参量而非磁化强度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易输运通常源于相对论性自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling），并需外磁场或铁磁磁化支撑，零场反铁磁体一般缺乏此类整流效应；尽管理论预言非共面反铁磁序可在无自旋轨道耦合、无磁化、无外场条件下产生大非互易性，但尚未在真实材料中得到验证。

Method: 对 CoTa3S6 开展零场下的非线性电阻率（nonlinear resistivity）测量，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）分析能带色散（band dispersion）与磁序的关系。

Result: 零场下沿 [001] 方向观察到非互易输运，其幅度超过典型铁磁体，且由反铁磁序参量（antiferromagnetic order parameter）而非磁化强度决定；计算表明非共面反铁磁序在无自旋轨道耦合时即可诱导不对称能带色散，证实该响应的非相对论起源。

Conclusion: 研究确立了反铁磁畴（antiferromagnetic domains）电学读出（electrical readout）的新机制，并将非共面反铁磁体（non-coplanar antiferromagnets）确立为不依赖自旋轨道耦合或磁化即可实现铁磁类功能响应的候选材料平台。

Abstract: In non-centrosymmetric bulk materials, electrons flowing in the opposite directions often exhibit different properties. This diode-like effect (i.e., non-reciprocal electron transport) typically originates from relativistic spin-orbit coupling, and its amplitude scales with external magnetic field or magnetization in ferromagnets. Although such rectification is usually absent in antiferromagnets at zero field, recent theories predict that non-coplanar antiferromagnetic order can host large non-reciprocity even without spin-orbit coupling, magnetization or external magnetic fields. This phenomenon - the spontaneous topological diode effect - can potentially be used for efficient electrical readout of antiferromagnetic domains, while it has not been verified for real materials. Here, we report the observation of this effect in a chiral-lattice non-coplanar antiferromagnet CoTa3S6. Nonlinear resistivity measurements reveal the non reciprocal nature of electron transport along the [001] direction at zero field. The observed non-reciprocity exceeds that in typical ferromagnets, whose amplitude is governed by antiferromagnetic order parameter rather than magnetization. First-principles calculations show that the non-coplanar antiferromagnetic order induces asymmetric band dispersions even without spin-orbit coupling, establishing the non-relativistic origin of the observed response. The present findings demonstrate a new mechanism for efficient electrical readout of antiferromagnetic domains, and highlight non-coplanar antiferromagnets as a promising material platform for realizing ferromagnet-like functional responses without relying on spin-orbit coupling or magnetization.

</details>


### [204] [Prussian blue and Prussian white thin-film electrodes: Single-step electrodeposition for anode and cathode applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37023)
*Michael Pohlitz,Mohammed Fayis Kalady,Larissa de O. Garcia,Lindiomar Borges de Avila,Darius Pohl,Falk Röder,Flavio Abreu Araujo,Axel Lubk,Daniel Wolf,Christian K. Müller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在 -0.20 至 +0.40 V（vs. SCE）范围内单步恒电位沉积固定 10 mC 电荷的普鲁士蓝（Prussian Blue, PB）/普鲁士白（Prussian White, PW）族薄膜，揭示沉积电位对化学组成与纳米尺度形貌的非单调调控，并指出晶格参数不足以区分 PB/PW 相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PB/PW 族薄膜的沉积条件与其化学状态、纳米结构之间的关系尚不清晰，而这是评估其离子存储、传感等功能的前提，故需建立电位—组成—形貌的实验对应关系。

Method: 在 Au/Cr/Si 基底上由同一水浴以单步恒电位法生长薄膜（-0.20 至 +0.40 V vs. SCE，固定沉积电荷 10 mC），并采用电子显微镜、元素分析（EDS）、X 射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, XPS）与拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）进行表征，辅以截面元素分布 mapping。

Result: 低电位沉积物中 K 掺入更多，Fe 2p 与拉曼特征更接近还原态 PW 环境；表面 K/Fe 比在 1.129–1.322 间非单调变化（占检测 K+Fe 总量的 52.95–56.94%）。形貌呈非单调演化：+0.30 V 为大面状晶粒，+0.40 V 为细小锥状晶与裂纹。PB 样与 PW 样区域的局域立方晶格参数分别为 (10.31±0.10) Å 与 (10.36±0.10) Å，误差重叠，无法仅凭晶格间距区分；截面 mapping 显示局域连续涂层内骨架元素分布基本均匀。

Conclusion: 研究将沉积条件与不同的化学及形貌初始状态相关联，但未确定体相化学计量（bulk stoichiometry）或相纯度（phase purity）。作者提出离子存储、传感、电致变色（electrochromism）、阻变（resistive switching）与离子处理等方向作为后续需功能验证的研究机会。

Abstract: This study examines how deposition potential influences the composition and nanoscale structure of Prussian Blue (PB)/Prussian White (PW)-family films, providing an experimental basis for assessing their proposed functions. Films were grown directly on Au/Cr/Si in a single potentiostatic step from a common aqueous bath at $-0.20$ to $+0.40$\,V versus a saturated calomel electrode, with a fixed deposition charge of 10\,mC. Electron microscopy, elemental analysis, X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy, and Raman spectroscopy revealed potential-dependent chemistry and nonmonotonic morphology. EDS indicated greater K incorporation in lower-potential deposits, whose Fe~2p and Raman features were consistent with more reduced, PW-like environments. Surface K/Fe ratios varied nonmonotonically from 1.129 to 1.322 across the series, corresponding to 52.95--56.94\% K within the detected K+Fe population. Larger faceted crystallites at $+0.30$\,V contrasted with finer pyramids and cracks at $+0.40$\,V. Selected PB-like and PW-like regions had local cubic lattice parameters of $(10.31\pm0.10)$\,Å and $(10.36\pm0.10)$\,Å, respectively; their overlapping uncertainties prevent discrimination by lattice spacing alone. Cross-sectional maps showed broadly uniform framework-element distributions within locally continuous coatings. Together, these observations relate deposition conditions to contrasting chemical and morphological starting states without establishing bulk stoichiometry or phase purity. A literature-grounded outlook identifies ion storage, sensing, electrochromism, resistive switching, and ion handling as research opportunities requiring functional validation in these films.

</details>


### [205] [First-Principles Investigation of Irida-Graphane: A Wide-Band-Gap Semiconductor Derived from Irida-Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37095)
*Djardiel S. Gomes,Isaac M. Félix,Marcelo L. Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统研究了"铱石墨烷"（irida-graphane），即金属性二维碳同素异形体铱石墨烯（irida-graphene）的全氢化衍生物：其全氢化使金属态转为间接宽带隙半导体，并具备良好热稳定性、力学各向同性及深紫外强吸收特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金属性二维碳同素异形体中打开能隙（band gap）是其应用于电子与光电器件的重要前提，而全氢化（full hydrogenation）已被证明是实现该目标的有效途径。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT），在PBE与HSE06两个泛函水平上计算结构、力学、电子与光学性质，并结合声子（phonon）计算与从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics, AIMD）模拟。

Result: 所有碳原子为sp³杂化，氢原子位于褶皱碳骨架两侧，晶格参数相对铱石墨烯收缩4.3%；氢化能为−0.53 eV/H（放热）；无声子虚频且2000 K下结构稳定；力学各向同性，面内刚度约156 N/m、泊松比约0.1、临界应变约20%；带隙为4.68 eV（PBE）与6.0 eV（HSE06），可见光区透明（反射率低于0.3%），深紫外吸收系数达10⁵–10⁶ cm⁻¹量级。

Conclusion: 铱石墨烷可作为紫外光电子学（ultraviolet optoelectronics）与透明涂层（transparent coatings）的候选宽带隙（wide-gap）材料。

Abstract: Opening a band gap in metallic two-dimensional carbon allotropes is an important step toward their use in electronic and optoelectronic devices, and full hydrogenation has proven to be an effective route to achieve it. Here, we propose irida-graphane, the fully hydrogenated derivative of the irida-graphene, a monolayer composed of three-, six-, and eight-membered rings, and investigate its structural, mechanical, electronic, and optical properties using density functional theory (DFT) at the PBE and HSE06 levels combined with phonon and \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations. All carbon atoms adopt $sp^3$ hybridization, with the hydrogen atoms of the three- and six-membered rings bonded to opposite sides of a buckled carbon framework, and the lattice parameter contracts by 4.3% with respect to irida-graphene. The hydrogenation energy of $-0.53$ eV/H indicates that the hydrogenation of irida-graphene from molecular hydrogen is exothermic. The absence of imaginary phonon frequencies confirms the dynamical stability of irida-graphane, and AIMD simulations show that its structure remains intact at temperatures up to 2000 K. Irida-graphane is mechanically isotropic, with an in-plane stiffness of approximately 156 N/m, a Poisson's ratio of approximately 0.1, and critical strains of approximately 20%. Hydrogenation transforms the metallic parent structure into a semiconductor with an indirect band gap of 4.68 eV (PBE) and 6.0 eV (HSE06). Irida-graphane is transparent in the visible range, with reflectivity below 0.3%, and absorbs strongly in the deep ultraviolet, with absorption coefficients of the order of $10^{5} - 10^{6}$ cm$^{-1}$. These results suggest irida-graphane as a candidate wide-gap material for ultraviolet optoelectronics and transparent coatings.

</details>


### [206] [Magnetic crystal generation with site-resolved magnetic moments via Gaussian-mixture retargeting and finite-budget yield prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37222)
*Wen-Chuang Shang,Chenyu Shen,Mikito Tsujimoto,Tomonori Tanaka,Yoshihiro Gohda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GMMag 是一个基于条件变分自编码器（conditional variational autoencoder）的工作流，可同时生成晶体结构及其位点分辨磁矩（site-resolved magnetic moments），并利用高斯混合采样（Gaussian-mixture sampling）在不重新训练的情况下导向指定的化学与磁性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是构建能够同时控制化学成分与磁性目标、且无需重新训练的晶体结构生成方法，并系统理解模型设计与采样策略对生成质量、多样性和候选积累的影响。

Method: 方法采用图神经网络编码器（graph neural network encoder）与 Transformer 解码器（transformer decoder）构成的条件变分自编码器；通过高斯混合采样实现目标导向生成，并系统考察编码器/解码器设计、磁矩大小与相对符号表示以及采样选择的影响。

Result: 结果显示，更高的筛选通过率（screening pass rate）并不必然带来更多具有参考数据集未出现化学式的合格候选；论文对同一化学式重复生成限制候选积累的机制进行建模，并通过两次各一千万次抽取（ten-million-draw）的实验加以检验。

Conclusion: 作为应用，该工作流识别出若干含 Nd 候选，其化学式未见于参考数据集，同时具有负形成能（negative formation energies）与较高的估计饱和磁化强度（estimated saturation magnetization）。

Abstract: We introduce GMMag, a workflow for generating crystal structures together with their site-resolved magnetic moments, built on a conditional variational autoencoder with a graph neural network encoder and a transformer decoder. Gaussian-mixture sampling redirects generation towards selected chemical and magnetic targets without retraining. We systematically investigate how encoder and decoder design, the representation of magnetic-moment magnitudes and relative signs, and sampling choices affect reconstruction, generation quality and diversity. Because a higher screening pass rate does not necessarily yield more qualifying candidates with distinct chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, we model how repeated generation of the same chemical formulas limits their accumulation and test the model in two separate ten-million-draw runs. As an application, the workflow identifies Nd-containing candidates with chemical formulas absent from the reference dataset, negative formation energies and high estimated saturation magnetization.

</details>


### [207] [Enhancing magneto-optical coupling and scattering from magnetic vortices by optical polarization texturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37233)
*Vakhtang Jandieri,Chenglong Jia,Ramaz Khomeriki,Nicolas Tsagareli,Qian Li,Douglas H. Werner,Jamal Berakdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用球形严格耦合波方法（spherical rigorous coupled-wave method）研究结构光场与磁涡旋（magnetic vortex）的耦合与散射，揭示空间非共线磁化对偏振转换、能流与磁化动力学的调控，并证明偏振织构光场可在纳秒尺度驱动涡核进动乃至翻转核极性与手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁织构（magnetic textures）为光子功能提供新途径：电磁场不仅由材料组成与几何结构控制，也受内部磁构型调控；反过来，场的空间织构可用于增强与磁性物质的有效耦合，并作用于涡旋拓扑与磁动力学。

Method: 建立球形严格耦合波方法（spherical rigorous coupled-wave method），在其中引入与涡旋织构相关的空间依赖各向异性磁导率（anisotropic magnetic permeability）；据此计算结构电磁场被磁涡旋散射与耦合的过程，并比较线偏振与径向偏振（radially polarized）照明。

Result: 磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）的非对角分量介导向入射场中不存在的互补偏振通道的转换；生成场沿涡旋磁化分布呈现显著空间局域化，并具有非零的方位向电磁能流分量。线偏振与径向偏振照明的散射场振幅相差约一个数量级，说明入射场与各向异性磁响应的空间重叠至关重要。数值模拟通过逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）定量展示了结构有效场激励整流对涡旋动力学的反作用。

Conclusion: 偏振织构光场可驱动涡核进动（vortex-core precession），并在足够强激励下于纳秒时间尺度翻转核极性（core polarity）与手性（chirality），表明磁织构与结构光场的耦合可作为光控磁拓扑的有效手段。

Abstract: Magnetic textures offer a route toward photonic functionality in which electromagnetic fields are controlled not only by material composition and geometry but also by the internal magnetic configuration. Conversely, spatial texturing of these fields is a qualitatively new tool to increase the effective coupling strength to magnetic matter and to act on the the vortex topology and magnetic dynamics. Here, we investigate the coupling and scattering of structured electromagnetic fields by a magnetic vortex, focusing on how spatially non-collinear magnetization governs polarization conversion, electromagnetic energy flow, and magnetization dynamics. We develop a spherical rigorous coupled-wave method that incorporates the spatially dependent anisotropic magnetic permeability associated with the vortex texture. The calculations show that the off-diagonal components of the magnetic susceptibility mediate conversion into complementary polarization channels that are absent from the incident field. The generated fields exhibit pronounced spatial localization associated with the vortex magnetization profile and support a nonzero azimuthal component of electromagnetic energy flow. A comparison between linearly and radially polarized illumination reveals an approximately one-order-of-magnitude difference in the scattered fields amplitudes due to polarization texturing, demonstrating the importance of spatial overlap between the incident field and the anisotropic magnetic response. The back-action on the magnetic vortex dynamics is quantitatively demonstrated by numerical simulations for the inverse Faraday effect due to the rectification of structured effective-field excitation. It is shown that polarization textured optical fields can drive vortex-core precession and, at sufficiently strong excitation, reverse the core polarity and chirality on nanosecond time scale.

</details>


### [208] [Unconventional SOC-activated Altermagnetic Spin Splitting and Half-Angle Locking of Spin Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37367)
*Weiwei Chen,Ziyu Zhou,Pan Gao,Yanan Pan,Zhongjun Li,Ye Yang,Jianhui Zhou,Rui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出自旋轨道耦合（SOC）与交错交换可共同激活非相对论性自旋分裂消失平面上的额外自旋分裂，并在六角α-MnTe中出现绕Γ点、面外自旋极化的d波分裂，其角向图样随面内Néel矢量旋转半角反向共转，进而导致Néel矢量取向与自旋电导极大方向之间近似半角锁定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规交错磁（altermagnetic）自旋分裂源自交错交换与子格依赖的各向异性跃迁，无需自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）；但在非相对论贡献消失的动量平面上，自旋分裂机制及其与输运的关联尚不清晰，需明确SOC与交错交换的联合作用。

Method: 推导低能有效模型（low-energy effective model）解析SOC与交错交换联合激活的自旋分裂；以六角α-MnTe为对象开展第一性原理自旋输运（first-principles spin-transport）计算，并基于有效模型进行解析输运计算以追溯角向耦合来源。

Result: 在α-MnTe中，SOC激活的贡献在Γ点附近产生d波自旋分裂并具有面外自旋极化，其主导角向图样以面内Néel矢量旋转角的一半反向共转；第一性原理计算显示Néel矢量取向与使有符号纵向、横向自旋电导（signed longitudinal and transverse spin conductivities）最大的电场方向之间近似呈半角锁定。

Conclusion: SOC激活的交错磁自旋分裂与方向性自旋输运由同一动量—Néel矢量角向耦合所支配，从而将微观分裂机制与输运现象联系起来，为探测Néel矢量取向及调控自旋流产生提供理论基础。

Abstract: Conventional altermagnetic spin splitting arises from staggered exchange and sublattice-dependent anisotropic hopping without requiring spin-orbit coupling (SOC). Here we derive a low-energy effective model showing that SOC and staggered exchange can jointly activate additional spin splitting on momentum planes where the nonrelativistic contribution vanishes. In hexagonal $α$-MnTe, this SOC-activated contribution produces d-wave spin splitting near $Γ$ with out-of-plane spin polarization. Its leading angular pattern counter-rotates by half the in-plane Néel-vector rotation angle. First-principles spin-transport calculations reveal a corresponding approximate half-angle locking between the Néel-vector orientation and the electric-field directions maximizing the signed longitudinal and transverse spin conductivities. Analytical transport calculations based on the effective model trace this locking to the same angular coupling between momentum and the Néel vector that governs the spin-splitting pattern. Our findings connect the microscopic mechanism of SOC-activated altermagnetic spin splitting to directional spin transport, providing a theoretical basis for probing Néel-vector orientation and controlling spin-current generation.

</details>


### [209] [Back-End-Of-Line Integration of 2-Terminal Ferroelectric Hafnia Synaptic Weights on Commercial CMOS Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37530)
*Alexander Flasby,Anwesha Panda,Nathan Savoia,Laura Bégon-Lours*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报告了在商用180 nm CMOS后道工序（BEOL）中首次集成两端铁电突触权重（ferroelectric synaptic weights），采用WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2]双层材料栈，并借助硬件感知（hardware-aware）神经网络仿真揭示热预算与在线训练性能之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将新兴材料功能化到CMOS后道工序（back-end-of-line, BEOL）是实现神经网络加速器与神经形态系统（neuromorphic systems）的关键路径，需要在已交付的商用芯片上实现突触器件的单片集成。

Method: 提出一种新材料栈：将WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2]双层结构夹在TiN与Mo电极之间，并在商用180 nm CMOS裸片的BEOL中集成两端铁电突触器件；分别在450 °C与500 °C下退火，并采用硬件感知神经网络仿真评估。

Result: 450 °C退火的器件可在低于1.8 V下编程，循环间波动（cycle-to-cycle variation）低于3 %；500 °C退火的突触权重表现出更渐进的权重更新（gradual weight updates），且底层NMOS晶体管在完整集成流程后仍保持功能。

Conclusion: 热预算（thermal budget）与器件级训练性能之间存在权衡，该权衡通过硬件感知仿真得到说明；所提方案验证了在商用CMOS后道集成铁电突触权重的可行性。

Abstract: Functionalising the back-end-of-line (BEOL) of CMOS with emerging materials is a key route to neural network accelerators and neuromorphic systems. In this work, we demonstrate for the first time the integration of 2-terminal ferroelectric synaptic weights in the BEOL of as-delivered commercial 180 nm CMOS dies. A novel material stack is proposed, in which a WOx/[HfO2-ZrO2] bilayer is sandwiched between TiN and Mo electrodes. For devices annealed at 450 °C, programming below 1.8 V is achieved with a cycle-to-cycle variation below 3 %. Synaptic weights annealed at 500 °C exhibit more gradual weight updates, while the tested underlying NMOS transistors remain functional after the complete integration process. The trade-off between thermal budget and on-device training performance is illustrated using hardware-aware neural network simulations.

</details>


### [210] [The Acridine Polymorph Landscape: Characterization and Selective Control by Additives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37597)
*Adam Lahouari,Hengyu Zhou,Nikolaos Galanakis,Michael Kilgour,Daisuke Kuroshima,Jihye Han,Bart Kahr,Mark E. Tuckerman,Alexander G. Shtukenberg,Jutta Rogal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与理论，解析吖啶（acridine）的多晶型景观（polymorph landscape），并揭示添加剂选择性控制晶型的机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体多晶型（polymorphism）是控制结晶过程的主要难题，需理解晶型结构与热力学稳定性及添加剂作用之间的关系。

Method: 采用熔融结晶实验解析未知的VIII型结构；用拓扑晶体结构预测（topological crystal structure prediction）复现已知无溶剂化物多晶型；结合微调机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential）与非谐自由能（anharmonic free energy）计算评估稳定性；并将添加剂诱导的晶型偏好与混合晶体取代缺陷能及拓扑相似性关联。

Result: 实验解析出VIII型新结构，预测方法复现全部七种已知结构的无溶剂化物多晶型；计算重现实验稳定性关系并显示浅自由能景观（shallow free energy landscape）；添加剂诱导的晶型偏好与取代缺陷能及晶体结构拓扑相似性相关。

Conclusion: 研究构建了吖啶多晶型的整体图景，将晶体结构、热力学稳定性与添加剂控制结晶三者相联系。

Abstract: Polymorphism in molecular crystals presents a major challenge for controlling crystallization.
