Here, we combine experiment and theory to characterize the polymorph landscape of acridine and investigate its selective control by additives. Melt crystallization experiments resolve the previously unknown structure of form VIII, while topological crystal structure prediction recovers all seven known unsolvated polymorphs with determined crystal structures. Combining a fine-tuned machine-learned interatomic potential with anharmonic free energy calculations reproduces the experimentally observed stability relationships and reveals a shallow free energy landscape. Finally, experimentally observed additive-induced polymorph preferences correlate with mixed-crystal substitutional defect energies and topological similarities between additive and acridine crystal structures. Together, these results provide a comprehensive picture of acridine polymorphism, linking crystal structure, thermodynamic stability, and additive-controlled crystallization.

</details>

### [211] [The Glass Transition: From Competing Theories to Discriminating Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36071)
*Massimo Pica Ciamarra*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文指出，近期数值模拟的两方面进展（深度平衡与理论特指量的直接测量）正使玻璃化转变减速机制的各竞争理论从“均能解释现象”转向可被判别性检验，但尚未形成唯一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体接近玻璃转变（glass transition）时的显著减速现象仍未解决，主因是热力学、促进（facilitation）、弹性、激发（excitation）等多种成熟且相互对立的框架都能解释玻璃态唯象学的主要特征，故与常见观测相符不足以判别微观机制。

Method: 评述两类互补的数值进展：一是深度平衡（deep equilibration）将受控平衡研究拓展至以往只能靠外推获得的区域，从而在更宽动力学范围检验既有预言；二是新数值方法可直接获取构型熵（configurational entropy）、静态长度（static lengths）、弹性性质与局部激活势垒（activation barriers）等理论特指量。

Result: 这些进展对热力学、促进基、弹性与激发基描述构成约束，产生一系列判别力递增的检验，可揭示看似成功的规律在何处失去普适性，并识别因果性控制激活势垒的微观要素。

Conclusion: 当前图景尚非唯一理论，而是判别性不断增强的检验集合；其价值在于定位各理论的适用边界并厘清控制激活势垒的微观成分。

Abstract: The dramatic slowing down of liquids approaching the glass transition remains unresolved, partly because competing theoretical explanations are difficult to discriminate. Indeed, several mature, contrasting frameworks account for the main features of glassy phenomenology, making agreement with familiar observations a weak basis for identifying the microscopic mechanism responsible for the slowdown. Recent numerical advances are changing this situation in two complementary ways. Deep equilibration extends controlled equilibrium studies into regimes previously accessible only by extrapolation, allowing established predictions to be stress-tested over a broader dynamical range. At the same time, new numerical methods increasingly provide direct access to theory-specific quantities such as configurational entropy, static lengths, elastic properties, and local activation barriers. I discuss how these developments constrain thermodynamic, facilitation-based, elastic, and excitation-based descriptions. The emerging picture is not yet a unique theory, but a set of increasingly discriminating tests that can reveal where apparently successful laws cease to be universal and identify the microscopic ingredients that causally control the activation barrier.

</details>


### [212] [No friction required: Self-locking in entangled assemblies of curved filaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36330)
*Benjamin R. Greenvall,Jay van den Berg,Ross E. Meinhart,Patrick A. Staudenmayer,Alfred J. Crosby,Gregory M. Grason*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证了“纯弹性内聚（purely elastic cohesion）”机制：内禀曲率细丝（intrinsically-curved filaments）组装体无需摩擦或化学键，仅靠抵抗形变的弹性力与无牵引排斥力的竞争即可获得结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为绳状组装体的结构完整性依赖外加载荷与细丝间摩擦（inter-filament friction）共同防止自发解缠（如断绳散股），该文旨在质疑这一长期图景并探寻无吸引性物理/化学键条件下维持结构的内聚机制。

Method: 构建无摩擦（friction-free）的内禀螺旋细丝组装模型，考察细丝数量与偏好形状的影响；提出广义断裂准则（generalized fracture criterion）用于判断自发解缠，并通过含摩擦细丝的实验与模拟进行验证。

Result: 拥挤效应产生弹性预应力（elastic pre-stresses），使组装体或实现自锁缠结（self-locking entanglement），或通过类孤子解缠前沿（soliton-like detanglement front）的传播发生自发解缠；实验与模拟证实形状弹性代价对自锁的作用，并表明存在一类无论摩擦系数多大都无法自锁的束状结构。

Conclusion: “纯弹性内聚”机制可推广解释更广泛缠结结构中的自锁现象，并为可刺激响应组装与解组装的新型活性细丝材料（active filament materials）提供设计原理。

Abstract: Filaments are incredible material building blocks that, when entangled together, form robust structures with remarkable adaptive mechanics, from stiff cables to stretchable knits. Basic questions about the mechanism that holds together such structures, especially assemblies like rope, without attractive physical or chemical bonds between constituents are among the oldest considered in materials science. Contradicting a long-standing picture that structural integrity of rope-like assemblies rely on a combination of external loading and inter-filament friction to prevent spontaneous detangling (e.g. fraying of a cut rope), we demonstrate that assemblies of intrinsically-curved filaments can achieve structural integrity purely from the competition of elastic forces that resist shape-change and (traction-free) repulsive forces that prevent overlap. We analyze a friction-free model of assembly of intrinsically-helical filaments, and show that depending on filament number and preferred filament shape, overcrowding in the assemblies generate elastic pre-stresses that give rise to either self-locking entanglement or spontaneous detanglement through propagation of a soliton-like detanglement front through assemblies. We test a generalized fracture criterion for spontaneous detangling and confirm the role of shape-elastic costs to self-locking via experiment and simulation of frictional filaments, demonstrating a range of bundled structures which cannot be made self-locking for any value of friction. The mechanism of ``purely elastic cohesion" illuminated here sheds light on self-locking in a broader class of entangled structures, and further, offers design principles for new classes of active filament materials capable of stimuli-responsive assembly and disassembly.

</details>


### [213] [Tribocharging of Granular Materials Dictated by Geometric and Thermoelectric Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36757)
*Donggeun Kang,Ji-Ho Mun,Seungil Baek,Jaeuk Seo,Sirichok Jungthawan,Yong Woon Kim,Yong-Hyun Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出一种基于颗粒几何与热电响应相互作用的颗粒摩擦起电（tribocharging）理论，用碰撞摩擦生热产生的尺寸依赖温度不对称解释同种材料间的电荷转移与极性反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料摩擦起电（tribocharging）广泛存在于火山喷发、沙尘暴和制药加工等场景，但其机制仍不清楚；既有热电模型（thermoelectric models）虽提供定量框架，却难以解释相同材料之间的起电现象。

Method: 构建以颗粒几何与热电响应（thermoelectric response）耦合为核心的理论：碰撞引起的摩擦加热（frictional heating）在颗粒间产生依赖尺寸的温度不对称，进而生成驱动电荷转移的热电压；并发展多碰撞理论（multi-collision theory）描述累积效应。

Result: 充电极性由塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）的符号决定，电荷量级由颗粒尺寸与热学性质决定；该理论可解释实验中观察到的随尺寸和材料变化的极性反转，如石英（quartz）与尼龙（nylon）。

Conclusion: 颗粒摩擦起电可归因于几何—热电耦合机制，为同种材料起电及极性反转提供了统一解释，将颗粒尺寸与热学性质纳入定量预测框架。

Abstract: Tribocharging of granular materials is a widespread phenomenon ranging from volcanic eruptions and dust storms to pharmaceutical processing, yet its underlying mechanism remains elusive. Recent thermoelectric models have provided a quantitative framework for triboelectric charge transfer, but they have difficulty accounting for charging between identical materials. In this Letter, we propose a theory of granular tribocharging based on the interplay between particle geometry and thermoelectric response. Collision-induced frictional heating produces a size-dependent temperature asymmetry between particles, generating a thermoelectric voltage that drives charge transfer. The sign of the Seebeck coefficient determines the charging polarity, while particle size and thermal properties set its magnitude. Our multi-collision theory explains the experimentally observed size- and material-dependent reversal of charging polarity, as in quartz and nylon.

</details>


### [214] [Mixed symmetries in active non-reciprocal systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36817)
*Yuv Agarwal,Ylann Rouzaire,Daniel J. G. Pearce*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文將兩種可獨立控制之對稱性引入非互易XY模型（non-reciprocal XY model），推導完整流體動力學描述（hydrodynamic description），揭示不同對稱性組合可產生具有一個或兩個序參量（order parameter）之多樣行為。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性材料（active materials）在微觀尺度遠離平衡並展現豐富現象，非互易性（non-reciprocity）為其中作用-反作用對稱破缺的子類；現有模型多依組分之對稱性階數分類，但許多生物實例中組分似同時具備兩種或以上對稱性，此現象尚缺乏研究框架。

Method: 在非互易XY模型上引入兩種可獨立調控的對稱性，構建格點模型（lattice model），並推導其完整的流體動力學描述，以釐清驅動其行為的機制。

Result: 該格點模型展現由所選對稱性決定之廣泛行為；每一種對稱性組合可導出不同的流體動力學模型，具有一個或兩個同時存在之序參量，其取向序（orientational order）程度各異，且可呈現活性或非活性特徵。

Conclusion: 對稱性組合可系統性地決定非互易活性系統的宏觀動力學形態；流體動力學描述提供了連結微觀對稱性與宏觀行為之機制性框架，並支持生物系統中多重對稱性共存之建模需求。

Abstract: Active materials are driven out of equilibrium at the microscopic scale and display diverse phenomenology; non-reciprocity is a subclass of activity in which action-reaction symmetry is also broken. These models are often grouped by the order of symmetry of their components, such as polar bird flocks or hexatic cellular tissues. However, many biological examples exist in which the components appear to have two or more symmetries simultaneously. To study this effect we introduce two independently controllable symmetries to the non-reciprocal XY model. This lattice model demonstrates wide ranging behavior dictated by the chosen symmetries. We derive a full hydrodynamic description of our lattice model that highlights the mechanisms driving its behavior. Each combination of symmetries can lead to a different hydrodynamic model with one or two simultaneous order parameters with different levels of orientational order that can appear active or not.

</details>


### [215] [The constrained molecular dynamics (CMD) to obtain spatio-temporal memory functions of viscoelastic liquids: stability analysis of equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37020)
*Olivier Benzerara,Jörg Baschnagel,Alexander N. Semenov*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文讨论约束分子动力学（constrained molecular dynamics, CMD）方法，用于直接评估波矢与时间依赖的粘弹模量（viscoelastic moduli），并重点分析不同CMD变体的稳定性，指出最高效的稳定化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性分子系统（如玻璃化液体与非晶固体）存在长程动力学不均匀性（dynamical heterogeneities），其时空分辨的局部应力关联可用于获取粘弹性记忆函数（memory functions），包括波矢依赖的剪切与压缩松弛模量；但标准分子动力学（MD）模拟无法直接获得这些记忆函数。

Method: 采用一种称为约束分子动力学（CMD）的新方法，直接评估波矢与时间依赖的粘弹性模量；研究聚焦于不同CMD变体的稳定性分析，以确定最高效的稳定化算法，并建立关于稳定化阶段关键参数（如波矢分辨质量流，wave-vector resolved mass current）时间依赖性的理论预测，与不同CMD算法的模拟数据进行比较。

Result: 理论预测与模拟数据表现出极好的一致性（excellent agreement），验证了理论方法的有效性，并表明其能够准确预测CMD轨迹中最重要的初始部分。

Conclusion: CMD方法可实现粘弹性记忆函数的直接评估，且所提出的稳定性分析确定了最高效的稳定化算法；理论框架与模拟的吻合确认了该方法的可靠性。

Abstract: Mechanical stress is one of the main characteristics of viscoelastic molecular systems, including vitrifying liquids and amorphous solids which are known to show long-range dynamical heterogeneities. It is therefore important to deal with time- and space-resolved local stress whose correlations can be used to obtain viscoelastic memory functions of such systems, in particular, their wave-vector dependent shear and compression relaxation moduli. However, such memory functions are not directly accessible within standard molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. Here we discuss a new method called the constrained molecular dynamics (CMD) which allows for a direct evaluation of the wave-vector and time dependent viscoelastic moduli. The study is focused, in particular, on the stability analysis of different variants of the CMD, revealing the most efficient stabilization algorithm. Our theoretical predictions for the time-dependence of the most important parameters (like the wave-vector resolved mass current) during the stabilization stage are compared with the simulation data using different CMD algorithms. An excellent agreement is observed, thus confirming the validity of our theoretical approach and its ability to accurately predict the most important initial part of CMD trajectories.

</details>


### [216] [Perimeter shell properties of branched polymer adsorbents in various conformational states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37076)
*Viktoria Blavatska,Jaroslav Ilnytskyi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作用简单立方格点上的蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）研究支化大分子（branched macromolecules）的结构可及性与周界壳层（perimeter shell）性质，发现支化降低周界壳尺寸但提高周界壳平均配位数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画支化大分子在吸附相关过程中周界壳层的结构可及性，并厘清链构象状态与支化程度如何共同调控吸附位点数量与配位特征。

Method: 在简单立方格点上构建三类组成链构象：共线单体（对应刚性棒）、自规避行走（self-avoiding walk, SAW，对应柔性链）与紧密堆积单体（对应塌缩链），并以蒙特卡洛模拟计算周界壳尺寸、周界壳平均配位数（mean coordination number）与每单体配位可及性等可观测量。

Result: 支化在所有构象状态下均减小周界壳尺寸、提高周界壳平均配位数，原因在于支化核附近单体局域密度升高及共享吸附位点或微笼（microcage）的形成；刚性伸展结构给出最高的每单体周界壳尺寸（长链极限接近4），柔性链则给出最大的平均配位数。

Conclusion: 组成链中单体的共线性与降低支化程度同向作用（使周界壳尺寸增大），而紧密堆积与支化程度增加同向作用（使周界壳尺寸减小），表明链构象与支化拓扑可统一地调节吸附可及性。

Abstract: The structural accessibility and perimeter shell properties of branched macromolecules are studied using Monte Carlo simulations on a simple cubic lattice. Constituent polymer chains of these macromolecules are constructed in an algorithmic way as: a set of collinear monomers; a self-avoiding walk (SAW); tigthly packed monomers. These mimic the ground states for a rigid rod, flexible chain, and collapsed chain conformational states, respectively. We analyzed the set of observables that are related to adsorption, such as perimeter shell size (number of available sites for particle adsorption) per monomer $\langle \bar{n}_a \rangle$, mean coordination number of perimeter shell $\langle n_{\rm bond} \rangle$ (average number of contacts per adsorbed particle), and coordination accessibility per monomer $\langle \bar{n}_s \rangle$. Our results demonstrate that branching reduces the perimeter shell size while it increases mean coordination number of perimeter shell across all conformational states, due to higher local monomer density near branching cores and formation of shared adsorption sites or microcages. Stiff extended structures yield the highest values for $\langle \bar{n}_a \rangle$ (approaching $4$ in the limit of long chains), whereas the flexible ones provide the largest values for $\langle n_{\rm bond} \rangle$. We can note that the collinearity of monomers in constituent chains works in the same direction as reduction of branching ($\langle \bar{n}_a \rangle$ grows), whereas the { tight packing of them} -- in the same direction as the increase of branching ($\langle \bar{n}_a \rangle$ decreases)

</details>


### [217] [Phase Separation in a Mixture System of Active and Passive Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37193)
*Reo Arakaki,Tetsuya Hiraiwa,Ryo Akiyama*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过分子动力学模拟发现，自驱动颗粒（self-propelled particles, SPPs）与被动颗粒（passive particles）的混合物在自驱动速度足够高时会分离为两相：一相为有序的被动颗粒，另一相为两类颗粒的混浊混合相；该相分离（phase separation）源于SPP自驱动运动与被动颗粒热运动的相互作用，并可用类耗尽相互作用（depletion interaction）机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究由不同运动能力（motility）组分构成的混合体系中是否以及如何发生相分离，并解释其物理机制；此类体系有望为天然系统中不同活性元素共存现象提供理解，并为SPPs的潜在应用提供启示。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）研究SPPs与被动颗粒混合体系，考察自驱动速度对体系相行为的影响；并借助有效排斥体积（effective excluded size）增大与类耗尽相互作用机制进行解释。

Result: 当自驱动速度足够高时体系发生两相分离：一相为有序被动颗粒相，另一相为两类颗粒的无序混合相；单独任一种颗粒类型均不出现相分离。随自驱动速度增大，SPPs将周围颗粒推开，有效增大其排斥体积，从而促进被动颗粒聚集并使SPPs向稀相分离。

Conclusion: 相分离源于SPP自驱动运动与被动颗粒热运动的协同作用，其机制类比耗尽相互作用；研究结果有助于理解由不同运动能力元素组成的天然体系，并提示SPPs的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We carried out molecular dynamics simulations of a mixture of self-propelled particles (SPPs) and passive particles. When the self-propulsion speed was sufficiently high, the system separated into two phases. One phase consists of ordered passive particles, and the other is a disordered mixture of both particle types. The separation arises from the interplay between the self-propelled motion of SPPs and thermal motion of the passive particles, since phase separation does not appear with either particle type alone. As the self-propulsion speed increases, the SPPs push surrounding particles away, effectively increasing their excluded size. As a result, the increase in the effective excluded sizes promotes the accumulation of passive particles and the segregation of SPPs into the dilute phase through a mechanism analogous to the depletion interaction. This mechanism explains the phase separation observed in the present study. Our findings provide insights into the potential applications of SPPs and contribute to our understanding of natural systems composed of elements with different levels of motility.

</details>


### [218] [Molecular shape controls dipolar correlations in polar liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37489)
*Marceau Hénot*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用无氢键模型极性液体的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），揭示分子形状通过偶极相互作用与位阻效应（steric effects）的耦合决定Kirkwood相关因子（Kirkwood correlation factor）g_K，并给出冷却时g_K升高、降低或非单调变化三种情形，立方晶格偶极最小模型可复现该三区域并解释非单调源于第一近邻层与第二近邻层的竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性液体的介电常数（dielectric constant）不仅取决于分子偶极矩大小，还取决于近邻偶极间的短程取向关联（short range orientational correlations），但分子结构如何决定该关联（以g_K量化）尚不清晰，本文旨在厘清分子形状的作用。

Method: 对不含氢键（hydrogen bonding）的模型极性液体开展分子动力学模拟，将g_K理解为偶极相互作用与位阻效应共同作用的结果；并构建立方晶格（cubic lattice）上的偶极最小模型进行对照验证。

Result: 分子形状起核心作用：随分子形状不同，g_K在降温时可升高、可降低，也可呈非单调变化；立方晶格最小模型复现了这三种区域（three regimes）。

Conclusion: 非单调情形源于近邻层间的竞争——第一近邻层倾向于平行排列（alignment），第二近邻层倾向于反平行排列（antialignment）；分子形状通过调节偶极相互作用与位阻效应的平衡决定取向关联的走向。

Abstract: The dielectric constant of polar liquids depends not only on the magnitude of the molecular dipole moment but also on short range orientational correlations between neighboring dipoles. How molecular structure determines these correlations, quantified by the Kirkwood correlation factor $g_\mathrm{K}$, remains unclear. Using molecular dynamics simulations of model polar liquids without hydrogen bonding, we show that molecular shape plays a central role. We interpret $g_\mathrm{K}$ as resulting from the combination of dipolar interactions and steric effects, which together favor either alignment or antialignment of neighboring molecules. Depending on molecular shape, $g_\mathrm{K}$ can either increases or decreases upon cooling, or change monotony. A minimal model of dipoles on a cubic lattice reproduces these three regimes and attributes the nonmonotonic case to a competition between the first layer of neighbors, which favors alignment, and the second layer, which favors antialignment.

</details>


### [219] [Activation force dependence of the fluidization transition in supercooled active water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37562)
*Quoc Tuan Truong,Victor Teboul*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过激活模型（activation model）研究激活力（activation force）对过冷水中流化转变（fluidization transition）的影响，发现协同运动存在最优力约3.5 pN，并在转变处观察到协同运动（动态异质性，dynamic heterogeneity）的峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已发现少量活性分子（active molecules）的运动可触发过冷水的流化转变，但激活力大小如何调节该转变及协同运动（cooperative motions）尚不明确，本文旨在探究激活力对转变的影响。

Method: 采用能够重现促进机制（facilitation mechanisms）的激活模型，调节激活力以改变活性分子克服笼效应（caging）所诱导能垒（energy barrier）的概率，并考察其对促进机制与协同运动的影响。

Result: 激活力增大活性分子越过能垒的概率，诱导间歇性松散结构（intermittent loose structure）并降低运动分子周围的能垒，从而促进邻近分子运动；存在使协同运动最优的力，约为3.5 pN，处于蛋白质马达力（protein motor forces）范围内；在转变处观察到协同运动（动态异质性）的峰值。

Conclusion: 激活力可用于调控活性玻璃形成体（active glass-formers）中的促进机制与协同运动；转变处动态异质性的峰值表明，动态异质性正是对该转变具有物理重要性的协同运动。

Abstract: Using an activation model reproducing facilitation mechanisms, we found recently a fluidization transition in supercooled water triggered by the motion of a few active molecules. We investigate here the effect of the activation force on the transition. The activation force increases the probability for active molecules to overcome the energy barrier induced by caging. The induced molecular motions result in an intermittent loose structure leading to a decrease of energy barriers around the moving molecule, thus facilitating the motion of nearby molecules. Therefore we expect the force, if small enough, to increase the facilitation mechanism, and as a result cooperative motions induced by it. As a result the activation force permits to tune facilitation and cooperative motions in active glass-formers. We find an optimum force for cooperative motions that we evaluate to be around 3.5pN, a value in the range of protein motor forces. As expected for a phase transition we observe peaks of cooperative motions (dynamic heterogeneity) on the transition. This result shows that dynamical heterogeneity are the cooperative motions of physical importance for the transition.

</details>


### [220] [Water at the molecular frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37581)
*Mischa Bonn,Marie-Laure Bocquet,Andre Geim,Laura Fumagalli,Angelos Michaelides,Lyderic Bocquet*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该 Perspective 提出，纳米尺度与界面水不应被视为零散的尺度异常，而应作为独立的“水的分子机制（molecular regime）”加以研究；该机制下连续介质描述（continuum descriptions）需被重新审视，分子层面理解成为必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量关键用水（细胞内与膜内输运、土壤岩石渗流、过滤与脱盐）并非体相水（bulk water），而是处于纳米/分子受限或界面状态，关系全球水危机、水-能源纽带（water-energy nexus）、催化、电化学与健康等议题。

Method: 本文为观点性综述（Perspective），非实验或计算研究；其论证依托三类进展的汇聚：原子级精确的纳流控器件（nanofluidic devices）、新型光谱学与显微技术、以及机器学习加速的模拟（machine-learning-accelerated simulations）。

Result: 受限与界面作用既可使体相性质发生重整化（renormalization），也可产生非直觉新现象，包括奇异相（exotic phases）、反常输运（anomalous transport）与违背连续介质预期的介电响应（dielectric responses）。

Conclusion: 应将上述发现统一理解为水的分子机制存在的证据，而非孤立异常；识别并探索该机制对基础物理及依赖水与溶解离子的技术理性设计均具意义。

Abstract: Much of the water that matters most - flowing through biological cells and membranes, percolating through soils and rocks, or processed by filtration and desalination technologies - is not bulk water. It is water in nano- and molecular-scale confinement or at interfaces. Nanoscale water is therefore central to many challenges of our time, from the global water crisis and the water-energy nexus to catalysis, electrochemistry, and health. Beyond its applications, nanoscale water has emerged as a rich scientific field in its own right: discoveries are accumulating rapidly, revealing that confinement and interfacial forces produce a range of behaviours absent in the bulk. Sometimes this amounts to a renormalization of bulk properties; in other cases, new and non-intuitive phenomena emerge - exotic phases, anomalous transport, and dielectric responses that defy continuum expectations. A convergence of atomically precise nanofluidic devices, novel spectroscopies and microscopy approaches, and machine-learning-accelerated simulations is now exposing the molecular mechanisms behind these behaviours. This Perspective argues that the recent findings should not be viewed as isolated nanoscale anomalies, but as evidence for a molecular regime of water in which continuum descriptions must be rethought and molecular-level understanding becomes essential. Recognising and exploring this regime should have consequences ranging from fundamental physics to the rational design of future technologies that rely on water and dissolved ions.

</details>


### [221] [On-the-fly Estimation of Quantum-Corrected Thermodynamics in LAMMPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37586)
*Robert Ramji,Ilya Mazalov,Ricardo Buarque,Alexandria Do,Xiaoxu Ruan,Tod A. Pascal*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出 FixXPT，即在 LAMMPS 分子动力学框架内原生实现二/三相热力学（Two/Three-Phase Thermodynamics, xPT）方法，用于从平衡轨迹的速度自相关函数（velocity autocorrelation function, VACF）导出的态密度函数计算含量子修正的绝对热力学量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: xPT 方法此前缺少在主流分子动力学（molecular dynamics）框架中的原生实现，且涉及原子重排序、计算调度、FFT 后端、单位制及分子模式速度划分等非平凡集成难题，长分析窗口的内存与扩展瓶颈亦有待解决。

Method: FixXPT 提供两种自相关器（autocorrelator）：基于 Wiener-Khinchin 定理的 FFT 相关器（一次累积窗口末端操作）与内存仅随窗口长度对数增长的内联多 tau（multi-tau）相关器；各窗口速度历史分布于 MPI 进程（MPI ranks），节点内存随进程数近似恒定并随 GPU 数量下降。

Result: 相同速度帧经 FixXPT 与独立 pyxPT 后处理器（post-processor）重放，采样充分的组在各分析模式下热力学观测量一致至 0.1–0.5%；稀疏离子组分在统计不确定度内一致；多 tau VACF 随缓冲区增大单调收敛至 FFT 结果，16 倍缓冲区扫描下漂移由 0.8% 降至 0.1%。

Conclusion: FixXPT 消除了长分析窗口的主要扩展瓶颈并降低内存开销，以可直接集成的 LAMMPS 包（package）形式发布，与现有 pyxPT 实现结果一致。

Abstract: We present a native implementation of the Two/Three-Phase Thermodynamics (xPT, $x \in \{2,3\}$) method within the LAMMPS molecular dynamics framework, termed FixXPT. xPT is an approach for obtaining absolute, quantum-corrected thermodynamics (entropy, enthalpy, free energy, heat capacity, diffusivity) from the density of states function, obtained from the velocity autocorrelation function of an equilibrium trajectory. FixXPT addresses non-trivial integration challenges, including atom reordering, compute scheduling, FFT backends, unit systems, and partitioning of atomic velocities into molecular modes. Two autocorrelators are provided: a Wiener-Khinchin FFT correlator with one cumulative window-end operation, and an in-line multi-tau correlator whose memory footprint grows only logarithmically with the window length, removing the principal scaling bottleneck for long analysis windows. The velocity history of each window is distributed across the MPI ranks, so the memory the fix adds to a node stays nearly constant as ranks are added and its cost falls with the number of GPUs. Replaying identical velocity frames through FixXPT and the stand-alone pyxPT post-processor reproduces every thermodynamic observable of well-sampled groups to within 0.1 to 0.5% across the full analysis-mode matrix; sparsely populated ionic species agree within their statistical uncertainty, and the multi-tau VACF converges monotonically to the FFT result as its buffer grows, from 0.8% to 0.1% drift across a 16-fold buffer sweep. The code is distributed as a drop-in LAMMPS package.

</details>

### [222] [Spin generalizations of the classical elliptic Calogero--Inozemtsev model and integrable models of interacting tops associated with the $\rm{BC}_n$ root system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37165)
*D. Domanevsky,A. Zotov*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文为与 \(\mathrm{BC}_n\) 根系相关的一类相互作用陀螺模型构造了含谱参数的 Lax 对，并借助参数化结合 Yang–Baxter 方程将其与椭圆 Calogero–Inozemtsev 模型及 Haldane–Shastry–Inozemtsev 型长程自旋链联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于把含谱参数 Lax 对这一可积性核心工具推广到与 \(\mathrm{BC}_n\) 根系相关的相互作用陀螺（interacting tops）模型族，并通过 Yang–Baxter 结构统一处理其量子可积性及与已知长程自旋链模型的关系。

Method: 构造方法基于参数化 \(\mathrm{BC}_n\) 结合 Yang–Baxter 方程（associative Yang–Baxter equations），其中同时涉及量子 \(R\)-矩阵（quantum \(R\)-matrices）与满足反射方程（reflection equation）的边界 \(K\)-矩阵（boundary \(K\)-matrices）；再结合 Polychronakos 冻结 prescription（Polychronakos freezing prescription）处理量子哈密顿量。

Result: 所得模型中，特殊情形给出椭圆 Calogero–Inozemtsev 模型（elliptic Calogero–Inozemtsev model）的自旋推广；相互作用陀螺的量子哈密顿量经 Polychronakos 冻结后，可重现近期引入的 Haldane–Shastry–Inozemtsev 型各向异性量子长程自旋链（anisotropic quantum long-range spin chains）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，参数化结合 Yang–Baxter 方程可为 \(\mathrm{BC}_n\) 型相互作用陀螺提供统一的 Lax 对框架，并在特定极限下涵盖椭圆 Calogero–Inozemtsev 自旋推广及 Haldane–Shastry–Inozemtsev 型长程自旋链，体现了这些模型之间的结构联系。

Abstract: We construct Lax pairs with a spectral parameter for a family of models of interacting tops associated with the $\rm{BC}_n$ root system. The construction uses the parametric $\rm{BC}_n$ associative Yang--Baxter equations, which involve both quantum $R$-matrices and boundary $K$-matrices satisfying the reflection equation. Special cases yield spin generalizations of the elliptic Calogero--Inozemtsev model. We also show how quantum Hamiltonians of interacting tops, combined with the Polychronakos freezing prescription, reproduce recently introduced anisotropic quantum long-range spin chains of Haldane--Shastry--Inozemtsev type.

</details>

### [223] [An end-to-end framework for uncertainty-aware validation of generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37398)
*Giada Badaracco,Sean Benevedes,Christina Reissel,Philip Harris,Gaia Grosso*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA框架，将密度估计与不确定性量化结合，并把拟合不确定性作为冗余参数纳入拟合优度检验，从而在物理科学中更稳健地验证生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习密度估计方法缺乏可传播到统计检验的不确定性量化，使生成模型只能以点零假设（point-null hypothesis）方式接受标准拟合优度检验；而有限训练集拟合的模型永远不精确，检验集足够大时任何足够强力的检验终将拒绝它。

Method: 采用不确定性可解析处理的高斯混合模型（Gaussian mixture model），将拟合不确定性作为冗余参数（nuisance parameter）剖面化，把传统点零假设检验推广为复合检验（composite hypothesis），形成端到端（end-to-end）框架。

Result: 在受控二维玩具模型与模拟QCD背景过程的对撞机物理基准上验证；纳入不确定性后，同一模型可被信任的检验集规模比标准检验允许的大一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为物理科学中稳健使用生成模型提供了统计上可靠的基础；此时拒绝信号意味着真实的模型错误设定（mismodeling），而非检验集足以分辨拟合误差。

Abstract: Density estimation with generative models is a common task in the physical sciences, for example in background estimation in particle physics. Existing machine learning techniques, however, lack methods to quantify uncertainties that can be propagated into a statistical test. That limits the validation of generative models to standard goodness-of-fit tests, in which the generative model is treated as exact (point-null hypothesis). But a model fitted to a finite training set is never exact, so if its uncertainties are neglected, any sufficiently powerful goodness-of-fit test will eventually reject it once the test set is large enough to resolve this estimation error. We propose SIGMA, an end-to-end framework combining density estimation with principled uncertainty quantification. We use Gaussian mixture models, for which uncertainties are tractable, and further construct a new goodness-of-fit test in which these uncertainties are profiled as nuisance parameters to account for the statistical error of the fit, promoting the conventional point-null test to a composite one. A rejection then signals genuine mismodeling rather than a test set large enough to resolve the error of the fit, provided the propagated uncertainties faithfully cover that error. We demonstrate the framework on a controlled two-dimensional toy model and on a collider physics benchmark modeling the QCD background process. With uncertainties included, the same model can be trusted on test sets up to an order of magnitude larger than the standard test allows. Our work thus provides a statistically grounded basis for a robust use of generative models in the physical sciences.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [224] [ODAT-SE: A modular and parallel framework for simulation-based inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36746)
*Tatsumi Aoyama,Kazuyoshi Yoshimi,Yuichi Motoyama,Akito Nakano,Hengyu Li,Yusuke Konishi,Takeo Hoshi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: ODAT-SE 4.0 是一个开源 Python 框架，通过公共接口把优化/采样算法与既有正向模拟求解器连接，用于求解需反复正向模拟的反问题，并在贝叶斯工作流、张量列优化与两级 MPI 并行方面做了扩展与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作面向通过重复正向模拟（forward simulation）求解的反问题（inverse problems），承接为量子束衍射表面结构分析开发的 2DMAT，并将其推广为跨领域的通用平台。版本 3 起算法与特定求解器解耦，动机在于使现有模拟代码可与任意搜索算法组合使用。

Method: 框架通过统一接口（参数映射、约束与目标函数评估）将优化与采样算法连接到求解器。相对 2DMAT 新增两级 MPI 并行（two-level MPI parallelism）、检查点与续算、随机搜索、SciPy 全局优化器、张量列优化（tensor-train optimization）以及贝叶斯工作流（估计配分函数比、模型证据、有效噪声水平与边缘后验分布）。

Result: 贝叶斯工作流在线性回归上与闭式解一致；张量列优化在连续、二值与混合整数问题上完成基准测试；两级并行在不同进程分解下给出相同最优解。在正电子衍射（positron diffraction）示例中，识别出相关的结构解与接近所施加扰动的有效噪声尺度，同时暴露出对称相关模式权重收敛不完全的问题。

Conclusion: 求解器包、模板与示例库支持将该框架作为反问题分析的通用平台使用；对称相关模式权重的收敛不完全也提示了后续需要关注的方向。

Abstract: Open Data Analysis Tool for Science and Engineering (ODAT-SE) is an open-source Python framework for inverse problems that are solved by repeated forward simulations. It succeeds 2DMAT, which was developed for surface-structure analysis with quantum-beam diffraction and has since been applied to other fields. In version 3, the search algorithms were separated from the application-specific solvers, which became independent packages, and the software was renamed; this paper describes the current version 4.0. The framework connects optimization and sampling algorithms to solvers through a common interface for parameter mapping, constraints, and objective evaluation, so that an existing simulation code can be used with any of the algorithms. Relative to the previously published 2DMAT, it provides two-level MPI parallelism, which distributes candidate evaluations over processes and each evaluation over a parallel solver, checkpoint and continuation facilities, random search, global optimizers from SciPy, tensor-train optimization, and a Bayesian workflow that estimates partition-function ratios, model evidence, effective noise levels, and marginal posterior distributions. We validate the Bayesian workflow against closed-form results for a linear regression, benchmark tensor-train optimization on continuous, binary, and mixed-integer problems, and verify the two-level parallelization by the same optimum for all process decompositions. Taking up the positron-diffraction example of the 2DMAT paper, the workflow identifies the correlated structural solutions and an effective noise scale close to the imposed perturbation, while exposing incomplete convergence of symmetry-related mode weights. Solver packages, templates, and a gallery of examples support the use of the framework as a general platform for inverse-problem analysis.

</details>


<div id='nucl-ex'></div>

# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Evidence of partonic collectivity in OO collisions through the measurement of $φ$, K$^0_{\rm S}$, and $Λ$ elliptic flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36237)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: ALICE合作组首次在√s_NN=5.36 TeV的OO碰撞中测量φ、K⁰_S与Λ(Λ̄)的椭圆流v₂，观察到低p_T质量次序及中等p_T重子-介子分离（Λ流值高于介子，φ跟随K⁰_S），结果支持部分子集体性与夸克重组强子化图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小碰撞系统（small collision system）中是否存在部分子集体性（partonic collectivity）是关键问题；在OO碰撞中测量φ介子椭圆流尚属首次，可为检验Pb-Pb中观察到的集体性特征是否在轻核-核系统中延续提供补充证据。

Method: 利用ALICE探测器在√s_NN=5.36 TeV的OO碰撞中，于中心与半中心碰撞下测量φ、K⁰_S及Λ(Λ̄)强子的椭圆流系数（elliptic-flow coefficient, v₂）随横动量（transverse momentum, p_T）的变化。

Result: 低p_T处观察到v₂的质量次序（mass ordering），与系统集体膨胀相符；在p_T>3 GeV/c的中等p_T区，出现显著性为10σ的重子-介子分离（baryon-meson splitting），Λ(Λ̄)的v₂大于介子，而质量接近Λ的φ却表现出与K⁰_S一致的行为。

Conclusion: φ介子的行为不利于纯质量驱动的解释，支持经由集体流动的部分子介质中夸克重组（quark recombination）实现强子化（hadronization）；结果表明Pb-Pb碰撞中的部分子集体性关键特征在轻核-核系统中依然存在。

Abstract: The first measurement of the elliptic-flow coefficient ($v_{2}$) of $φ$, K$^0_{\rm S}$, and $Λ(\overlineΛ)$ hadrons performed in OO collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV with the ALICE detector is presented as a function of transverse momentum ($p_{\rm T}$) in central and semi-central collisions. For the $φ$ meson, in particular, this is the first measurement of the elliptic flow in a small collision system. At low $p_{\rm T}$, a characteristic mass ordering of $v_{2}$ is observed, consistent with collective expansion of the system, in which heavier hadrons are shifted to higher $p_{\rm T}$ by a common velocity field. At intermediate $p_{\rm T}$, above 3 GeV/$c$, a baryon$-$meson splitting with a $10σ$ significance emerges, with the $Λ(\overlineΛ)$ baryon exhibiting larger $v_{2}$ values than mesons, while, despite its mass being close to the mass of the $Λ$, the $φ$ follows the K$^{0}_{\rm S}$. The $φ$ meson behavior disfavors a purely mass-driven interpretation of the observed patterns and supports the role of hadronization via quark recombination from a collectively flowing partonic medium. These results provide complementary evidence that key signatures of partonic collectivity observed in Pb$-$Pb collisions persist in light nucleus$-$nucleus systems.

</details>


### [226] [Measurement of antideuteron and net-$Λ$ number fluctuations at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36307)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 在LHC的Pb–Pb碰撞中，ALICE首次测量了反氘核–净Λ数关联；该关联与净Λ数的归一化二阶累积量均符合Thermal-FIST中的统计强子化模型，表明反氘核–反质子关联由核合成过程主导，而与非核子强子的关联主要反映强子化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前反氘核与反质子的事件逐事件关联测量偏离了描述轻味强子产额与涨落的统计强子化模型参量化，该偏离可能源于反氘核等复合态形成中的核合成过程；需借助不含Λ组分的关联量分离核合成与强子化两类效应。

Method: 利用ALICE探测器在Pb–Pb碰撞中进行首次反氘核–净Λ数关联测量；因Λ超子并非反氘核的组成成分，该量主要对强子化过程 induced 的量子数关联敏感，并与Thermal-FIST实现的统计强子化模型预言及净Λ数归一化二阶累积量进行对比。

Result: 反氘核–净Λ数关联与净Λ数的归一化二阶累积量均被Thermal-FIST中的统计强子化模型良好描述。

Conclusion: 结果构成有力证据：反氘核–反质子关联由核合成过程驱动，而反氘核与非核子强子之间的关联主要对强子化机制敏感。

Abstract: The recent measurements of event-by-event correlations between the antideuteron and antiproton numbers in Pb$-$Pb collisions at the LHC deviate from the statistical hadronization model parameterizations that describe the yields and fluctuations of light-flavored hadrons. This discrepancy may stem from nucleosynthesis processes involved in the formation of composite states like antideuterons. In this letter, we present the first measurement of the antideuteron$-$net-$Λ$ number correlation obtained in Pb$-$Pb collisions with the ALICE detector. This quantity is mostly sensitive to the quantum-number correlations induced by the hadronization process, as $Λ$ hyperons are not constituents of antideuterons. Both the antideuteron$-$net-$Λ$ number correlation and the normalized second-order cumulant of the net-$Λ$ number are well described by the statistical hadronization model implemented in Thermal-FIST. This establishes strong evidence that the antideuteron$-$antiproton correlation is driven by the nucleosynthesis processes, while the correlation between antideuterons and non-nucleonic hadrons is primarily sensitive to the hadronization mechanism.

</details>


### [227] [Improved nonflow control for multiparticle correlators and system-size-dependent evolution from OO to Pb--Pb collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36922)
*Neelkamal Mallick,Suraj Prasad*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该工作验证了一套改进的非流（nonflow）扣除框架（基于多重数重加权与 v1-估计量标度），并应用于 AMPT 弦熔模型预言 OO 碰撞中的对称平面关联量（SPCs）与归一化对称累积量（SCs），将其与 Pb–Pb 结果置于统一系统尺寸轴上比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCs 与 SCs 可约束重离子碰撞初始条件与输运性质，但小系统中非流贡献显著，会伪造类流信号，因此需可靠的非流控制方法，并借轻离子碰撞（OO、Ne–Ne）探明这些关联的系统尺寸依赖与非线性模式耦合强度。

Method: 提出改进的非流扣除框架，包含多重数重加权（C0）与基于 v1 估计量的标度（C1）两种方案；在无集体流的受控玩具模型与 Pythia 8 Angantyr 模型中验证其有效性，并将较可靠方案用于 AMPT 弦熔模型下 √sNN = 5.36 TeV 的 OO 碰撞预言。

Result: 在无流基准模型中，C1 方案相比 C0 提供更可靠的非流控制；据此得到的 OO 碰撞 SPCs 与归一化 SCs 以中快度带电粒子多重数 ⟨Nch⟩(|η|<0.5) 为函数，与 ALICE 及 AMPT 的已发表 Pb–Pb 测量置于同一系统尺寸轴上比较。

Conclusion: 基于 v1 估计量的标度（C1）是更可信的非流扣除手段，可将轻离子与重离子系统统一到共同系统尺寸轴上考察多粒子关联，从而澄清其系统尺寸依赖及非线性模式耦合强度。

Abstract: Multiparticle correlations of event-plane angles, known as symmetry plane correlators (SPCs), and correlations of anisotropic flow amplitudes of different harmonics, known as symmetric cumulants (SCs), help constrain the initial conditions and the transport properties of the system formed in heavy-ion collisions. Such multiparticle correlations are robust against nonflow biases in heavy-ion collisions; however, in small systems the nonflow contribution is sizeable and can distort SPCs and SCs at low multiplicity, mimicking a flow-like signal. Evidence of nuclear-geometry-induced collectivity in light-ion collisions, such as OO and Ne--Ne, has been established at the LHC. Measurements of SPCs and SCs in these systems, and their comparison to heavy-ion collisions, can therefore clarify the system-size dependence of these correlations and the strength of nonlinear mode coupling. In this work, an improved nonflow subtraction framework based on multiplicity reweighting ($\czero$) and $v_1$-estimator-based ($\cone$) scaling is validated for selected SPCs and SCs using a controlled toy model and the \textsc{Pythia~8~Angantyr} model, where collective flow is absent by construction. We find that $\cone$ provides the more reliable nonflow control and apply it to predict the SPCs and normalized SCs in OO collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.36$~TeV with the \textsc{ampt} string-melting model. The results are compared with published Pb--Pb measurements from ALICE and from \textsc{ampt} as a function of midrapidity charged-particle multiplicity $\langle N_{\rm ch}\rangle(|η|<0.5)$, placing light- and heavy-ion systems on a common system-size axis.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Activity-based temporal hypergraphs: from individual activity to collective social dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36333)
*Marco Mancastroppa,Giulia Cencetti,Iacopo Iacopini,Giovanni Petri,Alain Barrat*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述了基于活动（activity-based）的时间超图（temporal hypergraphs）模型，说明活动机制可控制动力学时间尺度、提供高建模灵活性并刻画主体行为异质性，同时介绍了 HADx 与 EATH 两类代表性模型及其从经验数据提取活动的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会系统兼具时间性与群体性交互特征，时间超图能同时刻画二者；构建时间变化超网络（time-varying hyper-networks）模型有助于生成经验社会系统的替代数据（surrogates）、理解其涌现性质的机制，并研究其上动力过程的演化。

Method: 聚焦活动驱动的时间超图模型：论述活动机制如何控制动力学时间尺度、提升建模灵活性并捕捉主体行为异质性；并给出两类范例——高阶活动驱动模型（HADx）与涌现活动时间超图模型（EATH），后者利用由活动和群体记忆（group memory）驱动的高阶机制生成经验系统的替代数据。

Result: HADx 表明，选择活跃交互的不同底层规则会产生不同的高阶动力学（higher-order dynamics）；EATH 则可借助活动与群体记忆驱动的高阶机制，生成经验系统的替代数据集；活动亦能直接由经验数据中提取。

Conclusion: 基于活动的时间超图为现实社会动力学建模与其所支撑的复杂动力过程（从社会传染（social contagion）到观点动力学（opinion dynamics））研究之间搭建了桥梁，具有可观的应用前景。

Abstract: Temporal hypergraphs provide an interesting representation for many social systems, as they integrate both the temporal and group nature of social interactions. Designing models of time-varying hyper-networks yields thus a convenient way to generate surrogates of empirical social systems, to understand the mechanisms underlying their emergent properties, and to investigate the evolution of dynamical processes taking place on such structures. Here, we focus on a specific and powerful class of these models, the activity-based temporal hypergraphs. We show how activity mechanisms provide control over the time scale of the dynamics, allow high modelling flexibility, and capture heterogeneity in agents' behavior. We also highlight how the activity can straightforwardly be extracted from empirical data to create surrogate datasets. We present paradigmatic examples of such models: the higher-order activity-driven models (HADx), which show that different underlying rules for the selection of active interactions produce different higher-order dynamics; the Emerging Activity Temporal Hypergraph model (EATH), able to generate surrogates of empirical systems by using higher-order mechanisms driven by both activity and group memory. We conclude discussing future perspectives for activity-based temporal hypergraphs, highlighting the potential of this framework to bridge realistic modelling of social dynamics with the study of the complex dynamical process they can support, from social contagion to opinion dynamics.

</details>


<div id='physics.acc-ph'></div>

# physics.acc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [A pathway to improve Nb$_3$Sn for SRF accelerator cavities via Zr inclusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36479)
*M. Tripathi,M. S. Shakel,J. Lee,H. E. Elsayed-Ali,S. Posen,G. V. Eremeev*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 在 Nb₃Sn 薄膜中共溅射引入 Zr 可改善表面形貌、降低孔洞密度，并在最优 Zr 含量下提升超导性能、形成更薄的表面氧化铌层，使 Nb₃Sn:Zr 成为提升超导射频腔性能的候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nb₃Sn 有望在粒子加速器及用于轴子暗物质搜寻（axion dark matter searches）的多特斯拉磁场高 Q₀ 腔中超越铌基超导射频（superconducting radio-frequency, SRF）腔的性能，因此需要探索改善 Nb₃Sn 薄膜微结构与超导特性的途径。

Method: 采用共溅射（co-sputtering）方式将 Zr 掺入 Nb₃Sn 基体，并研究 Zr 掺入对 Nb₃Sn 薄膜微结构（microstructure）与超导性能（superconducting properties）的影响。

Result: 随 Zr 原子分数（atomic fraction）增加，表面形貌（surface morphology）改善，体相与表面孔洞密度（void density）降低；在最优 Zr 含量下超导性能提升，且表面形成的氧化铌（niobium oxide）层更薄。

Conclusion: 共溅射制备的 Nb₃Sn:Zr 被认为是推动 SRF 腔性能极限提升的有前景材料。

Abstract: Nb$_3$Sn has the potential to exceed the performance of niobium-based superconducting radio-frequency (SRF) cavities, both in particle accelerators and in high-$Q_0$ cavities operating in multi-tesla magnetic fields for axion dark matter searches. We studied how the incorporation of Zr into the Nb$_3$Sn matrix by co-sputtering affects the microstructure and superconducting properties of Nb$_3$Sn films. As the atomic fraction of Zr increased, the surface morphology improved and the bulk and surface void densities decreased. At the optimal Zr content, the superconducting properties improved and a thinner surface niobium oxide formed. These results identify co-sputtered Nb$_3$Sn:Zr as a promising material to advance the performance limits of SRF cavities.

</details>

### [230] [Probing the inner disk edge of rotating neutron stars with dipole excitations in finite nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37394)
*P. S. Koliogiannis,N. Paar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于相对论能量密度泛函构建系统变化对称能（symmetry energy）的物态方程（equation of state, EOS）族，计算广义相对论下均匀自转中子星，量化中子丰核的电偶极极化率（electric dipole polarizability）测量对最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO）与星表相接的转变质量、半径及轨道频率不确定度的约束作用；该约束将转变质量与赤道半径的散布缩小约七至十倍，并把内边缘轨道频率限制在约20 Hz带宽内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核物态方程（nuclear equation of state）的不确定性同时影响快速自转中子星（rapidly rotating neutron stars）的结构与周围时空中的轨道运动，并决定最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）与星表相接的转变条件，即盘内边缘由星表决定还是由轨道不稳定性截断的分界；因此需要定量评估有限核实验约束对上述转变及盘内边缘轨道性质不确定度的削减程度。

Method: 采用密度依赖点耦合相互作用（density-dependent point-coupling interactions）的相对论能量密度泛函（relativistic energy density functionals），构造系统改变对称能（symmetry energy）的物态方程族，在广义相对论（general relativity）下计算均匀自转中子星；将完整方程族的结果与符合有限核偶极极化率（dipole polarizability）测量值的子集结果进行对比。

Result: 偶极极化率约束使转变质量（transition mass）与赤道半径（equatorial radius）在考察的各自转频率（spin frequencies）下的散布缩小约七至十倍；沿该转变线，每个自转频率下盘内边缘的轨道频率被限制在窄于20 Hz的带宽内；结合实测中子星自转，若将千赫兹准周期振荡（kilohertz quasi-periodic oscillation）解释为盘轨道运动，可进一步收窄质量—半径区域，从而给出独立的质量估计以检验对称能约束。

Conclusion: 有限核的偶极响应（dipole response）既可约束自转中子星的结构，也可约束其内吸积盘（inner accretion disks）的轨道性质，为对称能提供独立探测途径。

Abstract: The nuclear equation of state shapes both the structure of rapidly rotating neutron stars and the orbital motion in their surrounding spacetime. Its uncertainty affects the mass and radius at which the innermost stable circular orbit reaches the stellar surface, a condition that defines the transition between disks whose inner edge is set by the surface and those truncated by orbital instability. We quantify how measurements of the electric dipole polarizability of neutron-rich nuclei reduce the uncertainty in this transition and in the orbital properties at the disk inner edge. We compute uniformly rotating neutron stars in general relativity for a family of equations of state that systematically vary the symmetry energy, derived from relativistic energy density functionals with density-dependent point-coupling interactions. We compare the results for the full family with those for the subset consistent with the measured dipole polarizabilities of finite nuclei. The dipole polarizability constraint reduces the spread in transition mass and equatorial radius by factors of about seven to ten across the spin frequencies considered. Along this transition, it also confines the orbital frequency at the disk inner edge to a band narrower than $20$~Hz at each spin. Combined with a measured neutron-star spin, a kilohertz quasi-periodic oscillation interpreted as disk orbital motion further restricts the mass--radius region, allowing an independent mass estimate to probe the symmetry-energy constraint. The dipole response of finite nuclei thus informs both the structure of rotating neutron stars and the orbital properties of their inner accretion disks.

</details>


### [231] [A Quiet Night in the Millihertz Sky? LISA Forecasts from LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37617)
*Ignacio Magaña Hernandez,Antonella Palmese*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于 GWTC-5.0 种群约束与 O3 中等质量黑洞双星搜索结果，预测 LISA 可探测的双黑洞数量与质量：参考种群下 4 年期望探测约 6.6 个（信噪比>8），约 39% 为可多波段观测的源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面观测推断的高质量黑洞种群会显著影响 LISA 可探测双星的数量，需借助最新种群约束定量评估 LISA 的探测前景及其与地面第三代探测器的联合观测潜力。

Method: 采用 GWTC-5.0 发布的种群后验以及 O3 中等质量黑洞双星（intermediate-mass black hole binaries）搜索约束，外推预测 LISA 可探测双黑洞的数量与质量；并考察以断裂幂律（broken power law）叠加两或三个高斯峰描述初级质量分布或双星共同质量分布的替代种群模型。

Result: 参考 GWTC-5.0 种群给出 4 年内信噪比>8 的期望探测数为 6.6^{+5.5}_{-2.2}（中位数与 90% 可信区间）。三峰共同质量分布模型将 300–1000 M☉ 外推后中位数约翻倍至 ~14，其 90% 可信区间上端由数十升至数千，但极端计数受近似限制可靠性存疑。参考种群下 39^{+22}_{-11}% 的 LISA 可探测双星在任务期内或结束后 10 年内并合，属潜在多波段（multiband）源，且几乎均可被处于并合阶段的参考 Cosmic Explorer 配置探测。

Conclusion: 地面高质量黑洞种群的测量结果可同时约束 LISA 探测预测与通过旋近（inspiral）和并合阶段观测同一批双星的前景，凸显地基与空间引力波观测的互补性。

Abstract: The high-mass black hole population inferred from ground-based observations can substantially affect the number of binaries detectable by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). We use population constraints based on data from the latest LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-5.0), as well as constraints from the O3 search for intermediate-mass black hole binaries, to forecast the number and masses of binary black holes detectable by LISA. Using the released GWTC-5.0 population posterior, we obtain $6.6^{+5.5}_{-2.2}$ expected detections with signal-to-noise ratio $>8$ in 4 years (median and $90\%$ credible interval). The alternative population models we consider use a broken power law with two or three Gaussian peaks for either the primary-mass distribution or a common mass distribution for both black holes. For the three-peak model with the common mass distribution, extrapolating from $300$ to $1000M_\odot$ roughly doubles the median expected number of LISA detections to $\sim14$. The upper end of its $90\%$ credible interval rises from tens to thousands of expected detections, although approximations may limit the reliability of the most extreme counts. For the reference GWTC-5.0 population, $39^{+22}_{-11}\%$ of the binaries detectable by LISA merge during the mission or within $10$ years after its end and are potential multiband sources. Essentially all of these mergers would be detectable by a reference Cosmic Explorer configuration if it is operating at merger. Ground-based measurements of the high-mass population can therefore inform both LISA forecasts and the prospects for observing the same binaries through inspiral and merger.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Generative Refinement Learning for Temporal Interpolation in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36751)
*Pu Ren,Michael W. Mahoney,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iFLEX 通过学习修正简单的加权平均插值，重建时间分辨率不足的科学场中间时刻数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算昂贵的模拟与实际观测常提供低于下游任务需求的时间分辨率（temporal resolution）数据；固定格点平均会引入误差，而单纯的运动估计无法确定物理场值的变化。

Method: 模型先以加权平均估计缺失场（更接近目标时刻的观测权重更大），训练时将该估计从观测中间场中减去得到残差（residual），由条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion model）学习生成，条件包括两次观测、目标时间、观测间隔及可用物理参数。

Result: 在 Navier-Stokes Kraichnan 湍流、上海雷达降水与海表面温度三组数据上，iFLEX 的重建误差低于对比基线（含光流法与视频帧插值扩散模型）；雷诺数（Reynolds number）36,000 的受控对比中，相对 Frobenius 范数误差由直接生成场的约 13.7 降至 10.5。

Conclusion: 学习插值残差可有效提升科学场中间时刻的重建精度，优于直接生成场或通用视频帧插值方法。

Abstract: Computationally expensive simulations and real-world observations often provide data at a lower temporal resolution than downstream tasks require. Averaging observations at fixed grid points can introduce errors, and motion estimates alone do not determine how the physical field values change. In this work, we study whether learning to correct a simple numerical interpolation improves the reconstruction of intermediate scientific fields. Our model, interpolative FLow EXpert (iFLEX), first estimates the missing field by taking a weighted average of the two observations at each grid point, giving more weight to the observation closer to the requested time. During training, subtracting this estimate from an observed intermediate field gives the residual that a conditional diffusion model learns to generate. Both observations condition the model, together with the target time, the interval between observations, and available physical parameters. To reconstruct an unobserved intermediate field, the generated residual is added to the weighted average. We evaluate iFLEX on Navier-Stokes Kraichnan turbulence, Shanghai radar precipitation, and sea surface temperature. Across these datasets, iFLEX achieves lower reconstruction error than the evaluated baselines, including optical-flow and diffusion models developed for video frame interpolation in computer vision. In a controlled turbulence comparison at a Reynolds number of 36,000, learning the residual reduces relative Frobenius norm error from about 13.7 for direct field generation to 10.5, with the diffusion backbone and training setup held fixed. Code is available at https://github.com/paulpuren/iflex.

</details>


### [233] [Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113)
*Min Chen,Junqi Wang,Zeguan Wu,Wenhao Huang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将神经网络（neural networks, NNs）中从记忆到泛化的延迟转变（grokking）视作类相变现象，通过标度（scaling）与重整化群（renormalization group, RG）理论构建定量物理框架。对两层网络模加任务，以权重平方范数为序参量、训练轮次与权重衰减为热与场控制坐标，经Fiedler向量定义有效几何并执行实空间RG，导出的临界指数与经验估计在1-10%内吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking表现为记忆到泛化的延迟转变，其动力学是否可被标度与重整化群理论定量描述尚不明确；本文旨在为这一神经泛化转变建立一致的定量物理框架。

Method: 对两层网络模加任务，用训练模型系综定义序参量（平方权重范数）及热容与磁化率的涨落代理量，以训练轮次和权重衰减作为热与场控制坐标；通过对损失关于逐神经元范数的二阶展开构造精确耦合矩阵，用其Fiedler向量定义有效几何，继而以块自旋（block-spin）聚合相邻神经元并参数化耦合矩阵为带间强度，执行实空间RG并线性化局部RG映射。

Result: 谱排序带来耦合强度的单调空间衰减，而随机置换下不存在该性质；线性化局部RG映射给出相关标度指数及五个导出临界指数，直接经验估计与RG预测在1-10%内相符。

Conclusion: 该框架为grokking提供了一致的有效描述，表明标度与重整化群形式体系可作为研究神经网络动力学的工具。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

</details>


### [234] [Dynamical coarse-graining of neural-network training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36180)
*Lin-Juan Wang,Da Li,Huiling Duan,Jianxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为神经网络的动力学粗粒化建立了一个由三个定理组成的系统框架：先定义学习算子并给出下降定理，再给出精确闭合定理与动力学粗粒化定理，并在有限宽度非线性 ReLU 网络上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的动力学粗粒化可在远小于参数空间的状态空间中刻画宏观可观测量，并实现对训练过程的预测与控制；但尚需判定学习算子是否足以刻画学习动力学，以及如何构建与评估粗粒化模型，故需系统化的理论框架。

Method: 依据神经网络训练律定义学习算子（learning operator）并建立其下降定理（descent theorem），用以判定该算子是否足以刻画学习动力学；随后依次提出精确闭合定理（exact closure theorem）与动力学粗粒化定理（dynamic coarse-graining theorem），形成构建与评估粗粒化模型的系统框架，并以有限宽度非线性 ReLU 网络作为示例进行演示。

Result: 在有限宽度非线性 ReLU 网络上，对固定模式的动力学粗粒化模型被精确导出，且仅由五个独立矩（independent moments）构成；该模型能精确预测训练输出与演化中的学习算子。

Conclusion: 三个定理为神经网络训练的动力学粗粒化模型的构建与评估提供了系统框架；当精确闭合不可用时，该框架亦可为更一般神经网络的近似粗粒化模型开发提供指导。

Abstract: Dynamical coarse-graining of neural networks gives macroscopic observables and enables training to be predicted, and controlled in a state space far smaller than the parameter space. In this paper, we first define the learning operator following the training law of neural networks and establish a descent theorem for the learning operator, which serves to determine whether the operator is sufficient to characterize the learning dynamics. Then the exact closure theorem and the dynamic coarse-graining theorem are presented in sequence. The three theorems established in this paper provide a systematic framework for constructing and assessing dynamical coarse-grained models of neural-network training. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of the theorems with a finite-width nonlinear ReLU network. Its dynamic coarse-grained model for a fixed pattern is exactly derived and composed of five independent moments. The model exactly predict the training outputs and evolving learning operator. The framework may also guide the development of approximate coarse-grained models for more general neural networks when exact closure is unavailable.

</details>

# physics.class-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Asymptotic Analysis of the Biased van der Pol Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36398)
*Alain J. Brizard,Desmond L. Carrico*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 对偏置 van der Pol 方程在强耗散（ν≫1）与偏置参数 a∈(−1,1) 条件下作渐近分析，得到弛豫振荡周期的解析表达式，与数值结果误差在 0.1% 以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 van der Pol 方程在强耗散极限下的弛豫振荡（relaxation oscillation）周期已有较成熟结果，但加入偏置项 a 后系统沿稳定极限环（limit cycle）的振荡行为如何随偏置变化尚需定量的渐近刻画。

Method: 采用简化奇异摄动分析（abridged singular perturbation analysis），在耗散参数 ν≫1、偏置参数 a 取实数且位于 −1 与 1 之间的条件下，对大振幅弛豫振荡的周期进行渐近求解。

Result: 获得带偏置的弛豫振荡周期 T(a,ν) 的渐近解析结果；在 ν≫1 时该结果与数值计算的偏差在 0.1% 以内。

Conclusion: 简化奇异摄动方法能够以极低计算成本给出偏置 van der Pol 方程弛豫振荡周期的高精度（0.1% 量级）近似估计，适用于强耗散大振幅极限。

Abstract: We perform an asymptotic analysis of the biased van der Pol equation $\ddot{x} - ν\,(1 - x^{2})\,\dot{x} + x = a$, where the dissipation parameter is large $(ν\gg 1)$ and the bias parameter $a$ is a real number between $-1$ and $1$, corresponding to large-amplitude relaxation oscillations along a stable limit cycle. Using an abridged singular perturbation analysis, the period of relaxation oscillations $T(a,ν)$ is obtained that is accurate within $0.1\%$ of the numerical results for $ν\gg 1$.

</details>

### [236] [Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121)
*Haiqian Yang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作提出 Prospector：一个在领域知识图谱（knowledge graph, KG）上逐步遍历取证的小型模型，用于专业工程领域的科学推理。它通过逐次调用少量工具渐进构建上下文，并给出可逐边核验的证据链（chain of evidence）。在半导体制造文献图谱上，其性能接近同等图谱与工具条件下的最强现成模型，显著优于闭卷或预选子图的基线，并可迁移至生物启发材料、PFAS 与材料性质等其他领域图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工程领域的科学推理依赖散落于文献、常属专有且未进入前沿大模型（LLM）训练数据的知识，前沿模型在此类领域倾向给出泛泛答案。知识图谱可用于支撑（ground）LLM 推理，但图谱规模过大无法整体输入，常见 LLM+KG 系统在推理开始前就预选子图、提前判定相关性，存在局限。

Method: 训练紧凑模型 Prospector，使其以逐步遍历（traverse）方式在领域知识图谱上推理，依据已获证据继续寻求新证据。模型一次调用一个工具、调用少量工具集，渐进构建上下文，并以可沿边核验的证据链形式作答，证据可逐条对照工具从图谱检索到的内容。

Result: 在半导体制造文献构建的图谱上，Prospector 接近同等图谱与工具条件下最强现成模型的水平，并远超闭卷作答或基于预选子图作答的现成模型。仅在半导体图谱上训练，仍可迁移到生物启发材料、全氟和多氟烷基物质（PFAS）及材料性质三个不同领域的未见图谱。单 GPU 部署时，其响应更快、成本远低于托管服务，且专有数据保留在所有者硬件上。策略的潜在状态（latent state）在已见与未见图谱上均可指示其下一步动作，调节潜在状态可使策略在行为模式间切换。

Conclusion: 结果提示一种实用的 LLM + KG 范式：面向私有数据的科学与工程推理中，在已发表文献构建的图谱上训练的紧凑模型，可将其遍历能力迁移到仍保留在所有者手中的未见图谱。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning in specialized engineering fields depends on knowledge scattered across the literature, often proprietary and absent from frontier LLM training data. In such domains, frontier models tend to give generic answers. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can ground LLM reasoning. Because a graph is too large to provide in full, typical LLM+KG systems preselect a portion, deciding relevance before reasoning begins. Here we train a compact model, Prospector, to reason over a domain knowledge graph by traversing it step by step, seeking evidence in light of what it has already found. Prospector calls a small set of tools one at a time to progressively build its context, and answers with a chain of evidence that can be checked edge by edge against what its tools retrieved from the graph. On a knowledge graph built from the semiconductor-manufacturing literature, Prospector approaches the strongest off-the-shelf models given the same graph and tools, and it far exceeds off-the-shelf models answering closed-book or from a pre-selected portion of the graph. Although trained only on the semiconductor graph, Prospector transfers to unseen knowledge graphs from three other distinct fields: bio-inspired materials, per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and materials properties. Served on a single GPU, Prospector answers faster and at a fraction of the cost of a hosted service, while keeping proprietary data on the owner's hardware. We find that the policy's latent state is indicative of its next move on both seen and unseen graphs, and modulating the latent state can steer the policy from one behavior mode to another. These results suggest a practical LLM + KG paradigm for scientific and engineering reasoning over private data, in which a compact model trained on a graph from published literature transfers its traversal to unseen graphs that stay with their owners.

</details>

### [237] [Quaternionic Buzano-Dragomir Inequality and Deutsch Entropic Uncertainty Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35881)
*K. Mahesh Krishna*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文证明 Buzano-Dragomir 不等式（Buzano-Dragomir inequality）在四元数 Hilbert 空间（quaternionic Hilbert space）中成立，并据此导出四元数数值半径（quaternionic numerical radius）的 Dragomir 不等式及 Deutsch 熵不确定性原理（Deutsch entropic uncertainty principle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅以推广既有不等式并导出相关结论为目标；从内容看，旨在将经典（复）Hilbert 空间上的相关不等式拓展至四元数（quaternion）框架。

Method: 采用的方法是将 Buzano-Dragomir 不等式推广到四元数 Hilbert 空间，并以该推广作为工具进行推导。

Result: 获得三项结果：Buzano-Dragomir 不等式在四元数 Hilbert 空间中成立；由此得到四元数数值半径的 Dragomir 不等式；以及四元数 Hilbert 空间中的 Deutsch 熵不确定性原理。

Conclusion: Buzano-Dragomir 不等式可有效推广至四元数 Hilbert 空间，并为四元数数值半径不等式与 Deutsch 熵不确定性原理提供统一推导途径；摘要未涉及进一步的应用或局限。

Abstract: We show that the Buzano-Dragomir inequality holds for quaternionic Hilbert spaces. Using this generalization, we derive the Dragomir inequality for the quaternionic numerical radius and Deutsch entropic uncertainty principle for quaternionic Hilbert spaces.

</details>

### [238] [SuperJEPA: Pretraining with a Supervision-Augmented Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36121)
*Dylan Sheldon Rankin,Ho Fung Tsoi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出 SuperJEPA，将联合嵌入预测架构（joint-embedding predictive architecture, JEPA）与监督目标结合，验证在已有大规模高保真标注模拟数据的高能物理（high energy physics, HEP）场景下，自监督目标仍能提升下游喷注标记（jet tagging）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型（foundation models）通常依赖远超标注集规模的未标注数据，而高能物理领域已能提供大规模高保真标注模拟样本，因此有必要检验：当标注样本已充足时，自监督目标（self-supervised objectives）是否仍优于单纯的监督预训练（supervised pretraining）。

Method: 引入 SuperJEPA，即在联合嵌入预测架构上增加监督目标，构成预测式自监督与监督联合的预训练方案，并在多种任务、模型规模与预训练数据规模下与纯监督预训练进行比较。

Result: 在多个下游喷注标记任务、多种模型规模及多种预训练数据规模上均观察到性能提升；该增益在冻结编码器（frozen encoder）与微调编码器（fine-tuned encoder）两种设置下均持续存在。

Conclusion: 结果支持在未来大规模粒子物理基础模型中采用预测式自监督（predictive self-supervision），表明即使标注数据丰富，自监督目标仍具附加价值。

Abstract: Foundation models often obtain powerful results through very large unlabeled training datasets that dwarf available labeled datasets in size, but high energy physics already provides large samples of high-fidelity labeled simulation. We therefore ask whether self-supervised objectives improve over supervised pretraining alone when large labeled samples already exist. We introduce SuperJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architec- ture augmented with a supervised objective, and find improved downstream jet-tagging performance across multiple tasks, model sizes, and pretraining dataset sizes. The gains persist for both frozen and fine-tuned encoders, motivating predictive self-supervision in future large-scale particle-physics foundation models.

</details>


### [239] [Update on transverse-spin azimuthal asymmetries in single-hadron SIDIS on transversely polarised deuterons at COMPASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36224)
*Artur Hoghmrtsyan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CERN的COMPASS实验利用2022年160 GeV/c μ子束与横向极化氘靶，从完整数据样本中同时提取了单强子SIDIS中的全部八个横向自旋相关方位角不对称性，其中Collins与Sivers不对称性较此前基于约70%统计量的结果得到更新，其余六个为首次发表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMPASS长期开展半单举深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）计划，旨在全面理解核子结构；横向极化氘靶数据可提供对多种横动量依赖部分子分布函数（TMD PDFs）及次领头扭度（subleading-twist）部分子关联的敏感性，而此前仅基于部分统计量报告了部分不对称性。

Method: 使用160 GeV/c高能μ子束轰击横向极化氘靶，于2022年采集数据；在单强子SIDIS中识别出八个横向自旋相关方位角不对称性（azimuthal asymmetries），并从完整2022年氘靶样本中同时提取全部八个量。

Result: Collins与Sivers不对称性基于完整样本更新后与此前约70%统计量的结果一致；其余六个不对称性首次以完整2022年统计量给出。

Conclusion: 结合已有的质子结果，这些测量完成了COMPASS关于SIDIS中横向自旋效应的综合计划，并为全局分析（global analyses）提供了关键输入。

Abstract: Over the years, the COMPASS experiment at CERN has pursued an extensive SIDIS programme aimed at a comprehensive understanding of the structure of the nucleon, using a high-energy muon beam and polarised targets. Within this programme, measurements with a transversely polarised deuteron target were performed in 2022 using the 160 GeV/c muon beam. In single-hadron SIDIS on such a target, eight transverse-spin-dependent azimuthal asymmetries arise, providing sensitivity to several transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution functions and to subleading-twist partonic correlations. We report the simultaneous extraction of all eight asymmetries from the complete 2022 deuteron sample. The Collins and Sivers asymmetries, previously presented using about 70% of the 2022 statistics, are updated with the full sample and found consistent with the earlier result, while the six remaining asymmetries are presented for the first time with the full 2022 statistics. Combined with the existing proton results, these measurements complete a comprehensive COMPASS programme on transverse-spin effects in SIDIS, providing essential input for global analyses.

</details>


### [240] [Prospects for exploring quantum-correlated neutral $D\overline{D}$ decays at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36529)
*Ying-Hao Wang,Yi-Feng Wu,Pei-Rong Li,Yu Zhang,Xiao-Rui Lyu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文评估了在拟建的超级陶粲装置（Super Tau-Charm Facility, STCF）上，利用量子关联（quantum-correlated）中性粲介子对，通过双标记（double tag）方法测量粲混合、间接CP破坏及强子参数的前景与预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索STCF对粲物理关键参数（粲混合、间接CP破坏、强子参数）的精确测量能力，并为CKM角γ拟合提供独特输入，同时与Belle II和LHCb的含时测量形成互补。

Method: 基于√s = 3.773与4.030 GeV处e⁺e⁻→D(*)D̄(*)产生的相干DD̄对的含时积分双标记率（time-integrated double tag rates）；一端D以准味特定衰变（quasi flavour-specific）标记，另一端D̄以味特定模式、CP本征态模式或自共轭模式探测；假设每个能量点积分亮度为1 ab⁻¹评估预期灵敏度。

Result: 结果表明具备精确测定粲混合参数、间接CP破坏参数、强相位差（strong-phase differences）及相干因子（coherence factors）的能力。

Conclusion: 强子参数的约束可为CKM角γ拟合提供独特输入，且粲混合与间接CP破坏参数的测量与Belle II、LHCb的含时测量相互补充。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for determining charm mixing, indirect $C\!P$ violation and hadronic parameters in charm decays with quantum-correlated neutral charm mesons at the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility. The study is based on time-integrated double tag rates of coherent $D\overline{D}$ pairs produced at $\sqrt{s}=3.773$ and $4.030$ GeV, from $e^{+}e^{-}\to D^{(*)}\overline{D}^{(*)}$ processes. Here, one $D$ is tagged with the quasi flavour-specific decays $D\to K^-π^+π^+π^-$, $K^-π^+π^0$, and $K^-π^+$, while the other $\overline{D}$ is detected with the final states in flavour-specific mode, $C\!P$ eigenstate mode, or self-conjugate mode. The expected sensitivities are evaluated assuming an integrated luminosity of $1~{\rm ab}^{-1}$ at each energy point, demonstrating the capability on precise determination of the charm mixing parameters, the indirect $C\!P$ violation parameters, the strong-phase differences, and the coherence factors. The constraints of the hadronic-parameters provide unique inputs to the CKM angle $γ$ fit, while the measurements of charm mixing and indirect $C\!P$ violation parameters are complementary to the time-dependent measurements in the Belle II and LHCb experiments.

</details>


### [241] [Antenna-coupled microstrip summing network for wave-like dark matter searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36684)
*Chelsea Bartram,Taj A. Dyson,Jacob Laurel,Sephora Ruppert,Matthew O. Withers,Osmond Wen,Andrew K. Yi,Chao-Lin Kuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于微波缝隙天线阵列（slot-antenna array）与微带求和网络（summing network）容性耦合的读出方案，用于从大体积、多波长（V ≫ λ³）谐振腔中高效提取轴子（axion）信号，并在19–21 GHz频段完成设计演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腔体轴子探测实验中，通过插入式紧凑线天线实现谐振腔与微波传输线耦合，该方法对正圆柱腔体足够，但对ADMX-VERA等面向更高频率的大体积多波长腔体难以高效提取功率。

Method: 采用电容耦合至微带“求和网络”的微波缝隙天线阵列，将多条传输线信号相干合成单一输出；该架构借鉴宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）探测器设计，并据此设计并表征19–21 GHz新型高频谐振腔。

Result: 论文给出采用该读出方案的新型高频谐振腔（19–21 GHz）的设计与表征结果。

Conclusion: 该读出架构有望通过在单一大体积内集成多个求和网络腔体，达到Dine-Fischler-Srednicki-Zhitnitsky（DFSZ）模型的灵敏度。

Abstract: A commonly used technology for axion and other wave-like dark matter searches is a microwave resonator. In such experiments, the detected signal strength depends on the coupling between the resonator and the microwave transmission line that delivers power to the amplifier. In Axion Dark Matter eXperiment (ADMX) and other cavity searches, this coupling is typically achieved using a compact wire antenna inserted into the cavity. While this approach is adequate for right-cylindrical cavities, it can become difficult to efficiently extract power from large-volume, multi-wavelength ($V \gg λ^3$) resonators targeted for higher frequencies, such as those designed by ADMX-VERA (Volume Enhanced Resonant Axion). Here, we present a solution based on a microwave slot-antenna array capacitively coupled to a microstrip "summing network," which coherently combines signals from multiple transmission lines into a single output. This summing architecture is adapted from designs originally developed for cosmic microwave background (CMB) detectors. We demonstrate the design and characterization of a new high-frequency resonator that employs this readout scheme in the $19-21$ GHz range. Finally we discuss the potential to reach Dine-Fischler-Srednicki-Zhitnitsky (DFSZ) sensitivity using multiple summing network cavities fitted into a large volume.

</details>

Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器在 4.26–4.95 GeV 27 个质心能量点、总积分亮度 13.0 fb⁻¹ 的 e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，首次搜索 e⁺e⁻ → π⁰K⁺K⁻J/ψ 过程，未发现与奇异矢量态或 Zc/Zcs 中间态相符的共振结构，并给出 90% 置信水平的截面上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在首次搜寻 e⁺e⁻ → π⁰K⁺K⁻J/ψ 这一过程，并检验其中是否存在粲偶素样矢量态（charmonium-like vector states）及 Zc/Zcs 等奇异中间态信号。

Method: 利用 BESIII 探测器在 27 个质心能量点（4.26–4.95 GeV）、总积分亮度 13.0 fb⁻¹ 的 e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，提取该过程的截面谱（cross section spectra）以及中间态的不变质量分布（invariant mass distributions）。

Result: 未观察到与所考察的奇异候选态相符的共振结构；据此给出玻恩截面（Born cross section）在 90% 置信水平（confidence level）下的上限及其最佳拟合值，同时也给出中间态产生截面（production of the intermediate states）的上限。摘要未报告具体数值。

Conclusion: 在现有数据精度下，e⁺e⁻ → π⁰K⁺K⁻J/ψ 过程中未见显著共振信号，结果为该过程及其中间态的产生设定了 90% 置信水平的上限约束。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at 27 center-of-mass energies between 4.26 and 4.95 GeV corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of $13.0~\text{fb}^{-1}$, we report the first search for the process $e^{+}e^{-} \to π^0 K^+K^-J/ψ$. We additionally probe the cross section spectra for potential charmonium-like vector states and invariant mass distributions for intermediate $Z_c/Z_{cs}$ state signals, and find no resonant structures consistent with these exotic candidates. Given the absence of observed resonant signals, upper limits at the 90\% confidence level are determined for the Born cross sections, along with their best-fit values, as well as for the production of the intermediate states.

</details>


### [243] [Improved measurement of $ψ(3686)\to π^+π^- η$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37182)

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的约27亿ψ(3686)事例，对ψ(3686)→π⁺π⁻η衰变在ρ(770)⁰质量区进行分波分析，测定ψ(3686)→ρ(770)⁰η分支比，首次以35.7σ显著性观测ψ(3686)→a₂(1320)±π∓，并由此给出η-η′赝标量混合角θ_P。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对ψ(3686)共振附近π⁺π⁻η末态，需要区分连续谱贡献与ψ(3686)共振的干涉效应，并利用该过程提取中间共振态信息及η-η′赝标量混合角（pseudoscalar mixing angle）等物理参数。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，在π⁺π⁻不变质量[0.3, 2.2] GeV/c²（ρ(770)⁰质量区）内开展分波分析（partial wave analysis）；并借助能量扫描数据（energy-scan data）测量ψ(3686)共振附近截面线形（cross section line-shape），以估计连续谱贡献及其与共振的干涉。

Result: 测得ψ(3686)→ρ(770)⁰η分支比（branching fraction）为(1.34±0.03±0.13)×10⁻⁵；首次观测到ψ(3686)→a₂(1320)±π∓衰变，显著性为35.7σ，其部分分支比为(2.13±0.05±0.32)×10⁻⁵；由此得到η-η′赝标量混合角θ_P为(-11.2±1.6±3.6)°或(-19.4±3.1±5.7)°（第一项为统计误差，第二项为系统误差）。

Conclusion: 该分析确立了ψ(3686)→ρ(770)⁰η与ψ(3686)→a₂(1320)±π∓的衰变分支比，并基于此过程给出两种可能的η-η′混合角取值，为赝标量介子混合及ψ(3686)衰变机制研究提供实验约束。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we perform a partial wave analysis of the decay $ψ(3686)\toπ^{+}π^{-}η$ with the $π^{+}π^{-}$ invariant mass in the $ρ(770)^0$ mass region, [0.3, 2.2] $\mathrm{GeV}/c^2$. The cross section line-shape around the $ψ(3686)$ resonance is measured using energy-scan data sets, enabling the estimation of continuum contribution and its interference with the $ψ(3686)$ resonance. The branching fraction of $ψ(3686)\toρ(770)^0η$ is determined to be $(1.34 \pm 0.03\pm0.13) \times 10^{-5}$. The $ψ(3686)\to a_{2}(1320)^{\pm}π^{\mp}$ decay is observed for the first time with a significance of $35.7σ$, and its partial branching fraction is measured to be $(2.13 \pm 0.05\pm0.32) \times 10^{-5}$. The $η$-$η^{\prime}$ pseudoscalar mixing angle $θ_P$ is then found to be $(-11.2 \pm 1.6\pm3.6)^\circ$ or $(-19.4\pm 3.1\pm5.7)^\circ$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约89.98亿$J/\psi$事例，对稀有衰变$J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$的衰变分支比（branching fraction）进行了迄今最精确的测量，并搜寻仅耦合第二、三代轻子的新规范玻色子（gauge boson）$Z^{\prime}$，未发现显著信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有四轻子衰变分支比的精确测量可检验理论预言（theoretical prediction），而针对$U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$模型中新玻色子$Z^{\prime}$的搜寻可探测超出标准模型（Standard Model）的轻子味道相关相互作用。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器采集的$(8998\pm39)\times10^6$个$J/\psi$事例，测量$J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$的衰变分支比；通过$J/\psi\to Z^{\prime}\mu^+\mu^-$（$Z^{\prime}\to\mu^+\mu^-$）在$(0.212,2.6)$ GeV/$c^2$的$\mu^+\mu^-$不变质量（invariant mass）区间搜寻$Z^{\prime}$，并提取$T$-odd观测量$A_T$以检验$CP$对称性。

Result: 测得分支比$B(J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-)=[0.91\pm0.04\,(\mathrm{stat.})\pm0.07\,(\mathrm{syst.})]\times10^{-6}$，与理论预言在一个标准差（standard deviation）内一致；$T$-odd观测量$A_T=[0.05\pm0.04\,(\mathrm{stat.})\pm0.01\,(\mathrm{syst.})]$，与$CP$守恒（$CP$ conservation）一致。未观测到显著$Z^{\prime}$信号，在90%置信水平（confidence level）下给出耦合常数$g^{\prime}$从0.001至0.294的上限（upper limit）。

Conclusion: 该分支比测量为目前最精确结果且与理论相符，$CP$不对称性检验与$CP$守恒一致；$Z^{\prime}$耦合常数上限在$0.7$ GeV/$c^2$以下的$Z^{\prime}$质量区间与Belle II给出的上限相当。

Abstract: We present a precision measurement of the branching fraction of the rare decay $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$, and a search for a new gauge boson $Z^{\prime}$ that couples only to the second and third lepton generations, using a data sample of $(8998 \pm 39) \times 10^6$ $J/ψ$ events collected by the BESIII detector. The branching fraction of the $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ decay is measured to be $B(J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-)=[0.91\pm0.04~{\rm (stat.)}\pm0.07~{\rm (syst.)}]\times 10^{-6}$, representing the most precise measurement to date and consistent with the theoretical prediction within one standard deviation. Additionally, we search for $CP$ asymmetry in the $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-μ^+μ^-$ decay and determine the T-odd observable to be $A_T = [0.05\pm 0.04~{\rm (stat.)}\pm0.01~{\rm (syst.)}]$, which is consistent with $CP$ conservation. In the search for $J/ψ\to Z^{\prime}μ^+μ^-$ via $Z^{\prime}\toμ^+μ^-$, no significant $Z^{\prime}$ signal is observed in the $μ^+μ^-$ invariant mass region of $(0.212,~2.6)$~GeV$/c^2$, and we set upper limits on the ${\rm U}(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$ model coupling constant $g^{\prime}$ ranging from 0.001 to 0.294 at the 90\% confidence level, which is comparable to the upper limits set by Belle II for $Z^{\prime}$ masses below $0.7$~GeV$/c^2$.

</details>


### [245] [High significance measurement of reactor coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering on germanium by CONUS+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37318)
*N. Ackermann,H. Bonet,C. Buck,J. Hakenmüller,G. Heusser,M. Lindner,W. Maneschg,M. Meier,S. Mertens,D. Piani,M. Rank,T. Rink,E. Sánchez García,Y. Shi,L. Schönfeld,I. Stalder,H. Strecker,J. Wendel,J. Woenckhaus*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于莱布施塔特核电站 CONUS+ 高纯锗探测器，实现迄今最精确的反应堆反中微子相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEνNS）测量，以 10.2σ 显著度观测到 1555±152 个事件，与标准模型预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在提升反应堆反中微子相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, CEνNS）的测量精度，并借此在低动量转移区域检验标准模型（Standard Model）并测定弱混合角（weak mixing angle）。

Method: 采用 CONUS+ 高纯锗（high-purity germanium）探测器在莱布施塔特（Leibstadt）核电站进行反应堆运行期取数，累积曝光量（exposure）为 1296 kg·days，并采用盲分析（blind likelihood analysis）进行似然拟合。

Result: 测得 1555±152 个 CEνNS 事件，显著度为 10.2σ，与标准模型预期 1701±198 个事件在 0.6 个标准差内相符；同时给出约 10 MeV 动量转移下的弱混合角 sin²θ_W = 0.225±0.022，与标准模型预言一致。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了迄今最精确的反应堆反中微子 CEνNS 测量，并在低动量转移能区验证了标准模型对 CEνNS 截面与弱混合角的预言。

Abstract: We report the most precise measurement to date of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) from reactor antineutrinos using the CONUS+ high-purity germanium detectors at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on, a blind likelihood analysis yields $1555 \pm 152$ CE$ν$NS events, with a significance of 10.2 $σ$, in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events within 0.6 standard deviations. The result also determines the weak mixing angle at a momentum transfer of about 10 MeV, $\sin^2θ_W = 0.225 \pm 0.022$, consistent with the Standard Model prediction.

</details>


### [246] [Analysis of CONUS+ run 2: Detector performance, background model and CE$ν$NS measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37345)
*N. Ackermann,H. Bonet,C. Buck,J. Hakenmüller,G. Heusser,M. Lindner,W. Maneschg,M. Meier,S. Mertens,D. Piani,M. Rank,T. Rink,E. Sánchez García,Y. Shi,L. Schönfeld,I. Stalder,H. Strecker,J. Wendel,J. Woenckhaus*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CONUS+ 实验在莱布施塔特核电站用升级后的 2.42 kg 高纯锗探测器分析第二轮数据，以 1296 kg·day 反应堆开启曝光在 140–170 eVee 阈值下观测到 1555 ± 152 个相干弹性中微子-核散射（CEνNS）事件，显著性 10.2σ，与标准模型预期一致（比值 0.91 ± 0.14）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEνNS 是对中微子性质高度敏感的探针，但其探测需将能量阈值推至亚 keV 尺度并有效控制本底，因此需要更低本底、更大质量的探测器来提升灵敏度。

Method: 将原三个 1 kg 高纯锗（HPGe）探测器中的三个替换为更大的 2.42 kg 二极管完成升级，并对 run 2 数据开展探测器表征、本底模型、信号预测与预期灵敏度分析；采用盲分析（blind analysis）与直接拟合信号计数量的似然方法提取 CEνNS 信号。

Result: 新探测器单位质量本底较原探测器降低约一倍，能量阈值达 140–170 eVee；在 1296 kg·day（反应堆开启）与 86 kg·day（反应堆关闭）曝光下得到 N_s = 1555 ± 152 个 CEνNS 事件，显著性 10.2σ，与标准模型预期 1701 ± 198 个事件相符，N_s/N_SM = 0.91 ± 0.14。

Conclusion: 升级后的 CONUS+ 探测器在更低本底与亚 keV 阈值条件下以 10.2σ 显著度确认了反应堆反中微子的 CEνNS 信号，其结果与标准模型预测在误差范围内一致，验证了该实验路线在中微子性质探测上的能力。

Abstract: Coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) provides a uniquely sensitive probe of neutrino properties, but its detection requires pushing energy thresholds to the sub-keV scale while controlling backgrounds. The CONUS+ experiment, located at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant, reported the first observation of CE$ν$NS using reactor antineutrinos in 2025. Following a detector upgrade that replaced three of the original 1 kg high-purity germanium detectors with larger 2.42 kg diodes, this paper presents the analysis of the CONUS+ run 2 dataset, including the detector characterization, the background model, the signal prediction and expected sensitivity, and the extraction of the CE$ν$NS signal. The new detectors reach a background level per unit mass reduced by approximately a factor of two relative to the original detectors, while reaching energy thresholds between 140 and 170 eV$_{ee}$. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on and 86 kg$\cdot$days reactor-off, a blind likelihood analysis fitting directly the number of signal counts yields $N_s = 1555 \pm 152$ CE$ν$NS events, corresponding to a significance of 10.2$\,σ$. The result is in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events, with $N_s/N_{\mathrm{SM}} = 0.91 \pm 0.14$.

</details>
