Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约27.12亿个ψ(3686)事例，首次实验研究χ_cJ→pK⁻K⁻Ξ̄⁺+c.c.衰变，首次观测到χ_c1与χ_c2通道（显著性均为9.0σ），并给出χ_c0通道的支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对χ_cJ→pK⁻K⁻Ξ̄⁺+c.c.这一含重子-反重子末态的粲偶素衰变此前缺乏实验研究，需通过高统计量数据检验相应衰变机制并测量其分支比（branching fraction）。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器收集的(2.712±0.014)×10⁹个ψ(3686)事例，重建χ_cJ（J=0,1,2）到pK⁻K⁻Ξ̄⁺及其共轭（c.c.）末态，测量分支比并评估信号显著性（significance）。

Result: χ_c1与χ_c2衰变道均为首次观测，显著性各为9.0σ，分支比分别为(9.6⁺²·²₋₂.₀±0.8)×10⁻⁶与(7.5⁺¹·⁸₋₁.₅±0.5)×10⁻⁶；χ_c0道未见显著信号，在90%置信水平（confidence level）给出12.7×10⁻⁶的分支比上限。

Conclusion: 该工作是χ_cJ→pK⁻K⁻Ξ̄⁺+c.c.的首个实验研究，确认了χ_c1与χ_c2道存在并确立了χ_c0道的上限，为粲偶素（charmonium）衰变到重子-反重子（baryon-antibaryon）对的机制研究提供实验约束。

Abstract: By analyzing $(2.712\pm0.014)\times10^9$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector, we report the first experimental study of the decays $χ_{cJ}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.~(J=0,1,2)$. The decays $χ_{c1}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.$ and $χ_{c2}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.$ are observed for the first time with significances of $9.0σ$ each, and their branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal{B}_{χ_{c1}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.} =(9.6^{+2.2}_{-2.0}\pm0.8)\times10^{-6}$, and $\mathcal{B}_{χ_{c2}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.} =(7.5^{+1.8}_{-1.5}\pm0.5)\times10^{-6}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. No significant signal is observed for $χ_{c0}\to p K^- K^- \bar Ξ^{+}+c.c.$, and an upper limit on its branching fraction of $12.7\times10^{-6}$ is established at the 90\% confidence level.

</details>

Strauss,A. M. Szelc,P. Sanchez-Lucas,C. Thorpe,D. Totani,M. Toups,C. Touramanis,L. Tung,G. A. Valdiviesso,R. Van de Water,A. Vázquez-Ramos,L. Wan,M. Weber,H. Wei,T. Wester,A. J. White,A. Wilkinson,P. Wilson,T. Wongjirad,E. Worcester,M. Worcester,S. Yadav,E. Yandel,L. Yates,S. Yebes,B. Yu,H. Yu,J. Yu,B. Zamorano,J. Zennamo,C. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SBND的宇宙线标记系统（CRT）曾作为独立探测器CRT Beam Telescope在2017–2018年收集数据，本研究据此首次利用SBND CRT数据开展超出标准模型的新物理搜寻，针对长寿命粒子（LLP）衰变为电子—正电子对，未发现显著超出，并给出有效产生截面的90%置信水平上限约$10^{-38}$ cm$^2$（衰变长度0.3–100 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBND除中微子物理目标外，还可搜寻经中微子—核上散射（upscattering）产生并衰变为可见末态的长寿命粒子（long-lived particles, LLP）。CRT Beam Telescope原用于调试与性能研究，但其收集的大统计量数据（对应$2.95\times10^{20}$质子打靶）也可用于搜寻位移的LLP衰变。

Method: 所用数据来自位于Booster Neutrino Beam（BNB）束流线、距中微子靶110 m处的CRT Beam Telescope（2017–2018年独立运行），对应$2.95\times10^{20}$质子打靶。分析针对LLP衰变为电子—正电子对（electron-positron pairs）的位移衰变信号，并与预期本底比较。

Result: 未观测到相对预期本底的显著超出（no significant excess）；对实验室系衰变长度0.3–100 m范围，给出有效产生截面（effective production cross section）的90%置信水平上限，量级约为$10^{-38}$ cm$^2$。

Conclusion: 该结果是使用SBND CRT Beam Telescope数据开展的首次超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）物理搜寻，通过探测米量级衰变长度的位移衰变特征，补充了现有搜寻结果。

Abstract: The Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) is a liquid argon time projection chamber in Fermilab's Short-Baseline Neutrino Program, positioned 110 meters from the neutrino target along the Booster Neutrino Beam (BNB). In addition to its neutrino physics goals, SBND enables searches for new physics, including long-lived particles (LLPs) produced via neutrino-nucleus upscattering that decay into visible final states. Although neutrino data taking with the full SBND experiment began in December 2024, a subset of the Cosmic Ray Tagger (CRT) system operated from 2017--2018 as a standalone detector, referred to as the CRT Beam Telescope, collecting data corresponding to $2.95\times 10^{20}$ protons on target from the BNB. Originally intended for commissioning and performance studies, the substantial dataset collected also enables searches for displaced LLP decays. This paper presents the first search for physics beyond the Standard Model using data from the SBND CRT Beam Telescope, targeting LLP decays into electron-positron pairs. We observe no significant excess above the expected background and set 90% confidence-level upper limits on an effective production cross section at the level of $10^{-38}$ cm$^2$ for laboratory decay lengths of 0.3-100 m. These results complement existing searches by probing displaced decay signatures at meter-scale decay lengths.

</details>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [A 3D-$Δ$PDF-like measure for hidden anisotropy in disordered materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36104)
*Helena Barker,Thomas C. Nicholas,Andrew L. Goodwin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的相关函数（correlation function），用于量化无序材料（disordered materials）中隐藏的局域各向异性（hidden local anisotropy）；其构建思路类比三维差分对分布函数（3D-ΔPDF），旨在识别因体相（bulk）对称性平均而丢失的结构关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序材料的体相平均会掩盖局域结构信息，常规方法难以显现隐藏的局域各向异性，因此需要一种能捕捉这类被平均掉的结构关联的工具。

Method: 借鉴三维差分对分布函数（3D-ΔPDF）的思想，构造一个简单的相关函数（correlation function），以识别在体相对称性平均过程中消失的结构相关性。

Result: 摘要仅说明该方法可识别被平均掩盖的结构关联，未给出具体定量结果或验证数据。

Conclusion: 该相关函数方法可推广应用于无序晶体（disordered crystals）、非晶材料（amorphous materials）与液体（liquids）等多种体系。

Abstract: We introduce a simple correlation function for quantifying hidden local anisotropy in disordered materials. Developed in analogy with the three-dimensional difference pair distribution function (3D-$Δ$PDF), our approach identifies structural correlations that are lost through averaging over bulk symmetry. The same approach might be applied to models of disordered crystals, amorphous materials, and liquids alike.

</details>


### [250] [p-Adic statistical field theory of fluctuation effects in hierarchical neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36400)
*W. A. Zúñiga-Galindo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将 Buice-Cowan 神经场论移植到以 p-adic 数描述的无限自相似树状网络上，发现临界行为由耦合沿层级的衰减指数（而非空间维度）决定，并给出 Ginzburg 判据、圈修正方程与对数周期修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 Buice-Cowan 场论的连续极限以欧氏空间中的普通扩散（ordinary diffusion）刻画神经活动传播，无法同时编码皮层网络的层级组织（hierarchical organization）与小世界性质（small-world property），因此需要一种能内蕴层级结构的连续化框架。

Method: 把神经元组织为以 p-adic 数（p-adic numbers）描述的无限自相似树状结构；路径积分（path integral）形式保持不变，有限网络成为连续理论的精确限制，短程核不再约化为 Laplacian，而是各自对应非局域的 p-adic 扩散算子（对按层级固定比例衰减的耦合即 Taibleson-Vladimirov 算子），临界理论为超度量 Reggeon 场论（ultrametric Reggeon field theory）。

Result: 上临界维度（upper critical dimension）等于耦合衰减指数的两倍；获得 Ginzburg 型判据、圈修正的层级 Wilson-Cowan 方程、标度的对数周期修正（log-periodic corrections）与幂律关联；树上的精确模拟显示雪崩尺寸指数（avalanche-size exponent）在慢衰减时为平均场值 3/2，快衰减时趋向有向渗流（directed percolation）值。

Conclusion: 决定平均场指数是否适用的是耦合沿层级衰减的快慢而非空间维度；该层级网络在衰减指数小于维度时自然具备小世界性质，而欧氏理论需假设长程耦合才能得到同样性质。

Abstract: The Buice-Cowan field theory [PRE 75, 051919 (2007)] describes fluctuations and correlations of neural activity through a path integral whose mean-field limit is the Wilson-Cowan equation and whose critical behavior belongs to the directed percolation class. In its continuum limit the neurons are the points of a Euclidean space and activity spreads by ordinary diffusion, which encodes neither the hierarchical organization of cortical networks nor their small-world property. We formulate a version of the theory in which the neurons are organized in an infinite, self-similar, tree-like structure, described by the p-adic numbers. The path integral carries over unchanged, and finite networks are exact restrictions of the continuum theory. The main change is in the linear response: in the Euclidean theory every short-range kernel reduces to the Laplacian, while in the p-adic theory each connectivity kernel gives its own nonlocal p-adic diffusion operator. For couplings that decay by a fixed factor per level of the hierarchy this is the Taibleson-Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is an ultrametric Reggeon field theory whose upper critical dimension is twice the decay exponent of the coupling. Hence the decay of the coupling up the hierarchy, and not the dimension of space, decides whether mean-field exponents apply. We obtain a Ginzburg-type criterion, loop-corrected hierarchical Wilson-Cowan equations, log-periodic corrections to scaling and power-law correlations. Exact simulations on a tree support the predictions: the avalanche-size exponent takes the mean-field value 3/2 when the coupling decays slowly and decreases toward the directed-percolation value when it decays fast. The hierarchical network is also small-world when the decay exponent is smaller than the dimension, a property that the Euclidean theory obtains only by assuming long-range coupling.

</details>


### [251] [Hierarchical connectivity sets the upper critical dimension of neural avalanches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36404)
*W. A. Zúñiga-Galindo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文表明层级连接（hierarchical connectivity）可解除"指数3/2神经雪崩需N≥4维"的约束：在p进Buice–Cowan场论中，层级衰减耦合选择Taibleson–Vladimirov算子，临界理论为超度量有向渗流，上临界维数N_c=2σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模指数（size exponent）为3/2的神经雪崩（neural avalanches）通常由平均场临界分支（mean-field critical branching）解释，但短程耦合下该解释要求维度N≥4，构成一个约束；本文旨在说明层级连接可消除该维度限制。

Method: 在p进（p-adic）版本的Buice–Cowan场论（field theory）中，采用每层级按p^{-(N+σ)}衰减的耦合，该耦合选取Taibleson–Vladimirov算子；并在树上进行精确模拟（exact simulations）。

Result: 临界理论被识别为超度量有向渗流（ultrametric directed percolation），其上临界维数（upper critical dimension）为N_c=2σ。树上精确模拟给出：当σ≤N/2时τ_S=3/2，当σ>N/2时τ_S连续下降。

Conclusion: 层级连接使规模指数3/2的雪崩无需N≥4的维度条件，临界行为由超度量有向渗流刻画，且指数3/2在σ≤N/2范围内稳健成立、超出后连续减小。

Abstract: Neural avalanches with size exponent $3/2$ are usually explained by mean-field critical branching, which for short-range coupling requires dimension $N\ge4$. We show that hierarchical connectivity removes this constraint. In a $p$-adic version of the Buice--Cowan field theory, a coupling that decays by $p^{-(N+σ)}$ per hierarchical level selects the Taibleson--Vladimirov operator, and the critical theory is ultrametric directed percolation with upper critical dimension $N_c=2σ$. Exact simulations on a tree give $τ_S=3/2$ for $σ\le N/2$, decreasing continuously for $σ>N/2$.

</details>


### [252] [Atom as an Agent: Emergent Order by Collective Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36673)
*Koji Hashimoto,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出“原子即智能体（Atom-as-an-Agent, AaaA）”原理，将源于语言/符号涌现的集体预测编码（collective predictive coding, CPC）理论移植到物理系统，论证宏观序参量可由原子间相互推断的“通信”中自动涌现，且无需参照微观哈密顿量/拉格朗日量的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：宏观序参量（macroscopic order parameter）从微观系统中涌现的内在机制是什么。作者试图借鉴语言与符号从人类交流中涌现的机制，为这一长期问题提供统一的、数据驱动的解释框架。

Method: 将每个原子视为通过推断（inference）预测彼此行为的智能体，把 CPC 的通信信息学（communication informatics）应用于三类经典自旋系统：二维铁磁与反铁磁 Ising 模型、具竞争条纹序的二维自旋模型，以及全连接 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick 自旋玻璃模型（spin-glass）。方法仅依赖观测到的自旋构型（spin configurations）。

Result: 在所有示例中，包括复本重叠（replica overlap）在内的恰当宏观序参量均被自动学习出来，作者将其称为“集体预测序（collective predictive order）”。此外，在铁磁与反铁磁 Ising 模型中，该集体预测序的平衡分布给出的涌现势（emergent potential）具备 Landau 势（Landau potential）所特有的对称性与热演化特征。

Conclusion: 集体预测序的涌现不依赖微观哈密顿量/拉格朗日量的任何结构，表明 AaaA 原理提供了一种完全数据驱动的宏观序涌现方法，即“机器学习物理（machine learning physics）”。

Abstract: What is the intrinsic reason of emergence of macroscopic order parameter out of microscopic systems? In this article, we apply to physics the collective predictive coding (CPC), the theory of language/symbol emergence. As our language emerges out of our human communications, physical macroscopic order parameter emerges out of communications among atoms --- each atom is regarded as an agent who predicts each other's behavior through inference. We present three examples of classical spin systems: two-dimensional ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, a two-dimensional spin model with competing stripe orders, and the fully connected Sherrington--Kirkpatrick spin-glass model. In all of these examples, proper macroscopic order parameters including the replica overlap emerge automatically through communication informatics of CPC, only from observed spin configurations. We call the learned macroscopic coordinate the collective predictive order. Furthermore, for the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic Ising models, the equilibrium distribution of the collective predictive order yields an emergent potential with the symmetry and thermal evolution characteristic of a Landau potential. Notably, the emergence of the collective predictive order does not refer to any structure of microscopic Hamiltonians/Lagrangians. Thus our ``Atom-as-an-Agent" (AaaA) principle offers a completely data-driven method for the emergence of macroscopic order, that is, machine learning physics.

</details>


### [253] [Replica Analysis of Overfitting in Vector Generalized Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36904)
*M Diaz-Ortiz,ACC Coolen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作将基于统计物理（statistical physics）的广义线性模型（Generalized Linear Models, GLMs）过拟合理论推广到向量广义线性模型（Vector Generalized Linear Models, VGLMs），并在复制对称（replica symmetric, RS）假设下用复制方法（replica method）求解，通过多个回归模型实例验证了理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGLMs 由任意有限个协变量的线性组合驱动结果，能刻画更复杂的统计模式，但参数数量显著更多，因而比 GLMs 更易过拟合（overfitting），这限制了其实际使用；而现有基于统计物理的过拟合理论仅适用于 GLMs，尤其在后基因组医学推断与数据分析中过拟合仍是主要障碍，故需将理论推广至 VGLMs。

Method: 将 GLMs 的过拟合理论推广至 VGLMs；指出推广带来原工作所没有的数学挑战，主要源于理论中所有序参量（order parameters）维度复杂度的增加；最终借助复制方法（replica method）在新的复制对称（RS）假设（ansatz）下求解该问题，建立通用的 VGLM 过拟合理论。

Result: 新的问题在复制方法下仍可求解；通过在若干相关 VGLM 回归模型上的应用，展示了该理论的优点并验证了其正确性。

Conclusion: GLMs 的过拟合理论可成功推广到 VGLMs，为该类模型的实际应用扫除过拟合障碍，并为后基因组医学推断与数据分析等领域开辟新的应用前景。

Abstract: We show how the successful recent statistical physics based theory of overfitting in Generalized Linear Models (GLMs) can be generalized to a much larger family of regression models: the so-called Vector Generalized Linear Models (VGLMs), in which not a single linear combination of covariates drives the outcome, but where one has an arbitrary finite number of such linear combinations. The latter more general and complex models can describe more sophisticated statistical patterns in data, but are even more prone to overfitting than GLMs due to their significantly larger number of parameters; this often limits their use. We show that the generalization of the overfitting theory from GLMs to VGLMs causes many mathematical challenges that were absent from the earlier work, mostly related to the increased dimensional complexity of all order parameters in the theory, but that the new problem is once more solvable using the replica method. This opens up the field to many novel and welcome applications, especially in post-genome medical inference and data analysis, where overfitting is and remains a major barrier to progress. After developing the general VGLM overfitting theory, within the replica symmetric (RS) ansatz, we illustrate its merits and its correctness via application to several relevant VGLM regression models.

</details>


### [254] [Perfect sampling of Ising spin glasses with bounding chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37300)
*Lukas Péron,Louise Budzynski*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于“从过去耦合”（coupling from the past）与“界定链”（bounding chains）的完美采样（perfect sampling）算法，在多种图族与耦合分布下的表现，涵盖单点界定链与分块界定链的理论保证及数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃（Ising spin glass）上完美采样算法的可行性边界（如树唯一性（tree-uniqueness）区域）与不同图结构下的性能瓶颈尚缺乏系统刻画，论文旨在厘清其收敛条件与失效原因。

Method: 在 Dobrushin 条件下证明单点界定链的聚结（coalescence）需 O(n log n) 次更新；在稀疏随机图与周期格点（periodic lattices）上开展数值实验；对 Erdős–Rényi 随机图 G(n,d/n) 与标准高斯耦合引入分块界定链（block bounding chain），并借鉴已有分块 Glauber 动力学快速混合（rapid mixing）的证明；另在 Sherrington–Kirkpatrick 模型上进行实验。

Result: 数值结果提示稀疏随机图上高效采样可延伸至整个树唯一性区域、超出该阈值后出现减速；对充分大的 d，分块界定链在树唯一性区域内以 O(n log n) 次分块更新（每次 O(n) 操作）实现聚结；周期格点上单点界定链受限于其界定包络（bounding envelope）的松散性，而 SK 模型在所有测试温度下均伴随损伤扩散（damage spreading）导致的缓慢聚结。

Conclusion: 现有界定表示与耦合方式构成自旋玻璃完美采样的主要瓶颈，需发展替代性的界定表示（bounding representations）与耦合策略以提升算法性能。

Abstract: We study perfect sampling algorithms for Ising spin glasses based on coupling from the past and bounding chains, on several graph families and coupling distributions. For the single-site bounding chain, we establish coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ updates under Dobrushin conditions. On sparse random graphs, our numerical results suggest that efficient sampling extends throughout the tree-uniqueness regime, with a slowdown beyond this threshold. On Erdős-Rényi random graphs $\mathcal{G}(n,d/n)$ with standard Gaussian couplings, we introduce a block bounding chain and prove its coalescence in $O(n\log n)$ block updates in the tree-uniqueness regime for sufficiently large $d$, each block update requiring $O(n)$ operations, adapting a proof in~\cite{Efthymiou_Zampetakis_2024} for the rapid mixing of block Glauber dynamics. On periodic lattices, our experiments instead suggest that the single-site bounding chain is limited by the looseness of its bounding envelope, whereas on the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, slow coalescence is accompanied by damage spreading at every inverse temperature tested. These observations motivate alternative bounding representations and couplings as strategies to improve perfect sampling on spin glasses.

</details>


### [255] [Chaos picture of spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37426)
*Wenlong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出“混沌图像（chaos picture）”，作为自旋玻璃（spin glasses）的一个新颖、连贯且直观的理论框架，旨在调和持续约四十年的液滴模型（droplet）与复制对称破缺（replica symmetry breaking）之争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋玻璃领域长期存在液滴模型与复制对称破缺两种图景的争论，缺乏统一且直观的理论解释，本文试图提出混沌图像以调和这一长达四十年的分歧。

Method: 通过论证随机液滴（random droplets）在钉扎（pinning）与动态过滤（dynamic filtering）作用下形成层级能量景观（hierarchical energy landscape），并描述尺度依赖液滴随温度降低的渐进冻结过程，与相空间中层级自由能景观（hierarchical free energy landscape）的碎裂过程相对应。

Result: 该图像表明分叉过程伴随温度混沌（temperature chaos），产生动态纯态共存（dynamic pure state coexistence）而不违背液滴标度（droplet scaling）；界面几何并非这些机制的关键，可自然纳入随维数增加由平面、分形到空间填充（space-filling）的界面交叉，并与平均场极限（mean-field limit）一致。

Conclusion: 混沌图像为老化（aging）、再生（rejuvenation）与记忆效应（memory effect）等自旋玻璃动力学提供了深刻洞见，并对复杂的元态框架（metastate framework）给出简化的重新诠释。

Abstract: The chaos picture is a novel, coherent, and intuitive theoretical framework for spin glasses, reconciling the 40-year-old droplet vs. replica symmetry breaking debate. First, we argue the formation of a hierarchical energy landscape by pinning and dynamic filtering of random droplets. As the temperature decreases, the chaos picture describes a progressive freezing of scale-dependent droplets in real space, which mirrors a hierarchical free energy landscape fracturing process in phase space. This bifurcation process is accompanied by temperature chaos, leading to dynamic pure state coexistence without violating the droplet scaling. Crucially, the interface geometry is not critical to these mechanisms, naturally incorporating the interface crossover from planar, fractal, to space-filling as the dimensionality increases, in line with the mean-field limit. Finally, the chaos picture provides profound insights into spin-glass dynamics such as aging, rejuvenation, and memory effect, while offering a streamlined reinterpretation of the complex metastate framework.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Detailed balance and phase transition in Nash equilibria of mean-field Ising game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36518)
*Ken Hiura,Hajime Koike,Kiyoshi Kanazawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究含熵控制成本（entropic control cost）的有限状态平均场博弈（mean-field game），证明大种群极限下平稳纳什均衡（stationary Nash equilibria）满足细致平衡（detailed balance）且分布呈吉布斯形式（Gibbsian form），并将其应用于铁磁平均场伊辛博弈（ferromagnetic mean-field Ising game）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画熵正则化控制成本下平均场博弈的平稳均衡结构，并借助统计力学类比理解其相变与非平稳动力学行为。

Method: 在大种群极限下分析有限状态平均场博弈的平稳纳什均衡；将熵控制成本引入铁磁平均场伊辛博弈，用中心流形约化（center-manifold reduction）处理临界温度附近动力学。

Result: 平稳纳什均衡满足细致平衡，其分布为吉布斯形式且以值函数（value function）充当哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）；伊辛博弈中随温度降低出现由无序到有序的二阶相变（second-order phase transition）；临界温度附近得到非平稳纳什均衡，并揭示向平稳均衡松弛过程中的临界慢化（critical slowing down）。

Conclusion: 熵控制成本使平均场博弈均衡具备吉布斯–细致平衡结构，并在伊辛型博弈中呈现二阶相变与临界慢化等统计力学特征。

Abstract: We study a finite-state mean-field game with entropic control cost. In the large population limit, we show that its stationary Nash equilibria satisfy the detailed balance condition and their distributions take the Gibbsian form with the value function playing the role of a Hamiltonian. We apply the entropic control cost to a ferromagnetic mean-field Ising game and investigate its stationary and non-stationary Nash equilibria. We find that the stationary Nash equilibria exhibit a second-order phase transition from disordered to ordered equilibria as temperature decreases. A center-manifold reduction yields non-stationary Nash equilibrium near the critical temperature and reveals the critical slowing down in the relaxation toward the stationary Nash equilibrium.

</details>
