### [257] [A Correspondence Between Qubit- and Boson-Based Sensing Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36026)
*Zachary Bogorad,Roni Harnik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文借助 Holstein–Primakoff 变换（Holstein–Primakoff transformation, HPT）统一并拓展了基于量子比特（qubit）与玻色子（boson）的新物理探测方案，提出一类性能更优的广义 GHZ 态（GHZ states），并给出哈密顿量不在 Lindblad 张成中定理（Hamiltonian-Not-in-Lindblad-Span theorem）在玻色模式集合上的自然推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，将非经典态（non-classical states）用于搜寻超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）新物理的研究兴趣激增，量子比特与玻色子两类体系并行发展；二者通过 HPT 密切相关，但尚缺乏统一框架与系统性新见解。

Method: 以 HPT 为核心工具：先构建统一框架，将若干已有的量子比特型与玻色子型探测提案纳入同一表述；再由该变换自然导出 GHZ 态的一种新推广，并分析其制备可行性；最后将该变换用于定理层面的推广。

Result: 所提出的广义 GHZ 态在稀有相互作用（rare interactions）探测中表现更优，兼具 GHZ 态的相干优势（coherent advantage）与更强的错误抑制（error rejection）；同时得到 HPT 对玻色模式集合的哈密顿量-不在-Lindblad-张成定理扩展。

Conclusion: HPT 不仅是连接量子比特与玻色子体系的数学桥梁，还为量子新物理探测提供了统一框架、新型量子态资源与定理层面的推广，表明该变换对本质上量子力学的（inherently quantum-mechanical）新物理探测具有建设性价值。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a surge of interest in adapting non-classical states to search for physics beyond the Standard Model, including both qubit- and boson-based systems. These two classes of systems are closely related through the Holstein--Primakoff transformation (HPT). In this work, we show that the HPT offers novel insights into inherently quantum-mechanical detection of new physics. We begin by discussing how the HPT can be used to unify certain existing qubit- and boson-based proposals in a single framework. We then show how it naturally reveals a novel generalization of GHZ states which offer superior performance for the detection of rare interactions. We demonstrate that these novel states can be readily prepared, and combine the coherent advantage of GHZ states with superior error rejection. Finally, we show that the HPT also reveals a natural extension of the Hamiltonian-Not-in-Lindblad-Span theorem for collections of bosonic modes.

</details>


### [258] [Regression on high-dimensional phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36073)
*Andrew J. Larkoski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文引入大重数展开（large multiplicity expansion）以解析分析粒子物理中的回归任务，并以能量流多项式（EFPs）为具体对象，证明任意两个EFP在均匀相空间上的协方差随重数平方反比被强烈压低，进而用于解析求解e+e-→强子事件的回归问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理中的机器学习任务通常以回归或向最优解的弛豫形式表达，缺乏解析理解；论文旨在通过大重数展开为这类回归任务提供显著的分析性认识。

Method: 采用大重数展开分析回归任务；具体研究基于能量流多项式（EFPs）的回归——EFPs是洛伦兹不变相空间上红外与共线安全可观测量的完备线性基；并证明协方差的标度行为，给出EFP展开系数的重数依赖。

Result: 任一EFP的期望值在大重数极限下趋于常数，而任意两个EFP在均匀相空间上的协方差被高度压低，按重数平方的倒数标度；该反直觉结果依赖置换不变性、动量守恒与旋转不变性之间的精细抵消（cancellation）。协方差标度进一步意味着EFP展开系数必须按重数二次方标度。

Conclusion: 以e+e-→强子事件中的EFP回归为例，展示了可通过大重数极限与QCD常见固定阶微扰论的互补展开解析地求解回归。

Abstract: Machine learning tasks in particle physics are typically expressed in terms of regression, or relaxation toward some optimal solution as defined by an objective or loss function. In this paper, we introduce a large multiplicity expansion for analyzing regression tasks, which enables significant analytic understanding. For concreteness, we study regression with energy flow polynomials (EFPs), a complete linear basis of infrared and collinear safe observables on Lorentz invariant phase space. While the expectation value of any EFP scales like a constant in the large multiplicity limit, we prove that the covariance on uniform phase space of any two EFPs is highly suppressed and scales like the inverse of the squared multiplicity. This very unintuitive result relies on detailed cancellation guaranteed by the interplay of permutation invariance, momentum conservation, and rotational invariance. This scaling of the covariance with multiplicity further implies that the coefficients in an expansion of EFPs must scale quadratically in multiplicity. We present an example of regression with EFPs as applied to $e^+e^-\to$ hadrons events, and show how the regression can be solved analytically through the complementary expansions of the large multiplicity limit and of familiar fixed-order perturbation theory of QCD.

</details>


### [259] [Projected sensitivity to the ADD large extra dimensions model in the monojet plus missing transverse momentum final state at HL-LHC, HE-LHC, and FCC-hh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36107)
*Emine Gurpinar Guler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出 HL-LHC、HE-LHC 与 FCC-hh 上单喷注加缺失横动量末态对大额外维度（large extra dimensions, ADD 模型）灵敏度的统一投影，首次用数据驱动方式测量本底能量标度行为并以 ATLAS Run-2 结果做校准锚定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未来对撞机（future collider）投影多将本底随质心能量（center-of-mass energy）的标度视作常数，且各能级研究缺乏统一的选取、显著性与校准方案，导致跨能级比较与可达范围（reach）估计的可信度不足。

Method: 信号用 Pythia 8 生成，与 ATLAS/CMS 在 HEPData 发布的拟合后标准模型本底（post-fit SM background）联合；期望显著性综合 ATLAS 信号区十三个排他 E_T^miss 区间。本底随 √s 的能量标度由专门的 Z(νν̄)+jets 与 W(ℓν)+jets 样本按 E_T^miss 函数测量，并通过与 n 相关的透明校准锚定至 ATLAS Run-2 期望 95% CL 极限，选取响应对照公开 cutflow 验证。

Result: 相对常数因子处理，数据驱动的本底标度使 FCC-hh 投影可达范围降低 39%；校准因子与 1 一致（1.001±1.4%）。n=2 时 95% CL 排他极限 M_D 分别为 12.5、15.2、22.0 TeV，5σ 发现极限为 9.8、12.0、17.0 TeV，较 ATLAS Run-2 的 M_D>11.2 TeV 提升 1.1–2.0 倍。

Conclusion: 本底能量标度的实测处理显著影响高能对撞机投影，常数假设会高估灵敏度；所建立的数据锚定校准框架可作为未来对撞机同类研究的模板，结论限于所述 Run-2 锚定的探测器与不确定度假设。

Abstract: We present a unified projection of the sensitivity to the Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali (ADD) model of large extra dimensions in the monojet plus missing transverse momentum final state at the HL-LHC ($\sqrt{s}=14$ TeV, 3 ab$^{-1}$), the HE-LHC (27 TeV, 15 ab$^{-1}$) and the FCC-hh (100 TeV, 30 ab$^{-1}$), for $n=2,3,4,6$ extra dimensions, with one selection, significance estimator and calibration throughout. Signal samples are generated with Pythia 8 and combined with the post-fit Standard-Model background yields released by ATLAS and CMS on HEPData; the expected significance combines the thirteen exclusive $E_{\mathrm{T}}^{\mathrm{miss}}$ bins of the ATLAS signal region, matching the binning of the published likelihood fit. Two elements are new. First, the energy scaling of the background to higher $\sqrt{s}$, including the $13\to14$ TeV step, is measured as a function of $E_{\mathrm{T}}^{\mathrm{miss}}$ from dedicated $Z(ν\barν)$+jets and $W(\ellν)$+jets samples rather than assumed constant; this reduces the projected FCC-hh reach by 39% relative to a constant-factor treatment. Second, the framework is anchored to the published ATLAS Run-2 expected 95% CL limits through a transparent $n$-dependent calibration, determined directly for all five values of $n$ and found consistent with unity ($1.001\pm1.4\%$), so that it acts as a consistency check rather than a sizeable correction; the selection response is validated against the published ATLAS cutflow. The projected 95% CL exclusion reach in $M_D$ for $n=2$ is 12.5, 15.2 and 22.0 TeV at the three colliders, with $5σ$ discovery reach of 9.8, 12.0 and 17.0 TeV, extending the ATLAS Run-2 bound of $M_D>11.2$ TeV by factors of 1.1-2.0. The calibration scheme provides a template for analogous future-collider studies; the projections hold within the stated Run-2-anchored detector and uncertainty assumptions.

</details>


### [260] [Probing Charged Higgs Bosons through $H^\pm \to W^\pm A$ Decay in the Type-I Two-Higgs Doublet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36780)
*Abdesslam Arhrib,Mohamed Krab,Tanmoy Mondal,Prasenjit Sanyal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在Ⅰ型双希格斯二重态模型（type-I Two-Higgs Doublet Model, 2HDM）中，通过带电希格斯玻色子的玻色子衰变道 \(H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A\) 在 \(4b+2j+\ell\nu\) 末态搜寻轻赝标量 \(A\)，可在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）上探测大范围参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻赝标量（pseudoscalar）\(A\)（质量小于 125 GeV 希格斯玻色子的一半）在Ⅰ型 2HDM 中仍未被现有对撞机约束排除；而一旦 \(H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A\) 运动学开放，该玻色子衰变道会自然主导，削弱基于费米子衰变的现有带电希格斯限制，因而需要新的搜寻策略。

Method: 利用标准模型样希格斯玻色子的未探测衰变 \(h_{125} \to AA\) 成对产生轻赝标量，并针对带电希格斯经 \(H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A\) 衰变的 \(4b+2j+\ell\nu\) 末态开展对撞机分析（collider analysis），与基于费米子衰变道 \(H^\pm \to \tau^\pm\nu\) 和 \(H^\pm \to tb\) 的搜寻形成互补。

Result: 该道对 moderate-to-large \(\tan\beta\) 取值尤为敏感，可作为 LHC 上费米子衰变搜寻的优良互补探针；分析表明其在高亮度 LHC 上可探测Ⅰ型 2HDM 参数空间的相当大区域。

Conclusion: 通过 \(H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A\) 的 \(4b+2j+\ell\nu\) 末态搜寻是探测Ⅰ型 2HDM 中轻赝标量与带电希格斯的有力且互补的途径，适用于高亮度 LHC 阶段。

Abstract: A light pseudoscalar lighter than the 125 GeV Higgs boson remains allowed within the type-I Two-Higgs Doublet Model (2HDM), consistent with current collider constraints, including Higgs signal strength measurements and direct searches for additional Higgs bosons. At the Large Hadron Collider (LHC), the light pseudoscalar $A$ can be pair-produced through the undetected decay of the Standard Model-like Higgs boson, $h_{125} \to AA$, when this channel is kinematically accessible (i.e., $m_A < m_{h_{125}}/2$). For the charged scalar $H^\pm$, once open, the bosonic decay channel $H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A$ naturally dominates over the fermionic $H^\pm \to τ^\pmν$ and $H^\pm \to tb$ channels, while relaxing the existing $H^\pm$ limits. As a result, we propose searching for the charged Higgs boson through $H^\pm \to W^{\pm(*)} A$ in the $4b+2j+\ellν$ final state. This channel is particularly sensitive to moderate-to-large $\tanβ$ values and provides an excellent complementary probe to searches based on fermionic $H^\pm$ decays at the LHC. Our collider analysis demonstrates that this channel can probe a substantial region of the type-I 2HDM parameter space at the high-luminosity LHC.

</details>


### [261] [Evaluation of a Double Integral with Exponentials and Two Generalized Laguerre Polynomials and Its Associated Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35995)
*Snigdha Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文以有限和（finite sums）形式解析计算了一类出现在恒定外磁场下带电粒子量子场论计算中的定反常积分，该积分含广义拉盖尔多项式（generalized Laguerre polynomials）与横向动量因子乘积，并给出直至四阶矩（r=4）的显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类积分出现在涉及电荷 ±e 的基本带电粒子处于恒定外磁场 B 的量子场论（quantum field theory）计算中，需要解析结果以支撑相关场论计算。

Method: 对含广义拉盖尔多项式 L_l^a、L_n^b 及横向动量（transverse momentum）因子 k_⊥^{μ_1}…k_⊥^{μ_r} 的二维横向积分，采用解析方法以有限和（finite sums）形式进行求值，矩指标 μ_i∈{1,2}。

Result: 得到该定反常积分（definite improper integral）的有限和解析表达式，并给出直至第四阶矩（r=4）的显式表达式，据称已足以满足多数场论计算的需求。

Conclusion: 所给有限和形式及 r≤4 的显式结果可为含恒定磁场的量子场论计算提供可直接使用的解析工具。

Abstract: We analytically evaluate in terms of finite sums, the following definite improper integral, which appears in the quantum field theoretical calculation involving elementary charged particles having charge $\pm e$ in presence of a constant external magnetic field $\mathbf{B}=B\hat{\mathbf{z}}$ $$ I_{abln}^{μ_1\dotsμ_r}(\mathbf{q}_\perp,|eB|) = \int\!\!\dfrac{d^2k_\perp}{(2π)^2}k_\perp^{μ_1} \dots k_\perp^{μ_r} \exp \bigg(-\frac{\mathbf{k}_\perp^2+\mathbf{p}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg) L^a_l \bigg(\frac{2\mathbf{k}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg) L^b_n \bigg(\frac{2\mathbf{p}_\perp^2}{|eB|}\bigg)\bigg|_{\mathbf{p}_\perp=(\mathbf{q}_\perp+\mathbf{k}_\perp)} $$ where $μ_i \in \{1,2\}$, $l\ge 0$, $n\ge 0$ and $L_l^a(x)$ are the generalized Laguerre polynomials. We also provide explicit expressions of the result up to the fourth moment i.e. $r=4$, which are sufficient for most of the field theoretical calculations.

</details>


### [262] [What the Primary Lund Plane Omits: A Model-Stitching Diagnosis of a Jet-Tagger Representation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37137)
*Saurabh Rai,Sanmay Ganguly*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文用模型缝合（model stitching）方法分离表征（representation）质量与下游计算（downstream computation）能力，证明 Lund-plane 与 Particle Transformer（ParT）喷注标记器（jet tagger）的性能差距源于表征缺陷而非计算不足；该差距仅在低信号效率下的背景排除（background rejection）中显现，且重建完整 Cambridge/Aachen 去簇树（declustering tree）可修复 72% 的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同喷注表征方式导致的性能差异可能来自表征本身较弱，也可能来自下游计算较弱，而仅凭独立性能（standalone performance）无法区分二者，因此需要一个能解耦这两个因素的方法。

Method: 采用模型缝合（model stitching）：将一个网络冻结的早期层与另一网络冻结的后期层通过单个可训练线性映射（linear map）连接。以已知不完整的 Lund 表征（主去簇分支）作为阳性对照，另用分裂三重态（splitting triplets）上的超图读取器（hypergraph reader）检验高阶关联的影响；并在完整 Cambridge/Aachen 去簇树上重建表征，结合推理时的节点掩蔽（masking）实验进行分析。

Result: 缝合后的主分支在下游可达到其自身下游可恢复的水平，但远低于 ParT 的背景排除能力，说明缺陷属表征性而非计算性；超图读取器未带来改善，排除高阶关联因素。该差距在 AUC 上不可见（保持在目标值 1% 以内），仅出现在低信号效率的背景排除中，并定位于 0.6% 的背景喷注——这些喷注位于顶夸克质量（top mass）及以上，组分更多但主分裂更少。完整去簇树重建使独立排除率上升，弥合 72% 的差距。掩蔽实验表明修复后网络仍几乎完全依赖主分支，次级分裂的作用是使表征可学习（learnable）而非提供可读取的信息。主分支上最大张角的分裂（接近 top 与 W 的开角）价值约为随机分裂的近两倍。在夸克/胶子标记（quark/gluon tagging）中主分支缺陷小得多，保留 ParT 排除率的 77.6%（top 为 46.9%），完整树仅弥合 33% 的差距（top 为 72%）。

Conclusion: 表征质量与下游计算能力可通过模型缝合有效分离；Lund-plane 标记器的不足主要来自表征信息缺失，且这一差距高度局域化、易被 AUC 等整体指标掩盖。补充次级分裂可提升表征可学习性，该效应在 top 标记上远强于夸克/胶子标记。

Abstract: Lund-plane and Particle Transformer (ParT) jet taggers can differ in performance because one representation is weaker, or because a weaker downstream computation reads an adequate one. Standalone performance cannot separate these. Model stitching can: it links the frozen early layers of one network to the frozen late layers of another through a single trained linear map. As a positive control we use an incomplete Lund representation, the primary declustering branch, whose deficit is known independently. Stitched into ParT's downstream, it reaches a background rejection at least as much as its own downstream recovers, yet far below ParT's background rejection: the deficit is representational, not computational. A hypergraph reader over splitting triplets does no better, ruling out higher-order correlations as the cause. The gap is invisible in AUC, which stays within 1\% of its target value, and appears only in background rejection at low signal efficiency. It localizes to 0.6\% of background jets, at and above the top mass, with more constituents but fewer primary splittings than typical background. Rebuilding the representation on the full Cambridge/Aachen declustering tree repairs the deficit as predicted: standalone rejection rises, closing 72\% of the gap. Masking nodes at inference shows the repaired network still relies almost entirely on the primary branch; the secondary splittings make the representation learnable rather than supplying information it reads. Along the primary branch, the widest-angle splitting, near the top and $W$ opening angles, is worth nearly twice a random one. For quark/gluon tagging the primary branch is far less deficient, retaining 77.6\% of ParT's rejection (46.9\% for top), and the full tree closes only 33\% of the gap (72\% for top).

</details>


### [263] [Clock--WEP Tests of String-Derived Dilaton and Modulus Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35947)
*Ahmed Farag Ali*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将弦紧致化与跑动膨胀子模型中的轻标量方向与低能可观测量联系，构造通用的Damour–Donoghue弱等效原理（WEP）与时钟漂移联合拟合，用MICROSCOPE等数据对弦来源标量斜率矢量施加约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦紧致化（string compactification）与跑动膨胀子（runaway dilaton）模型可含轻标量方向，其运动改变规范耦合、QCD能标、夸克质量与Yukawa参数，因此低能精密实验可用于检验此类高能模型。

Method: 在假设单一长程标量或对齐多场方向的条件下，用规范动能函数、物质度规/Yukawa斜率、源电荷、标量轨迹与标量范围确定WEP矢量与时钟漂移矢量；采用最小双荷（two-charge）Damour–Donoghue拟合，输入Ti–Pt MICROSCOPE、Be–Ti扭秤与⁸⁵Rb–⁸⁷Rb原子干涉仪数据，并加入Yb⁺ E3/E2长期α漂移行。

Result: 仅WEP拟合给出D_m̂=(-6.06±34.91)×10⁻¹²、D_e=(8.80±46.46)×10⁻¹²，相关系数-0.99958；时钟输入为α̇/α=1.0(1.1)×10⁻¹⁸ yr⁻¹，在|ζ|=2.37×10⁻⁸ yr⁻¹时与WEP边缘灵敏度相当（ζ=φ̇/α_E），对基准φ̇=ξH₀、α_E=10⁻³α₀得ξ/α₀≃0.34。Damour–Piazza–Veneziano跑动膨胀子估计在n=2、参数为量级1时η~10⁻¹²，远高于MICROSCOPE量级WEP标度；代表性同位素双荷模型中，MICROSCOPE量级的Be–Ti类行可将长WEP轴缩小27.8倍，重复的Ti–Pt类行几乎无影响。

Conclusion: 模型在提供r=D_m̂/D_e、标量振幅与ζ后才可检验；该方法可在模型给出低能数据后检验弦导出膨胀子或模量（modulus）部门的紧致化层级预言。

Abstract: String compactifications and runaway-dilaton models can contain light scalar directions. Motion along these directions changes gauge couplings, the QCD scale, quark masses, and Yukawa parameters. A given string-derived sector then predicts low-energy slopes. Its gauge kinetic functions, matter-metric or Yukawa slopes, source charge, scalar trajectory, and scalar range determine a Damour--Donoghue weak-equivalence-principle (WEP) vector and a clock-drift vector. The fit assumes one long-range scalar or an aligned multi-field direction. With that assumption, WEP tests and secular clock drifts probe related projections of the same scalar-slope vector. The minimal two-charge Damour--Donoghue fit uses Ti--Pt MICROSCOPE, Be--Ti torsion-balance, and $^{85}$Rb--$^{87}$Rb atom-interferometer data, then adds the Yb$^+$ E3/E2 secular $α$-drift row. The WEP-only fit gives $D_{\hat m}=(-6.06\pm34.91)\times10^{-12}$ and $D_e=(8.80\pm46.46)\times10^{-12}$, with correlation $-0.99958$. The clock input is $\dotα/α=1.0(1.1)\times10^{-18}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$. It matches the WEP marginal reach when $|ζ|=2.37\times10^{-8}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, with $ζ=\dot\varphi/α_E$. For the benchmark $\dot\varphi=ξH_0$ and $α_E=10^{-3}α_0$, this gives $ξ/α_0\simeq0.34$. A model becomes testable after it supplies $r=D_{\hat m}/D_e$, the scalar amplitude, and $ζ$. The Damour--Piazza--Veneziano runaway-dilaton estimate lies near $η\sim10^{-12}$ for order-unity $n=2$ parameters, well above the MICROSCOPE-level WEP scale. In the representative-isotope two-charge model, a MICROSCOPE-level Be--Ti-like row would reduce the long WEP axis by a factor $27.8$; a repeated Ti--Pt-like row would barely change it. The calculation tests compactification-level predictions for string-derived dilaton or modulus sectors after the model supplies its low-energy data.

</details>


### [264] [Formation and Charge Relaxation of Julia-Zee Dyons in Classical Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36023)
*Masaki Yamada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在（3+1）维经典格点模拟中，展示膨胀背景下 SU(2)→U(1) 对称破缺模型中 Julia–Zee 双荷子（Julia–Zee dyon）的形成与演化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对称破缺相变过程中形成的带电磁单极子（即双荷子）在膨胀背景下的产生与后续演化行为。

Method: 采用（3+1）维经典格点模拟（classical lattice simulation），将规范场与希格斯场（Higgs field）制备为热态并施加紫外滤波（ultraviolet filter），随后经质量淬火（mass quench）与膨胀过程演化。

Result: 经典双荷子可携带连续电荷，观测到电荷量超过电荷单位 g 的四倍；其电荷幅度在演化中趋于减小，与周围场发生电荷交换的图景一致。晚期磁单极子（magnetic monopole）持续存在，而双荷子数目及电荷范围下降，模拟结束时仅剩少数双荷子。

Conclusion: 膨胀背景下的经典格点模拟表明双荷子可携带大电荷并随演化逐渐失去电荷，磁单极子则相对稳定存续，二者的不同演化行为具有区分意义。

Abstract: We demonstrate the formation and subsequent evolution of Julia--Zee dyons in $(3+1)$-dimensional classical lattice simulations of an $\mathrm{SU}(2)\to\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry-breaking model in an expanding background. The gauge and Higgs fields are prepared thermally, subjected to an ultraviolet filter, and evolved through a mass quench and subsequent expansion. Classical dyons can carry continuous electric charge, and we find charge magnitudes exceeding four times the electric charge unit $g$. Their measured electric-charge magnitudes tend to decrease during the subsequent evolution, consistent with charge exchange with the surrounding fields. At late times, the magnetic monopoles persist while the number of dyons and their observed charge range decrease, with only a few identified dyons remaining at the end of the simulations.

</details>


### [265] [Can inflationary axion fluctuations dominate over misalignment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36033)
*Seung J. Lee,Sarunas Verner,Bingrong Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文探讨轻稳定轴子暗物质丰度能否由暴胀期间的场涨落主导，而非传统的错位机制（misalignment）。分析表明，在正则最小耦合情形下涨落主导丰度会因标度不变谱违反普朗克等曲率极限约八个数量级；但通过引入依赖暴胀子的动能函数（kinetic function）或与Ricci标量的非最小耦合可使谱变蓝，从而满足限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻稳定轴子暗物质的遗迹丰度通常由错位机制（misalignment）设定，本文旨在检验暴胀涨落（inflationary fluctuations）能否取而代之主导丰度。

Method: 采用解析方法追踪模式从de Sitter膨胀到辐射主导（radiation domination）的瞬时转捩，暴胀期间忽略轴子质量、之后保留；并考虑依赖暴胀子的动能函数（kinetic function）及与Ricci标量的非最小耦合对谱的修正。

Result: 由于能量密度对场呈二次依赖，即使平均位移为零，涨落主导丰度也带有等曲率扰动（isocurvature perturbation）。正则最小耦合下谱为标度不变，涨落主导暗物质超出普朗克等曲率极限约八个数量级；蓝倾谱可缓解此问题，恒定动能斜率下需n_η−1≳0.27且m_η≳200 GeV（H_I=10^10 GeV），或n_η−1≳0.34且m_η≳4.5 keV（H_I=10^13 GeV）；跑动斜率可将质量降至1.4 GeV与0.7 eV。

Conclusion: 对QCD轴子而言，其振荡起始较晚，任何足以满足枢轴尺度（pivot scale）极限的单一幂律蓝谱都会给出过小的涨落丰度，因此错位机制仍占主导。

Abstract: The relic abundance of light stable axion dark matter is usually set by misalignment. We study whether the inflationary fluctuations of the axion can dominate instead, and we follow the modes analytically through an instantaneous transition from de Sitter expansion to radiation domination, neglecting the axion mass during inflation and keeping it afterward. Since the energy density is quadratic in the field, a fluctuation-dominated abundance carries an isocurvature perturbation even when the mean displacement vanishes. For a canonical minimally coupled axion the spectrum is scale invariant, and fluctuation-dominated dark matter exceeds the Planck isocurvature limit by about eight orders of magnitude. An inflaton-dependent kinetic function or a nonminimal coupling to the Ricci scalar tilts the spectrum blue, moving the fluctuation power from CMB scales to small scales. Imposing the Planck limit at the pivot scale, we find that fluctuation-dominated dark matter with a constant kinetic slope requires the spectral tilt $n_η-1\gtrsim0.27$ and the axion mass $m_η \gtrsim200 \rm{GeV}$ at $H_I=10^{10}\rm{GeV}$, and $n_η-1\gtrsim0.34$ and $m_η\gtrsim4.5\,$keV at $H_I=10^{13}\rm{GeV}$. A kinetic function with a running slope lowers these masses to $1.4~\rm{GeV}$ and $0.7\,$eV, respectively. For the QCD axion, whose oscillations start late, we estimate that any single power-law spectrum blue enough to satisfy the pivot limit gives too little fluctuation abundance, and misalignment dominates.

</details>


### [266] [How particle physics stops ultra-relativistic bubbles and shapes their gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36160)
*Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，一阶宇宙相变中极端相对论性气泡产生的引力波谱，可由气泡尺寸的能量密度函数近似描述；主导能量损失来自粒子散射（常处于LPM区域），因而谱形依赖粒子物理耦合、相互作用类型及粒子数是否守恒，并延伸讨论破缺的整体对称性导致的弹性与非弹性碰撞混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上气泡碰撞及碰撞后的经典演化被视为引力波（gravitational wave, GW）产生的重要环节，但摘要指出这些过程对谱的影响可忽略，因此需要一个以能量密度随气泡尺寸变化为核心的替代性描述框架。

Method: 将GW谱用气泡尺寸函数的能量密度来近似，忽略气泡碰撞与碰撞后经典演化的贡献，并以粒子散射作为主导能量损失机制，其中常涉及Landau–Pomeranchuk–Migdal（LPM）区域；在此基础上分析谱对粒子物理耦合、相互作用类型（规范、Yukawa、标量四次等）及粒子数守恒与否的依赖，并讨论破缺整体对称性下的弹性（elastic）与非弹性（inelastic）碰撞混合。

Result: GW谱依赖于粒子物理耦合强度，可在“包络（envelope）”与“体流（bulk flow）”两种极限之间插值；同时依赖相互作用类型以及粒子数是否守恒。对破缺整体对称性（broken global symmetries），碰撞表现为弹性与非弹性的混合。

Conclusion: 极端相对论性气泡的GW谱可由气泡尺度的能量密度而非碰撞动力学来确定，粒子散射（含LPM效应）是关键物理输入；该框架将谱形与粒子物理微观参数直接联系起来，并涵盖整体对称性破缺情形。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave spectrum produced by ultra-relativistic bubbles in a first-order cosmological phase transition can be approximated in terms of their energy density as a function of bubble size. Bubble collisions and post-collision classical evolution negligibly change it. The dominant energy loss is particle scattering, often in the Landau--Pomeranchuk--Migdal regime. So the GW spectrum depends on the particle-physics coupling (interpolating between the `envelope' and `bulk flow' limits), on the interaction type (gauge, Yukawa, scalar quartic, ...), and on whether particle number is conserved. Finally, we consider broken global symmetries, which give a mixture of `elastic' and `inelastic' collisions.

</details>


### [267] [LLMs Can Tackle High Energy Theoretical Physics, yet Gaps Remain: Part I, Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33134)
*Tong Liu,Bohan Li,Gongrui Cheng,Zixiong Yu,Yulan He,Volker Tresp*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出QFT-Bench基准（75道研究生至研究级量子场论问题），评测39个闭源与开源模型，最强模型准确率超过95%，但仍存在计算、约定与指令遵循错误，且思维链（CoT）存在明显的推理不忠实性（reasoning unfaithfulness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）已开始处理传统上需理论物理学家长期训练的问题，但其能力边界与失效位置尚不明确，高能理论物理领域缺乏系统性定量评测。

Method: 构建QFT-Bench基准，涵盖量子场论（QFT）核心子领域共75道需大量推理与推导的研究生至研究级题目，每题均有明确目标与闭式参考答案；评分结合符号验证（symbolic verification）与LLM评判器（LLM-based judge），并对模型思维链（CoT）进行监测分析。

Result: 前沿模型在非平凡QFT计算中表现可观，最强模型准确率超过95%；但强模型仍在计算、约定和指令遵循上出错，弱模型多在物理概念或推导层面失败；当前模型表现出非平凡的推理不忠实性，即答案正确但推理不足以逻辑支撑结论。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了当前LLM在QFT能力的定量评估，是迈向高能理论物理更广泛评测的第一步。

Abstract: Beyond math and code, Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to tackle problems that have traditionally required substantial training for theoretical physicists, but it remains unclear how far this capability extends and where it might break. As a first step toward pushing LLMs in high-energy theoretical physics, we introduce QFT-Bench, a benchmark of 75 graduate- to research-level problems requiring extensive reasoning and derivation across core Quantum Field Theory (QFT) subareas. Each question has a well-defined target and closed-form reference answer. Evaluation combines symbolic verification with LLM-based judge. Across 39 proprietary and open-source models, frontier models already demonstrate substantial capabilities on nontrivial QFT calculations, with the strongest models exceeding 95% accuracy. At the same time, clear gaps remain. Even strong models still make errors in calculations, conventions, and instruction following, while weaker models often fail at the level of physical concepts or derivations. We further monitor models' CoTs and show that current models exhibit nontrivial reasoning unfaithfulness, i.e., they could reach correct answers but the reasoning itself does not justify and logically support them. These results provide a quantitative assessment of current LLM capabilities in QFT and a first step toward broader evaluation in high-energy theoretical physics.

</details>


### [268] [Quark mixing and CP violation due to torsional interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36611)
*Arnab Chakraborty,Amitabha Lahiri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 费米子在时空中运动产生挠率（torsion），由此导致一种与引力无关的流-流相互作用（current-current interaction）；该相互作用一般为手征性（chiral）且非普适（non-universal），在夸克部分会引入类似CKM的新矩阵并修正CP破坏，从而对K介子衰变等过程产生影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探讨时空挠率所诱导的四费米子相互作用（four-fermion interaction）是否具有可观测效应，并关注其手征性与非普适性如何在夸克味物理中体现。

Method: 通过分析使挠率相互作用对角化的费米子基与质量基（mass basis）、味基（flavor basis）之间的差异，推导夸克部分的结构，得到新的类CKM矩阵（CKM-like matrices），并考察附加相位对CP破坏及重相位不变量（rephasing invariants）的影响。

Result: 对角化挠率相互作用会产生新的类CKM矩阵，CP破坏因附加相位而普遍被修正；即使挠率相互作用在味基中是对角的，由于挠率耦合常数（torsional coupling constants）的非普适性，味改变过程（flavor-changing processes）仍会受到影响，例如K介子衰变。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了存在挠率相互作用时的重相位不变量，并表明即使挠率基（torsion basis）与味基对齐，Jarlskog不变量（Jarlskog invariant）也会被修正，说明挠率效应可能在味物理观测中留下痕迹。

Abstract: The dynamics of fermions in spacetime generates torsion, ultimately leading to a current-current interaction independent of gravity. This torsional four-fermion interaction is generically chiral and non-universal and may have observable consequences. Furthermore, the fermion basis in which this torsional interaction is diagonal may be different from the mass basis and the flavor basis. We work out the structure of the quark sector, where new CKM-like matrices appear as a result of diagonalizing the torsional interaction. CP violation is modified in general due to the appearance of additional phases. We also consider various processes which are affected by it such as kaon decays in particular and show that even when the torsional interaction is taken to be diagonal in the flavor basis, flavor-changing processes are still affected due to the non-universality of the torsional coupling constants. Finally, we discuss rephasing invariants in the presence of the torsional interaction and show how the Jarlskog invariant is modified even when the torsion basis is aligned with the flavor basis.

</details>


### [269] [An open EFT for dark matter searches with matter-wave interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36044)
*Leonardo Badurina,Kathryn M. Zurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将物质波干涉仪（matter-wave interferometer, MWI）视为开放量子系统（open quantum system）、把暗物质（dark matter, DM）视为环境，用Schwinger-Keldysh形式构建开放有效场论（open EFT），以统一描述DM引起的相移（phase shift）与退相干（decoherence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MWI可敏感探测DM诱导的相移与对比度损失，但缺乏从微观理论出发、能同时刻画相移与退相干并适用于宏观复合粒子干涉仪的系统框架。

Method: 从微观理论推导干涉仪的有效作用量，投影到多体世界线（worldline）扇区，明确重探针极限；将影响作用量（influence action）分解为迟滞核（retarded kernel，控制因果响应）与Keldysh核（控制环境涨落与对比度损失），并给出领头阶对应：四点相互作用为DM气泡图（bubble diagram），长程相互作用为介质内穿衣传播子（dressed in-medium propagator）。

Result: 针对弹性、自旋无关的亚GeV DM与核子散射：在DM波粒边界以下因玻色增强（Bose enhancement），有雄心的复合粒子干涉仪（composite-particle interferometer, CPI）可探测质量低于1 eV的Higgs门户（Higgs-portal）DM，覆盖未探索的模型空间；对于通过轻强子性标量（hadrophilic scalar）耦合核子的DM，DM背景在很大参数空间内使媒介子介质内质量变为快子（tachyonic）。

Conclusion: 开放EFT框架清晰区分相移与退相干的实时核结构，并将MWI/CPI确立为探测轻质量DM（含亚eV Higgs门户与强子性标量耦合）的有力探针。

Abstract: Matter-wave interferometers (MWIs) are a genuine quantum probe of dark matter (DM), being sensitive to DM-induced phase shifts and loss of contrast. Treating the interferometer as an open quantum system and the dark sector as its environment, we derive an open EFT to study these signatures using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism. Starting from a microscopic theory, we derive the interferometer's effective action, which we then project onto the many-body worldline sectors relevant for atom interferometers and MWIs employing mesoscopic objects, i.e., composite-particle interferometers (CPIs). This construction clarifies the heavy-probe limit and organizes phase shifts and decoherence as distinct real-time components of the influence action: the retarded kernel controls the causal response of the environment, while the Keldysh kernel controls environmental fluctuations and hence loss of contrast. At leading order, these kernels reduce to: (i) DM bubble diagrams in the case of four-point interactions, and (ii) dressed in-medium mediator propagators in the case of long-range interactions, where the dressing is provided by the DM environment. Within this framework, we study the signatures of elastic spin-independent sub-GeV DM scattering off nucleons and find that: (i) owing to Bose enhancements below the DM wave-particle boundary, ambitious CPIs could probe Higgs-portal DM with mass below 1 eV in an unexplored region of model space; (ii) for DM coupled to nucleons via a light hadrophilic scalar, the DM background can drive the mediator's in-medium mass tachyonic over much of parameter space; outside these regions, CPIs could probe provide powerful probes of light DM.

</details>


### [270] [Dark Matter Coherent Freeze-Out Mechanism: A Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36049)
*Steven Ferrante,Lillian Luo,Maxim Perelstein,Bingrong Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出同时包含弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）与类轴子粒子（ALP）的暗物质场景，二者通过类似QCD轴子-核子相互作用的非导数二次耦合相联系。即使普朗克压低的微小耦合也能显著改变两类粒子的宇宙学历史，产生"相干冻结（coherent freeze-out）"新机制和被称为"ALP miracle"的参数窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WIMP与ALP均为有吸引力的暗物质候选者，但以往研究多单独考虑单一组分；本文旨在考察二者共存并通过非导数耦合相互作用时对宇宙学演化的影响。

Method: 构建WIMP与ALP以非导数二次耦合（modeled on the axion-nucleon interaction in QCD）相互作用的场景，分析早期宇宙中ALP场获得真空期望值及在弱标度附近或以下发生的逆相变（inverse phase transition），并追踪WIMP退耦与ALP相干振荡的演化。

Result: WIMP可在远超通常冻结（freeze-out）时间后仍保持平衡，最终通过"相干冻结"机制退耦，其湮灭截面可比标准热靶值高若干数量级仍符合观测丰度；ALP相干振荡在相变后才开始，初始条件由相变热物理确定，其丰度由WIMP质量与耦合唯一预言。

Conclusion: 当参数处于弱标度与普朗克标度的自然值时即可得到观测丰度，故称"ALP miracle"；该场景偏好ALP质量高于约10^-3 eV，远大于其他流行ALP模型，其参数空间与QCD轴子带当前允许区域重叠，其中很大部分有望被IAXO实验探测。

Abstract: Both Weakly-Interacting Massive Particle (WIMP) and Axion-Like Particle (ALP) are attractive particle dark matter candidates. In this paper we consider a scenario in which both WIMP and ALP are present. The two species interact via a non-derivative quadratic coupling modeled on the axion-nucleon interaction in QCD. We show that even a small (Planck-suppressed) coupling can drastically modify the cosmological history of both species. In the very early Universe, the ALP field acquires a vacuum expectation value, and the system undergoes an inverse phase transition at a temperature around or below the weak scale. WIMPs may remain in equilibrium far beyond the usual freeze-out time, eventually decoupling via a novel "coherent freeze-out" mechanism. WIMP annihilation cross sections several orders of magnitude above the canonical thermal target value can be consistent with the observed relic density in the coherent freeze-out scenario. At the same time, coherent oscillations of the ALP field do not begin until after the phase transition, and the initial conditions for these oscillations are set by the thermal physics of the phase transition. The ALP relic density is then predicted unambiguously in terms of the WIMP mass and coupling. We term this scenario "ALP miracle", since the observed relic density is obtained when these parameters are around their natural values, the weak and the Planck scale. The ALP miracle scenario prefers the ALP mass above about $10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}$, far larger than other popular ALP dark matter models. The ALP miracle parameter space overlaps with a currently allowed region of the QCD axion band, a large fraction of which is expected to be probed by the proposed IAXO experiment.

</details>


### [271] [The Harmonic Structure of Multiparticle Scattering: Amplitude Projection and Non-Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36052)
*Nathaniel Craig,Brian Henning,Michael Stadlbauer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文为四维无质量散射振幅（massless scattering amplitudes）建立基于多粒子相空间谐函数（harmonics on multi-particle phase space）的投影形式（projection formalism），并将其用于在壳割线计算与重整化群方程，导出新的非重整化定理（non-renormalization theorems）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分波展开（partial-wave decomposition）主要适用于两粒子情形，缺乏对任意粒子数相空间的有效投影工具；同时有效场论（effective field theories）中割线计算的化简与抵消条件、以及一般圈数下的非重整化性质有待系统刻画。

Method: 通过在多粒子相空间上构造谐函数，发展适用于任意粒子数的投影形式，作为分波展开的推广；将其应用于在壳割线（on-shell cuts）计算，把割线计算改写为投影系数（projected coefficients）乘积之和；并结合在壳重整化群方程（on-shell renormalization group equation）进行推导。

Result: 证明任意割线计算均可写为投影系数乘积的求和，并给出该求和为零的条件；结合在壳重整化群方程，得到对有效场论成立的新非重整化定理，且适用于一般圈数（general loop orders）。

Conclusion: 该投影形式为多粒子振幅分析提供了统一框架，把分波展开、割线计算与重整化群方法联系起来，并给出具有一般圈数有效性的非重整化结论。

Abstract: We develop a projection formalism for four-dimensional massless scattering amplitudes based on the construction of harmonics on multi-particle phase space. This provides a generalization of the partial-wave decomposition valid for any number of particles. Applying this formalism to calculations of on-shell cuts, we show that any cut calculation can be rewritten as a sum of products of projected coefficients and derive a condition for this sum to vanish. Using the on-shell renormalization group equation in combination with our projection formalism, we derive new non-renormalization theorems for effective field theories valid at general loop orders.

</details>


### [272] [Cosmic-string calorimetry with a 220\,PeV neutrino](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37282)
*Bo-Qiang Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: KM3NeT 观测到的 220 PeV 中微子事件 KM3-230213A 在无源对应体的情况下，可被解释为宇宙弦网络（cosmic-string network）的能量释放；该事件相当于一个量热计（calorimeter），其通量直接测量网络已消耗的功率，由此给出对弦张力与粒子能量标度乘积 $G\mu\varepsilon_p$ 的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KM3NeT 报告的 KM3-230213A 是一个 220 PeV 的中微子事件，且未识别出源。若其来自宇宙弦网络，则能量守恒使该事件成为测量网络功率消耗的量热计，从而为约束宇宙弦参数提供新的独立途径。

Method: 以能量守恒为出发点，将超高频中微子事件视为对宇宙弦网络总功率的 calorimetric 测量；提出网络可通过 critical current 处的 cusp 以单位效率（unit efficiency）供给该功率，并将所得通量与脉冲星计时（pulsar timing）给出的限制进行对比。

Result: 在 $f_\nu=0.1$ 下得到 $G\mu\varepsilon_p=(2.8\text{--}4.3)\times10^{-11}$。脉冲星计时已达 $10^{-10}$ 量级，故单次事件即可与计时阵列的灵敏度相竞争。对于仍通过引力辐射（gravitational radiation）耗能的网络分支，通量与计时联合要求网络约三分之一能量注入粒子，该份额可由更多高于 100 PeV 的事件测得。

Conclusion: 单个超高能中微子事件可作为宇宙弦网络的量热探针，其参数限制已能与脉冲星计时阵列匹敌；未来对 100 PeV 以上事件的统计将可测定网络中转化为粒子的能量份额。

Abstract: KM3NeT has reported KM3-230213A, a $220$\,PeV neutrino with no source identified. If it comes from a cosmic-string network, energy conservation makes the event a calorimeter. The flux measures the power the network has spent, not how it emits, and a cusp at critical current can supply that power at unit efficiency. The event gives $Gμε_p=(2.8\text{--}4.3)\times10^{-11}$ for $f_ν=0.1$. Pulsar timing already reaches $10^{-10}$, so one event competes with the arrays. For the branch that still radiates gravitationally, flux and timing together require a third of the network's energy in particles, a share that further events above $100$\,PeV would measure.

</details>


### [273] [Custodial scalars and Higgs branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36081)
*Luc Darmé,Baptiste Filoche,Sebastian Monath*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出，对于大多数带规范相互作用的标量场（gauged scalar fields），标量势（scalar potential）可被系统地组织成一棵按对称性递增排列的算符树（ordered tree）；借助中介整体对称性（intermediary global symmetries），部分"custodial scalars"在树图层次可自然偏轻甚至严格无质量（称为 accidents）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量势的一般结构中质量层级与对称性之间的关系缺乏系统刻画，尤其非超对称情形下"自然轻"或"无质量"标量分量（custodial scalars、accidents）的成因需要更清晰的组织框架。

Method: 将标量势按对称性递增的算符层级组织为有序树，利用树中体现的中介整体对称性分析树图层次的质量结构；并借助矩映射（moment maps）将该结构与超对称理论（supersymmetric theories）的希格斯分支（Higgs branch）相联系，从而引入辛几何（symplectic geometry）工具研究非超对称标量势的真空。

Result: 该组织方式在树图层次自然给出 custodial scalars 的可技术性偏轻或严格无质量；矩映射建立了与超对称希格斯分支的对应，使辛几何工具可用于非超对称标量势真空分析；文中给出从非阿贝尔（non-abelian）accident 到由 custodial scalars 生成类标准模型（Standard Model-like）希格斯扇区的多种应用。

Conclusion: 标量势的对称性树结构与辛几何/矩映射视角为理解 custodial scalars 的质量层级和构造类标准模型希格斯扇区提供了系统工具，并将超对称中的希格斯分支技术迁移至非超对称标量势研究。

Abstract: We show that, for most gauged scalar fields, the scalar potential can be systematically organised into an ordered tree of increasingly symmetric operators. The intermediary global symmetries represented in the tree allow at tree-level a subset of the scalar field components, the custodial scalars, to be technically lighter than the scalar mass scale, or even exactly massless (so-called accidents). We further demonstrate, using the notion of moment maps, that such structure can be related to the Higgs branch of supersymmetric theories, allowing the use of powerful symplectic geometry tools in studying the vacuum of non-supersymmetric scalar potentials. We present various applications, from an example of a non-abelian accident to a Standard Model-like Higgs sector generated from custodial scalars.

</details>


### [274] [Nonradial oscillation modes of axion condensed neutron stars in the Cowling approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37308)
*Antonio Gómez-Bañón,Pantelis Pnigouras,José A. Pons*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在轻夸克轴子模型中，有限密度修正在中子星内产生轴子场，进而使轴子凝聚中子星的平衡结构与振荡谱发生改变；本文在相对论Cowling近似下研究非径向（l=2）振荡谱，发现平衡结构使f模与p模频率升高至约20%，而动力学耦合部分抵消该位移（f模几乎完全抵消），并使高于轴子质量阈值的流体模态新增轴子辐射阻尼通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻QCD轴子模型下，有限密度修正可导致中子星内部出现非零轴子场；此前工作仅分析径向振荡，而非径向振荡谱对星体结构变化与新增阻尼通道较为敏感，因此需要考察轴子凝聚对非径向模态的影响。

Method: 采用相对论Cowling近似（relativistic Cowling approximation），在保持流体与轴子微扰完全耦合、动力学演化的前提下，计算轴子凝聚中子星的非径向（l=2）振荡谱。

Result: 凝聚态通过两条途径改变流体谱：改变后的平衡结构使f模与p模频率升高最多约20%，而与轴子场的动力学耦合部分补偿该位移，对f模几乎完全补偿。频率高于轴子质量的流体模通过轴子发射获得新阻尼通道；f模在所有支持凝聚的位形下均低于该阈值，p模阻尼时标可短至约10 ms，短于其引力波阻尼时标，但某些轴子质量下出射轴子振幅近乎为零。谱中还包含虚部达kHz量级的强阻尼轴子主导准正规模，以及类似引力原子能级的束缚轴子态；在传播阈值以下，束缚p模与这些能级发生避免交叉（avoided crossing），交换流体主导与轴子主导特征。

Conclusion: 轴子凝聚对非径向振荡谱产生可观的定量与定性影响：既改变模态频率，又引入轴子辐射阻尼与轴子主导模态及束缚态，并出现避免交叉等模式混合现象；这些效应为通过振荡谱探测中子星内部轴子凝聚提供了可能。

Abstract: In light QCD axion models, finite-density corrections to the axion potential can source a nonvanishing axion field inside neutron stars. Extending our previous analysis of radial oscillations, we study the nonradial ($l=2$) oscillation spectrum of such axion condensed neutron stars in the relativistic Cowling approximation, keeping the fluid and axion perturbations fully coupled and dynamical. The condensate modifies the fluid spectrum in two ways: the altered equilibrium structure raises the $f$- and $p$-mode frequencies by up to $\sim20\%$, while the dynamical coupling to the axion field partially compensates this shift, almost entirely for the $f$-mode. Fluid modes with frequencies above the axion mass acquire a new damping channel through axion emission. The $f$-mode remains below this threshold for all configurations supporting a condensate, whereas the $p$-modes are damped on timescales that can be as short as $\sim10$ ms, shorter than their gravitational-wave damping times, except for certain axion masses where the outgoing axion amplitude nearly vanishes. The spectrum also contains a family of strongly damped axion-dominated quasinormal modes, with imaginary parts of order kHz, and a sequence of gravitationally bound axion states analogous to gravitational atom levels. Below the propagation threshold, the bound $p$-modes undergo avoided crossings with these levels, exchanging their fluid- and axion-dominated character.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [When is a bosonic operator a Hamiltonian?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33545)
*Felix Fischer,Hermann Schulz-Baldes,Daniel Burgarth,Davide Lonigro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究连续变量量子信息中由正交算符 x 与 p 的厄米多项式（Hermitian polynomials）所定义的量子动力学的唯一性问题，并给出一个仅凭多项式系数即可判定存在零个、一个或无穷多个物理上不同动力学的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厄米多项式是连续变量量子信息（continuous-variable quantum information）的基本构件，超导电路与离子阱中已可常规制备越来越复杂的此类多项式。然而这类多项式未必定义唯一的量子动力学，这种障碍无法被代数论证察觉，且可能使有限维截断（finite-dimensional truncation）不收敛或悄然收敛到错误的动力学。

Method: 提出一种方法并将其实现为算法：仅从多项式系数出发判定上述三种情形（唯一、不存在、无穷多）中的哪一种发生以及解族的规模，无需用户提供泛函分析（functional-analytic）方面的输入。

Result: 对高斯算子（Gaussian operators），答案恒为唯一（一个）；对非高斯算子（non-Gaussian operators）——连续变量量子计算（continuous-variable quantum computing）不可或缺的情形——其余结果在简单且物理自然的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）上已经出现。算法亦被应用于近期文献中提取的算子。

Conclusion: 多项式形式的动力学定义存在非平凡的适定性（well-posedness）障碍，高斯与非高斯情形之间存在定性分界；所提算法提供了可操作的判定工具，可用于检验文献中已有算子的定义良定性。

Abstract: Hermitian polynomials in the quadrature operators $x$ and $p$ are the basic ingredients of continuous-variable quantum information, and increasingly exotic ones are now routinely engineered in superconducting circuits and trapped ions. However, such a polynomial need not define unique quantum dynamics: it may define exactly one, none, or an infinite family of physically distinct ones. Such obstructions are invisible to algebraic arguments and can compromise finite-dimensional truncations, which may fail to converge or silently converge to the wrong dynamics. We develop a method, implemented as an algorithm, which decides which case occurs, and how large the family is, from the coefficients alone, with no functional-analytic input from the user. For Gaussian operators the answer is always one; for non-Gaussian ones, a regime continuous-variable quantum computing cannot do without, the other outcomes occur already for simple and physically natural Hamiltonians. We apply the algorithm to operators drawn from the recent literature.

</details>


### [276] [An algebraic characterisation of $n$-qubit pure-state invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36076)
*Markus Frembs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在 Bloch 表示下研究 n 量子比特纯态，构造由 Pauli 期望值构成的二次函数族，完整刻画其产生与状态无关纯度约束的全部情形，并将动力学稳定子族与辛李代数及辛 2-设计联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度矩阵的 Bloch 表示中，Pauli 期望值的二次函数与态的纯度、不确定性及信道性质密切相关，但哪些函数能给出与状态无关的纯度约束缺乏完整刻画。

Method: 在前期工作基础上定义 Pauli 期望值的一族二次函数，完整刻画所有产生状态无关纯度约束（state-independent purity constraints）的情形；随后引入在状态更新下稳定的动力学约束，并用射影 Pauli 群上辛形式（symplectic form）的二次细化之 Arf 不变量区分辛情形与正交情形。

Result: 纯态不变量族结构丰富，与辛极空间（symplectic polar spaces）几何紧密关联；动力学稳定子族对应辛李代数 sp(2^{n-1})⊂su(2^n) 的不同嵌入并具有 Pauli 算子生成集；Arf 不变量可等同于 C^{2^n} 上由 Pauli 算子生成且受限的四元数结构（quaternionic structures）。

Conclusion: 据此刻画了 Pauli 可观测量方差的所有状态无关不确定性关系（uncertainty relations），以及输出保真度与输出纯度均与状态无关的全部 Pauli 信道；并证明纯态不变量的射影稳定子群轨道（n≥2 时为 Clifford 群真子群）构成复射影态 2-设计（2-designs），其酉提升构成辛 2-设计。

Abstract: We study the set of $n$-qubit pure quantum states. Specifically, we consider their incarnation as density matrices in Bloch representation. Building on prior work, we define a family of quadratic functions in Pauli expectation values, and fully characterise all instances that result in state-independent purity constraints. The resulting family of pure-state invariants is surprisingly rich in structure, with strong ties to the geometry of symplectic polar spaces. After charting the general landscape, we define a sub-family of pure-state invariants that satisfies the dynamical constraint of being stable under state update. We prove that it corresponds with (different embeddings of) symplectic Lie algebras $\mathfrak{sp}(2^{n-1})\subset\mathfrak{su}(2^n)$, which admit a generating set of Pauli operators. We carefully distinguish the symplectic case from its orthogonal counterpart, using the Arf invariant of quadratic refinements of the symplectic form on the projective Pauli group, which we further identify with quaternionic structures on $\mathbb{C}^{2^n}$ that are both generated by and restricted to Pauli operators. As a consequence of this analysis, we reveal previously largely overlooked structure in the $n$-qubit Pauli group. We highlight this in a number of examples. We characterise all state-independent uncertainty relations in variance for Pauli observables, as well as all Pauli channels for which the output fidelity and output purity are state-independent. We further show that the orbits of the projective stabiliser groups of pure-state invariants, which for $n\geq2$ are proper subgroups of the Clifford group, form complex projective state $2$-designs, while their unitary lifts form symplectic $2$-designs.

</details>


### [277] [Radial power-like potentials: from the Bohr-Sommerfeld $\ell \neq 0$ state energies to the exact ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36421)
*J. C. del Valle,A. V. Turbiner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出两类新的 Bohr-Sommerfeld（B-S）型量子化条件，分别适用于非奇异与奇异径向势，并系统检验其对幂次型与对数型势的能谱精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 B-S 量子化条件在处理 $d$ 维径向势（尤其奇异势）时精度有限，需要一个统一且可解析化的方案来逼近精确能谱。

Method: 针对 $V=\operatorname{sign}(m)r^m$（$m=-3/2,-1,-1/2,1,3,4,6$）及 $V=\log r$（对应 $m=0$）在 $d=2,3$、$\ell=1$–$5$、$n_r\in[0,100]$ 范围内计算 B-S 能级 $E_{BS}$，并与精确解及经 Langer 修正（Langer correction）的结果 $E^{(L)}_{BS}$ 比较；同时引入无量纲 WKB 修正（WKB correction）$\gamma$。

Result: 大 $n_r$ 下 $E_{BS}$ 趋于精确值；$m=2$（径向谐振子）与 $m=-1$（$3D$ Coulomb 势的 $d$ 维类比）分别在第一、第二量子化条件下与精确谱完全一致；四次振子 $V=r^4$ 的结果与 Langer 修正版本总体吻合良好。

Conclusion: 对任意幂次型势，在径向量子数作微小参数平移后可写出能谱解析式；引入极小的 $\gamma$ 即可对所有研究的 $n_r,\ell$ 重现精确能量，且 $m\geq0$ 时 $\gamma$ 尤其微小。

Abstract: Two new Bohr-Sommerfeld (B-S) type quantization conditions for $d$-dimensional radial potentials are proposed for non-singular and singular potentials, respectively. The power-like potentials \hbox{$V=\operatorname{sign}(m)\,r^m$} for $m=-3/2,-1,-1/2, 1,3,4,6$ and $V=\log r$ (that corresponds to $m=0$) are studied in detail for radial quantum numbers $n_r \in [0,100]$ and for angular momenta $\ell=1,2,3,4,5$ in dimensions $d=2,3$. It is shown that for large $n_r$ the B-S energies $E_{BS}$ always tend to the exact ones. For $m=2$ (the radial harmonic oscillator) and $m=-1$ (the $d$-dimensional analogue of the $3D$ Coulomb potential) the B-S spectrum coincides with the exact one for the first and second quantization conditions, respectively. In general, up to a small parametric shift of the radial quantum number, the analytic formulas for the energy spectrum can be written for arbitrary power-like potential. A comparison of $E_{BS}$ with the energies $E^{(L)}_{BS}$, obtained for the radial potentials modified by the Langer correction for $d=3$, are in good agreement in general: in detail, it was checked for the quartic oscillator $V=r^4$. It is demonstrated that introducing a dimensionless {\it WKB correction} $γ$ into the r.h.s. of the proposed B-S type quantization condition allows us to reproduce the exact energies with $γ$ being very small for positive degrees $m \geq 0$ and sufficiently small for non-positive ones $m < 0$ for all $n_r, \ell$ studied.

</details>


### [278] [Recovering classical gauge geometry from quantum master constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37493)
*Alejandro Corichi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究量子约束的精确可归一化解构成射影空间的辛子流形（symplectic submanifold）而经典第一类约束（first-class constraints）只给出辛形式退化的曲面，并论证当量子理论被视为基本且约束由单一正主算子（master operator）实施时，退化方向（degenerate directions）如何被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典第一类约束定义的曲面辛形式退化，而量子约束的可归一再化解给出辛子流形，二者结构不一致；论文希望在量子为基本、约束由单个正主函数（master function）施加的框架下，说明退化方向如何被恢复以及与余各向同性（coisotropy）的关系。

Method: 对零集为 Morse–Bott 临界流形的非负主函数，证明线性化哈密顿向量场（Hamiltonian vector field）的像等于零集的辛正交补，且余各向同性等价于该线性化平方为零；并结合二次主函数（quadratic masters）、半经典波包（semiclassical packet）协方差与 SL(2,R) 非阿贝尔模型做具体分析。

Result: 精确量子物理空间对应同一二分法的相反（旋转）情形；二次主函数谱底为 (ℏ/2)Tr₊L，故第一类零集不携带 ℏ 阶偏移，这解释了主约束方案检验中观察到的 ℏ 与 ℏ² 偏移差异；半经典波包极限协方差的二阶时间导数以规范分布（gauge distribution）为像，两固定总能量振子给出精确主演化与约化辛极限；SL(2,R) 模型得到相干与 Weyl 主符号、本质自伴性、有限时间传播估计及谱底 ℏ²/2。

Conclusion: 线性化哈密顿向量场的像由零集固定，但定理将其等同于主动力学的响应，从而可由量子态触及；由此给出三类实现，统一刻画了精确量子物理空间、谱底偏移与半经典协方差剪切，并在非阿贝尔模型中验证了谱底 ℏ²/2。

Abstract: Exact normalizable solutions of quantum constraints form a symplectic submanifold of the space of rays, whereas classical first-class constraints define a surface on which the symplectic form is degenerate. We ask how the degenerate directions can be recovered when quantum theory is regarded as fundamental and the constraints are imposed through a single positive master operator. For a nonnegative master function whose zero set is a Morse--Bott critical manifold, the image of the linearized Hamiltonian vector field is the symplectic orthogonal of the zero set, and coisotropy is equivalent to this linearization having square zero. The image is fixed by the zero set, but the theorem identifies it with the response of the master dynamics, which makes it accessible to quantum states. Three realizations follow. The exact quantum physical space is the opposite, rotational case of the same dichotomy. For quadratic masters, the bottom of the spectrum is $(\hbar/2)\Tr_+L$, so first-class zero sets carry no offset of order $\hbar$; within the harmonic approximation this explains the $\hbar$ versus $\hbar^2$ offsets observed in tests of the master-constraint programme. Finally, the second time derivative of the limiting covariance of a semiclassical packet, which can be determined by bounded probes, has the gauge distribution as its image. Two oscillators at fixed total energy give exact master evolution, an exact realization of this covariance shear, and the reduced symplectic limit. For the non-Abelian SL(2,$\R$) model we compute the coherent and Weyl master symbols, prove essential self-adjointness and a finite-time propagation estimate, and derive the spectral bottom $\hbar^2/2$.

</details>


### [279] [Optimal quantum discrimination at Liouvillian exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37660)
*Aritra Ghosh,M. Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，在具有唯一稳态（unique stationary state）与非零弛豫间隙（relaxation gap）的有限维 Lindblad 系统中，奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）虽增强谱响应，却不改变可靠探测所需观测时间随扰动强度的标度律；但通过选择提升量子 Fisher 信息率（quantum Fisher information rate）的扰动方式，EP 可在相同衰减率下缩短达到既定探测精度所需时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米传感（non-Hermitian sensing）的核心争议在于奇异点能否真正使弱扰动更易被探测。该文旨在厘清 EP 增强的谱响应与探测所需观测时间标度之间的关系，界定 EP 传感的根本极限。

Method: 针对有限维 Lindblad 系统，在唯一稳态与非零弛豫间隙条件下分析可靠探测弱扰动所需观测时间随扰动强度的标度行为；并考察选择性扰动对量子 Fisher 信息率的影响，进而以处于 EP 的光力（optomechanical）模型为例，比较其与非 EP 参照在相同衰减率下的探测表现。

Result: 尽管谱响应增强，EP 在观测时间随扰动强度缩放的标度律上不提供根本优势；然而通过选取增强量子 Fisher 信息率的扰动，EP 可相对相同衰减率的非 EP 参照缩短达到预设探测精度所需的时间。光力模型示例表明，借助腔输出可在更短时间内以同等可靠性探测弱力。

Conclusion: 研究确立了 EP 处扰动探测的基本极限，并为基于光力谐振器、超导量子比特（superconducting qubits）与离子阱（trapped ions）的更快非厄米传感器设计提供指导。

Abstract: Central to non-Hermitian sensing is the question of whether exceptional points (EPs) can make weak perturbations easier to detect. We show that despite enhanced spectral response, in finite-dimensional Lindblad systems with a unique stationary state and a nonzero relaxation gap, EPs offer no fundamental advantage in how the observation time required for reliable detection of a weak perturbation scales with the perturbation strength. We nevertheless demonstrate that by selectively choosing perturbations that enhance the quantum Fisher information rate, EPs can shorten the observation time needed to reach a predefined detection accuracy relative to a comparable non-EP reference at the same decay rate. As an illustration, we show using an optomechanical model at an EP that we can detect a weak force through the cavity output at the same reliability in less time than a non-EP reference. Our results establish fundamental limits on perturbation detection at EPs and can guide the design of faster non-Hermitian sensors based on optomechanical resonators, superconducting qubits, and trapped ions.

</details>


### [280] [Meet-in-the-Middle Compilation of the $[\mathrm{CNOT}]$ Class with Fibonacci Anyons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37552)
*Matvei Fedin,Andrey Morozov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作借助 GPU 流水线在编织群（braid group）上系统搜索低泄漏的 [CNOT] 类 Fibonacci 任意子（Fibonacci anyon）门，在长度 L=69 处找到泄漏与误差均约 10^-10 的编织，并从理论上证明了泄漏的代数性质、指数下界与极值分布极限律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两量子比特 Fibonacci 任意子门编译的核心困难在于泄漏（leakage, ℓ）：四维计算子空间会泄漏到融合空间（fusion space）的第五个态上，因此需要寻找低泄漏的编织代表元。

Method: 构建 GPU 流水线，在不同编织群子集上搜索 [CNOT] 类的低泄漏代表元；先穷举五生成元正字母表至 L=21，随后逐步收窄到三生成元、边界 σ_3 及回文编织（palindromic braids），并仅考虑 Garside 左正规形（Garside left normal form）以扩展到 L=72。同时给出关于泄漏 ℓ 的代数与渐近性质的理论证明。

Result: L=21 以内穷举得到 ℓ~10^-3、d_[CNOT]~10^-8；L=69 时找到 ℓ~10^-10、d_[CNOT]~10^-10 的编织。理论上证明 ℓ∈Z[φ]，任意非零泄漏随编织长度呈指数下界，并得到极限律 F_{3,∞}(ℓ)=ℓ^2 与 F_{5,∞}(ℓ)=ℓ^4。

Conclusion: 基于分布函数 F 的渐近分析表明，对于足够长的编织，穷举搜索在寻找低泄漏编织方面的渐近表现优于随机采样（random sampling）；该工作兼顾了直接枚举最优编织与泄漏性质的理论刻画。

Abstract: Compilation of two-qubit Fibonacci anyon gates is complicated by leakage ($\ell$) from the four-dimensional computational sector to the fifth state of the fusion space. We construct a GPU pipeline to find low-leakage representatives of the $[\text{CNOT}]$ class on different subsets of the braid group.
  We exhaustively explore the full positive five-generator alphabet up to $L=21$ with minimal leakage $\ell\sim10^{-3}$ and error $d_{[\text{CNOT}]}\sim 10^{-8}$ on it. To reach larger lengths, the search space is successively narrowed to three-generator, boundary-$σ_3$, and palindromic braids, and by considering only the Garside left normal form. These optimizations allow us to reach $L=72$. For $L=69$, a braid with $\ell\sim10^{-10}$ and $d_{[\text{CNOT}]}\sim 10^{-10}$ is found.
  In addition to directly enumerating optimal braids, we proved that $\ell\in\mathbb Z[\varphi]$, and any nonzero leakage obeys an exponential lower bound in the braid length, and obtained the limit laws $F_{3,\infty}(\ell)=\ell^2$ and $F_{5,\infty}(\ell)=\ell^4$ for our sets. Using asymptotics for the distribution function $F$, we showed that for sufficiently long braids, the asymptotics of exhaustive search are better than the asymptotics of random sampling for finding low-leakage braids.

</details>


### [281] [Symmetry-resolved fermionic correlations in iron-oxide ground spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36047)
*Federico Petrovich,Daniel Staros,Daniel N. Blaschke,Jian-Xin Zhu,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以FeO与Fe₂O₂模型为例，展示在简并基态空间中，不同的对称性分辨方式（局域磁化 vs. 总自旋量子数）会把同一能量子空间分解成相关性质截然不同的态（Slater行列式 vs. 纠缠非高斯态），并指出对称性分辨、模式纠缠、约化密度矩阵关联与守恒算符结构提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的对称性通常用于组织多体哈密顿量（many-body Hamiltonian）的本征空间，但当存在简并（degeneracy）时，不同对称算符集合可将同一能量子空间分解为相关性质差异极大的态，这种微妙作用尚需系统研究。

Method: 采用一系列FeO与Fe₂O₂模型进行研究：对FeO使用Hubbard模型（基态16重简并），分别以局域磁化（local magnetization）和总自旋量子数（total-spin quantum numbers）分辨基态空间，并引入密度-密度Hund相互作用（Hund interactions）；Fe₂O₂作为更大规模的例子，用于考察空间模式纠缠（spatial mode entanglement）与费米子非高斯性（fermionic non-Gaussianity）。

Result: FeO中局域磁化分辨给出Slater行列式（Slater determinants）基，总自旋量子数则从同一基态流形中选出纠缠且非高斯的态；加入Hund相互作用后独立自旋旋转被破坏，基态空间约化为最大磁化的自旋反转对，这些态在自然占据数上极接近Slater行列式，却具有有限的二体累积量（two-body cumulants）与轨道维度显著偏离Slater值的守恒算符代数（conserved-operator algebras）。Fe₂O₂中空间模式纠缠可较强而费米子非高斯性仍较弱，守恒算符结构同样可区分该态与对应的Slater变换类。

Conclusion: 对称性分辨（symmetry resolution）、模式纠缠、约化密度矩阵（reduced-density-matrix）关联与守恒算符结构，为相互作用且简并的电子基态提供互补的信息。

Abstract: Symmetries of quantum systems provide a natural way to organize the eigenspaces of many-body Hamiltonians. When degeneracies exist, however, they play a more subtle role as different sets of symmetry operators can resolve the same energy subspace into states with very different correlation properties. We study this interplay using a sequence of FeO and Fe$_2$O$_2$ models. For a Hubbard model of FeO, the ground space is 16-fold degenerate. Resolving it by local magnetizations gives a basis of Slater determinants, while total-spin quantum numbers select entangled and non-Gaussian states from the same ground manifold. Adding density-density Hund interactions breaks the independent spin rotations and reduces the ground space to a spin-reversal pair with maximal magnetization. These states are extremely close to Slater determinants in their natural occupations, yet have finite two-body cumulants and conserved-operator algebras whose orbit dimensions differ substantially from the Slater values. Fe$_2$O$_2$ provides a larger example in which spatial mode entanglement can be substantial while fermionic non-Gaussianity remains weak, with the conserved-operator structure again distinguishing the state from the corresponding Slater transformation class. Together, these examples show that symmetry resolution, mode entanglement, reduced-density-matrix correlations, and conserved-operator structure provide complementary information about interacting and degenerate electronic ground states.

</details>


### [282] [Generation of quantum light with tunable Wigner negativity with a semiconductor quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36233)
*Hubert Lam,Petr Steindl,Yann Portella,Kiarn T. Laverick,Kian Hwee Lim,Juan R. Alvarez,Aristide Lemaitre,Maria Maffei,Alexia Auffeves,Dario A. Fioretto,Pascale Senellart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用腔耦合单个半导体量子点（semiconductor quantum dot）的相干控制，确定性产生负值可调的 Wigner 负态（Wigner-negative states），经损耗校正后保真度与量子纯度均超过 95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner 负态（Wigner-negative states）是连续变量（continuous-variable）光学量子计算实现量子优势（quantum advantage）的关键资源，但既有制备途径或为概率性（频率转换加光子探测），或依赖量子发射器（quantum emitter）的单光子非线性，因而需要可扩展且可调的确定性制备方案。

Method: 在腔（cavity）中嵌入单个半导体量子点（semiconductor quantum dot），通过对量子点跃迁的光学相干控制（optical coherent control）制备零光子与单光子的量子叠加态；采用零拍测量（homodyne measurement）在相空间（phase space）中逐点表征所生成量子态。

Result: 随激发功率增加观察到 Wigner 函数负性（Wigner-function negativity）的出现，且负值程度可调；经收集与传输损耗校正后，相对于理想态的保真度（fidelity）与量子纯度（quantum purity）均高于 95%，并实现接近最优的正交压缩（quadrature squeezing）。

Conclusion: 基于成熟半导体技术可产生种类丰富的 Wigner 负态，为连续变量光学量子技术（continuous-variable optical quantum technologies）提供了有前景的实现路径。

Abstract: Wigner-negative states of light are key resources for quantum advantage in continuous-variable optical quantum computing. They can be produced probabilistically via frequency conversion and photon detection, or deterministically by exploiting the single-photon nonlinearity of a quantum emitter. Here, we generate such Wigner-negative states of light with a tunable negativity using a single semiconductor quantum dot in a cavity. Optical coherent control of the quantum-dot transition allows to generate a quantum superposition of zero and one photon. The generated quantum states are characterized point-by-point in the phase space through homodyne measurement. The emergence of Wigner-function negativity is observed as the excitation power is increased. After correcting for collection and propagation losses, we obtain fidelities to the ideal state and quantum purities above 95%, together with near-optimal quadrature squeezing. The ability to generate a wide variety of Wigner-negative states with mature semiconductor technology opens promising opportunities for continuous-variable optical quantum technologies.

</details>


### [283] [Quantum Fisher Information Deficit as a Geometric Signature of Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37092)
*Zhang-Chi Yin,Wei Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在具有局域带电对合（local charged involutions）的强 Z₂ 对称态中，强到弱自发对称破缺（SW-SSB）表现为在对称中性对旋转下量子费舍尔信息（QFI）相对最大值的持续亏损，并以此给出热 SW-SSB 的信息几何刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SW-SSB 已成为描述开放系统量子相（quantum phases in open systems）的框架，但无法用常规带电两点关联函数（charged two-point correlators）刻画，需要新的、可量化的长程序诊断量。

Method: 引入围绕对称中性对旋转（symmetry-neutral pair rotations）的最大 QFI 亏损量；通过模型无关的不等式层级（inequality hierarchy）将其与保真度（fidelity）及 Rényi-1 关联函数等价为渐近长程序诊断量；对对称投影吉布斯态（symmetry-projected Gibbs states）推导由对操作注入平均能量控制的下界。

Result: 该 QFI 亏损与有限角度态重叠（finite-angle state overlaps）及沿同一对生成轨道（pair-generated orbit）的无穷小 Bures 几何约束相联系；对对称投影吉布斯态，局域性（locality）使能量代价在系统尺寸与端点间距上一致有界，从而在任意固定有限温度下维持低于最大 QFI 的有限间隙（finite gap）。

Conclusion: 在普通带电长程序缺失时，该持续存在的几何约束为热 SW-SSB 提供了定量的信息几何（information-geometric）刻画，扩展了 SW-SSB 的可观测判据。

Abstract: Recently, strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB) has been established as a framework for describing quantum phases in open systems. However SW-SSB cannot be characterized by conventional charged two-point correlators. In this work, we show that, for strongly $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric states with local charged involutions, SW-SSB manifests as a persistent deficit from the maximal quantum Fisher information (QFI) under symmetry-neutral pair rotations. A model-independent inequality hierarchy establishes the equivalence of this deficit with fidelity and Rényi-1 correlators as asymptotic long-range-order diagnostics. It thereby connects finite-angle state overlaps to a constraint on the infinitesimal Bures geometry along the same pair-generated orbit. For symmetry-projected Gibbs states, we further derive a lower bound on the deficit governed by the mean energy injected by the pair operation. Crucially, locality keeps this energy cost uniformly bounded in system size and endpoint separation, maintaining a finite gap below the maximal QFI at any fixed finite temperature. When ordinary charged long range order is absent, this persistent geometric constraint provides a quantitative information-geometric characterization of thermal SW-SSB.

</details>


### [284] [Experimental evidence for quantum Brownian motion in electron-metal decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37293)
*Wayne Cheng-Wei Huang,Zilin Chen,Herman Batelaan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作首次提供实验证据，表明量子布朗运动（quantum Brownian motion）在电子运动退相干（motional decoherence）中起关键作用，并首次观测到电子—金属退相干及其连续可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双缝实验的科学意义核心在于运动退相干及其机制；运动态退相干是理解量子—经典过渡（quantum-to-classical transition）的基础，也关系到小型化量子电子器件运行与电子显微镜分辨率极限。该研究旨在实验上验证退相干机制并落实长期悬而未决的理论预言。

Method: 采用实验手段研究电子在微通道体系中的运动退相干，基于电子微通道系统（electron-microchannel systems）作为开放量子平台，检验 Anglin-Paz-Zurek 方案所预言的电子—金属退相干效应及其可调性，并与理论进行定量比较。

Result: 首次获得电子—金属退相干（electron-metal decoherence）的清晰特征，并证明其可连续调节（continuous tunability）；实验与理论定量吻合，结束了对该方案长达二十年的实验探索。

Conclusion: 电子微通道系统可作为自由电子量子光学（free-electron quantum optics）的可行开放量子平台；实验结果为进一步用自由电子检验 Caldeira-Leggett 量子耗散理论（quantum dissipation theory）奠定基础。

Abstract: Feynman once remarked that the double-slit experiment has in its heart the only mystery of quantum mechanics. Arguably, the scientific significance of double-slit experiments lies in motional decoherence and the mechanism behind it. Decoherence of motional states underpins our understanding of quantum-to-classical transition. It is also central to the operation of miniaturized quantum electron devices and poses a resolution limit for electron microscopes. In this work, we provide experimental evidence for the critical role of quantum Brownian motion in electron motional decoherence. We demonstrate, for the first time, clear signatures of electron-metal decoherence and its continuous tunability. Our experiment concludes a two-decade long search for experimental realization of the Anglin-Paz-Zurek proposal, establishing electron-microchannel systems as viable open quantum platforms for free-electron quantum optics. The quantitative agreement between experiment and theory further paves the way for testing the Caldeira-Leggett quantum dissipation theory with free electrons.

</details>


### [285] [Dynamical quantum simulations as laboratories for quantum information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37589)
*Su Yeon Chang,Hailey S. Murray,Brian Barch,Greeshma Oruganti,Luis Pedro García-Pintos,Martin Larocca,Mark M. Wilde,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出将动力学量子模拟（dynamical quantum simulation）中为计算响应函数而制备的多体态，视为量子信息研究的"实验室"。以有限 XX 与 XXX 自旋链中局域脉冲格林函数（Green function）协议为案例，分析源旋转所编码的经典比特如何传播、编码与读取，并发现表示（representation）与读出（readout）之间存在清晰分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力学量子模拟通常只针对特定响应函数或关联函数（correlation function）设计，但底层协议实际准备了包含远超目标可观测量的信息的多体态家族。本文旨在挖掘这些"副产品"态作为量子信息研究平台的潜力。

Method: 选取有限 XX 与 XXX 自旋链中用于计算格林函数的局域脉冲协议，将相反的源旋转（source rotation）解释为向多体系统编码一个经典比特，追踪该记录（record）的传播与局域子系统编码方式；并比较费米子协方差数据（fermionic covariance data）与简单自旋测量在不同链型下的信息获取能力，进一步扩展至局域源角度估计、贝尔态恢复（Bell state recovery）、量子通信速率及双分与真多分纠缠（bipartite and genuine multipartite entanglement）分析。

Result: 在 XX 链中，费米子协方差数据可精确重构带符号响应，而 XXX 链中简单自旋测量可恢复更大比例的局域可用信息；这一反转的顺序从有限脉冲区分延续到局域源角度估计。相同约化态还可用于贝尔态恢复、量子通信速率评估及系统演化纠缠的直接获取。

Conclusion: 为响应计算而设计的动力学量子模拟可自然地用于提出更广泛的量子信息问题，其已制备的多体态构成可复用的信息资源。表示与读出的分离表明，测量方式的选择对信息提取效率具有本质影响。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum simulations are commonly designed to access a particular response function or correlation function. Yet the underlying protocol prepares a family of many-body states that contains information well beyond the observable for which the simulation was originally designed. In this work, we explore the idea of using these states as laboratories for quantum information. As a case study, we consider the local-pulse protocols used to compute Green functions in finite XX and XXX spin chains, and we interpret opposite source rotations as encoding a classical bit into the many-body system. This allows us to follow how the resulting record propagates, how it is encoded across local subsystems, and how readily it can be accessed with different measurements. We also find a clear separation between representation and readout. While fermionic covariance data exactly reconstruct the signed response in the XX chain, simple spin measurements recover a larger fraction of the locally available information in the XXX chain. This reversed ordering persists when we move from finite-pulse discrimination to local source-angle estimation. Going beyond the source record, the same reduced states also allow us to reinterpret results in terms of Bell state recovery and quantum communication rates, as well as providing direct access to the evolving bipartite and genuine multipartite entanglement of the system. Our results show that dynamical quantum simulations developed for response calculations can naturally be used to ask a much broader set of quantum-information questions about the many-body states they already prepare.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Thermodynamic characterization of message-passing: Framework and initial study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36348)
*Kalana G. Abeywardena,Stark C. Draper*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文用失配成本（mismatch cost）框架研究消息传递译码器（message-passing decoder）的热力学效率，以[7,4]汉明码（Hamming code）在二进制擦除信道（binary erasure channel）上的译码为案例，揭示译码动态、输入统计与硬件实现共同决定不可逆能量损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递译码器的能量效率备受关注，但缺乏可计算的热力学损耗下界工具；失配成本框架能刻画系统偏离热力学最优初始分布时产生的额外熵，为量化不可逆能量损失提供依据。

Method: 将译码器建模为主译码程序加局部消息更新的子程序（subroutines），把存储器视为外部复制子系统并排除其热力学成本；以[7,4]汉明码经二进制擦除信道传输为对象，计算由信道诱导的初始化统计与算法状态分布逐步演化所决定的失配成本。

Result: 信道诱导的初始统计与译码过程中算法状态分布的逐步演化共同决定总失配成本；相同的译码动态在不同热力学最优状态分布（即不同物理实现）下会产生不同热力学成本；将输入分布按可纠正与不可纠正擦除模式（erasure patterns）条件化后，呈现出不同的热力学特征。

Conclusion: 热力学成本由译码动态、输入统计与硬件特性共同支配；这为面向热力学信息的译码器设计（thermodynamically-informed decoder design）以实现高能效物理实现提供了方向。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamic efficiency of message-passing decoders using the mismatch cost framework. This framework quantifies the excess entropy generated when a system operates away from the thermodynamically optimal initial distribution. It provides a computable lower bound on irrecoverable energy loss. The decoder is modeled as a main decoding program with local message updates implemented as subroutines. Memory is treated as an external copying subsystem whose thermodynamic cost is excluded. Studying the $[7,4]$ Hamming code transmitted over a binary erasure channel, we show how the channel-induced initialization statistics and the step-wise evolution of the algorithmic state distribution during decoding determine the total mismatch cost. We further show that different thermodynamically optimal state distributions, representing different underlying physical implementations of the decoder, can produce different thermodynamic costs for identical decoding dynamics. Conditioning the input distribution on correctable and non-correctable erasure patterns further reveals distinct thermodynamic signatures. These results demonstrate that thermodynamic cost is jointly governed by decoding dynamics, input statistics, and hardware characteristics. This points to opportunities for thermodynamically-informed decoder design to realize energy-efficient physical implementations.

</details>
